  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的3D重建模型（无论是基于视觉、距离传感器还是两者融合）在薄物体上表现不佳，因为薄物体在RGB图像和3D点云中占用的空间很小，导致重建精度低。作者假设触觉数据可以通过其局部形状和形变信息辅助薄物体的重建。

Method: 作者收集了首个薄物体多模态数据集VisTa3D，包含同步的RGB图像、深度图、触觉响应图以及惯性测量、相机位姿和标定信息，真实值由激光扫描获得。基于该数据集，对现有3D重建模型进行了基准测试，并提出了首个视觉-距离-触觉三模态融合的3D重建基线模型。

Result: 在VisTa3D上的基准测试表明，现有3D重建模型对薄物体确实表现出较低的重建保真度。引入触觉数据后，提出的视觉-距离-触觉融合模型作为基线，验证了触觉数据对薄物体3D重建的积极作用。

Conclusion: 触觉数据能够为薄物体3D重建提供富含局部形状和形变信息的补充模态，从而改善重建效果。VisTa3D数据集为该研究方向提供了标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: State-of-the-art 3D reconstruction models, whether from visual, range, or both, tend to underperform on thin objects. This is partially due to the small amount of space such objects occupy in RGB images and in 3D point clouds. To test the extent of their errors, we collected the first thin object dataset comprising of synchronized RGB images, depth maps, and tactile response maps, where each frame is associated with inertial measurements, camera pose and calibration, and groundtruth depth and segmentation maps obtained from laser scanning of thin objects. We hypothesize that tactile data can aid in the reconstruction of thin objects as their response maps provide local shape and deformation information. Our dataset, termed VisTa3D, comprises of 387 scenes covering 70 thin objects over 17 environments. We benchmarked current 3D reconstruction models on VisTa3D and found that, indeed, they exhibit low fidelity on thin objects. To test if tactile data can help, we introduce the first visual-range-tactile 3D reconstruction model as a baseline. Code and data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/shaniaguo/VisTa3D.

</details>


### [23] [Generating Multi-view Adversarial Examples for Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20748)
*Qi Song,Ziyuan Luo,Haoliang Han,Renjie Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVAP-G，一种多视角对抗扰动生成器，无需逐场景优化即可在单次前向传播中生成跨视角一致的不可察觉扰动，有效攻击3D重建基础模型VGGT，揭示了3D基础模型存在的严重安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真的视觉几何Transformer（VGGT）虽能实现多视角图像的3D重建，但部署时存在严重安全漏洞。传统对抗扰动需要昂贵的逐场景优化，而通用对抗扰动（UAPs）依赖单一静态模式，无法有效攻击VGGT。因此需要一种高效且有效的多视角对抗攻击方法。

Method: 提出MVAP-G（多视角对抗扰动生成器），通过跨视图对抗对齐机制处理多视角图像，在单次前向传播中生成跨视角一致且不可察觉的扰动。该方法避免了推理阶段的迭代优化，同时保证扰动在不同场景间的一致性。

Result: 实验表明，MVAP-G在推理阶段无需迭代优化即可显著降低VGGT的3D重建性能，验证了该方法对VGGT的有效攻击能力。

Conclusion: 本文率先探索3D基础模型上的多视角对抗攻击，揭示了严重的安全漏洞，强调构建鲁棒3D视觉系统的紧迫性，并开源了相关代码。

Abstract: The Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) enables unified feed-forward 3D reconstruction from multi-view images. However, deploying such a high-performance model may expose critical security vulnerabilities. Traditional adversarial perturbations require costly per-scene optimization, while Universal Adversarial Perturbations (UAPs) rely on a single static pattern and fail to effectively attack VGGT. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MVAP-G}, a multi-view adversarial perturbation generator that produces imperceptible consistent perturbations across multiple views in a single feed-forward pass. To ensure perturbation consistency across diverse scenes, we design a cross-view adversarial alignment mechanism to process multi-view images. Experiments demonstrate that MVAP-G significantly degrades VGGT performance without iterative optimization during inference. This work pioneers multi-view adversarial attacks on 3D foundation models, uncovering severe vulnerabilities and underscoring the urgent need for robust 3D vision systems. The code is available at https://github.com/qsong2001/mvap-g.

</details>


### [24] [Identity-Preserving Text-to-Video Generation via Agentic Enhancement and Semantic Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20749)
*Jiayi Gao,Changcheng Hua,Jiaqi Tang,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AESR，一个轻量级的身份保持视频生成增强框架，通过全局智能体提示增强模块和样本级视觉语义修复模块，在不修改闭源生成模型参数的前提下，提升身份保持视频生成的质量，在ACM MM 2026挑战赛中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业视频生成模型虽在视觉质量和运动真实性上表现优异，但在复杂提示下仍存在身份漂移、指令遵循不完整和视觉细节缺失等问题。由于这些模型通常是闭源黑盒，无法通过参数优化直接改进，因此需要一种无需修改底层模型的轻量级增强方案。

Method: AESR包含两大核心模块：(1) 全局智能体提示增强模块——从官方文档学习特定模型的提示格式、从人机交互数据获取以人为中心的视频生成先验，并通过智能体循环积累测试域经验形成可复用playbook；(2) 样本级视觉语义修复模块——使用VLM定位视频中的错误片段并设计修复指令，将选中帧编辑为显式视觉参考，引导视频编辑模型修复局部语义或身份相关错误。此外采用轻量级Mixture-of-Experts选择策略，从不同生成和精修路径中挑选可靠输出。

Result: 在ACM MM 2026身份保持视频生成挑战赛的官方评估协议下，该系统MIPL_Video在Track 1中排名第一，证明了AESR在实际身份保持视频生成任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: AESR展示了在不依赖闭源模型内部参数的前提下，通过提示增强和经验积累以及语义修复相结合，能够有效缓解身份漂移和指令遵循不完整等问题，为黑盒视频生成模型的质量提升提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation aims to synthesize videos that follow natural-language instructions while maintaining the visual identity of a given subject. Recent commercial video generation models have achieved strong visual quality and motion realism, but they still suffer from identity drift, incomplete instruction following, and missing visual details under complex prompts. Since these models are usually closed-source black boxes, directly improving them through parameter optimization is often infeasible. We therefore propose Agentic Enhancement and Semantic Repair (AESR), a lightweight enhancement framework for identity-preserving video generation. To improve prompt construction before generation and mitigate the above failures, AESR introduces a global agentic prompt enhancement module. This module learns model-specific prompting formats from official documentation, acquires human-centered video generation priors from human-interaction data, and accumulates test-domain identity-preserving generation experience into a reusable playbook through an agentic loop. To further repair errors in videos generated with enhanced prompts, AESR introduces a sample-level visual semantic repair module, which uses a VLM to locate erroneous video segments and design repair instructions, edits selected frames into explicit visual references, and guides a video editing model to fix local semantic or identity-related errors. We also adopt a lightweight Mixture-of-Experts selection strategy to choose reliable outputs from different generation and refinement paths. Under the official evaluation protocol of the ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge, our system MIPL\_Video ranked first in Track 1, demonstrating the effectiveness of AESR for practical identity-preserving video generation. The code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/AESR.

</details>


### [25] [SPARK-SAM: Self-Prompt Adaptation with Response Knowledge for SAM in Infrared Small Target Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20754)
*Aji Mao,Zhenming Peng,Bailin Mu,Tian Pu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARK-SAM，解决可提示分割模型（如SAM）直接迁移到红外小目标分割（IRSTD）任务时空间提示与目标域掩码响应之间的不匹配问题。通过学习目标域响应知识并以图像条件联合自提示状态调节解码器，SPARK-SAM在三个基准上分别达到75.78%、86.49%和68.34%的IoU，仅增加0.726M参数，且在14个重训练的SAM变体中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可提示分割模型虽然提供可复用的接口，但直接迁移到红外小目标分割任务时，空间提示与目标域掩码响应之间存在严重不匹配——实验诊断显示，即使用目标覆盖松散的框提示，官方SAM2.1在三个基准上的最佳IoU仅为4.69%、1.64%和2.28%，性能极低，亟需针对性的自提示适应策略。

Method: SPARK-SAM包含两个核心机制：（1）学习目标域响应知识并用图像条件联合自提示状态（image-conditioned joint self-prompt state）调节解码器；（2）训练结合基准掩码监督与可靠性感知的响应指导（reliability-aware response guidance）。此外还包含高分辨率提示精炼和高分辨率提示精炼等技术，并通过冻结权重的干预实验测量输出对联合自提示状态的敏感性。

Result: SPARK-SAM在NUAA-SIRST、NUDT-SIRST和IRSTD-1K三个基准上分别达到75.78%、86.49%和68.34%的IoU，仅需0.726M额外参数，在14个重训练的SAM变体和适应方法中排名第一。消融实验显示响应指导和提示精炼在三个数据集上均带来一致的精度提升。

Conclusion: 响应适应（response adaptation）在预测点获得可靠目标定位之前就已达到最终IoU的大部分，提示监督将预测提示候选与目标位置对齐。该方法验证了通过学习目标域响应知识和联合自提示状态能有效弥合SAM与IRSTD任务之间的域差距。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation models provide a reusable interface, but direct transfer to automatic infrared small-target segmentation (IRSTD) exposes a mismatch between spatial prompts and target-domain mask responses. In a diagnostic using target-covering loose-box prompts deterministically derived from test reference masks, the best official SAM2.1 results are only 4.69%, 1.64%, and 2.28% IoU on NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, and IRSTD-1K. We introduce SPARK-SAM (Self-Prompt Adaptation with Response Knowledge for SAM), which learns target-domain response knowledge and conditions the decoder through an image-conditioned joint self-prompt state. Training combines benchmark-mask supervision with reliability-aware response guidance. SPARK-SAM achieves 75.78%, 86.49%, and 68.34% IoU with 0.726M additional parameters, ranking first on two benchmarks among 14 retrained SAM variants and adaptations evaluated as automatic image-to-mask methods. The staged IRSTD-1K diagnostic shows that response adaptation reaches most of the final IoU before the predicted points acquire reliable target grounding. Prompt supervision aligns the predicted prompt candidates with target locations, and frozen-weight interventions measure output sensitivity to the joint self-prompt state. Matched ablations show consistent accuracy gains from response guidance and high-resolution prompt refinement across all three datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/Sakauma/SPARK-SAM.

</details>


### [26] [Vis-Poison: Poisoning Visual Knowledge in Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20756)
*Rujin Liang,Zhongpu Chen,Yuhao Lei,Xin Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vis-Poison，一种新型视觉知识投毒攻击方法，无需篡改任何文本元数据，仅通过构造视觉上合理的投毒图像即可攻击多模态检索增强生成（RAG）系统。在黑盒场景下，该方法对30k规模的多模态知识库实现了40.16%-65.40%的端到端攻击成功率，且对多种MLLM保持平均60%以上的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态RAG系统日益依赖图像作为外部知识源，但现有的攻击方法主要依赖篡改文本元数据（如标题、摘要等）来实施投毒。由于缺少仅针对视觉证据本身的攻击手段，多模态RAG系统面对纯视觉层面的安全威胁时缺乏防护，因此需要一种不依赖任何文本操控的纯图像投毒攻击来揭示这一安全漏洞。

Method: Vis-Poison通过一个自动化的多智能体（multi-agent）方法构建视觉上合理的投毒图像。该攻击的核心特点是投毒图像本身即为攻击者控制的载荷，不涉及对标题、摘要、元数据或任何关联文本的篡改。评估涉及两条代表性多模态RAG流水线、四种嵌入模型和六种生成模型。

Result: 在黑盒设置下，Vis-Poison对30k条目的多模态知识库实现了40.16%\至65.40%的端到端攻击成功率。此外，即使面对仅凭参数化知识就能正确回答的多种MLLM，Vis-Poison仍保持有效，平均成功率超过60%。

Conclusion: Vis-Poison成功证明了多模态RAG系统仅依赖视觉证据即可被投毒攻击所破坏，且该攻击在多种嵌入模型、生成模型和RAG流水线下均具有广泛的适用性和有效性，揭示了现有多模态RAG系统在视觉层面存在的严重安全漏洞。

Abstract: While multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems increasingly rely on images as external knowledge sources, the introduction of poisoned visual evidence can severely compromise multimodal large language model (MLLM) generation. Unlike prior attacks that rely on altering textual metadata, we introduce Vis-Poison, a novel visual knowledge poisoning attack where the poisoned image itself is the attacker-controlled payload, without manipulating captions, summaries, metadata, or other associated text. Specifically, this attack is instantiated through an automated multi-agent method that constructs visually plausible poisoned images. To assess its impact, we evaluate Vis-Poison across two representative multimodal RAG pipelines, four embedding models, and six generation models. Empirically, Vis-Poison achieves an end-to-end attack success rate of 40.16\% to 65.40\% against 30k-entry multimodal knowledge bases in \emph{black-box} settings. Moreover, Vis-Poison remains effective against various MLLMs that can answer correctly from parametric knowledge alone, with an average success rate above 60\%. Code and data are available at https://github.com/SWUFE-DB-Group/Vis-Poison.

</details>


### [27] [DiGS-Avatar: Single-Image Animatable 3D Human Reconstruction via UV-Space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20759)
*Jiakun Li,Li Fang,Hao Zhu,Fei Hu,Long Ye,Yuan Zhang,Jinyao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiGS-Avatar将单张图像的三维人体重建重新定义为基于扩散模型的UV潜在空间补全任务，通过教师-学生框架确保三维一致性，在0.71秒内重建出可动画的三维化身，实现了最先进的视觉保真度和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像三维人体重建方法常产生过度平滑的纹理和几何不一致问题；而基于扩散模型的方法依赖多视角合成作为三维重建的前置步骤，计算成本高且容易产生视角不一致。因此需要一种既高效又能保证三维一致性的生成式重建方法。

Method: 将任务重构为基于扩散的UV-潜在空间补全任务，从设计上保证三维一致性。引入教师-学生框架：多视角教师网络提供几何对齐的伪真实潜在表示，用于监督单视角扩散学生网络。将推断出的潜在表示作为鲁棒的结构骨架，注入高层语义特征以精确恢复细粒度纹理细节而不破坏空间完整性，最后将精炼后的表示解码为三维高斯图元。

Result: 大量实验表明，DiGS-Avatar在视觉保真度和零样本泛化方面达到最先进或高度具竞争力的水平，且重建完整可动画三维化身仅需0.71秒。

Conclusion: DiGS-Avatar通过将单图像三维人体重建转化为扩散式UV潜在补全任务，有效避免了多视角合成的高成本与不一致问题，在效率与质量之间取得了优越的平衡，为实时可动画三维化身重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Single-image 3D human reconstruction often suffers from over-smoothed textures and geometric inconsistencies. While diffusion models improve generative quality, their reliance on multi-view synthesis prior to 3D reconstruction is computationally expensive and prone to view inconsistency. We propose DiGS-Avatar, which reformulates this task as an efficient, diffusion-based UV-latent completion task, ensuring 3D consistency by design. To capture accurate spatial structure, we introduce a teacher-student framework where a multi-view teacher provides geometrically aligned pseudo-ground-truth latents to supervise a single-view diffusion student. Treating this inferred latent as a robust structural skeleton, our method injects high-level semantic features to accurately recover fine textural details without disrupting spatial integrity. The refined representation is then decoded into 3D Gaussian primitives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiGS-Avatar achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive visual fidelity and zero-shot generalization, while reconstructing a fully animatable 3D avatar in just 0.71 seconds. Code is available at https://github.com/KLMAV-CUC/DiGS-Avatar.

</details>


### [28] [CARD: Diagnosing Belief to Action Routing Failures in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20763)
*Souptik Kumar Majumdar,Fabian Kögel,Andreas Bulling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARD（Cross-Axis Routing Diagnostic）方法，用于诊断视觉语言模型（VLM）中不同认知轴（beliefs, knowledge, intentions等）表征是否被下游预测实际使用。通过在一个新提出的协作网格世界基准Relay Chain上对开源VLM进行激活引导（activation steering）实验，发现模型存在关键路由失败：模型未能将伙伴的信念表征整合到下一步动作预测中，导致有价值的合作信息被浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明VLM内部能编码心理状态（信念、知识、意图等），但尚不清楚这些表征是否真正被下游预测任务所使用。作者旨在弥合"表征存在"与"表征被利用"之间的鸿沟，系统性地诊断跨轴信息路由问题。

Method: 提出CARD诊断框架：沿一个认知轴（如belief）进行激活引导（activation steering），同时测量另一个轴（如action prediction）的预测响应变化。配套设计了一个新的协作式网格世界基准Relay Chain，用于在受控环境中评估VLM的多代理协作推理能力。

Result: 在开源VLM上应用CARD后发现关键路由失败：模型虽然内部编码了关于合作伙伴的信念信息，但在预测下一步动作时并未使用这些表征，导致有价值的信息在决策过程中被忽略。

Conclusion: CARD揭示了VLM中"编码但不使用"的现象——模型具有心理状态表征能力，却未将其有效路由到行为预测中。这为未来改进VLM的多代理协作推理和认知-行为对齐提供了诊断工具和基准。

Abstract: Linear probes and activation steering have uncovered that vision-language models (VLMs) internally represent mental states such as agents' beliefs, knowledge, and intentions. However, it is unclear whether and how these representations are used by downstream predictions along these axes. To close this gap, we introduce Cross-Axis Routing Diagnostic (CARD), which steers activations along one axis while measuring the response of a different axis's prediction. Applied to open-weight VLMs on Relay Chain -- a new cooperative grid-world benchmark we propose -- we diagnose a critical routing failure: models fail to incorporate belief representations into their next action prediction, effectively leaving valuable information about their partners unused.

</details>


### [29] [Multi-Modal Traffic Sign Detection with Semantic Attributes for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20874)
*Meda Lazar,Sourab Sridhar,Shashwata Gupta,Alexandra Tripcea,Varun Ravi,Senthil Yogamani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合相机与LiDAR的多模态交通标志检测框架，通过强度感知的形变融合模块、双运动模型跟踪器和语义属性分类管线，解决了跨区域泛化、远距离小目标检测和透视畸变下的时序跟踪三大难题，在覆盖60+国家、2500+小时数据的评估中实现了0.49%的目标漏检率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠交通标志检测是自动驾驶全球部署的前提，但现有视觉方法存在三大根本局限：（1）跨区域泛化能力差，各国交通标志外观差异大；（2）远距离小目标检测性能退化，200米处标志仅占10x10像素；（3）车辆接近标志时产生强非线性透视畸变，导致时序跟踪不稳定。

Method: 提出多模态检测框架：（1）Intensity-Aware Deformable Fusion模块，将LiDAR的后向反射线索与相机特征对齐，锚定于几何不变量而非区域特定的视觉外观；（2）双运动模型跟踪器，显式建模车辆接近过程中的非线性透视变换，改善相比线性运动假设的时序一致性；（3）语义属性分类管线，估计遮挡程度、可读性、标志嵌入度和道路相关性，为下游规划提供可操作上下文。

Result: 在覆盖60+国家、2500+小时驾驶数据的自有数据集上（221,068个评估序列），所提管线实现了0.49%的目标漏检率（OMR），展示了商用级自动驾驶系统中全球可泛化的交通标志感知能力。

Conclusion: 通过相机-LiDAR多模态融合，以几何不变量替代区域特定外观作为检测锚点，配合显式非线性运动建模和语义属性评估，可以在全球范围内实现鲁棒、长距离、区域无关的交通标志感知，满足商用级自动驾驶系统的需求。

Abstract: Reliable traffic sign detection is a prerequisite for the global deployment of autonomous driving systems, where regulatory compliance and road safety depend on perceiving signs correctly across regions, ranges, and weather conditions. Despite recent progress, vision-based methods continue to face three fundamental limitations: poor cross-regional generalization due to high diversity across countries, degraded performance on small-object detection at long ranges (traffic signs occupy as little as $10{\times}10$ pixels at 200m), and fragile temporal tracking under the strongly non-linear perspective distortion that occurs as a vehicle approaches a sign. In this paper, we address the problem of robust, long-range, region-agnostic traffic sign perception by combining camera and Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) sensing. We present a multi-modal detection framework whose Intensity-Aware Deformable Fusion module aligns retro-reflective LiDAR cues with camera features, anchoring detection on geometric invariants rather than region-specific visual appearance. We further introduce a dual motion-model tracker that explicitly accounts for non-linear perspective transformations during vehicle approach, substantially improving temporal consistency over linear motion assumptions. Additionally, we develop a semantic attribute classification pipeline that estimates occlusion level, readability, sign embeddedness, and road relevance, providing actionable context to downstream planning. Extensive evaluation on our dataset, spanning 60+ countries and 2,500+ hours of driving data, shows that the proposed pipeline achieves an Object Miss Ratio (OMR) of 0.49% across 221,068 evaluation sequences, demonstrating globally generalizable traffic sign perception in commercial-grade autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [30] [MotionPhys: Detecting AI-Generated Videos via Physical Consistency of Optical-Flow Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20770)
*Haojin He,Hao Tan,Zichang Tan,Ajian Liu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MotionPhys框架，通过将稀疏运动轨迹视为物理证据而非外观伪影，从多时间尺度建模轨迹几何演化，以检测AI生成视频中难以察觉的物理运动不一致性，并在多个数据集上验证了其跨生成器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI视频生成模型虽然能产生视觉上高度逼真、时间过渡平滑的视频，但视觉真实感不等于物理运动一致性。现有生成模型主要优化像素或潜在空间的分布匹配，未显式引入惯性、连续力、轨迹几何等真实世界物理约束。实验表明生成视频在短帧序列上视觉合理，但在完整物体动作过程中无法保持物理运动一致性，产生系统性的统计偏差。

Method: MotionPhys是一种轻量级且可解释的框架，核心思想是将稀疏运动轨迹作为物理证据而非依赖外观伪影或生成器特定痕迹。具体方法包括：在多时间尺度上建模轨迹的几何演化，揭示传统视觉线索难以捕捉的细微运动不一致性，并将其转换为紧凑表示以进行高效检测。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，MotionPhys能够有效检测生成视频中的物理不一致性，并且在不同视频生成器之间具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MotionPhys证明了基于物理运动一致性的轨迹级分析是检测AI生成视频的有效途径，相比依赖外观伪影或生成器特定痕迹的方法，该框架更加鲁棒且具有跨生成器的通用性。

Abstract: Modern AI video generation models can produce videos with high visual fidelity and seemingly smooth temporal transitions. However, visual realism does not necessarily imply physical motion consistency. Existing generative models mainly optimize distribution matching in pixel or latent spaces, without explicitly enforcing real-world constraints such as inertia, continuous forces, and trajectory geometry. Our experiments show that AI-generated videos remain visually plausible over short sequences of consecutive frames, yet fail to preserve physical motion consistency throughout a complete object action, resulting in systematic statistical discrepancies in their motion trajectories. Based on this observation, we introduce MotionPhys, a lightweight and interpretable framework that treats sparse motion trajectories as physical evidence rather than relying on appearance artifacts or generator-specific traces. By modeling the geometric evolution of trajectories across multiple temporal scales, MotionPhys reveals subtle motion inconsistencies that are difficult to capture with conventional visual cues and transforms them into a compact representation for efficient detection. Experiments on multiple datasets show that MotionPhys can effectively detect physical inconsistencies in generated videos and generalizes well across different video generators.

</details>


### [31] [A Collaborative Multi-Modality Interaction for VLA-based End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20890)
*Jingtao Sun,Xiaohai He,Yike Zhang,Dong Huang,Yaonan Wang,Ajmal Mian,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型的端到端自动驾驶系统，通过多模态交互和多轨迹规划优化，解决了现有VLA模型中决策推理不可靠、可解释性差以及异构传感器间缺乏有效多模态交互的问题，在开放环和闭环数据集上展现出更安全、可解释的驾驶决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将端到端自动驾驶视为视觉问答任务，导致决策推理不可靠且缺乏可解释性；同时，异构传感器之间缺乏有效的多模态交互，限制了在长尾驾驶场景中的鲁棒场景感知和可靠驾驶推理。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1)亲和力引导的最优传输（Affinity-Guided Optimal Transport）实现主-辅模态的双向交互；(2)分布一致性模态迁移（Distribution-Consistent Modality Transfer）完成异构模态分布迁移与跨模态交互；(3)多模态多轨迹规划（Multi-modal Multi-Trajectory Planning）结合感知导向轨迹细化（Perception-Oriented Trajectory Refinement）以应对长尾驾驶场景。

Result: 在开放环和闭环数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在安全长时域驾驶推理和道路场景感知方面相比现有驾驶系统均有提升，验证了多模态交互和多轨迹规划与优化对可扩展VLA系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 多模态交互与多轨迹规划优化的结合能够显著提升VLA-based自动驾驶系统的可靠性、可解释性和安全性，为可扩展的VLA自动驾驶系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving by jointly integrating perception, reasoning, and decision making within a unified multimodal framework. However, most existing VLA models formulate end-to-end autonomous driving as a visual question answering task, leading to unreliable and less interpretable decision reasoning. In addition, they fail to establish effective multi-modal interaction across heterogeneous sensors, thereby limiting robust scene perception and reliable driving reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. To this end, we propose a robust VLA-based end-to-end autonomous driving system that combines multi-modality interaction with multi-trajectory planning and optimization, enabling more reliable, interpretable, and safer driving decisions. Our method comprises three core components: (1) Affinity-Guided Optimal Transport for main-auxiliary modality two-way interaction; (2) Distribution-Consistent Modality Transfer for heterogeneous modality distribution transfer and cross-modal interaction; (3) Multi-modal Multi-Trajectory Planning along with Perception-Oriented Trajectory Refinement for better driving decisions to long-tail driving scenarios. Experimental results in open-loop and closed-loop datasets demonstrate improvements in safety long-horizon driving reasoning and road scene perception over existing driving systems, highlighting the ability of our mutli-modality interaction and multi-trajectory planning and optimization for scalable VLA-based systems.

</details>


### [32] [M2Depth: Unifying Monocular Depth Foundation Priors with Multi-View Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20788)
*Byeonggwon Lee,Sanggi Lee,Siwoo Lee,Khang Truong Giang,Soohwan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将深度基础模型（DFM）与级联MVS流水线通过双向互细化策略紧密耦合的框架，利用注意力融合和离散深度区间实现先验引导的成本体细化，在标准基准上超越SOTA方法，且在稀疏视图设置下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的MVS方法在未见场景中泛化能力差，尤其在遮挡区域或视图重叠有限的区域表现不佳。近期工作虽引入深度基础模型提供单目深度先验，但采用静态单向融合方案，未能充分利用两种模态的互补优势。

Method: 提出双向互细化策略：MVS深度用于解决单目预测的尺度模糊性，而单目深度增强MVS估计的结构完整性和细节精度。引入先验引导的成本体细化机制，通过注意力融合和离散化深度区间有效整合多视图与单目信息，促进局部几何一致性。

Result: 在标准基准上优于SOTA的MVS方法，生成更完整、泛化性更强的深度图且边界清晰。尽管未专门针对稀疏视图设计，该框架在稀疏视图设置下仍与专门的稀疏视图方法表现相当，并保持更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 双向互细化策略能够有效解决单目-多视图融合中信息利用不充分的问题，提升MVS在复杂场景中的泛化能力和深度图质量，同时具备良好的稀疏视图适应能力。

Abstract: Deep learning-based Multi-View Stereo (MVS) has advanced significantly but often generalizes poorly to unseen scenes, particularly in occluded areas or regions with limited view overlap. To mitigate this, recent approaches integrate Depth Foundation Models (DFMs) into MVS pipelines to provide monocular depth priors. However, existing methods typically rely on a static, one-way fusion scheme, which fails to fully exploit the complementary strengths of both modalities. We propose a novel framework that overcomes this limitation by tightly coupling a DFM with a cascade MVS pipeline through a bidirectional mutual refinement strategy. Our method leverages MVS depth to resolve the scale ambiguity in monocular predictions, while the monocular depth, in turn, enhances the structural completeness and fine-grained detail of the MVS estimate. Furthermore, we introduce a prior-guided cost volume refinement mechanism that effectively integrates multi-view and monocular information via attention-based fusion and discretized depth bins, thereby promoting local geometric consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art MVS approaches on standard benchmarks, producing more complete and generalizable depth maps with sharp boundaries. Furthermore, although not explicitly designed for sparse-view settings, our framework generalizes remarkably well, competing favorably with even dedicated sparse-view methods while maintaining a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [33] [Masking Is Not Enough: Generative Restoration for Multimodal De-Identification in Medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21133)
*Shiva Shrestha,Zongxing Xie,Chen Zhao,Liran Ma,Zhipeng Cai,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClinX是一个面向医学图像-文本数据的端到端多模态PHI（受保护健康信息）脱敏框架，通过OCR检测可见标识符、ClinX-PRISM生成式修复模块抑制烧录标识符，并结合文本侧渐进式脱敏层级（正则掩码、上下文感知掩码、重写净化）。在MedVQA上联合评估PHI泄漏与下游效用，证明仅靠OCR掩码不足以独立使用，而基于修复的脱敏能在保留临床视觉上下文的同时大幅降低可恢复PHI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像-文本数据通过可见图像内容和伴随文本都可能泄露受保护健康信息（PHI），这成为隐私保护医学AI系统的障碍。现有医学视觉-语言基准主要强调任务效用，而去标识化方法往往与下游推理能力分开评估，缺乏端到端的统一框架。

Method: ClinX包含两个主要组件：(1) 图像侧：使用OCR检测可见标识符，构建二值PHI掩码，应用ClinX-PRISM（无跳跃连接的生成式修复模块）结合面向隐私的后处理来抑制烧录标识符；(2) 文本侧：通过渐进式脱敏层级（正则掩码→上下文感知掩码→重写净化）逐步降低PHI暴露。评估在MedVQA任务上联合衡量PHI泄漏和下游效用。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用OCR掩码作为独立方案是不够的，而基于修复（restoration）的脱敏方法能更好地保留临床相关的视觉上下文，同时显著减少可恢复的PHI信息。

Conclusion: ClinX提供了一个统一的端到端多模态PHI脱敏框架，将图像侧和文本侧的脱敏策略整合到一个评估体系中，证明独立的OCR掩码不足以保护隐私，生成式修复方法在隐私保护与临床效用保持之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Medical image-text data can expose protected health information (PHI) through both visible image content as well as accompanying text, creating a barrier to privacy-preserving medical AI systems. This risk is especially prominent in multimodal systems, where images, questions, reports, and clinical context may enter training, evaluation, or inference pipelines. Existing medical vision-language benchmarks primarily emphasize task utility, while de-identification methods are often evaluated separately from downstream reasoning. We introduce ClinX, an end-to-end multimodal PHI sanitization framework for medical image-text data. ClinX detects visible identifiers with optical character recognition (OCR), constructs binary PHI masks, and applies ClinX-PRISM, a no-skip generative restoration module with privacy-oriented post-processing for burned-in identifier suppression. In parallel, text-side PHI is reduced through progressive de-identification levels: regex masking, context-aware masking, and rewrite-based sanitization. We evaluate ClinX in medical visual question answering (MedVQA), jointly measuring PHI leakage and downstream utility across image-side, text-side, and combined de-identification settings. Results show that OCR-only masking is not sufficient as a standalone solution, and restoration-based sanitization better preserves clinically relevant visual context while sharply reducing recoverable PHI.

</details>


### [34] [CertVLA: Certified Defense against Physical Visual Attacks for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20791)
*Hui Lu,Zhijie Peng,Yuqi Lin,Zaijia Yang,Jiaming He,Shuhan Ye,Yi Yu,Hanwei Zhu,Bingquan Shen,Alex Kot,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CertVLA是针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在闭环控制中的首个认证防御框架，通过校准行为一致性动作区域和确定性覆盖掩码，为补丁攻击和纹理攻击提供可证明的闭环行动证书，确保每个已认证的rollout仅执行与攻击擦除的干净预测一致的动作块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略容易受到局部物理扰动（如补丁攻击和纹理攻击）的威胁，但现有的认证补丁防御仅针对离散标签，无法直接对连续且时间相关的动作进行认证，因此需要一种能够为闭环VLA控制提供可证明安全保证的防御方法。

Method: CertVLA提出了一套组合式认证方法：(1) 提出校准的行为一致动作区域（calibrated region of behaviorally consistent actions）；(2) 使用确定性覆盖掩码确保至少一个被检查的预测是无攻击的；(3) 通过归一化动作分歧和单掩码锚点一致性验证来保证动作一致性；(4) 校准max-min-max情节分数以提供有限样本干净覆盖率；(5) 通过连接查询级决策将动作证书扩展到完整闭环rollout；(6) 在双掩码rollout正确性条件下证明一致性证书保障任务成功。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验中，CertVLA展现了针对补丁攻击的经验和认证有效性，并在纹理攻击上进行了额外的仿真验证。实验证明了证书独立于补丁内容、生成方法和物理变换。

Conclusion: CertVLA是被证明有效的VLA闭环控制认证防御框架，能够在有界支撑威胁模型下提供独立于补丁内容、生成方法和物理变换的可靠性保证，为连续动作空间的闭环机器人控制提供了首个可验证的安全保障。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are vulnerable to localized physical perturbations, yet existing certified patch defenses target discrete labels and cannot directly certify continuous, temporally correlated actions. We introduce CertVLA, a certified defense for closed-loop VLA control under bounded patch and texture attacks. CertVLA proposes a calibrated region of behaviorally consistent actions, while deterministic covering masks ensure that at least one checked prediction is attack-free. Specifically, CertVLA normalizes action disagreement by the benign variation of each mask pair and accepts a single-mask anchor only when it remains consistent under every second mask. It then calibrates the resulting max-min-max episode score to provide finite-sample clean coverage. Conjoining query-level decisions extends the action certificate to the complete closed-loop rollout. Furthermore, we prove that against any adaptive attacker satisfying the bounded-support threat model, every rollout certified by CertVLA executes only action chunks consistent with attack-erased clean predictions. Under dual-mask rollout correctness, this consistency certificate further guarantees task success. The certificate is independent of patch content, generation method, and physical transformation. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate the empirical and certified effectiveness of CertVLA against patch attacks, with additional simulation validation on texture attacks.

</details>


### [35] [Routing Before Looking: Query-Adaptive Evidence Acquisition for Long-form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20805)
*Tianyue Wang,Xuying Wu,Yuxiang Ma,Ruiming Liang,Jiaxuan Kang,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Leigang Qu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Route2Look是一个轻量级且模型无关的框架，用于长视频理解中的查询自适应证据获取。它通过"路由-查看-记忆"循环动态选择证据获取工具，并采用两阶段设计（先通过生成式与检索式轨迹的差分对比分析蒸馏路由技能，再在推理时应用硬路由规则和继续/停止准则），在长视频基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中，现有"先规划后感知"方法虽优于查询无关的流水线，但往往依赖单一主导策略（基于生成或基于检索），无法有效应对多样化查询需求。这种策略与查询不匹配的问题限制了视频智能体的证据获取能力。

Method: Route2Look包含三个工具：Global Browse（全局上下文浏览）、Temporal Ground（显式时间线索定位）和Semantic Retrieve（语义检索），在Route-Look-Memorize循环中运行。核心是一个路由策略，采用两阶段设计：(1) 通过生成式与检索式轨迹的差分对比分析来蒸馏路由技能；(2) 在推理阶段将蒸馏的技能与硬路由规则及继续/停止准则结合应用。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长视频基准上，Route2Look取得了最先进的性能，并在不同数据集和查询类型下均保持了强劲的帧效率。Oracle路由分析进一步验证了查询自适应证据获取的潜力。

Conclusion: Route2Look通过查询自适应的路由策略解决了长视频理解中单一策略的局限，展示了动态工具选择在提升证据获取效率和效果方面的价值，为未来长视频理解研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding remains challenging for video agents due to the mismatch between query demands and evidence acquisition strategies. Although recent planning-before-perception methods outperform query-agnostic pipelines, they often rely on a single dominant strategy, either generation-based strategy or retrieval-based strategy, limiting their ability to handle diverse query demands. We propose Route2Look, a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for query-adaptive evidence acquisition in long-form video understanding. Route2Look operates in a Route-Look-Memorize loop with three tools: Global Browse for holistic context, Temporal Ground for explicit temporal cues, and Semantic Retrieve for semantic search. The core component is a routing policy that dynamically selects evidence acquisition tools based on the query. To build this policy, Route2Look adopts a two-stage design: first distilling the routing skill from differential contrastive analysis between generation-based and retrieval-based trajectories, and then applying the distilled skill with hard routing rules and continue-or-stop criteria during inference. Experiments on challenging long-video benchmarks show that Route2Look achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining strong frame efficiency across datasets and query types. Oracle routing analysis further reveals the potential of query-adaptive evidence acquisition for future long-form video understanding.

</details>

*Zhuoyuan Li,Rui Zhao,Jin Wang,Hanwei Zhu,Cong Zhang,Giuseppe Valenzise,Weisi Lin,Kin-Man Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Action-JND，一种面向具身智能体的token压缩准则。其核心思想是将"可注意差异"(JND)概念从人类视觉系统扩展到具身感知领域，通过语言条件化的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略在闭环控制中的动作响应来定义token的可容忍变化范围，仅在动作偏差保持在可接受范围内时才允许token被压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体的token压缩方法（如token剪枝和KV-cache复用）通常基于冗余度或重要性线索（如视觉相似性、注意力分数、显著性）来指导压缩，但这些线索只是间接衡量了安全压缩的关键因素——即一个token在引起下游动作不可接受的偏差之前能被改变多少。这种接收者相关的容差与JND原理密切相关，需要一种直接基于下游动作响应的压缩准则。

Method: 1) 将JND概念从人类视觉系统（经典JND）和机器视觉（机器导向JND）延伸到具身感知，提出Action-JND，通过VLA策略的语言条件化动作响应来定义token的可注意性；2) 在深度视觉特征空间中开发轻量级token级JND估计器，预测在不改变策略响应的前提下可容忍的最大扰动；3) 将生成的action-tolerance分数作为即插即用的准则，用于VLA压缩范式（包括stale-KV复用和token剪枝），优先压缩action-tolerant的token。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试上使用OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT的实验表明，Action-JND一致性地提高了压缩的可靠性，尤其是在激进压缩比下效果显著。

Conclusion: 通过直接建模token扰动对闭环机器人动作的影响而非依赖间接线索，Action-JND为具身智能体的token压缩提供了更可靠的准则，在保证策略响应的前提下实现更高压缩率。

Abstract: Token compression has become a key technique for reducing the inference cost of large foundation models, with approaches such as token pruning and KV-cache reuse widely adopted in vision-language models and recently explored for embodied agents. In embodied agents, tokens not only support perception and semantic understanding but also directly affect latency-sensitive closed-loop robot action prediction. Existing schemes typically guide compression using redundancy or importance cues, such as visual similarity, attention scores, and saliency. However, these cues only indirectly measure the key factor for safe compression: how much a token can change before causing an unacceptable deviation in downstream actions. This receiver-dependent tolerance is closely related to the principle of just noticeable difference (JND). Classical JND characterizes signal tolerance in the human visual system, while machine-oriented JND extends this concept to downstream machine responses. Building on this progression, we introduce Action-JND, which extends JND modeling to embodied perception by defining noticeability through the language-conditioned action response of a vision-language-action (VLA) policy in closed-loop control. A token change is considered admissible only when the induced action deviation remains within a tolerated margin. To realize this concept, we develop a lightweight token-wise JND estimator in deep visual-feature space to predict the maximum tolerable perturbation while preserving policy responses. The resulting action-tolerance score serves as a plug-and-play criterion for VLA compression paradigms, including stale-KV reuse and token pruning, prioritizing action-tolerant tokens for compression. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark with OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT demonstrate that Action-JND consistently improves compression reliability, especially under aggressive compression ratios.

</details>


### [37] [TRACE: Training-time Report-guided and Clinically Ordered Concept Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20809)
*Wentao Yue,Tianyou Lai,Jiayu Luo,Qingyu Mao,Ziying Wang,Zhenyuan Ning,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，利用训练时的结构化放射学报告作为特权概念监督，在推理时仅需图像即可进行乳腺超声诊断。通过教师引导的概念编辑机制和缺失概念训练策略，实现了更具可解释性和跨域鲁棒性的诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有乳腺超声深度学习诊断方法大多采用端到端的图像到标签范式，缺乏可解释性和鲁棒性。虽然基于概念的方法提供了替代方案，但往往假设完整的标注或在推理时需要多模态输入，严重限制了实际应用。因此需要一种既能利用临床语义概念又能在测试时仅依赖图像的诊断框架。

Method: TRACE包含三个核心组件：(1) 教师引导的概念编辑机制，在恶性度感知的有序概念空间中精炼图像推导的概念；(2) 战略概念缺失训练（SCMT），处理不完整标注问题；(3) 通过编辑蒸馏训练图像自编辑器，实现推理阶段的自主概念精炼，无需报告输入。此外引入了BUSC基准数据集，将图像、标签和结构化属性相连。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，TRACE相比现有方法取得了更优的诊断性能和更强的跨域鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TRACE通过训练时报告引导+测试时纯图像推理的设计，成功弥合了概念级可解释性与实际临床可用性之间的鸿沟，为乳腺超声诊断提供了一种既能利用语义概念又无需多模态推理依赖的可行方案。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound diagnosis relies on clinically meaningful semantic concepts, yet most deep learning methods adopt end-to-end image-to-label paradigms that lack interpretability and robustness. While concept-based approaches offer a promising alternative, they often assume complete annotations or require multimodal inputs at inference, which significantly limits their real-world applicability. To tackle these issues, we propose Training-time Report-guided and Clinically Ordered Concept Editing (TRACE), a training-time report-guided framework that leverages structured radiology reports as privileged concept supervision while enabling image-only diagnosis at test time. TRACE refines image-derived concepts through a teacher-guided editing mechanism within a malignancy-aware ordered concept space. To address incomplete annotations, we introduce Strategic Concept Missing Training (SCMT) and train an image-only self-editor via edit distillation for autonomous concept refinement. Besides, we introduce BUSC, a concept-enriched benchmark linking images, labels, and structured attributes. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that TRACE achieves superior performance and improved cross-domain robustness compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [38] [Enhancing Localized Reasoning for Long Video Understanding via Efficient Segment-to-Video Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20814)
*Beibei Zhang,Chao Xu,Jun Lan,Zongyi Li,Lai Wei,Huijia Zhu,Tongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为S2V（Segment-to-Video Supervision）的高效方法，通过将基于局部片段的VQA标注迁移到整个视频进行训练，以增强多模态大语言模型在长视频理解中的细粒度推理能力，同时显著降低训练开销和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解（LVU）中，复杂且冗长的上下文包含大量干扰噪声，会掩盖局部细节，导致多模态大语言模型产生错误答案。现有基于推理增强的方法（如RFT）存在训练开销大（高标注成本、复杂奖励设计）以及推理延迟高（自反思和迭代感知机制导致冗长输出）的问题。

Method: 提出S2V方法：1）基于局部视频片段生成细粒度的问答对（VQA），利用短片段天然聚焦细节的优势捕捉全局视角下易被忽略的信息；2）将这些片段级VQA迁移回整个视频进行训练，使MLLM学会将细粒度细节与问答正确关联，同时避开整体视频中的干扰噪声；3）仅使用10K VQA样本和简单的准确率奖励进行强化学习（RL）训练；4）推理时仅需单次前向传播和有限输出token。

Result: 实验结果表明，S2V在多个LVU基准上持续提升性能，不仅在LVU准确性上超越通用MLLM和基于推理的方法，在训练和推理效率上也具有显著优势。

Conclusion: S2V通过片段级到视频级的监督迁移策略，以极低的训练开销和高效的单次前向推理实现了长视频理解性能的全面提升，为LVU提供了一个兼顾准确性、训练效率和推理效率的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Though Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown impressive potential in video understanding, long video understanding (LVU) remains challenging since distracting noise in complex and lengthy contexts can obscure localized details, misleading MLLMs to produce incorrect answers. Recent works mitigate these issues by incentivizing deep reasoning to include relevant evidence. However, these methods have two main problems: First, the reinforcement fine-tuning framework (RFT) they leveraged incurs substantial training overheads, including high annotation costs and complicated reward designs. Second, the self-reflective and iterative-perception mechanism in some methods causes lengthy outputs and high inference latency. To alleviate these problems, we propose a novel Segment-to-Video Supervision} method (S2V) to efficiently enhance fine-grained reasoning in LVU. Specifically, we generate question answer pairs (VQA) based on localized segments, and then transfer these segment-based VQA back to the whole video for training. Due to focusing on short segments, segment-based VQA can naturally notice details which tend to be overlooked from a whole-video perspective. Training on such data can enforce MLLMs to correctly associate fine-grained details with QA while avoiding distracting noise in the whole video. The S2V training involves just reinforcement learning (RL) with a simple accuracy reward based on only 10K VQA samples and the resulting S2V model predicts answer using a single forward pass with limited output tokens. Experimental results demonstrate that S2V can consistently improve LVU performance across multiple LVU benchmarks, outperforming both general MLLMs and reasoning-based methods not only in LVU accuracy but also in training and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [39] [Identify, Locate, Link: End-to-End Key-Value Extraction from Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20868)
*A. Said Gurbuz,Ahmed Nassar,Christoph Auer,Maksym Lysak,Lucas Morin,Matteo Omenetti,Tim Strohmeyer,Panagiotis Vagenas,Nikolaos Livathinos,Michele Dolfi,Peter Staar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于紧凑型256M参数视觉语言模型（SmolDocling）的端到端键值提取方法，无需OCR预处理即可直接从文档图像中完成键值对的识别、定位和关联。通过扩展DocTags标签体系、设计合成数据增强管线，并引入布局感知评估框架，在FUNSD、XFUND及大规模私有数据集上优于更大的zero-shot VLM基线，模型体积比Qwen2.5-VL小27倍且推理速度快5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档处理管线将OCR引擎与下游抽取模型级联，导致多阶段错误传播累积，影响结构化信息提取的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: （1）端到端微调SmolDocling（256M参数VLM），联合解决键值对的识别、定位与关联，无需OCR预处理；（2）扩展DocTags，引入key、value、region和link标签，支持多对多关系建模；（3）设计增强管线，结合合成表单填充与基于图的裁剪，保留完整键值子图；（4）引入布局感知评估框架，在文本匹配基础上增加空间边界框验证。

Result: 在FUNSD、XFUND及大规模私有数据集上，模型在布局感知评估下优于更大的zero-shot VLM基线；模型比Qwen2.5-VL（7B）小27倍，推理速度快5倍以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑型端到端VLM方案在保持高性能的同时显著降低了模型规模和推理成本，证明了小模型在文档键值抽取任务上对大规模模型的可行替代性，并计划公开模型权重以促进复现与社区应用。

Abstract: Document processing pipelines traditionally cascade optical character recognition (OCR) engines with downstream models for structured information extraction, leading to multi-stage error propagation. We fine-tune SmolDocling, a compact 256M-parameter vision-language model (VLM), to perform end-to-end key-value extraction directly from document images, jointly solving identification, localization, and association in a single pass without OCR preprocessing. We extend DocTags with specialized key, value, region, and link tags, enabling many-to-many relationships in a unified output sequence. To address data limitations, we design an augmentation pipeline combining synthetic form filling and graph-based crops that preserve complete key-value subgraphs. We further introduce a layout-aware evaluation framework extending text matching with spatial bounding box verification. On FUNSD, XFUND, and a large-scale private dataset, our model outperforms larger zero-shot VLM baselines under layout-aware evaluation, while being 27 times smaller than Qwen2.5-VL (7B) and over 5 times faster at inference. The model weights will be released publicly after publication.

</details>


### [40] [RDANet: Relative Degradation Aware Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20870)
*Rui Liu,Jing Nie,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向红外小目标检测的稳健性增强网络（RDANet），通过多尺度抗混叠下采样（MSAD）和原型引导的跳跃记忆（PGSM）两个模块，分别解决下采样过程中的目标结构丢失和背景变化下的局部对比度不稳定问题，在三个公开基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测面临目标极小、局部对比度弱且背景复杂多变的固有挑战。此外，现有检测器在目标尺度变化或场景背景变化时性能不稳定，根源在于现有方法无法在下采样过程中同时保持目标结构，也无法在背景偏移下维持判别性局部对比度，导致不同条件下的检测性能不均衡。

Method: 提出RDANet，包含两个核心模块：(1) 多尺度抗混叠下采样（MSAD）：引入多尺度抗混叠滤波结合像素折叠聚合，减少分辨率降低时的混叠效应，从而更好保留目标形状信息并抑制无关背景响应；(2) 原型引导跳跃记忆（PGSM）：从共享记忆中检索patch级原型并自适应地集成到当前表征中，以在多样化场景背景下维持稳定的局部对比度线索。

Result: 在三个公开基准上的实验表明，RDANet在大多数评估指标上取得了最佳性能。尺度分层和背景分层的评估进一步表明该模型在目标尺寸和场景复杂度变化下具有更稳定的检测行为。

Conclusion: RDANet通过针对性的模块设计有效解决了红外小目标检测中尺度敏感性和场景敏感性的问题，提升了检测的稳健性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection is still challenging in remote sensing imagery, because the targets are extremely small, exhibit weak local contrast, and are often embedded in complex and highly variable backgrounds. In addition to these inherent difficulties, we observe that existing detectors often show unstable performance when the target scale changes or when the scene background varies. This scale- and scene-sensitive degradation indicates that current methods are insufficient in simultaneously preserving target structure during feature downsampling and maintaining discriminative local contrast under background shifts, which finally results in unbalanced detection performance across different conditions. To improve detection robustness, this paper proposes a Relative Degradation Aware Network (RDANet) for infrared small target detection. RDANet consists of two dedicated modules: Multi-Scale Anti-Alias Downsampling (MSAD) and Prototype-Guided Skip Memory (PGSM). MSAD introduces multi-scale anti-alias filtering together with pixel-fold aggregation to reduce aliasing effects during resolution reduction, so that target shape information can be better preserved while irrelevant background responses are suppressed. PGSM further enhances the skip features by retrieving patch-level prototypes from a shared memory and adaptively integrating them into the current representation, which helps maintain stable local contrast cues under diverse scene backgrounds. Experiments on three public benchmarks show that RDANet achieves the best performance on most evaluation metrics, while scale- and background-stratified evaluations indicate more stable behavior across target sizes and scene complexity. The code is available at https://github.com/BIT-RuiLiu/RDANet.

</details>


### [41] [LoRC: Detecting AI-Generated Images via Low-Rank Collapse in Semantic Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20882)
*Haozhen Yan,Ruoxin Chen,Jiahui Zhan,Bo Wang,Youchang Xiao,Shouhong Ding,Liqing Zhang,Taiping Yao,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoRC是一个从几何视角检测AI生成图像（AIGI）的框架，利用现代生成器在"语义残差正交子空间"中表现出的低秩坍缩（rank degeneracy）这一架构无关签名。该方法通过解耦语义主导性来捕获生成解码瓶颈诱导的坍缩残差几何，在多个基准上平均提升7.0%准确率，并在39个未见过的生成器上达到97.0%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成器已能忠实建模宏观语义，生成的合成图像高度逼真，因此决定性的取证线索隐藏在微妙的非语义视觉差异中。现有检测方法难以捕捉这些细微差异，作者希望从几何角度重新审视AIGI检测，寻找一种跨架构通用的、稳健的取证签名。

Method: 作者识别出生成器在语义残差正交子空间中表现出低秩坍缩，即秩退化，同时保留主导语义方向。这一结构扁平化在最终解码阶段一致出现，形成跨多种生成器架构共享的瓶颈。基于此签名，提出LoRC框架，通过解耦语义主导性来捕获生成解码瓶颈诱导的坍缩残差几何。

Result: 在多个基准上平均提升7.0%的准确率，在39个未见过的生成器上达到97.0%的准确率，展示了强大的跨模型泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRC利用生成器解码瓶颈导致的低秩坍缩这一架构无关签名，实现了可靠且强泛化的AIGI检测，适用于复杂真实环境。

Abstract: Modern generators faithfully model macroscopic semantics, producing synthetic images that appear highly realistic. Consequently, decisive forensic cues reside in subtle non-semantic visual discrepancies. To reveal these cues, we revisit AIGI detection from a geometric perspective and identify an architecture-agnostic signature. Specifically, modern generators exhibit low-rank collapse (\textit{i.e.}, rank degeneracy) in the semantic-residual orthogonal subspace while largely preserving the dominant semantic direction. This structural flattening consistently emerges during the final decoding stage, forming a shared bottleneck across diverse generator architectures. Motivated by this signature, we propose \textbf{LoRC}, a framework that decouples semantic dominance to capture the collapsed residual geometry induced by the generative decoding bottleneck. Our method improves accuracy by an average of 7.0\% across multiple benchmarks and achieves 97.0\% accuracy on 39 unseen generators. These results demonstrate strong cross-model generalization and robustness, making LoRC a reliable approach for AIGI detection in complex real-world environments.

</details>


### [42] [Breaking High Confidence: Practical Face Impersonation under High-Security Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20884)
*Changjin Kim,Seunghun Paik,Dongsoo Kim,Jae Hong Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对高安全阈值设置下的人脸识别系统（FRS）的冒充攻击方法，在严格限速条件下仅需100次置信度查询即可在LFW基准上对Amazon Rekognition实现超过92%的冒充成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于人脸识别系统安全性的研究主要集中在中等安全阈值设置下，而实际中高风险场景（如银行应用、机场身份核验、执法场景）采用的高安全阈值设置尚未被充分研究，现有攻击方法在此类设置下失效。

Method: 聚焦于严格限速条件下的分数型冒充攻击（score-based impersonation attacks），提供针对攻击流水线各阶段差距的原理性数学分析，并基于此开发出能够增强冒充能力的攻击方法，即使在较高决策阈值下依然有效。

Result: 在LFW基准上，以每身份仅100次置信度查询的预算，在99置信度阈值（执法场景推荐设置）下对Amazon Rekognition的冒充成功率超过92%，且在多个开源人脸识别系统的同样严格决策阈值下表现出持续稳健的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了高安全阈值下FRS攻击研究的空白，证明了即使在高安全配置的人脸识别系统中，严格限速条件下仍可实现高成功率的冒充攻击，对该类系统的安全性设计提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Face recognition systems (FRSs) are increasingly deployed in critical real-world services for authentication, such as banking applications and airport identity checks, necessitating stringent security configurations. Consequently, the security vulnerabilities of FRSs have garnered significant attention. While existing studies have extensively explored FRS security, prior analyses have primarily focused on medium-security threshold settings, which are not directly applicable to FRSs operating under high-security constraints. In this paper, we propose the first successful impersonation attack against FRSs under high-security threshold settings. Among various threat models, we focus on a practical and challenging scenario: score-based impersonation attacks under strict rate limits. To precisely evaluate the feasibility of such attacks, we provide a principled mathematical analysis characterizing the gaps in each stage of the attack pipeline. Our method significantly enhances impersonation capabilities in score-based attacks, even under elevated decision thresholds. On the LFW benchmark, with a budget of only 100 confidence score queries per identity, our attack achieves an impersonation success rate exceeding 92\% against Amazon Rekognition at a confidence score threshold of 99-recommended setting for law enforcement scenarios. We further observe consistently robust performance across multiple open-source FRSs evaluated at similarly stringent decision thresholds.

</details>


### [43] [EviRank: Structured Relevance Evidence for Multimodal Image Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20886)
*Enjun Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Xinyu Zuo,Jinwen Luo,Ruiwen Tao,Lisheng Duan,Haijin Liang,Jin Ma,Junfu Pu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviRank将多模态图像重排序重构为语义约束满足问题，通过将任意查询（纯文本、纯图像或组合查询）解析为统一的证据包（六类语义槽，每个标注为必需、禁止或可忽略），将重排序转化为证据条件下的验证过程，结合确定性规则评分与证据锚定的列表式比较，实现无需训练的最优性能，并可蒸馏出保持90%能力的轻量级学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的图像搜索查询是多模态且组合式的，例如"找这件衬衫的粉色款"同时包含需保留的实体、需修改的属性以及需忽略的上下文。然而现有重排序器要么将多维相关性压缩为不透明的嵌入向量，要么依赖自由形式的链式推理（CoT），容易遗漏或幻觉细粒度约束。

Method: 借鉴NLP中基于量规和清单的评估方法，EviRank将任意类型的查询解析为统一的证据包：跨六种语义槽（如实体、属性、关系）的类型化标准，每个标准标记为required、forbidden或ignorable。重排序简化为证据条件下的验证流程，结合确定性量规评分与证据锚定的列表式比较，整个过程无需训练。可选地，显式证据可作为结构化监督信号用于蒸馏轻量级学生模型。

Result: 在覆盖文本到图像、图像到图像及组合图像检索的五个基准上，EviRank取得最先进（SOTA）性能；蒸馏后的学生模型以显著更低的计算成本保留教师模型90%以上的能力。

Conclusion: EviRank通过将多模态图像重排序形式化为语义约束满足问题，解决了现有方法在细粒度多约束查询上的不足，实现了透明、可验证且高效的重新排序，同时证明了显式证据既能指导无需训练的高性能推理，也能用于知识蒸馏实现效率与性能的平衡。

Abstract: Real-world image search queries are multimodal and compositional: ``find this shirt in pink'' specifies an entity to retain, an attribute to modify, and context to ignore. Yet existing re-rankers either compress such multifaceted relevance into an opaque embedding or rely on free-form chain-of-thought that easily omits or hallucinates fine-grained constraints. Drawing on rubric- and checklist-based evaluation from NLP, we recast multimodal image re-ranking as a semantic constraint satisfaction problem and propose EviRank, which parses any query - text-only, image-only, or composed - into a unified evidence package: typed criteria across six semantic slots (e.g., entities, attributes, relations), each labelled required, forbidden, or ignorable. Re-ranking then reduces to evidence-conditioned verification, combining deterministic rubric scoring and evidence-grounded listwise comparison in a single training-free procedure. The explicit evidence can further serve as structured supervision for optionally distilling a lightweight student. Across five benchmarks spanning text-to-image, image-to-image, and composed image retrieval, EviRank achieves state-of-the-art performance, and the distilled student preserves over 90% of the teacher's capability at substantially lower cost.

</details>

*Yi Zheng,Yifan Xu,Yan Zhou,Hejia Chen,Chunyu Qiang,Xiaoqiang Liu,Xiaohan Li,Shenze Huang,Yue Zhang,Guoying Zhao,Pengfei Wan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对面视听交互是人类沟通的核心，承载丰富的情感和社交信号，但现有情感多模态对话数据集存在情感标注不足、情感多样性差、规模小等问题，难以支持自然情感表达的研究。

Conclusion: EmotionDialogCN 是目前最大、最全面的视听情感对话数据集，其自然性、规模性和多模态一致性为情感计算与多模态对话研究提供了坚实的数据基础。

Abstract: Face-to-face audiovisual interaction is central to human communication, conveying rich emotional and social cues. However, existing multimodal dialogue datasets remain limited by inadequate emotion annotations, poor emotional diversity, and small scale. We introduce EmotionDialogCN, a large-scale audiovisual-emotional dataset designed to capture authentic face-to-face communication. It contains 21,880 dialogue sessions performed by 119 professional actors across 20 everyday scenarios, covering 18 emotion categories with over 400 hours of recordings, the largest and most comprehensive dataset of its kind. A novel data collection framework minimizes equipment interference, enabling natural and nuanced emotional expressions. EmotionDialogCN achieves an emotion distribution deviation of 0.64 from real human emotion statistics (versus 5.65 for prior datasets) and consistent subject framing (52-59% frame occupancy). Together, these properties translate into stable unimodal and multimodal performance across acoustic, lexical, and visual modalities, with fusion results further underscoring strong multimodal alignment and cross-modal complementarity.

</details>


### [45] [InfinityEdit: Infinite Video Editing with a Lightweight Edit-Ignition Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20910)
*Yunze Tong,Mushui Liu,Canyu Zhao,Shiyi Zhang,Didi Zhu,Peng Zhang,Wanggui He,Jinlong Liu,Ying Chen,Hao Jiang,Pipei Huang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"无限视频编辑"任务：给定前置片段和编辑指令，模型需生成延续流并应用编辑的下一个片段，且可重复处理无限序列的编辑指令。作者设计了数据收集流水线，并提出InfinityEdit，一种轻量级编辑适配器，通过三个注意力模块（历史交叉注意力、时序因果自注意力、编辑交叉注意力）使流式视频生成器获得无限编辑能力。推理时仅在有编辑请求的块中激活适配器，后续块由原始模型生成，从而在保持无限生成能力的同时完成编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的视频编辑方法大多依赖"原地编辑"假设，即将编辑后的视频与给定源片段在固定时间跨度内逐帧对齐。这种模式无法处理开放式流（如实时游戏重风格化或对持续镜头应用摄像机运动），因为编辑需要扩展到不断到达的未来帧，而非应用于静态输入片段。因此需要研究能在无限流上持续应用编辑的新范式。

Method: 1) 设计了面向无限视频编辑的数据收集流水线；2) 提出InfinityEdit轻量级编辑适配器，包含三个注意力模块：History cross-attention（用输入帧引导去噪帧）、Temporal causal self-attention（时序因果自注意力，仅允许信息从早帧流向晚帧）、Edit cross-attention（注入编辑请求）。推理时仅在编辑请求到达的块中激活适配器，后续块由原始模型配合重置锚帧生成，兼顾编辑应用与无限生成能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，InfinityEdit能够在每次编辑下忠实延续视频流，并且在无界编辑序列上保持稳定的生成质量。

Conclusion: InfinityEdit通过轻量适配器方案成功解决了无限视频编辑中"忠实延续而非逐帧重写"和"累积编辑下保持生成质量稳定"两大挑战，拓展了视频编辑从静态片段到开放流的新范式。

Abstract: With large pretrained models, existing methods have effectively improved instruction-based video editing. However, most of them rely on an in-place editing assumption. They align the edited video with the given source clip frame by frame over a fixed time span. This pattern fails for open-ended streams, e.g., restyling a live game or applying a camera move to an ongoing shot. In such cases, edits must extend to future frames as they arrive, rather than be applied to a static input clip. In this paper, we study this setting and name it infinite video editing: given a preceding segment and an edit request, a model must generate the next segment that continues the stream while applying the requested edit. This process repeats as an unbounded sequence of edit instructions arrives. This task brings two challenges: the edit must be a faithful continuation rather than a frame-wise rewrite, and generation quality must remain stable as edits accumulate. To address them, we first design a data-collection pipeline for infinite video editing. Based on the collected data, we propose InfinityEdit, a lightweight edit adapter that equips a streaming video generator with unbounded editing ability. The adapter contains three attention modules. History cross-attention guides the denoising frames using the input frames. Temporal causal self-attention keeps temporal cues flowing only from earlier frames to later ones. Edit cross-attention injects the edit request into generation. During inference, the adapter is activated only in the chunk where an edit request arrives. Subsequent chunks are generated by the original model with a reset anchor frame. This scheme applies the edit while preserving the original model's infinite generation ability. Extensive experiments show that InfinityEdit faithfully continues the stream under each edit, and stays stable over unbounded edit sequences.

</details>


### [46] [Explainable Deepfake Detection with Feature-robust Augmentation and Evidence-grounded Explanation Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20913)
*Zhu Xu,Jiaqi Tang,Pokai Chen,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释深度伪造检测框架，包含特征鲁棒增强（Feature-robust Augmentation）和基于证据的偏好优化（evidence-grounded preference optimization）两大创新，分别解决低质量图像检测精度下降和解释模型事实性错误两类问题，在ACM Multimedia 2026可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛中荣获第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释深度伪造检测不仅需要预测真伪，还需提供可解释的判定依据，对取证分析等实际场景至关重要。然而现有方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1)对图像质量退化鲁棒性差，低质量样本上检测精度骤降，而朴素的增强策略可能引发特征漂移；(2)解释模型可能遗漏操纵证据或产生幻觉性无关细节，损害可解释性的可信度。

Method: 提出两方面的创新：(1)特征鲁棒增强（Feature-robust Augmentation）——设计多样化的感知退化增强策略，并结合监督对比学习与均值教师（mean-teacher）架构，通过一致性约束稳定增强条件下的特征表示；(2)证据驱动的偏好优化（evidence-grounded preference optimization）——构建"已选-拒绝"（chosen-rejected）解释对进行学习，其中拒绝样本通过证据省略或无关信息注入的方式构造，引导模型优先关注真实的操纵痕迹。

Result: 该框架在ACM Multimedia 2026可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛中取得第一名成绩，验证了其在检测鲁棒性和解释事实性两方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过特征鲁棒增强与证据偏好优化相结合，该框架同时解决了深度伪造检测面临的低质量图像鲁棒性问题和解释可信度问题，为可解释深度伪造检测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Explainable deepfake detection extends binary classification by requiring models to not only predict authenticity but also provide interpretable justifications. This expanded scope is critical in practice, where users like forensic analysts need insight into the rationale behind the detection. Despite advancements, current approaches suffer from two critical deficiencies: (1)vulnerability to image quality degradation: detection accuracy plummets on low-quality samples, while naive augmentation strategies may induce feature drift and impair performance as diversity expands. (2) factually flawed explanations: explanation models may omit manipulation evidence or hallucinate irrelevant details, undermining interpretability. To address it, we propose a framework with two innovations. For robust deepfake detection, we introduce Feature-robust Augmentation, which comprises diversified degradation-aware augmentation strategies, and a supervised contrastive learning pattern paired with a mean-teacher architecture that stabilizes features against augmentations through consistency constraints. For explanation, we devise an evidence-grounded preference optimization process that guides model to prioritize genuine manipulation traces by learning from chosen-rejected explanation pairs, where rejected samples are constructed via evidence omission or irrelevant information injection. The proposed approach wins the first place in ACM Multimedia 2026 Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge.The code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/EDD.git.

</details>


### [47] [Semantically Compatible Knowledge Distillation for Cross-Domain Object Detection with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20916)
*Qifeng Zhang,Ting Xiang,Zeyuan Bai,Changjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLE-T框架，通过轻量级SLE Adapter注入局部纹理先验并重新格式化DINOv2特征为密集表示，解决VFM教师与学生检测器之间的空间尺度不匹配和语义不兼容问题，在三个DAOD基准上达到SOTA性能，且DINOv2-B版本仅需DINOv2-G约1/4训练时间即可获得相当或更优的伪标签质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VFM的域自适应目标检测方法存在两个关键问题：(1)教师与学生特征图之间存在空间尺度差异，导致语义不兼容，削弱特征对齐和伪标签学习；(2)域偏移导致源域训练的VFM教师容易漏检目标域目标，伪标签质量受限。

Method: 提出Semantic Localization-Enhanced Teacher (SLE-T)，一种基于语义兼容知识蒸馏的框架。核心组件是轻量级SLE Adapter，注入DINOv2：(1)将预训练的局部纹理先验注入DINOv2以增强跨域识别能力；(2)将DINOv2特征重构为与学生检测器在空间和语义上兼容的密集表示。教师知识通过伪标签学习或特征对齐两种方式迁移。

Result: 以DINOv2-B和DINOv2-L实例化SLE-T，与更大的DINOv2-G教师对比，在三个DAOD基准上取得SOTA性能。DINOv2-B版本以约DINOv2-G四分之一的训练时间和显著更少的GPU内存，产生具有竞争力甚至更优的伪标签。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实了教师-学生语义兼容性的重要性，表明SLE-T在有限计算资源下实现了高效的VFM知识迁移，为域自适应目标检测提供了轻量而有效的方案。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) offer strong generalization capabilities for domain-adaptive object detection (DAOD). However, existing VFM-based methods overlook the spatial-scale discrepancy between teacher and student feature maps, resulting in semantic incompatibility that weakens both feature alignment and pseudo-label learning. Moreover, domain shift can cause source-trained VFM teachers to miss target-domain objects, limiting the quality of their pseudo-labels. To address these issues, we propose the Semantic Localization-Enhanced Teacher (SLE-T), a semantically compatible knowledge-distillation framework built around a lightweight SLE Adapter for DINOv2. SLE Adapter injects pretrained local-texture priors into DINOv2 to improve cross-domain recognition and reformulates its features into dense representations that are spatially and semantically compatible with the student detector. SLE-T transfers the resulting teacher knowledge through either pseudo-label learning or feature alignment. We instantiate SLE-T with DINOv2-B and DINOv2-L (the ViT-B and ViT-L variants) and compare them with the larger DINOv2-G teacher. Extensive experiments on three DAOD benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, and ablation studies confirm the importance of teacher-student semantic compatibility. Notably, SLE-T with DINOv2-B produces competitive or superior pseudo-labels using approximately one-quarter of the training time of DINOv2-G and substantially less GPU memory, demonstrating efficient VFM knowledge transfer under limited computational resources.

</details>


### [48] [GAP-SAM: A Global Artifact Prior for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20929)
*Haozhen Yan,Siyuan Shan,Zijian Yu,Youqi Wang,Yan Hong,Jun Lan,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP-SAM，通过将图像与其冻结的VAE重建编码为全局伪造artifact token，注入SAM3的特征金字塔（零门控FiLM），在不指定空间区域的情况下调节稠密解码，实现AI生成图像操纵定位的OOD泛化，在六个数据集上平均Pixel-F1达79.8，超过最强基线12.6个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像操纵定位（像素级监督）的OOD性能落后于图像级检测，因为像素级监督将取证证据与数据集特有的掩码几何和语义边界纠缠在一起。现有方法（如COCO-ControlNet进行图像级分布对齐）虽改善性能，但VAE重建对齐方法迁移性差；此外存在"边界粘附"问题，即微调的语义分割模型倾向于将预测对齐到语义物体轮廓而非真实操纵边界。

Method: 1) 构建COCO-ControlNet，使用源图像的Canny边缘和深度图对齐语义与几何以改善OOD；2) 探究Mask-VAE重建对齐的局限性；3) 提出GAP-SAM：将图像与其冻结VAE重建编码为全局artifact token，通过零门控FiLM注入SAM3特征金字塔，在像素解码前调制稠密解码，不预设空间区域从而既能保留定位能力又能抑制语义边界捷径。

Result: 在六个数据集上GAP-SAM平均Pixel-F1达79.8，比最强先前方法高出12.6个点；在JPEG压缩、高斯模糊和缩放等所有测试的退化严重程度下均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 全局artifact token结合零门控FiLM注入冻结分割模型的方法，能够在不依赖空间区域先验的情况下有效分离伪造证据与语义边界，显著提升AI生成图像操纵定位的OOD鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI-generated image manipulation localization identifies edited pixels, but its OOD performance lags behind image-level detection partly because pixel supervision entangles forensic evidence with dataset-specific mask geometry and semantic boundaries. Extending image-level distribution alignment to localization, we construct COCO-ControlNet with source-image Canny edges and depth maps to align semantics and geometry, improving OOD performance across multiple localizers. Yet tighter Mask-VAE Reconstruction Alignment (Mask-VAE) underperforms COCO-ControlNet, showing that VAE reconstruction artifacts transfer poorly to local diffusion-inpainting artifacts. We also identify \emph{boundary adhesion}, where fine-tuned segmentation models snap predictions to semantic object contours rather than true manipulation boundaries. These findings motivate GAP-SAM, which encodes an image and its frozen VAE reconstruction into a global artifact token and injects it into SAM3's feature pyramid via zero-gated FiLM before pixel decoding. Without prescribing a spatial region, this token modulates dense decoding to preserve localization while suppressing semantic-boundary shortcuts. Across six datasets, GAP-SAM averages 79.8 Pixel-F1, outperforming the strongest prior method by 12.6 points. It also performs best at every tested severity of JPEG compression, Gaussian blur, and resizing.

</details>


### [49] [OccluRank: Controllable Occlusion-Aware Layout-to-Image Generation by Adding Just an Ordinal Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20932)
*Wenyang Hong,Yuan Wang,Yanbin Hao,Lanqing Xue,Ke Wang,Xiang Wang,Kuien Liu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OccluRank，一种仅需为每个包围盒添加一个序数层级即可实现遮挡感知的布局到图像生成框架，通过轻量级基于秩的条件编码和顺序感知实例交互模块，在没有额外几何输入或复杂推理的情况下引导遮挡实例间的信息交换。同时构建了合成训练数据集OccluLayout和评测基准OccluLayout-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布局到图像生成方法中，包围盒只能指定实例位置而无法表示实例间的遮挡顺序。已有方法要么依赖额外的几何条件，要么采用复杂的推理过程，要么简单聚合独立构建的实例表示而不显式建模遮挡依赖交互，缺乏简单可控的解决方案。

Method: 1) OccluRank：为每个包围盒仅添加一个序数秩（ordinal rank）编码用户指定的遮挡顺序，通过轻量级秩条件化实现；2) Order-aware Instance Interaction (OII)模块：在聚合前联合更新秩条件的实例表示，使指定顺序能引导遮挡实例间的信息交换；3) OccluLayout数据集：从已知场景几何直接推导遮挡顺序和amodal标注的合成训练数据集；4) OccluLayout-Bench：使用多个多模态大语言模型评估器评估实例存在性、空间布局、属性和遮挡顺序，辅以FID评估整体图像质量。

Result: 实验表明OccluRank在保持相当属性一致性和整体图像质量的同时，更可靠地保留目标实例、遵循指定布局并实现期望的遮挡关系。

Conclusion: 仅通过为每个包围盒添加一个序数秩即可实现有效且可控的遮挡感知布局到图像生成，无需额外几何输入或推理时优化，且基于场景几何构建的数据集和基于MLLM的评估基准为后续研究提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Layout-to-image generation enables explicit spatial control through bounding-box layouts, yet bounding boxes specify only instance locations and cannot represent their occlusion order. Existing methods may rely on additional geometric conditions, employ complex inference procedures, or aggregate independently constructed instance representations without explicitly modeling their occlusion-dependent interactions. We propose OccluRank, a simple and controllable occlusion-aware layout-to-image framework that augments each bounding box with only one ordinal rank. OccluRank encodes the user-specified occlusion order through lightweight rank-based conditioning and introduces an Order-aware Instance Interaction (OII) module to jointly update rank-conditioned instance representations before aggregation. This allows the specified order to guide information exchange among occluding instances without additional geometric inputs or specialized inference-time optimization. We further construct OccluLayout, a synthetic training dataset whose occlusion order and amodal annotations are derived directly from known scene geometry rather than estimated from partially occluded images using auxiliary prediction models. For comprehensive evaluation, we introduce OccluLayout-Bench, which uses multiple multimodal large language model evaluators to assess instance presence, spatial layout, attributes, and occlusion order, together with FID for overall image quality. Experiments show that OccluRank more reliably preserves target instances, follows specified layouts, and realizes desired occlusion relationships while maintaining comparable attribute consistency and overall image quality.

</details>


### [50] [LHMCF-Net: A Learned Hyperbolic Mean Curvature Flow Network for Medical Images Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20942)
*Shuangshuang Duan,Chunlei He,Shoujun Huang,Dexing Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LHMCF-Net，一种基于学习型双曲平均曲率流的医学图像分割模型。该模型通过二阶耗散双曲PDE引入速度场提供惯性和动量，使轮廓能绕过噪声局部极小值并贯穿低对比度或模糊区域；通过深度展开网络将PDE的迭代数值过程映射为离散演化阶段，实现端到端优化，在三个公开医学分割数据集上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Chan-Vese模型受限于一阶抛物型流的局限性（如陷入噪声引起的局部极小值、在低对比度或模糊区域传播不连贯），而深度先验擅长捕获复杂空间结构。作者希望将两者结合，利用双曲型几何演化克服一阶流的固有缺陷。

Method: 构建由二阶耗散双曲PDE控制的LHMCF模型，引入速度场使演化界面具备惯性及动量；随后设计LHMCF-Net深度展开网络，将PDE的迭代数值求解过程映射为一系列离散演化阶段，每阶段对应底层动力系统的物理可解释更新，使网络继承PDE的稳定性和几何一致性并支持端到端训练。

Result: 在三个公开医学分割数据集上的综合实验表明，LHMCF-Net在低对比度和边界不清等挑战性场景中取得了优越性能，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将双曲几何演化嵌入深度展开架构能有效提升医学图像分割的鲁棒性，验证了物理启发模型的潜力，为后续研究提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Motivated by the classical Chan-Vese model and the ability of deep priors to capture complex spatial structures, we develop a segmentation model that leverages learned hyperbolic mean curvature flow (LHMCF) as a mathematical foundation for integrating feature space data fidelity and deep structural priors within a unified high-dimensional framework. The proposed LHMCF model is governed by a second-order dissipative hyperbolic PDE, where the introduction of a velocity field provides inertia and momentum to the evolving interface. This hyperbolic mechanism enables the contour to bypass noise-induced local minima and propagate coherently through low-contrast or ambiguous regions, addressing limitations inherent to first-order parabolic flows. To solve the continuous LHMCF model, we construct a deep unfolding network, named LHMCF-Net, which maps the iterative numerical procedure of the PDE into a sequence of discrete evolution stages. Each stage corresponds to one physically interpretable update of the underlying dynamical system, allowing the network to inherit the stability and geometric consistency of the PDE while supporting end-to-end optimization. Comprehensive experiments on three publicly available medical segmentation datasets demonstrate that LHMCF-Net achieves superior performance, particularly in challenging scenarios with low contrast and unclear boundaries. These results highlight the effectiveness of embedding hyperbolic geometric evolution into deep unfolding architectures and underscore the potential of physically inspired models for robust medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [51] [SuppreSensing: Expert-Guided Feature Recalibration and Discrepancy Augmentation for Multimodal Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20944)
*Xin Wu,Zhenyu Gao,Qiankun Zhang,Shaoyong Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SuppreSensing，一种将多模态融合重构为选择性协作过程的遥感目标检测方法，通过多专家特征重校准、模态特异性属性增强和专家驱动的特征纯化模块，在DroneVehicle和VEDAI等数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态目标检测在遥感领域面临语义异质性和模态特定噪声干扰的双重挑战，现有融合方法存在对称性陷阱（对称性陷阱）以及跨模态异质性难以缓解等问题。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：1) Expert-driven Multimodal Feature Recalibration (EMFR)模块，将共享共识提取重构为输入自适应的多专家选择过程以缓解对称性陷阱；2) 模态特异性属性增强策略，通过建模双向差异模式增强特定模态特征，缓解跨模态异质性；3) Expert-driven Customized Feature Purification (ECFP)模块，基于"专业检查-综合分析-诊断更新"体检范式迭代过滤冗余并强化任务相关语义。

Result: 在DroneVehicle和VEDAI遥感数据集上取得最先进检测性能；在自然场景数据集FLIR和LLVIP上的跨域评估进一步验证了该方法在不同环境条件下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SuppreSensing通过将多模态融合形式化为选择性协作过程，有效同时建模共享信息和模态特定线索，在遥感及自然场景跨域目标检测中均展现出优异的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal object detection in remote sensing faces challenges due to semantic heterogeneity and modality-specific noise interference. To this end, we propose SuppreSensing, which reformulates multimodal fusion as a selective collaboration process that jointly models shared information and modality-specific cues. SuppreSensing first designs an Expert-driven Multimodal Feature Recalibration (EMFR) module, which reformulates shared-consensus extraction as an input-adaptive multi-expert selection process to alleviate the symmetry trap in multimodal fusion. Complementing this, a modality-specific attribute augmentation strategy is employed to enhance specific modality features by modeling bidirectional discrepancy patterns, mitigating cross-modal heterogeneity. Furthermore, we propose an Expert-driven Customized Feature Purification (ECFP) module based on a "specialized inspection-comprehensive analysis-diagnostic update" physical examination paradigm to iteratively filter redundancies and reinforce task-relevant semantics. Extensive experiments on the DroneVehicle and VEDAI datasets demonstrate that SuppreSensing achieves state-of-the-art detection performance. Cross-domain evaluations on natural scene datasets (FLIR and LLVIP) further validate its superior robustness and generalization capability across diverse environmental conditions.

</details>


### [52] [Kinematic Knowledge Maps for Pattern Alignment: Structured Latent Representational Learning in Multimodal Gait Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20969)
*Chen Dong,He Zonglin,Cheung Kenneth M. C*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScoliDetect，一种基于运动学知识图谱（KKM）和模板化运动学文本的可解释框架，用于从单目步态视频中进行青少年特发性脊柱侧弯筛查。通过双路交叉注意力与潜在瓶颈聚合实现视频、KKM和模板文本的三模态融合，在外部筛查队列上将ROC-AUC从0.961提升至0.972，并提供因子级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床AI受限于弱对齐的输入以及缺乏领域特定的可解释表示，特别是在处理密集视频流、结构化时间序列和模板化运动学文本时。现有方法难以同时实现强泛化性能和可验证的因子级解释，亟需显式编码结构拓扑的多模态融合方案。

Method: ScoliDetect框架构建于运动学知识图谱（KKM）——一种固定索引的结构化表示，编码绝对运动、自骨架配置和关节间信号相关性三个层面的步态特征。框架将视频、KKM和模板化运动学文本通过双向交叉注意力与潜在瓶颈聚合进行融合，并采用分阶段训练协议：先在架构选择后应用三模态对比预训练作为表示初始化。

Result: 在多中心队列（排除后n=1,858）上，预设的监督消融实验表明KKM中介的多模态融合优于单模态模型和晚期拼接方法。三模态对比预训练将外部ROC-AUC从0.961提升至0.972。KKM的结构化特性提供了直接映射到特定运动学相位和骨骼索引的因子级归因。

Conclusion: 将显式结构拓扑嵌入潜在空间能显著增强多模态模式分析系统的泛化能力和可解释性，为临床多模态AI提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical AI is limited by weakly aligned inputs and the absence of domain-specific interpretable representations, particularly when learning from dense video stream, structured time-series, and template-based kinematic text. Here we present ScoliDetect, an explainable framework for adolescent idiopathic scoliosis screening from monocular gait video, built around a kinematic knowledge map (KKM) and complementary template-based kinematic text derived from per-sequence pose statics. KKM is a fixed-index structured representation that encodes gait features across absolute motion, self-skeleton configuration and joint-joint signal correlation, providing anchor-referenced multimodal fusion and factor-level interpretation. We integrate video, KKM, and template-based kinematic text through bidirectional cross-attention with latent-bottleneck aggregation. In a multicenter cohort (n = 1,858 after exclusions), prespecified supervised ablations on an external screening cohort show that KKM-mediated multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal models and late concatenation. Under a staged training protocol, trimodal contrastive pretraining is applied after architecture selection as representation initialization, improving external ROC-AUC from 0.961 to 0.972. Furthermore, the structured nature of the KKM provides inherent, factor-level attributions mapped directly to specific kinematic phases and skeletal indices, offering verifiable interpretability. The results demonstrate that embedding explicit structural topologies into latent spaces significantly enhances both the generalization and explainability of multimodal pattern analysis systems.

</details>


### [53] [WA-JEPA: Rethinking the Video JEPA Paradigm for World-Action Modeling in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20974)
*Xinlin Wang,Yujiao Xiang,Yuheng Zhou,Jingqi Wang,Minqing Huang,Jiajie Huang,Dongxu Wei,Tingguang Zhou,Xiyang Wang,Gong Chen,Zhi Xu,Feiyang Tan,Hangning Zhou,Mu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WA-JEPA将V-JEPA范式从随机掩码补全和确定性回归改造为混合未来掩码预训练和条件流匹配的未来潜变量预测，并引入联合未来-动作预测器，实现自动驾驶规划中的世界-动作联合建模。在NAVSIM-v2上达到91.7 EPDMS，在HUGSIM封闭环路上取得0.4462 HD-Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V-JEPA虽然能学习强大的时空表征，但其随机掩码补全和确定性回归的设计本质上不适合自动驾驶规划，因为后者要求紧耦合动作的未来导向预测。作者希望重新思考V-JEPA范式，使其适配自动驾驶规划任务。

Method: 1) 混合未来掩码预训练：代替随机时空掩码，让模型从观测上下文推断未来潜在表征；2) 将确定性回归替换为条件流匹配，在潜在未来空间上生成合理的未来潜变量；3) 提出联合未来-动作预测器，在统一的时空潜空间中同时对未来场景token和自车轨迹进行去噪，使动作监督直接塑造规划相关的世界表征。

Result: 在nuPlan视频上预训练并在NAVSIM上微调后，WA-JEPA在NAVSIM-v2上达到91.7 EPDMS，超过最强端到端基线和世界-动作基线1.6和1.3 EPDMS；在无需HUGSIM特定微调的情况下，在封闭环路HUGSIM基准上取得最佳HD-Score 0.4462。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了V-JEPA原生世界-动作建模是自动驾驶规划的一个强大且可扩展的范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) learns powerful spatiotemporal representations from video through self-supervised latent feature prediction. However, V-JEPA is built around random-mask completion and deterministic regression, making it fundamentally ill-suited for autonomous driving planning that demands future-directed prediction tightly coupled with action. To address this, we rethink the V-JEPA paradigm and present WA-JEPA, a V-JEPA-native world-action model designed for autonomous driving planning. Instead of random spatiotemporal masking, WA-JEPA employs hybrid future-masked pre-training, where the model infers future latents from observed context. Departing from deterministic regression, we recast future prediction as conditional flow matching over latent futures, which substantially improves the model's ability to generate plausible future latents for downstream planning. Finally, a joint future-action predictor is proposed to denoise future scene tokens and ego trajectories together in a unified spatiotemporal latent space, allowing action supervision to directly shape planning-relevant world representations. Pre-trained on nuPlan videos and fine-tuned on NAVSIM, WA-JEPA reaches 91.7 EPDMS on NAVSIM-v2, surpassing the strongest end-to-end and world-action baselines by 1.6 and 1.3 EPDMS, and, without HUGSIM-specific fine-tuning, attains the best HD-Score of 0.4462 on the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark under the same evaluation protocol. These results validate V-JEPA-native world-action modeling as a powerful and scalable paradigm for autonomous driving planning. Code is available at https://github.com/AFARI-Research/WA-JEPA.

</details>


### [54] [MigrationNarrate: A Dataset for Detection of Migration Narratives in YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20984)
*Fatima Haouari,Carolina Scarton,Kalina Bontcheva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MigrationNarrate，这是首个用于检测英国移民叙事的多模态数据集，包含1,115个YouTube视频字幕，采用12个超级叙事和53个叙事标签的两级分类体系进行标注，并提供了基于预训练编码器模型和开源/闭源大语言模型的基准测试结果及错误分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙事在公共话语构建中至关重要，但移民叙事的检测研究严重不足，主要原因是缺乏专门的标注数据集。同时，公共传播已转向视频中心化平台，叙事通过多模态信号传达，而视频中的叙事检测尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了MigrationNarrate多模态数据集，收集1,115个YouTube视频字幕，使用12个移民超级叙事和53个叙事标签的两级分类体系进行人工标注；并使用预训练编码器模型（如BERT类）以及开源和闭源大语言模型进行基准实验。

Result: 提供了数据集的详细设计、收集和标注流程说明，给出了多种模型（预训练编码器、开源LLM、闭源LLM）的基准测试结果，并进行了深入的错误分析。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了移民叙事多模态检测的研究空白，其基准结果和错误分析为未来相关研究提供了重要参考和启示。

Abstract: Narratives are central to how social communication is framed, making their detection critical for understanding and analysing public discourse. Prior work has explored narrative detection and extraction across diverse domains; however, migration narratives remain significantly understudied, primarily due to the absence of dedicated annotated datasets. Furthermore, public communication has recently shifted towards video-centric platforms, where narratives are conveyed through multimodal signals and consumed at scale. Despite this shift, narratives in videos remain largely unexplored. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MigrationNarrate, the first multimodal dataset for detection of migration narratives in the UK, consisting of 1,115 YouTube video transcripts annotated using a two-level taxonomy of 12 migration super-narratives and 53 narrative labels. This paper details the dataset design, collection, and annotations; together with benchmark results using a combination of pre-trained encoder models and both open- and closed-source Large Language Models. Finally, a thorough error analysis offers insights for future work.

</details>


### [55] [Latent Ordinal Evidence, Misaligned Outputs: Inference-Time Ordinal Lens Alignment for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20999)
*Haiming Li,Yingsheng Liu,Jingmin Zhu,Siyuan Yan,Xieji Li,Jiajun Sun,Zhen Yu,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在序数回归任务（如年龄估计、图像质量评估、疾病分级）中将内部序数证据转化为有序数字token输出的可靠性问题。作者发现MLLM的隐藏状态中序数标签可线性恢复（Spearman相关性高达0.938），但原生数字token输出却很少暴露该信息（token行空间仅保留不到1.15%的序数方向）。为此提出Ordinal Lens Alignment (OLA)方法，在冻结骨干网络条件下于中间解码层训练轻量锚定透镜并将其融合为序数分布，仅在生成时校正数字token的logits，超越SOTA的LoRA微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM被广泛用于序数回归任务，但其自回归解码过程依赖数字token输出序数标签。核心问题是：MLLM内部的序数证据是否可靠地反映在其数字token输出中？作者发现隐藏状态中存在清晰的序数结构，但unembedding矩阵和数字token行空间严重过滤了该序数方向，导致原生输出与内部表征之间存在巨大差距（16-77绝对点精度差距），亟需一种无需微调就能弥合该差距的方法。

Method: OLA方法：1) 在冻结骨干网络的中间至深层解码器层训练轻量的W_S锚定透镜（lens）；2) 将多个透镜的输出融合为一个序数分布；3) 在生成阶段仅校正数字token的logits，不改动其他token输出。该方法属于推理时干预，不修改MLLM参数。

Result: 在4个序数基准和4个MLLM骨干上验证：序数标签可从隐藏状态线性恢复（Spearman相关性最高0.938），但原生数字token输出的序数方向保留率在所有16个模型-数据集组合中均低于1.15%，线性探针与原生输出间存在16-77绝对点精度差距。OLA在大多数设置中超越LoRA微调的OrderChain SOTA基线，在大多数单元中超越判别式序数基线，并在所有设置中优于离线透镜方案。

Conclusion: MLLM内部确实编码了丰富的序数信息，但其解码层（unembedding）在输出过程中大量丢失了这些信息。OLA通过推理时的轻量透镜校准，能在不微调模型的情况下有效恢复序数输出能力，表明冻结MLLM即可通过"读取"而非"改写"其内部表征来提升序数回归性能。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs apply the language model interface to visual inputs, where ordinal regression tasks such as age estimation, image quality assessment, and disease grading require autoregressive decisions over ordered class labels. We ask whether MLLMs reliably convert internal ordinal evidence into ordered digit-token outputs.
