Across four ordinal benchmarks and four MLLM backbones, ordinal labels are linearly recoverable from hidden states with Spearman correlation up to 0.938, and a task-designed prompt further sharpens this structure. Yet native digit-token outputs weakly expose it: the unembedding matrix filters the ordinal direction, and the digit-token row space retains below 1.15% across all 16 model-dataset combinations, with a 16 to 77 absolute-point accuracy gap between linear-probe and native outputs. We introduce Ordinal Lens Alignment (OLA), a frozen-backbone inference-time method that trains lightweight W_S-anchored lenses on mid-to-deep decoder layers, fuses them into an ordinal distribution, and corrects only digit-token logits at generation. OLA outperforms the SOTA LoRA-tuned OrderChain baseline in most settings while keeping the MLLM frozen, surpasses discriminative ordinal baselines in most cells, and improves over an offline lens in every setting.

</details>


### [56] [Triangulation-Free Bundle Adjustment with Graduated Non-Convexity for Camera Pose Refinement from Coarse Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21008)
*Nikolaos Kyriazis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需三角化的移动AR位姿细化方法，通过深度因子与对称交叉投影残差在每次迭代中重新表达结构，避免了将先验误差烘焙进结构中的问题。相比经典COLMAP三角化+BA细化（在15个ScanNet++场景中全部劣化，0.55°→0.74°），该方法在保持房间先验0.57°精度的同时，在330次扰动中零失败；物体尺度达到0.265°/1.80mm精度（先验0.456°），单CPU中位10秒/场景，远快于学习型精化器的2.5 GPU小时。该方法还支持渐变非凸性，能在32°扰动下恢复85%的测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动AR框架为每次拍摄附带度量位姿先验，将其转换为重建级位姿是神经渲染前必要的精化步骤。然而经典精化器（COLMAP三角化+先验种子BA）会降解已准确的ARKit先验，在15个ScanNet++场景中全部15个劣化（场景均值从0.55°退化为0.74°）。根因在于先进行三角化，将先验误差烘焙进优化器信任的结构中。作者旨在消除这一缺陷。

Method: 去除三角化步骤：每个关键点在自身反投影射线上拥有一个标量深度，每个匹配贡献两个对称的交叉投影残差，使结构在每次迭代中被重新表达。由于不提交任何结构，目标函数还支持渐变非凸性（GNC）来测量缺陷深度。该求解器在CPU上运行，无需GPU。

Result: 相同求解将房间先验保持在0.57°，330次扰动房间运行零失败；物体尺度从0.456°先验达到0.265°/1.80mm，单CPU中位10秒/场景，对比学习型精化器需2.5 GPU小时。经典精化在1-2°先验误差即崩溃，无经典方案能在32°先验误差下存续；本方法在16°/80mm下425/425全部恢复，32°/160mm下85%恢复。名义物体尺度精度与强基线（经典BA）相当而非更优，因基准处于噪声底限且经典BA作为强基线在文献中缺失。

Conclusion: 作者的方法证明移除三角化、在每次迭代重新表达结构是解决先验误差烘焙问题的关键。该方法在保持精度的同时具备极高的鲁棒性（尤其对大角度扰动），且计算成本远低于学习型方法。值得注意的是，在基准噪声底限下，该方法精度与经典BA相当而非更优，但经典BA在一场景中即使零扰动也已失败；重映射方法虽在匹配精度但丢弃先验，无法利用值得保留的先验或进行热启动。

Abstract: Mobile AR frameworks attach a metric pose prior to every casual phone capture, and turning it into reconstruction-grade poses cheaply on CPU is the step before novel-view synthesis. The least a refiner owes an accurate prior is not to make it worse. The workhorse refiner does. On 15 ScanNet++ iPhone room captures, COLMAP triangulation plus prior-seeded bundle adjustment degrades an accurate ARKit prior in all 15, 0.55 degrees to 0.74 degrees by scene-mean.
  The cause is the seeding. Structure is triangulated from the prior before anything is optimized, so the prior's error is baked into the structure the optimizer trusts. We remove the triangulation. Every keypoint owns a scalar depth along its own back-projected ray and each match contributes two symmetric cross-projection residuals, so structure is re-expressed at every iterate. The same solve holds the room prior at 0.57 degrees and never fails in 330 perturbed room runs, and at object scale reaches 0.265 degrees/1.80 mm from a prior at 0.456 degrees in a median of 10 s per scene on one CPU, against 2.5 GPU-hours for a learned refiner.
  Because no structure is committed, the objective also admits graduated non-convexity, which measures how deep the defect goes. Classical refinement collapses past 1-2 degrees of prior error, barely beyond a real ARKit prior, and no classical refinement arm survives 32 degrees. Ours recovers 425 of 425 runs through 16 degrees/80 mm and 85% at 32 degrees/160 mm, and perturbed rooms through 32 degrees.
  Nominal object-scale accuracy is on par rather than better, on a benchmark at its own noise floor, where classical bundle adjustment is a strong baseline absent from the literature. One scene fails for every solver already at zero perturbation. Re-mapping from position priors matches us in the prior's frame but discards it, so it cannot exploit a prior worth keeping or be warm-started.

</details>


### [57] [Dorsal Hand Images for Immersive (XR) and Privacy-preserving Age Assurance and Child Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21009)
*Riccardo Bovo,George Loukas,Josh P. Davis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用XR头显内建的以自我为中心的摄像头采集手背图像，作为面部识别的隐私更友好的替代方案，用于XR环境中会话内连续年龄保障。作者收集了436名覆盖成年/未成年边界的跨年龄、性别和种族的参与者的手背数据集，评估标准神经网络在18岁法定阈值上的表现，结果显示在challenge-31操作点上可实现零未成年人误入，验证了手背形态测量作为XR会话内年龄保障的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR环境需要确保内容对年龄适当，但现有年龄保障仅在注册时进行，无法在会话中实时验证当前用户年龄。基于面部的年龄识别方法在XR中不实用，因为需要摘下头显自拍，既破坏沉浸感又带来面部图片分享给第三方的隐私风险，导致XR平台缺乏持续、会话内且保护隐私的年龄保障途径。

Method: 利用XR头显固有的自我中心摄像头（egocentric cameras）采集手背图像作为面部替代模态；收集覆盖436名参与者的年龄和性别分层、种族多样化的数据集，在不受控光照和朝向条件下采集；在18岁法定年龄阈值上评估标准神经网络架构的年龄保障性能，并分析皮肤色调变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验分析确认性能对皮肤色调变化具有鲁棒性；在challenge-31操作点实现了零未成年人误入（zero minor admission），使该系统可作为年龄保障的一级筛选过滤器。

Conclusion: 手背形态测量（dorsal hand morphometrics）可作为一种有效且更保护隐私的生物特征模态，用于XR环境中的会话内年龄保障，填补了现有年龄保障方案在XR场景中的空白。

Abstract: Ensuring that Extended Reality (XR) environments are age-appropriate is an important regulatory and safety challenge. However, current age assurance operates only at registration and cannot verify the age of the active user during a session. Face-based approaches, the dominant solution in social media and adult platforms, are impractical in XR, because they require removing the headset and taking a self-captured image, often on a mobile app. This both breaks immersion and introduces the privacy risk of sharing face pictures with third parties, which leaves XR platforms without a viable path to continuous, in-session and privacy-preserving age assurance. We propose the dorsal part of the hand as an alternative to the face, by exploiting the egocentric cameras that XR headsets inherently and naturally use to capture gesture interactions. To evaluate this, we collect an age- and sex-stratified, ethnodiverse dataset of 436 participants spanning the minor--adult boundary, captured under unconstrained lighting and orientation conditions. To characterise what is achievable with off-the-shelf methods at the minor--adult boundary, we evaluate standard neural network architectures for age assurance at the legally critical 18-year threshold. Analysis confirms performance is robust to skin-tone variation. On this dataset, the challenge-31 operating point achieves zero minor admission, making the system a viable first-stage filter for age assurance. These findings position dorsal hand morphometrics as an effective and more privacy-preserving biometric modality for in-session age assurance in XR.

</details>


### [58] [Recognition-Conditioned Reasoning: A Training-Free Multimodal-LLM Pipeline for Fine-Grained Micro-Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21022)
*Fengshun Wang,Jin'ang Han,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、仅依赖提示词（prompt-only）的微动作（micro-action）细粒度理解系统，在MAC 2026微动作挑战赛的MA-Bench细粒度理解赛道中获得第一名。该系统完全基于冻结的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），针对八个子任务动态路由到经验上最适合的MLLM：判别式MLLM处理封闭式识别任务，生成式MLLM处理开放式描述与推理任务。在开放式任务上取得了统计显著的性能优势，平均得分2.68（五点量表），远优于第二名方法的1.44。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微动作（如坐立不安的手部或轻微头部倾斜）是人类无意识释放情绪与心理状态的重要信号，但现有模型不仅需要给微动作贴上类别标签，还需描述身体部位的移动并推理为何该片段属于某一细粒度类别。作者希望在不允许微调和使用真实标注的挑战赛约束下，构建一个能够同时完成识别、描述与推理任务的系统。

Method: 采用训练免费、纯提示词系统架构，完全基于冻结的MLLM构建。系统将八个子任务动态路由到经验上表现最佳的MLLM：对封闭式识别任务使用判别式MLLM，对开放式描述与推理任务使用生成式MLLM，通过动态任务路由实现模型与任务的匹配。

Result: 系统在MA-Bench细粒度理解赛道获得第一名。在开放式任务上，平均得分为2.68（五点量表），相比第二名的1.44提升显著，且该性能优势具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，在禁止微调和监督信息的严格限制下，仅通过动态路由和提示工程即可充分发挥冻结MLLM的能力，在微动作细粒度理解任务上超越其他竞争者。判别式与生成式MLLM的互补路由策略是提升开放式理解任务性能的关键。

Abstract: Micro-actions are subtle, short, low-amplitude body movements, such as a fidgeting hand or a slight head tilt, that humans perform with little conscious intent yet that reliably leak emotional and psychological state. Understanding them goes beyond assigning a label: a model must also describe which body parts move and reason, faithfully, about why a clip warrants a particular fine-grained category. We present the training-free, prompt-only system that won first place in the fine-grained understanding track (MA-Bench) of the MAC~2026 Micro-Action Challenge, where both fine-tuning and ground-truth supervision are disallowed. Built entirely upon frozen multimodal large language models (MLLMs), the system dynamically routes each of the eight sub-tasks to the MLLM empirically best suited for that task: a discriminative MLLM for closed-ended recognition tasks and a generative MLLM for open-ended description and reasoning tasks. This architecture achieves a statistically significant performance advantage on open-ended tasks, attaining an average score of 2.68 (on a five-point scale) compared to 1.44 for the second-best approach.

</details>


### [59] [COMET: Contrastive Motion-Enhanced Temporal Reasoning for Video Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21030)
*Chenghua Zhu,Zhaolu Kang,Qifan Shi,Siyan Wu,Kehan Jiang,Lei Wei,Lianyu Hu,Guangyuan Dong,Mingbo Yang,Rui Lu,Guibo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COMET框架，通过显式时间表示（泰勒帧差分运动分支）、外观-运动融合（时间注意力偏置增强的交叉注意力）和方向感知优化（时间先验蒸馏+前向-反向TC-GRPO）系统性强化视频多模态大语言模型的时间理解能力，在动作中心和时间推理任务上显著提升，同时保持静态感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频多模态大语言模型虽然进展显著，但细粒度的运动-时间理解仍然脆弱。核心瓶颈不仅是稀疏帧采样，还缺乏完整的时间建模流程：包括显式表示帧间变化、实现外观-运动交互、以及优化时间方向敏感性。

Method: COMET包含三部分：(1) 时间运动分支，基于泰勒帧差分显式编码运动证据；(2) 通过时间注意力偏置增强的交叉注意力，将运动证据注入外观流；(3) 优化阶段结合时间先验蒸馏与前向-反向TC-GRPO（将时间顺序转化为直接学习信号），强化模型对方向性运动模式的利用。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上，动作中心任务（STAR, SSv2）平均提升4.9%，时间推理任务（NExT-QA, CLEVRER, LLaVA-178K）相对BL-GRPO提升2.1%，而静态感知任务（PerceptionTest）保持持平。该增益模式同样迁移到InternVL2.5-8B，表明COMET跨模型家族具有泛化性。

Conclusion: COMET通过系统化的时间建模流水线有效增强了视频MLLM的运动-时间理解能力，在动作和时间推理任务上取得一致提升，同时不损害静态感知性能，且跨不同模型架构具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models have advanced significantly, yet fine-grained motion-temporal understanding remains fragile. The core bottleneck is not only sparse frame sampling, but also the lack of a complete temporal modeling pipeline for explicitly representing frame-to-frame change, enabling appearance-motion interaction, and optimizing temporal direction sensitivity. We propose COMET, a temporally grounded framework that systematically strengthens video MLLMs through explicit temporal representation, appearance-motion fusion, and direction-aware optimization. Architecturally, COMET introduces a temporal motion branch built on Taylor frame differences and injects its motion evidence into the appearance stream via temporal attention bias-enhanced cross-attention. For optimization, COMET combines temporal prior distillation with a forward-reverse TC-GRPO stage that turns temporal order into a direct learning signal and strengthens the model's use of directional motion patterns encoded by the temporal motion branch. The method achieves consistent overall improvements with a pronounced motion-temporal bias: on Qwen3-VL-8B, action-centric tasks (STAR, SSv2) improve by 4.9% on average, temporal reasoning tasks (NExT-QA, CLEVRER, LLaVA-178K) by 2.1% over BL-GRPO, while static perception tasks (PerceptionTest) remain on par. The same gain pattern also transfers to InternVL2.5-8B, indicating that COMET generalizes across model families.

</details>


### [60] [CoST: Semantic-Aware Urban Understanding via Spatial-Temporal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21041)
*Yutian Jiang,Jiabo Liu,Xixuan Hao,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoST是一个基于对比学习的时空框架，通过将空间上下文与多年时相语义对齐来学习跨区域泛化的地理空间表示，在八个城市指标任务上平均相对提升8.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星影像地理空间表示学习方法存在两大局限：一是依赖区域特定的辅助数据导致跨区域泛化能力差；二是忽略了多时相城市场景内的语义对齐，导致语义可解释性不足。

Method: CoST采用对比学习范式，构建空间-时间联合框架：显式建模空间相关性以捕获可迁移的地理结构特征；利用多年度城市变化语义将对齐学习到的表示与高层地理语义进行对齐，从而提取跨区域共享的地理规律。

Result: 在多种下游任务及未见过的场景中，CoST均取得了一致的优越性能，在八个城市指标设置下相比最强竞争方法平均相对提升8.7%。

Conclusion: CoST通过空间-时间语义对齐有效解决了跨区域泛化与语义可解释性问题，验证了结合空间相关性与多时相变化语义可学习到通用的、可迁移的地理空间表示。

Abstract: Geospatial representation learning from satellite imagery is a fundamental problem for large-scale urban analysis and real-world applications. Despite recent advances, current methods struggle with cross-region generalization and semantic interpretability due to their reliance on region-specific auxiliary data and the neglect of semantic alignment within multi-temporal urban imagery. Therefore, we present CoST, a novel \underline{Co}ntrastive-based \underline{S}patial-\underline{T}emporal framework that aligns spatial context with multi-temporal semantics to extract universal geographic regularities shared across regions. Specifically, CoST explicitly models spatial correlations to capture transferable geographic structures and exploits multi-year urban change semantics to align learned representations with high-level geo-semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoST consistently achieves superior performance across various downstream tasks and in unseen scenario, yielding an average relative gain of 8.7\% over the strongest competing methods across eight city-indicator settings. The code is available in \href{https://github.com/Arandinglv/CoST}{this repo}.

</details>


### [61] [CoAnchor: Robust Collaborative Perception under Spatio-Temporal Misalignment via Object-Level Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21055)
*Chi Li,Rui Lin,Aobo Ji,Dongzhu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoAnchor，一个以锚点为中心的时空对齐框架，用于异步协同感知，通过稀疏目标级时空锚点统一处理通信延迟和相对位姿噪声，在清洁场景下保持竞争力同时显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中协同感知的协同者消息同时受到通信延迟和相对位姿噪声的影响，导致过时观察、空间错位和不稳定的特征融合。现有方法通常仅从空间或时间单一维度处理这些问题，缺乏统一且高效的联合解决方案。

Method: CoAnchor构建稀疏的目标级时空锚点作为共享接口用于位姿修正，将空间细化、时间传播和当前时刻验证紧密集成在一个统一循环中，避免直接在稠密BEV特征上推理，从而保持修正过程轻量高效。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CoAnchor在清洁条件下保持竞争力，并在联合延迟和位姿扰动下显著提升鲁棒性，实现了良好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: CoAnchor通过锚点中心的统一时空对齐机制，有效解决了协同感知中延迟与位姿噪声联合干扰的问题，为异步协同感知提供了一种轻量且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative perception extends the sensing range of a single vehicle by fusing observations from nearby agents, which improves the robustness of autonomous driving. In realistic deployments, however, the received collaborator messages are often affected by both communication delay and relative-pose noise, which jointly cause stale observations, spatial misalignment, and unstable feature fusion. Existing methods usually address these issues from either the spatial or temporal side, but handling them jointly in a unified and efficient manner remains challenging. In this paper, we propose CoAnchor, an anchor-centric spatio-temporal alignment framework for asynchronous collaborative perception. Instead of directly reasoning on dense BEV features, CoAnchor builds sparse object-level spatio-temporal anchors as a shared interface for pose correction and tightly connects spatial refinement, temporal propagation, and current-time verification within one unified loop, while keeping the overall correction process lightweight. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets illustrate that CoAnchor remains competitive under clean settings and improves the robustness under joint delay and pose perturbations with a favorable practical accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [62] [Robust Validation to Geometric Perturbations for Autonomous Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21066)
*Gregoire Theau,Melanie Ducoffe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了安全关键领域自动驾驶系统中位姿估计的几何鲁棒性问题，指出现有梯度启发式方法（如APGD）在连续关键点回归任务上失败，甚至不如随机采样。作者将问题重新表述为全局Lipschitz优化（GLO），在YOLOv8-Pose + PnP求解器上验证，成功定位关键失效模式并将搜索空间剪枝超过80%。这是首个将几何鲁棒性验证扩展到连续关键点回归和深度目标检测的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域的自主系统（如基于视觉的导航与自主着陆）需要在动态操作条件（如相机旋转、光照变化）下对物理上合理的几何扰动具有保证的鲁棒性，而非抽象的像素级噪声。先前的第一阶空间攻击在分类任务上失效的发现表明，梯度启发式方法（如APGD）在更复杂的几何扰动空间中同样无法有效优化，这构成了鲁棒性验证的核心瓶颈。

Method: 本文将位姿估计鲁棒性问题重新形式化为全局Lipschitz优化（GLO）框架，利用其强大的理论收敛保证来有效定位全局最优解。评估对象为YOLOv8-Pose关键点检测器配合Perspective-n-Point（PnP）求解器，针对旋转和对比度扰动测试鲁棒性。GLO方法通过快速剪枝搜索空间来提高优化效率。

Result: 在评估中，GLO成功隔离了位置偏差超过安全运行限度的关键失效模式，同时快速将搜索空间剪枝超过80%。相比之下，标准梯度启发式方法（如APGD）表现不如简单的随机采样基线。

Conclusion: 这是首个将几何鲁棒性验证扩展到连续关键点回归和深度目标检测的研究，建立了迈向认证鲁棒自主感知的实际步骤。GLO为在复杂几何扰动空间中进行可靠的鲁棒性验证提供了原则性且具有理论保证的方法。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous systems in safety-critical domains demands guaranteed robustness against physically plausible geometric perturbations rather than abstract pixel-wise noise. In vision-based navigation and autonomous landing, machine learning components require rigorous validation under dynamic operational conditions such as camera rotations and lighting shifts. Extending findings on the failure of first-order spatial attacks in classification, we show that standard gradient-based heuristics (e.g. APGD) similarly fail on for pose estimation, often performing worse than a simple random sampling baseline.
  To overcome these optimization bottlenecks, we reformulate pose estimation robustness within the framework of Global Lipschitzian Optimization (GLO). We argue that GLO offers a principled approach to robust validation, effectively localizing global optima with strong theoretical convergence guarantees. We evaluate this framework on a YOLOv8-Pose keypoint detector with a Perspective-n-Point (PnP) solver against rotation and contrast. In our evaluations, GLO successfully isolates critical failure modes where position deviations exceed safe operational limits, while rapidly pruning the search space by over 80%. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to extend geometric robustness validation to continuous keypoint regression and deep object detection, establishing a practical step toward certifying robust autonomous perception.

</details>


### [63] [AT-ViT: Area-Targeted Multi-View Vision Transformer with Cross-Attention and Multi-Scale Patching for Plant Trait Recognition in Herbarium Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21067)
*Amani Sedrat,Takieddine Chehhat,Youcef Sklab,Hanane Ariouat,Abderrazak Sebaa,Eric Chenin,Jean-Daniel Zucker,Edi Profiti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AT-ViT，一种双分支Vision Transformer模型，通过多尺度多视角交叉注意力融合原始标本扫描图与分割图，并引入掩码引导的patch加权机制，使模型聚焦植物区域而非背景干扰，从而在多个植物性状分类任务上提升准确率、注意力定位精度和对背景扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物标本图像的性状自动识别对植物科学至关重要，但背景元素（如文字标签、安装伪影、比色卡）会导致模型产生捷径学习，即依赖非植物的虚假线索而非植物形态特征进行判断，这严重损害了模型的泛化能力和可解释性。

Method: AT-ViT采用双分支Vision Transformer架构：(1) 同时编码原始标本扫描图及其分割衍生图；(2) 通过多尺度、多视角的交叉注意力融合方案整合两条分支的信息；(3) 引入掩码引导的patch加权机制，放大植物相关区域、衰减背景驱动特征，从而引导模型关注植物器官并学习以植物为中心的表示。

Result: 在多个性状分类任务（如叶基形状、刺等）上，AT-ViT一致性地提升了准确率；相比CrossViT，植物区域注意力对齐度（Avg IoU_p）提升+15.66至+18.03个百分点，背景重叠度（Avg IoU_b）降低-27.92至-31.02个百分点；在背景扰动条件下，相比ResNet101最高提升+32.32准确率点，相比CrossViT最高提升+5.07点。

Conclusion: AT-ViT通过分割掩码引导的注意力机制有效缓解了标本图像中的背景捷径学习问题，显著提升了模型的空间注意力定位能力、分类准确率以及面对背景扰动时的鲁棒性和可解释性，为植物标本图像分析提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated plant traits recognition from herbarium images is essential for plant sciences, yet remains challenging because background elements (e.g., textual labels, mounting artifacts, and color charts) can introduce shortcut learning, leading models to rely on spurious non-plant cues rather than plant morphology. This bias degrades both generalization and interpretability. In this paper, we introduce AT-ViT, a dual-branch Vision Transformer that jointly encodes raw herbarium scans and their segmented-derived counterparts via a multi-scale, multi-view cross-attention fusion scheme. AT-ViT further incorporates a mask-guided patch weighting mechanism that amplifies plant-relevant regions and attenuates background-driven features. By learning from the original scans while being guided by segmentation masks through the mask-guided patch reweighting mechanism, the model is encouraged to focus on plant organs and learn plant-centric representations more effectively. Across multiple trait classification tasks (e.g., leaf base shape, thorns), AT-ViT delivers consistent accuracy gains, improves attention localization on plant regions, and exhibits increased robustness under synthetic background perturbations. Specifically, AT-ViT substantially improves spatial attention grounding, boosting plant-region alignment (Avg IoU_p: +15.66 to +18.03 pp) while reducing background overlap (Avg IoU_b: -27.92 to -31.02 pp) relative to CrossViT, and remains markedly more robust to background perturbations, outperforming ResNet101 by up to +32.32 accuracy points and CrossViT by up to +5.07 points under background-noise conditions.

</details>


### [64] [Gaussian-Mixture Latent Flow for Stochastic 3D Human Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21093)
*Yue Ma,Frederick W. B. Li,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜变量流模型和自适应高斯混合先验的随机人体运动预测方法，在保持高精度和多样性的同时提升了预测的物理合理性，并通过可逆模型实现自然的不确定性量化，在Human3.6M和AMASS数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机人体运动预测方法虽在精度和多样性上表现优秀，但往往忽略预测的物理合理性（如产生不现实的预测结果）以及不确定性量化，这两者对实际应用和下游任务至关重要。

Method: 提出一个隐式流模型（latent flow-based model），配备数据驱动的高斯混合先验。该先验从训练数据模式中自动学习，无需额外标注，能比传统单峰先验更有效地解耦多样化人体行为；同时模型的完全可逆性支持通过易处理的似然计算进行自然的不确定性量化。

Result: 在Human3.6M和AMASS数据集上的实验表明，该方法在预测精度和物理合理性两方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过引入数据驱动的高斯混合先验和可逆流模型，该方法同时解决了随机运动预测中的合理性和不确定性量化难题，验证了其在精度和合理性上的双重优势。

Abstract: Stochastic human motion prediction aims to forecast future motion distributions. Although recent studies have achieved strong performance in terms of accuracy and diversity, they often overlook plausibility (e.g., resulting in physically unrealistic predictions) and uncertainty quantification, both of which are essential for real-world applications and downstream tasks. To address these issues, we propose a latent flow-based model equipped with a data-driven Gaussian mixture prior that more effectively disentangles diverse human behaviors than conventional single-modal priors. This prior is derived from patterns in the training data without requiring additional annotations. Furthermore, the fully invertible nature of our model enables natural uncertainty quantification through tractable likelihood computation. Experiments on the Human3.6M and AMASS datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in both accuracy and plausibility.

</details>


### [65] [When does fusing hand-crafted knowledge with learned representations pay? A cost-normalized benchmark of stacking, substitution, and interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21098)
*Ahmad AlMughrabi,Albert Clop,Benjamin Busam,Ricardo Marques,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模受控实验（13个数据集、9个骨干网络、150到128万张图像），系统性地评测了在训练时注入固定手写Gabor先验（约2%开销）与数据驱动方法（自监督、迁移学习、增强、学习教师）的融合行为。研究发现三类结果：不同货币来源可叠加（先验对注意力骨干+VIT-B/16在224px提升+26点）；同货币来源相互替代（与有效自监督预训练组合不会超过更好的单一来源）；全强度融合入已有信息的初始化会干扰（ImageNet迁移-15到-17点）。并提出了可预测端到端增益的冻结特征分解Δ=G+readout(base)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺场景下，将先验知识与数据驱动学习融合具有吸引力，但缺乏受控的、系统的研究来明确说明这种融合何时有帮助、何时冗余、何时有害。现有工作未能在固定流程和固定子集条件下提供可比较的基准。

Method: 使用一个固定的手工知识源（仅在训练时注入的Gabor目标库，约2%额外开销），在冻结的recipe下与数据驱动替代方案（SimCLR、SimSiam、DINO、ImageNet迁移、增强、学习教师）进行对比。覆盖13个数据集、9种骨干网络、图像规模150到128万、分辨率32-224px、参数量2.5M-86M，包含大规模分类配置以及分割和检测移植实验。同时提出基于冻结特征的诊断和Δ=G+readout(base)的增益分解框架。

Result: 三类关键结果：(1)不同货币来源可叠加——先验与DeiT增强在注意力骨干上叠加，对ViT-B/16在224px提升+26点，预算翻倍时+6.7点；(2)同货币来源相互替代——与有效自监督预训练组合从未超过更好的单一来源；(3)全强度融合到已有信息的初始化会干扰——ImageNet迁移下降15-17点。冻结特征测量可事后区分这些结果但无法事前预测（规则仅能预判9个未见对中的一个）。冻结特征增益可预测端到端增益（误差在0.17点内，30个单元7个数据集），分解Δ=G+readout(base)在可测单元上符号一致率很高，可提前预测未见骨干家族的增益。

Conclusion: 先验与数据驱动学习的融合效果取决于源的"货币"（currency）类型：不同货币可叠加、同货币替代、全强度注入到已信息化的初始化会干扰。冻结特征诊断能事后解释结果但不能事前预测组合行为，但冻结特征增益本身可作为实践者在自有标签预算下预测端到端增益的可靠指标。

Abstract: Fusing prior knowledge with data-driven learning is attractive where data is scarce, yet no controlled account says when it helps, is redundant, or harms. We benchmark one fixed hand-crafted knowledge source, a pinned bank of Gabor targets injected only during training at $\sim$2\% overhead, against data-driven alternatives (SimCLR, SimSiam, DINO, ImageNet transfer, augmentation, learned teachers) under one frozen recipe with fixed subsets: 13 datasets, 9 backbones, 150 to 1.28M images, 32--224\,px, 2.5M--86M parameters ($\computeCells$ classification configurations over $\computeRuns$ runs, plus segmentation and detection transplants). Across the training-time combinations we measure, three outcomes recur (decision-level fusion differs). Different-\emph{currency} sources can stack: the prior composes with DeiT augmentation on attention backbones and is worth $+26$ points to ViT-B/16 at $224$\,px, $+6.7$ at twice that budget. Same-currency sources substitute: against effective self-supervised pretraining, the combination never usefully exceeds the better single source. Fusing at full strength into an already-informed initialization interferes in proportion to what it carries: ImageNet transfer, $-15$ to $-17$ points, removed by a weaker auxiliary weight. Frozen-feature diagnostics measured on each source alone separate these outcomes retrospectively but do not predict them: a rule built on them calls one of nine unseen pairs. At a practitioner's own label budget, the frozen-feature gain predicts the end-to-end gain to within $0.17$ points across 30 cells and seven datasets; the underlying decomposition, $Δ= G + \readout(\mathrm{base})$, holds in sign on $\auditRate\%$ of testable cells and is called an unseen backbone family's feature gain in advance. The project page is https://amughrabi.github.io/MomentAux.

</details>


### [66] [A2DINOv3: Rethinking Multi-Modal Object Detection via Socialized Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21099)
*Jiekang Feng,Zhihe Fan,Yunqi Zhu,Xinjie Yao,Yueying Zhang,Yike Gao,Ranxin Li,Guanzuo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出A2DINOv3，一种基于社交化学习的多模态目标检测框架，通过多专家协作和选择性交互机制将DINOv3视觉基础模型适配到RGB-红外多模态检测任务中，在四个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集跨模态融合策略往往不加区分地强制异构模态交互，会引入冗余信息并破坏预训练模型(DINOv3)的有价值表征能力。需要一种更精细的适配方法，既能保留各模态的专有知识，又能实现有效的互补信息交换。

Method: 提出A2DINOv3框架：(1) 将RGB和红外分支建模为异构专家，各自独立保留专有知识，通过选择性约束交互交换互补信息，以缓解有害的跨模态干扰并防止适配过程中预训练先验退化；(2) 引入社交化协作协议(SCP)和零初始化策略，逐步激活跨模态协作，实现从模态特定学习到合作表征学习的平滑过渡。

Result: 在四个多模态基准数据集上验证：包括航拍检测(GAIIC)、自动驾驶(FLIR)、低光监控(LLVIP)和多样化真实场景(M3FD)，A2DINOv3一致性地取得最先进的多模态目标检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过模拟社交化学习中的选择性协作思想，在适配视觉基础模型时实现了跨模态融合的精细化控制，既保持了预训练先验的完整性又充分利用了多模态互补信息，为多模态目标检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multi-modal object detection is essential for robust scene understanding in challenging conditions, including low-light and adverse environments. Recent vision foundation models (e.g., DINOv3) have exhibited strong representation capabilities, yet adapting them to multi-modal scenarios remains challenging. Existing dense cross-modal fusion strategies often force heterogeneous modalities to interact indiscriminately, which may introduce redundant information and disrupt the valuable pre-trained representations. To address this issue, we revisit multi-modal fusion from the perspective of socialized learning and propose adapter to DINOv3 (A2DINOv3), a multi-expert collaboration framework with a Socialized Collaboration Protocol (SCP). Specifically, RGB and infrared branches are modeled as heterogeneous experts that independently preserve their specialized knowledge while exchanging complementary information through selective and constrained interactions. This design mitigates harmful cross-modal interference and prevents degradation of pre-trained priors during adaptation. Furthermore, a zero-initialization strategy is introduced to gradually activate cross-modal collaboration, enabling a smooth transition from modality-specific learning to cooperative representation learning. Extensive experiments on four multi-modal benchmarks, including aerial detection (GAIIC), autonomous driving (FLIR), low-light surveillance (LLVIP), and diverse real-world scenarios (M3FD), demonstrate that A2DINOv3 consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in multi-modal object detection.

</details>


### [67] [CIVA: Critic-Induced Value-Subspace Attacks on Visual World-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21114)
*Jiancheng Wang,Mingli Zhu,Tong Zhang,Jiaqi Ruan,Wei Wang,Siyuan Liang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CIVA（Critic-Induced Value-Subspace Attacks），针对如DreamerV3这类基于循环潜在状态的视觉世界模型智能体，设计了一种白盒、因果、在线的攻击方法。核心洞察是批评家引导的扰动沿rollout集中在由受害者自身critic诱导的低维子空间中。CIVA通过离线SVD提取低秩值子空间，在线仅优化子空间系数并用EMA平滑后映射回像素空间，实现了高效且时序一致的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型智能体（如DreamerV3）通过循环潜在状态而非单帧观测进行决策，这削弱了逐帧观测攻击的效果，并使得在严格的逐帧扰动约束下，攻击扰动随时间剧烈变化。现有攻击方法难以有效攻击此类智能体，因此需要一种能够针对其值敏感的循环动态进行攻击的新方法。

Method: CIVA包含两个阶段：离线阶段使用critic引导的PGD对冻结的受害者模型进行探测，通过SVD提取低秩值子空间；在线测试阶段，仅优化子空间系数，使用指数移动平均（EMA）对系数进行平滑以保持时序一致性，最后映射回像素空间生成攻击扰动。

Result: 在DMC walker walk、Atari Pong和Crafter上的大量实验表明，CIVA一致性地优于五种近期方法。在DMC walker walk上实现了最大的奖励下降26.07%，同时保持较低的时序变化（TempAbs为0.646）。

Conclusion: CIVA验证了批评家诱导的扰动低维子空间假设，证明针对世界模型智能体的攻击可以通过离线降维与在线平滑系数优化的方式实现高效、时序连贯且强有效的攻击效果。

Abstract: Visual world-model agents such as DreamerV3 act through a recurrent latent state rather than a single observation, which weakens frame-wise observation attacks and makes their perturbations vary sharply over time under a strict per-frame perturbation constraint. We study white-box, causal, online attacks on such agents and propose Critic-Induced Value-Subspace Attacks (\textbf{CIVA}). Our key observation is that, along a rollout, critic-guided perturbations concentrate in a low-dimensional subspace induced by the victim's own critic. Based on this observation, CIVA first probes the frozen victim offline with critic-guided PGD and extracts a low-rank value-subspace by SVD. At test time, it optimizes only the subspace coefficients, smooths them with an exponential moving average (EMA), and maps them back to pixels. This design attacks value-sensitive recurrent dynamics while keeping the online optimization cheap and temporally coherent. Extensive experiments on DMC walker walk, Atari Pong, and Crafter show that CIVA consistently outperforms five recent methods; on DMC walker walk, it achieves the largest reward drop of 26.07\% while keeping temporal variation low, with TempAbs of 0.646.

</details>


### [68] [Llama-Mobile: Efficient 2.7-Bit Quantization of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21134)
*Luka Ribar,Jeevan Bhoot,Douglas Orr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向资源受限移动设备的视觉语言模型（VLM）量化框架，采用模型自生成训练数据的量化流程，无需访问原始训练设置，并结合一种支持Arm CPU高效执行的2.7比特/参数的新型量化格式，成功将Llama 3.2 11B Vision Instruct模型压缩至3.7GB，同时保持视觉问答任务的强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备上部署视觉语言模型（VLM）面临显著的内存和计算资源挑战，需要高效的模型压缩方法以实现资源受限硬件上的推理。

Method: 提出一个VLM量化框架，包含两部分：(1) 利用模型自身生成训练数据的量化管道，无需访问原始训练设置；(2) 一种新颖的2.7比特/参数格式，支持在Arm CPU上进行高效执行。

Result: 将Llama 3.2 11B Vision Instruct模型压缩到3.7GB（配合8比特激活），在标准视觉问答任务上保持了较强的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的量化框架能够在无需访问训练设置的情况下，有效地将大规模VLM压缩到可在移动设备上部署的规模，同时保持任务性能，为资源受限硬件上的VLM高效推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language models (VLMs) on mobile devices is challenging due to their significant memory and compute requirements. We present a framework for quantizing VLMs for efficient inference on resource-constrained hardware. Our approach combines a quantization pipeline that uses the model itself to generate training data and does not require access to the training setup, with a novel 2.7-bit-per-parameter format supporting efficient execution on Arm CPUs. We validate our approach by compressing the Llama 3.2 11B Vision Instruct model to 3.7 GB with 8-bit activations, preserving strong performance on a set of standard visual question answering tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Stream3Dv2: Geometric-Semantic Fusion Enhanced Streaming Zero-Shot 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21136)
*Jie Xu,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream3Dv2是一个无需训练的流式3D感知框架，通过嵌套的局部到历史架构、几何-语义融合机制和流形距离点云细化策略，实现鲁棒的开放词汇零样本3D分割与检测，并可与LLM智能体集成实现语言驱动的3D场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇零样本3D场景理解方法虽依赖视觉基础模型展现了潜力，但在实际部署中面临两大关键限制：(1) 无法高效处理流式RGB-D输入，计算开销高；(2) 对噪声2D分割掩码存在内在脆弱性，导致几何噪声和语义歧义。

Method: Stream3Dv2包含三大核心组件：(1) 嵌套的局部到历史（nested local-to-historical）架构，捕获多视角一致性并降低计算开销；(2) 几何-语义融合机制，利用语义引导显式解决几何噪声和语义歧义，将3D分割建模为点集合并与划分求解问题；(3) 基于流形距离的点云细化策略，通过局部流形图进行点到流形优化以缓解欧氏距离度量导致的边界划分失败，并利用几何边界框动态激活和更新历史实例以快速实现流形到流形细化。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，Stream3Dv2在基础开放词汇流式3D分割与检测任务上持续优于现有基线方法；此外，将该框架与基于LLM的智能体集成后，可实现先进的语言驱动3D场景理解。

Conclusion: Stream3Dv2通过训练免费的架构设计和多层级几何-语义协同机制，有效解决了流式3D感知中计算效率与鲁棒性的核心挑战，展现出在开放世界具身智能领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Recently, open-vocabulary zero-shot 3D scene understanding using vision foundation models has emerged as a promising alternative to data-intensive supervised methods. However, deploying these models in real-world scenarios is severely hindered by their inability to efficiently handle streaming RGB-D inputs and their inherent vulnerability to noise 2D segmentation masks. To address these critical limitations, we propose Stream3Dv2, a novel training-free framework designed for robust streaming 3D perception. Stream3Dv2 processes sequential data through an original nested local-to-historical architecture, capturing multi-view consistency while circumventing the high computational overhead so as to support timely responses. At its core, we introduce a comprehensive geometric-semantic fusion mechanism that resolves geometric noise and semantic ambiguity by explicitly utilizing semantic guidance and formulating 3D segmentation as solving point-and-set merging and partitioning problems. Furthermore, we present an innovative manifold-distance-based point cloud refinement strategy. This approach leverages local manifold graphs for point-to-manifold optimization that mitigates the boundary delineation failures caused by Euclidean-distance metrics, and employs geometric bounding boxes to dynamically activate and update historical instances for achieving rapid manifold-to-manifold refinement. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that Stream3Dv2 consistently outperforms existing baselines in foundational open-vocabulary streaming 3D segmentation and detection. Finally, we show that integrating our framework with an LLM-based agent enables advanced language-driven 3D scene understanding, underscoring its potential for open-world embodied intelligence. Code will be updated at https://github.com/SubmissionsIn/Stream3D.

</details>


### [70] [A Modular Agent for Reliable and Auditable Spatial Relation Verification in CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21140)
*Simon Vincent Abel,Heiko Hillenhagen,Michael Götz,Timo Ropinski,Ayhan Can Erdur,Daniel Santak Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化的医学影像智能体，用于轴向CT切片中的二元空间关系验证。系统通过语言解析、解剖结构定位和确定性几何验证三个显式阶段来分解任务，而非端到端直接预测。在MIRP空间问答基准上，最优混合配置达到94.1%准确率和94.2% F1，比直接使用Qwen2-VL提示高出42.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉语言模型（VLMs）在受控空间推理方面表现薄弱，难以可靠地在影像证据中锚定空间关系。由于放射学推理依赖于理解解剖结构和发现的相对位置，这种空间推理缺陷对诊断准确性构成风险，因此需要更可靠的方案。

Method: 采用模块化架构，将空间关系验证任务分解为三个显式阶段：(1) 语言解析——将自然语言查询转换为结构化关系元组；(2) 解剖结构定位——使用基于YOLO的检测器定位查询器官；(3) 确定性几何验证——基于对象中心使用确定性几何规则计算最终空间决策。

Result: 在MIRP空间QA基准上，最佳混合配置达到94.1%准确率和94.2% F1，比直接Qwen2-VL提示在准确率上提高42.5个百分点，同时保持了可解释的中间表示和可审计的推理阶段。

Conclusion: 显式的模块化空间验证可以作为未来面向报告生成的医学影像智能体的有前景的基础组件，相比端到端VLM方法提供了更高的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Reliable spatial understanding is an important prerequisite for future medical vision-language systems that aim to support radiological report generation and structured image understanding. While modern vision-language models (VLMs) show promising performance on many medical imaging tasks, recent evidence suggests they remain weak in controlled spatial reasoning and often fail to reliably ground spatial relations in image evidence. Given that radiological reasoning hinges on understanding the relative positions of anatomical structures and findings, this spatial weakness poses risks to diagnostic accuracy. We present a modular medical imaging agent for binary spatial relation verification in axial CT slices. Instead of directly predicting spatial answers end-to-end, the system decomposes the task into explicit stages: language parsing, anatomical localization, and deterministic geometric verification. Natural-language queries are converted into structured relation tuples, queried organs are localized with a YOLO-based detector, and the final spatial decision is computed from object centers using deterministic geometric rules. We evaluate the approach on the held-out MIRP spatial QA benchmark and compare it against representative end-to-end VLM baselines. The best-performing hybrid configuration reaches 94.1% accuracy and 94.2% F1, outperforming direct Qwen2-VL prompting by 42.5 percentage points in accuracy, while preserving interpretable intermediate representations and auditable reasoning stages. The results suggest that explicit modular spatial verification can serve as a promising building block for future report-oriented medical imaging agents.

</details>


### [71] [Human-JEPA: A Human-Centric Vision Model that Perceives and Anticipates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21160)
*Hui Wei,Licai Sun,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Human-JEPA是一种基于视频训练的人类中心视觉模型，通过锚定预测（anchored forecasting）和纯过去到未来分割的训练策略，在姿态估计和人重识别任务上以2.7倍更少的参数超越像素锚定专家模型，同时其预测头首次不会降低时序预测（anticipation）性能，统一服务"感知当下"和"预测未来"两个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类中心视觉模型仅在静态图像上预训练，虽然在静态密集感知上达到最优，但缺乏动作理解与未来预测能力。作者旨在训练一个能够同时感知当下和预测未来的人类中心视觉模型。

Method: 提出两种关键训练策略：(1) 锚定预测（anchored forecasting）：将密集目标锚定到初始化权重的一个冻结副本上，防止密集感知表征的静默崩溃；(2) 用纯过去到未来的时间分割替代块掩码（block masks），避免五点动作识别损失和十七点重识别性能的崩溃。模型架构采用类似JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）的编码器-预测头结构。

Result: 在冻结探针（frozen probes）评测下，Human-JEPA在姿态估计和人重识别任务上领先像素锚定专家模型，参数减少2.7倍；在高分辨率密集解析任务上略有让步；其发布的预测头是首个在时序预测任务上不降低性能的预测头。

Conclusion: 一个经过安全适配（safely adapted）的统一模型可以同时服务于人类理解的感知与预测两个层面，证明了视频预训练结合锚定预测是训练人类中心多任务视觉模型的有效范式。

Abstract: Machines that understand humans should perceive the present and anticipate the future. Existing human-centric vision model are pretrained on human images, set the state of the art in static dense perception, so motion and anticipation are out of reach. Here we present Human-JEPA, a human-centric vision model trained on video by anchored forecasting: dense targets are pinned to a frozen copy of the initialization, preventing a silent collapse of dense perception, and block masks are replaced by a pure past-to-future split, avoiding a five-point action tax and a seventeen-point re-identification collapse. Under frozen probes, Human-JEPA leads the pixel-anchored specialists on pose and person re-identification at 2.7 times fewer parameters, conceding high-resolution dense parsing, and its released predictor head is the first that does not degrade anticipation. A single safely adapted model thus serves both halves of understanding humans.

</details>


### [72] [Is Visual Prompting All You Need? Studying VLM Spatial Reasoning under Progressive Visual Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21170)
*Lars Benedikt Kaesberg,Tianyu Yang,Florian Valentin Wunderlich,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Daniel Kurzawe,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉呈现方式如何影响视觉语言模型（VLM）的表现。作者提出SPaRC基准测试中的"输入侧脚手架"方法，在保持视觉模态的前提下使空间结构更易于模型访问。实验表明，该方法可将任务准确率提升最高34.0个百分点，并可与GRPO训练互补（额外提升最高4.6个百分点），增益主要来源于减少接地（grounding）相关错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM研究关注视觉接地与下游推理之间的交互，但视觉呈现方式本身如何塑造模型表现和失败模式尚不清楚。作者希望在不改变底层推理问题的情况下，探究视觉呈现对任务表现的影响。

Method: 在SPaRC（基于网格的视觉空间规划基准）中引入轻量级输入侧脚手架（scaffolds），这些脚手架保留视觉模态但使空间结构更易获取。在多个VLM上评估脚手架效果，并结合GRPO强化学习训练进行联合测试，同时对端到端任务求解和物体检测进行错误分析。

Result: 脚手架在原始视觉设置基础上将任务准确率提升最高34.0个百分点；与GRPO训练结合时额外提升最高4.6个百分点（而原始视觉输入几乎无增益）。分析表明增益与接地相关错误的减少密切相关，而规则推理仍相对困难。

Conclusion: 视觉呈现方式是决定VLM基准测试衡量的是接地感知、下游推理还是两者混合的核心因素。这提示研究者应关注输入呈现设计对模型能力评估的影响。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly in multimodal reasoning, yet recent work shows that their failures often reflect an interaction between visual grounding and downstream reasoning. What remains less clear is how the visual presentation of a task shapes model performance and failure modes when the underlying reasoning problem is unchanged. We study this question in SPaRC, a benchmark for grid-based visual spatial planning, by introducing lightweight input-side scaffolds that preserve the visual modality while making spatial structure more accessible. Across multiple VLMs, these scaffolds improve task accuracy over the original visual setting by up to 34.0 percentage points and further complement GRPO-based training, yielding up to 4.6 additional accuracy points compared with near-zero gains on the original visual input. Analyses on both end-to-end task solving and object detection show that these gains are closely tied to reductions in grounding-related errors, while rule reasoning remains comparatively challenging. We find that visual presentation is a central factor that determines whether VLM benchmarks measure grounded perception, downstream reasoning, or a mixture of both.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Investigating Residual Hearing Loss: Quantification of Fibrosis in a Novel Cochlear OCT Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21189)
*Julia Dietlmeier,Benjamin Greenberg,Wenxuan He,Teresa Wilson,Rubing Xing,Jordan Hill,Adrienne Fettig,Madeline Otto,Teyhana Rounsavill,Lina A. J. Reiss,Jingang Yi,Noel E. O'Connor,George W. S. Burwood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将计算机视觉技术应用于耳蜗植入物引发的纤维化OCT图像分割，提出改进的2D-OCT-UNET模型，在慢性植入豚鼠的OCT数据集上实现了可靠的耳蜗纤维化负担评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合人工耳蜗（EAS）通过结合残余低频声学听力和电刺激来恢复听力，但植入物引发的耳蜗内纤维化会阻碍残余听力功能并降低EAS效果。因此，客观、高分辨率且可重复地评估纤维化负担，对于研究纤维化形成机制及改善CI患者预后至关重要。

Method: 研究团队生成了一个全新数据集，包含慢性植入豚鼠的光学相干断层扫描（OCT）图像，并进行手工标注。评估了多种最先进的语义分割模型，并提出了改进的UNET架构（2D-OCT-UNET），该模型在放大的OCT输入分辨率上运行，用于识别耳蜗纤维化及其他相关标注。

Result: 实验结果表明，改进的2D-OCT-UNET模型在耳蜗纤维化分割任务上取得了最佳性能，能够可靠地计算耳蜗纤维化负担。该方法的性能已在实验部分得到验证。

Conclusion: 这是首次成功将计算机视觉技术应用于植入耳蜗的纤维化OCT数据集。基于深度学习的纤维化负担计算方法具有临床转化潜力，有望减少纤维化负担并改善人工耳蜗患者的预后。数据集和项目代码已公开在GitHub上。

Abstract: Objective: Cochlear implants (CIs) are bionic prostheses that restores hearing via electrical stimulation of the auditory nerve. Hybrid CIs, which use electroacoustic stimulation (EAS), combine residual low-frequency acoustic hearing with CI electrical stimulation. Intracochlear fibrosis, which forms in response to the presence of the implant, may impede residual hearing function and gradually reduce the efficacy of EAS. It is therefore a translational objective to study the formation of cochlear fibrosis in rodents, with the goal of reducing fibrotic burden and improving outcomes for CI patients. Methods: We generate and annotate a novel dataset of optical coherence tomography (OCT) images from chronically implanted guinea pigs as part of an ongoing study focused on implant induced fibrosis. Objectively assessing fibrotic burden in this model, with high resolution and repeatability, presents an obvious use case for computer vision methods. Results: We present the results of several state-of-the-art semantic segmentation models and compare their efficacy for identifying cochlear fibrosis and other relevant annotations, using a new library of manually segmented OCT images. Conclusions: We find that the best performance is achieved by using a modified version of the well-known UNET architecture (which we term 2D-OCT-UNET) that operates on the upscaled OCT input resolution. Significance: For the first time, we have successfully applied computer vision techniques to an OCT dataset of implanted cochleae with fibrosis. Using this deep learning model, the cochlear fibrotic burden calculation can be reliably carried out as we verify in our experimental section. The dataset and the project code are available at: https://github.com/juliadietlmeier/CF-OCT-segmentation

</details>


### [74] [ES-VP : Energy-Shaped Dynamic Visual Prompting for Efficient Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21194)
*Can Jin,Ying Li,Jingchen Sun,Hongwu Peng,Jiahui Zhao,Yang Zhou,Lei Li,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ES-VP（Energy-Shaped Visual Prompting），一种通过低秩初始化和能量引导动态自适应生成图像特定提示的方法，在保持参数高效性的同时显著提升视觉提示性能。在5种架构、15个数据集上的实验显示，ES-VP相较现有SOTA方法（如DAM-VP）平均准确率提升2.6%，同时使用590倍更少的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉提示方法面临灵活性与高效性之间的权衡：固定提示忽略图像个体特征，而辅助网络生成多样化提示虽提升性能却显著增加参数使用并可能导致过拟合，同时辅助网络与预训练模型固有的偏差限制了可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: ES-VP通过两个关键机制生成图像特定提示：1）低秩初始化（low-rank initialization）减少参数需求；2）能量引导动态自适应（energy-guided dynamic adaptation）利用预训练模型自身直接生成自适应提示，无需额外辅助网络，从而保持参数效率并改善泛化。

Result: 在5种架构、15个数据集上广泛实验，ES-VP一致优于现有SOTA的单一和多样化VP方法。以CLIP架构为例，在4个数据集上相比SOTA方法DAM-VP平均准确率提升2.6%，同时VP参数使用量减少590倍。

Conclusion: ES-VP建立了一种高效且可泛化的模型适应新基准，通过利用预训练模型本身进行自适应提示生成，成功解决了视觉提示中灵活性与参数效率之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Visual prompting (VP) has emerged as a parameter-efficient method for adapting pre-trained models to downstream tasks. However, existing approaches encounter a trade-off between flexibility and efficiency. Some methods apply a fixed prompt to all images, ignoring individual image characteristics, while others introduce auxiliary networks to generate diverse prompts. Although the latter can improve performance, it also significantly increases parameter usage and the potential for overfitting to specific datasets. Furthermore, the auxiliary networks, combined with inherent biases in pre-trained models, limit scalability and generalization. In this paper, we propose Energy-Shaped Visual Prompting (ES-VP), a novel approach that generates image-specific prompts using low-rank initialization and energy-guided dynamic adaptation, achieving superior performance with fewer parameters compared to single-prompt methods. ES-VP directly utilizes the pre-trained model for adaptive prompt generation, ensuring both parameter efficiency and improved generalization. Extensive experiments conducted on five architectures across fifteen datasets demonstrate that ES-VP consistently outperforms current state-of-the-art (SOTA) single and diverse VP methods. For instance, using the CLIP architecture across four datasets, ES-VP outperforms the SOTA method DAM-VP by an average of 2.6\% in accuracy while utilizing 590$\times$ fewer VP parameters, thereby establishing a new benchmark for efficient and generalizable model adaptation.

</details>


### [75] [Anchoring Instruction Outside Mask: Exact Reference Caching for Efficient In-Context Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21229)
*Yangshuai Liu,Zheming Li,Jiaao Li,Kang He,Ziliang Lai,Zhitai Liu,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超越掩码（beyond-mask）的设计，通过静态文本锚点连接指令与参考分支，在扩散Transformer中实现多参考图像编辑时精确的K/V复用，无需增加参数即可加速推理，并通过teacher-forced velocity蒸馏和策略内蒸馏恢复生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文条件生成中，扩散Transformer需要同时处理文本指令和视觉参考，但每个参考图像引入数千token导致计算量随参考数量急剧增长。现有的结构化稀疏注意力虽能复用K/V并加速计算，却阻止了视觉参考关注文本指令，严重损害了多参考编辑中的指令遵循和参考保真度。

Method: 1) 采用超越掩码的token序列和注意力掩码联合重设计，使用静态文本锚点将指令连接到参考分支，保持精确的K/V复用且不增加参数；2) 通过teacher-forced velocity蒸馏恢复因架构转换而损失的生成质量；3) 随后进行短期的策略内蒸馏阶段，由教师模型监督学生访问的状态，这是扩散模型中首次将策略内蒸馏用于架构恢复。

Result: 在三个图像编辑基准上，方法达到与全注意力相当的生成质量。五张参考图像时，40步完整去噪过程加速3.92倍，静态文本锚点引入的开销可忽略；扩展实验中十张参考时加速达5.47倍。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了结构化稀疏注意力的计算效率与参考-指令注意力质量之间的冲突，在不牺牲生成质量的前提下显著加速多参考图像编辑过程，并为扩散模型中的架构恢复提供了新的策略内蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Omnimodal generation is central to a wide range of content creation and editing applications. In-context conditioning is essential to this paradigm. It allows diffusion transformers to process text instructions and visual references in a shared attention sequence. However, each reference image introduces thousands of tokens. Computation therefore grows rapidly with the number of references. Existing methods reduce computation through structured sparse attention, which limits interactions between reference and target tokens. This structure also makes the reference K and V independent of the denoising target, allowing them to be computed once and reused across steps. However, it blocks visual references from attending to the text instruction. This substantially degrades instruction following and reference fidelity in multi-reference editing. To resolve this conflict, we jointly redesign the token sequence and attention mask. Our beyond-mask design uses static text anchors to connect the instruction to the reference branch. It preserves exact K and V reuse without adding parameters. However, this direct architectural conversion degrades generation quality. We recover the lost performance through teacher-forced velocity distillation, followed by a short on-policy stage in which the teacher supervises student-visited states. To our knowledge, this is the first use of on-policy distillation for architectural recovery in diffusion models. Across three image-editing benchmarks, our method matches full-attention generation quality. With five reference images, it accelerates the complete 40-step denoising process by 3.92x, while static text anchors introduce negligible runtime overhead; the speedup reaches 5.47x at ten references in our scaling study.

</details>


### [76] [A VLM Answer Is Not an Anomaly Score: Rank Compression in Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21244)
*Inpyo Song,Jangwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示并系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）在无训练视频异常检测（VAD）中的"答案接口"问题——即答案尺度和读出规则的选择。实验表明，概率读出（利用完整输出分布）在四个7-8B VLM、所有答案尺度、基准和指标组合上均优于生成读出（仅用最大概率答案），平均提升5-13个点。作者将生成读出的缺陷命名为"生成答案秩压缩"（generated-answer rank compression），并论证答案接口是VLM-based VAD检测器的重要组成部分而非格式化细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测基准要求对每个片段输出标量异常分数并评估排序质量（AUROC/AP），但VLM-based检测器的答案接口（答案尺度与读出规则）可以改变最终排名，却被当作格式化细节而忽视。作者旨在系统研究这一接口的影响，因为不同接口组合可能产生截然不同的检测性能。

Method: 作者定义了VLM-based VAD检测器的答案接口，包含两个组成部分：(1) 答案尺度——定义允许的回答集合（例如二元答案、91个答案等）；(2) 读出规则——将模型输出分布映射为标量分数。生成读出仅使用最可能的答案，而概率读出利用完整输出分布。实验在四个7-8B VLM、多种答案尺度、解码策略、提示措辞和联合评分-解释提示下进行系统比较。

Result: 概率读出在所有测试的答案尺度、基准和指标组合上均优于生成读出，四组基准-指标组合的平均增益为5-13个点。即使答案尺度允许91个答案，生成读出也只产生4-18个不同分数（秩压缩严重），而概率读出保留更细腻的分数分辨率。该优势在每种解码策略、提示措辞和联合评分-解释提示下均持续存在。

Conclusion: 答案接口是VLM-based VAD检测器中具有实质影响的组件而非格式化细节，应当在论文中明确指定和评估。生成读出因"生成答案秩压缩"丢失了不同答案分布片段的相对顺序，而概率读出通过利用完整输出分布避免了这一问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models enable training-free video anomaly detection by answering questions about video segments. VAD benchmarks, however, require a scalar anomaly score for each segment and evaluate the resulting ranking using the AUROC or AP. A VLM-based detector should therefore define an answer interface: the answer scale specifies the admissible answers, and the readout rule maps the model's output distribution to a score. Because this interface can change the evaluated ranking, it is part of the detector rather than a formatting detail. The generated readout uses only the most likely answer, whereas the probability readout uses the full distribution over admissible answers. Across four 7-8B VLMs, the probability readout outperforms the generated readout for every tested combination of answer scale, benchmark, and metric, with average gains ranging from 5 to 13 points across the four benchmark-metric pairs. The gap arises because the generated readout keeps only one answer value per segment, so segment with different answer distributions can receive the same score and lose their relative order. We call this loss of relative order generated-answer rank compression. Even when the answer scale allows 91 answers, the generated readout produces only 4-18 distinct scores, whereas the probability readout retains substantially finer score resolution. The advantage persists under every decoding strategy, prompt wording, and joint scoring-explanation prompt we test. The answer interface is therefore a consequential component of VLM-based VAD and should be explicitly specified and evaluated.

</details>


### [77] [On the Transferability of Agricultural Weed Detection Under Cross-Field Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21254)
*Nikhilesh Prabhakar,Pranuthi Tenali,Wilfredo Abudeye Fernandez,Shekhar Borah,Athresh Karanam,Erik Blasch,Prabha Sundaravadivel,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨数据集/跨作物的UAV杂草检测泛化问题，引入棉花田杂草检测新数据集，并与大豆数据集进行跨作物迁移实验。结果发现仅用25个目标域标注样本进行少样本微调，比无监督域适应目标检测（DAOD）效果更好，表明源域选择加适量目标域监督比复杂的自适应算法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有杂草检测方法仅在单一作物和单一田块内评估，缺乏跨数据集、跨作物泛化能力的证据。实际应用中，模型在一种作物上训练后能否迁移到新田块或新作物尚无深入研究，导致每次部署新区域都需要重新标注数据，成本高昂。

Method: 1) 收集并标注了一个新的棉花田UAV杂草检测数据集，与已有的、在相似协议下采集的大豆数据集配对使用；2) 进行跨作物（棉花↔大豆）的检测器迁移实验；3) 对比无监督域适应目标检测（DAOD）与"域邻近源数据集预训练+目标域少样本微调"两种策略；4) 目标域标注预算从零到全量，系统刻画适应策略与标注成本之间的权衡。

Result: 在跨作物对比实验中，仅使用25个标注目标样本的少样本微调方法优于无监督DAOD方法，说明结合源域选择与少量目标域监督标注能够更有效地恢复跨作物检测性能。

Conclusion: 源域选择与适度目标域监督（如25个标注样本）比复杂的无监督域适应算法更高产，为实际杂草检测部署提供了降低标注成本的实用指导。

Abstract: Accurate agricultural weed detection in real-world field conditions is essential for precision agriculture, enabling targeted intervention and reducing yield loss. Recent work has reported strong detection performance from UAV-based imagery across a range of crops, yet existing approaches evaluate within a single crop and field, leaving practitioners with little evidence that a model trained on one crop will generalize to a new field or crop type. In this work, we characterize where cross-dataset weed-localization performance degrades and which modeling choices recover it, reducing the need to relabel every new deployment field. We introduce a newly collected and annotated UAV image dataset for agricultural weed detection in cotton fields and use it alongside an existing soybean dataset collected under a similar protocol. Using these datasets, we evaluate the performance of several strategies for transferring a detector trained on one crop to another, comparing unsupervised domain adaptive object detection (DAOD) against pretraining on a domain-adjacent source dataset followed by few-shot fine-tuning on the target dataset. Our analysis spans target-domain label budgets from zero to the full target dataset, characterizing the trade-off between adaptation strategy and annotation effort. We find that few-shot fine-tuning with as few as 25 labeled target examples outperforms unsupervised DAOD in our cross-crop comparison, suggesting that source domain selection combined with modest target supervision is more productive than algorithmic sophistication in adaptation.

</details>


### [78] [WildFin: An In-the-Wild Dataset for Fish Behavioral Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21281)
*Abigail G. Grassick,Jerome Tze-Hou Hsu,Ethan Lin,Ziang Liu,Max Whitton,Madelyn Hair,Liam Gutierrez,Haozheng Yu,Kristin Branson,Vivek Jayaraman,Michael A. Gil,Andrew M. Hein,Jennifer J. Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍WildFin，一个由生态学家构建的鱼类行为识别基准数据集，包含固定相机监测鱼群和动态潜水员跟踪个体两种真实场景，基于1,350小时野外工作和600小时专家标注生成9小时行为数据及超过200万帧标签。通过基准测试现代视觉基础模型，揭示了当前模型能力与现实水下行为分析需求之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外视频数据大量涌入生态学，但专家标注成本高昂成为利用瓶颈。虽然计算机视觉有潜力解决该问题，但现有模型在复杂海洋环境中频繁失效，缺乏专门的数据集来刻画和评估这些失败模式。

Method: 引入WildFin基准数据集，覆盖两种现实范式（固定相机监测鱼群、动态潜水员跟踪个体）；基于1,350小时野外工作和600小时专家标注，产出9小时行为数据及超过200万帧标签；对现代视觉基础模型进行基准测试，比较静态与时空架构的性能权衡。

Result: 基准测试量化了当前现代视觉基础模型在鱼类行为识别上的表现，揭示了静态与时空架构之间的性能权衡，并显示出当前模型能力与现实水下行为分析需求之间仍存在实质性差距。

Conclusion: WildFin为鱼类行为识别提供了首个由生态学家专门标注的大规模基准，系统地暴露了现有视觉模型的不足，为未来水下行为分析方法的改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in field technology have led to a massive influx of in-the-wild video data for ecological science. The primary bottleneck in leveraging this data is the high cost of expert annotation. While computer vision offers a potential solution, current models frequently fail when deployed in complex marine environments. To characterize these failures, we introduce WildFin, a novel benchmark for fish behavior recognition collected and annotated by ecologists.WildFin spans two critical real-world paradigms: stationary cameras monitoring groups of fish and dynamic divers following individual subjects. The dataset represents a massive curation effort, involving 1,350 hours of fieldwork and 600 hours of expert annotation to produce 9 hours of behavioral data with over 2 million frame-by-frame labels. We benchmark modern vision foundation models and quantify tradeoffs between static and spatiotemporal architectures, revealing the substantial gap that remains between current model capabilities and the demands of real-world underwater behavioral analysis. Project website: https://team-wildfin.github.io/.

</details>


### [79] [Difficulty-Calibrated Interpolation Paths for Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21286)
*Airin Akter Tania,Md Raihan Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出难度校准流匹配（Difficulty-Calibrated Flow Matching, DCFM），通过短暂的线性路径试点运行记录逐时刻损失，并将插值时间表设置为该难度分布的 quantile 函数，使轨迹在难以学习的区域停留更久。该方法仅引入一个超参数，不改变训练目标和梯度等价性，兼容无分类器引导，仅增加约2%训练开销。在CIFAR-10、MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上使用相同紧凑U-Net的对照实验中，校准路径在全采样预算下于CIFAR-10取得最佳FID，并在大批量、少更新的高计算瓶颈场景下显著优于所有固定时间表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件流匹配中，插值时间表预先固定，与数据和模型均无关，但该时间表显著影响收敛速度和生成质量。作者观察到沿路径的回归难度呈现系统性变化，因此希望从模型自身出发推导更优的时间表。

Method: 先用线性插值路径进行短时间试点运行，记录每个时刻的损失（作为难度分布），然后将时间表设定为该难度分布的 quantile 函数，使模型在速度最难学习的区域投入更多训练时间。方法仅有一个超参数、保持训练目标不变、与梯度等价性一致，并兼容无分类器引导。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上采用相同的紧凑U-Net模型进行对照实验：校准路径在CIFAR-10全采样预算下取得最佳FID；在大量批量、少更新次数的训练设定下（即计算资源最受限的场景），明显优于所有固定时间表方案；训练开销仅增加约2%。

Conclusion: 从数据-模型联合视角自适应地确定插值时间表是可行且有效的，尤其是在计算资源稀缺的场景下，DCFM提供了显著优于固定时间表的生成质量，且几乎不增加额外开销。

Abstract: Conditional Flow Matching trains generative models by regressing a network onto the velocity of a prescribed noise-to-data interpolation path. The interpolation schedule that shapes this path is known to affect convergence and sample quality, yet it is invariably fixed in advance, independent of both the data and the model. We show that the regression difficulty of Conditional Flow Matching varies systematically along the path, and we propose Difficulty-Calibrated Flow Matching, which derives the schedule from the model itself: a short pilot run with the linear path records the per-time loss, and the schedule is set to the quantile function of this difficulty profile, so the trajectory lingers where the velocity is hardest to learn. The method has a single hyperparameter, leaves the training objective and its gradient equivalence intact, composes with classifier-free guidance, and adds about two percent training overhead. In controlled experiments on CIFAR-10, MNIST, and Fashion-MNIST with an identical compact U-Net, the calibrated path attains the best FID on CIFAR-10 at full sampling budget and clearly outperforms all fixed schedules in the large-batch, few-update regime, precisely the setting where compute is scarcest.

</details>


### [80] [When Adaptation Hurts: Connecting Representational Drift to OOD Failures in MedSAM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21300)
*Marko Haralović,Sounic Akkaraju,Carlo Baretta,Vasil Zapryanov,Alexia Briassouli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了MedSAM在医学图像分割中的迁移泛化能力，比较六种适配策略（全模型和编码器LoRA、浅层和深层VPT、仅解码器和全量微调）在干净和含噪提示下的内外分布性能。发现微调在内外分布上最佳，编码器LoRA是最佳参数高效替代方案，并通过CKA揭示远域退化与解码器表征漂移强相关，提示抖动可增强模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础医学图像分割模型（如MedSAM）依赖提示质量和模型适配效果，但现有研究缺乏对其在不同域间泛化与适配策略的系统性比较，尤其缺乏对分布外（OOD）稳健性和表征变化机制的深入分析。

Method: 在ISIC 2018上训练六个适配变体（全模型LoRA、编码器LoRA、浅层VPT、深层VPT、解码器微调、全量微调），在近OOD（PH2）和远OOD（BUSI、CBIS-DDSM）数据集上于干净和不同程度噪声提示下评估；使用Centered Kernel Alignment（CKA）分析编码器与解码器表征相似性以解释鲁棒性差异。

Result: 适配提升内分布和近OOD表现但常损害远OOD性能；全量微调取得最佳权衡，编码器LoRA为最强参数高效方案，优于标准LoRA和VPT；CKA显示远OOD退化与解码器表征漂移强相关；随机0-100像素提示抖动产生更稳健且性能更优的模型。

Conclusion: 稳健的MedSAM适配需综合考虑提示噪声暴露、域偏移和表征保持三者；编码器LoRA通过适配编码器视觉特征变化同时保留解码器通路，获得比标准LoRA更强的远OOD鲁棒性。

Abstract: Foundation models for medical image segmentation, like prompt-based MedSAM, generalize well across domains and modalities, often in zero or few-shot setups. However, their performance depends on the quality of prompts and the adaptation of the models to custom datasets. This work systematically examines how MedSAM generalizes across diverse medical imaging benchmarks, with six adaptation strategies: full-model and encoder-only LoRA, shallow and deep visual prompt tuning (VPT), and decoder-only and full fine-tuning. Models are trained on the International Skin Imaging Collaboration Challenge (ISIC 2018) dataset and evaluated under clean and increasingly noisy prompts on IN and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) datasets: close-OOD PH2 (dermoscopy), far-OOD BUSI (Breast Ultrasound Images Dataset) and CBIS-DDSM (Curated Breast Imaging Subset of the Digital Database for Screening Mammography). We show that adaptation improves performance on IN and close-OOD data but often reduces performance on far-OOD data. Full fine-tuning provides the best tradeoff, while encoder-only LoRA is the strongest parameter-efficient alternative, outperforming standard LoRA and VPT under far-OOD shifts. Using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), we show that far-OOD degradation is strongly associated with drift in decoder representations, whereas encoder similarity alone does not explain robustness. This suggests encoder-only LoRA provides stronger robustness than standard LoRA by adapting the encoder to distribution shift in visual features, while preserving the decoder pathway. We further show that random 0-100 pixel jitter on prompts produces more robust and better performing models. We thus conclude that robust MedSAM adaptation requires the combined consideration of prompt noise exposure, domain shift, and representation preservation. We release our code: https://github.com/ImSounic/medsam-vpt

</details>


### [81] [Re$^3$Cap: Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning Enhancement via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21305)
*Haonan Jia,Shichao Dong,Zenghui Sun,Jiawen Zheng,Ziqi Miao,Gege Shi,Qiuyu Zhao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Re³Cap，一种基于多模态检索引导的推理策略，用于增强大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的图像描述生成能力。通过引入Caption Refinement Suggester（CRS）和Caption Quality Assessor（CQA）两个模块，识别并修正图像描述中的幻觉和遗漏，从而无需额外标注即可提升描述质量，在COCO-LN500基准上相比GRPO在关系推理任务上平均提升8.64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）在图像描述生成中虽有显著收益，但在鼓励LVLMs探索新颖推理策略方面存在局限，导致RL与监督微调（SFT）之间存在性能差距。作者认为多模态检索可以作为一种有效的推理信号用于描述精炼。

Method: 提出Re³Cap框架，包含两个核心组件：Caption Refinement Suggester（CRS）用于识别描述中的幻觉和遗漏并给出精炼建议；Caption Quality Assessor（CQA）用于评估候选描述的质量。通过检索引导的推理策略，在不引入额外标注的情况下迭代优化图像描述。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在图像描述任务上表现优异，甚至超过监督微调（SFT）。在COCO-LN500基准上，Re³Cap相比GRPO在关系推理任务上平均提升8.64%。

Conclusion: 多模态检索可以作为有效的推理信号引导图像描述精炼，弥补RL与SFT之间的性能差距，且无需额外标注，为LVLMs在图像描述任务中的推理能力增强提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated significant gains in image captioning, yet it is still limited in encouraging Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) to explore novel reasoning strategies. This limitation leads to a performance gap between RL and Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT). In this paper, we argue that multi-modal retrieval can serve as an effective reasoning signal for caption refinement. Based on this insight, we present the Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning (Re$^3$Cap), a retrieval-guided reasoning strategy that enhances image captioning without requiring additional annotations. Instantiated by Caption Refinement Suggester (CRS) and Caption Quality Assessor (CQA), this strategy identifies hallucinations and omissions in image captions, leading to more accurate and detailed descriptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method in image captioning, even compared with Supervised Fine-Tuning. Especially, Re$^3$Cap outperforms GRPO with an average improvement of 8.64% in relation reasoning on the COCO-LN500 benchmark.

</details>


### [82] [OmniAssistBench: Assistant-style Interaction Benchmark for Omni-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21360)
*Xianyun Sun,Chaoyou Fu,Zhengye Zhang,Feiyang Duan,Qingyuan Cao,Yonghui Niu,Sihang Yuan,Ge Zhang,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniAssistBench，一个用于评估实时视频辅助场景下全模态大语言模型（Omni-LLMs）的基准数据集。该基准通过逆向工程现有互联网视频构建多轮交互场景，要求模型沿预定义路线引导用户完成任务。实验发现当前最优模型Gemini-3-Pro仅得66.4分（满分100），开源模型Qwen3-Omni-Instruct得51.2分，模型在视觉提示理解、多轮上下文保持和响应时机把控等方面仍有显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的被动视频理解评估基于静态离线数据集，无法适应交互式视频助手这一新兴范式——因为模型的不可预测响应会动态改变用户的后续行为。评估此类主动引导型助手的挑战在于，相同的用户目标可通过多种路径实现，导致交互路径发散，难以客观比较模型表现。

Method: 作者提出OmniAssistBench：(1) 从源视频中提取预定义先验，约束模型引导用户沿完全相同的路线完成目标，解决交互路径发散问题；(2) 通过逆向工程现有互联网视频构建数据集——从视频逆向推导逻辑用户目标，并将视频切分为多轮片段以模拟连续交互；(3) 整个数据集构建流程耗时超过1000专家小时。

Result: 专有模型Gemini-3-Pro达到66.4分（满分100），开源模型Qwen3-Omni-Instruct达到51.2分。模型普遍能理解用户输入，但经常给出错误或不完整的回答，尤其在视觉提示（如手势）理解、多轮交互中历史上下文保持、以及延迟响应直到目标事件出现等方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前全模态大语言模型在作为实时视频交互助手方面仍有显著的提升空间，距离成为可靠的智能助手尚有不小差距。OmniAssistBench为这一新兴评估方向提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Recent omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) show great potential as real-time video assistants, which continuously perceive environments and guide users to achieve specific goals. Unlike traditional passive video understanding, interactive assistants should actively combine visual states, user goals, and prior knowledge to provide effective help. Evaluating this is rather challenging, as the model's unpredictable response dynamically changes the user's subsequent actions, which static offline datasets cannot accommodate. To address this bottleneck, we introduce OmniAssistBench. To solve the issue of diverging interaction paths where the same user goal can be achieved through various methods, we provide models with predefined priors derived from the source video, requiring them to guide users along the exact same routes. Since real interaction videos are rare, we construct the dataset by reverse-engineering existing Internet videos. We deduce logical user goals and segment the videos into multi-turn clips to simulate continuous interactions. This rigorous pipeline required over 1000 expert person-hours to build the dataset. Results show that the proprietary Gemini-3-Pro reaches 66.4 out of the max point of 100, while the open-source Qwen3-Omni-Instruct achieves 51.2. Although current models generally understand user inputs, they frequently provide incorrect or incomplete answers. Specifically, they struggle with visual prompts (e.g., hand gestures), fail to maintain historical context during multi-turn interactions, and fail to delay response until the target event. Results indicate substantial room for improvement before models can become reliable assistants.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Beyond Raw Transcripts: Structured Persona Extraction for LLM-Based Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20344)
*Iris Ye,Tianze Deng,Ozan Candogan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM数字孪生中人物信息的组织结构问题，发现限制预测准确率的关键瓶颈不是信息量，而是信息如何结构化。作者提出手工设计的BDE模式（背景、决策过程、评估）在同类基准上优于原始转录，但固定结构无法泛化到异质任务；通过提出一个LLM自动发现任务特定人物结构的pipeline，在13个异构子研究基准上恢复了性能，平均准确率比原始转录基线提升1.91个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM数字孪生旨在模拟个体在新环境或新问题下的行为。现有方法从调查转录或摘要中构建人物表征，前人研究表明压缩长转录为短摘要不会显著降低预测准确率，暗示信息量不是主要瓶颈。作者认为真正的限制在于人物信息在提供给模拟模型之前的结构组织方式，因此需要系统研究结构化表示相对于非结构化摘要的影响。

Method: 1) 引入手工设计的BDE schema（Background背景、Decision procedure决策过程、Evaluation评估），基于消费者行为理论，与原始转录和未结构化摘要进行对比；2) 在Twin-2K-500同质基准上进行实验，并以gpt-5.4-mini和Qwen3-8B作为鲁棒性检验；3) 提出自动结构发现pipeline，由LLM迭代提出并细化任务特定的人物结构和提取提示；4) 在13个不同子研究的基准上验证该方法。

Result: BDE固定schema在Twin-2K-500上比原始转录基线提升+1.91个百分点，但在异质任务上与基线统计上无显著差异。自动结构发现pipeline在13个异构子研究上恢复了性能，平均准确率相比原始转录基线提升+1.91个百分点，并消除了固定schema带来的显著损失。

Conclusion: LLM数字孪生的主要约束不是提供多少信息，而是信息如何结构化，且最优结构依赖于具体任务。这为数字孪生中人物表征的设计提供了新的视角和实用的自动化方法。

Abstract: LLM-based "digital twins" aim to simulate how an individual would behavein new environments or respond to novel questions, given some representation of that individual's prior responses. A common approach constructs this representation from survey transcripts or summaries responses. Prior work shows that compressing long transcripts into shorter LLM-generated summaries does not significantly reduce predictive accuracy, suggesting that information volume is not the primary bottleneck.
  In this work, we argue that the key limitation is instead structural:how persona information is organized before being provided to thesimulator model. We study this by comparing unstructured summaries with structured persona representations. First, we introduce a hand-craftedschema (BDE: Background, Decision procedure, Evaluation), grounded in consumer-behavior theory, and show that it improves predictive accuracy over raw transcripts by +1.91 percentage points on a homogeneous benchmark (Twin-2K-500), with similar gains on gpt-5.4-mini and Qwen3-8B as robustness checks. However, this fixed structure does not generalizeacross more heterogeneous tasks, where performance is statistically indistinguishable from the raw transcript baseline.
  To address this limitation, we propose an automatic structure-discovery pipeline in which an LLM iteratively proposes and refines task-specific persona structures and extraction prompts. On a benchmark of 13 diverse sub-studies, this approach restores performance, improving mean accuracy by +1.91 percentage points over the raw transcript baseline and eliminating significant losses observed with the fixed schema.
  Overall, our results suggest that the main constraint in LLM-based digital twins is not how much information is provided, but how it is structured -- and that the optimal structure depends on the task.

</details>


### [84] [When Vocabulary Comprehension Fails Clinical Reasoning: Evaluating Therapy Bots' Safety Risks for Generation Alpha](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20345)
*Manisha Mehta,Virendra Mehta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对Gen Alpha（2010-2024年出生）青少年使用生成式AI进行心理健康咨询的安全性，构建了两个基准测试集（64个青少年心理健康表达和75组多轮对话），评估了Claude、GPT-4o、Llama-3.1等主流LLM。结果发现模型虽能理解76-82%的词汇，但临床风险评估准确性仅64-72%，存在显著的"词汇理解-临床风险"差距（10-14个百分点），且在人类治疗师中不存在该差距。研究识别了六大失败模式（如讽刺掩盖、最小化接受、非正式风格偏差等），组合出现时漏诊率高达94%。仅重型脚手架干预才能达到人类水平（成本6.4倍），最终建议强制人工介入架构和监管框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gen Alpha青少年大量使用生成式AI获取心理健康支持（美国13.1%的青少年），但已发生多起与AI聊天机器人交互相关的青少年死亡事件。青少年的交流特点（夸张语言、讽刺性积极、语义漂移、情境多义）使LLM的临床风险评估能力未经验证，亟需系统性评估。

Method: 构建两个基准：(1) 64个Gen Alpha心理健康表达，经母语者（ICC=0.72）和临床医生（kappa=0.78）验证；(2) 75组多轮对话（780轮），包含标准版和Gen Alpha版配对。评估Claude、GPT-4o、Llama-3.1三种LLM架构的词汇理解和临床风险评估能力，并与人类治疗师对比。

Result: 模型词汇理解76-82%，临床风险校准仅64-72%，存在10-14百分点的差距（p<.001, d>0.48），人类治疗师仅3pp差距（不显著）。该差距在架构间一致，并随歧义性增加从7pp扩大至18pp。识别出六大失败模式：讽刺掩盖（29pp）、最小化接受（43pp）、非正式风格偏差（24pp）、风险分层歧义（19pp）、语义漂移（19pp）、情境依赖暴力（7pp）。三种以上模式组合时漏诊率达94%。轻量缓解无效，仅重型脚手架能达到人类水平（成本增加6.4倍）。34%基线漏诊率对应年146,880起被遗漏的心理危机。

Conclusion: 当前LLM驱动的心理健康AI对青少年安全不足，存在系统性词汇理解与临床风险评估之间的鸿沟。建议强制实施人类介入架构、季度青年群体专项验证、透明性能披露，以及针对面向青少年的心理健康AI建立监管框架。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems have become informal mental health support resources for Generation Alpha (Gen Alpha, born 2010-2024), with 13.1% of U.S. adolescents (5.4 million) using generative AI for mental health advice. While these systems, from therapy apps to general chatbots, rely on large language models trained on extensive psychological literature, their safety for youth communication patterns characterized by hyperbolic language, ironic positivity, rapid semantic drift, and contextual polysemy remains unvalidated. Following multiple adolescent deaths linked to AI chatbot interactions, systematic evaluation is critical. We present two benchmarks: (1) 64 Gen Alpha mental health expressions validated by native speakers (ICC=0.72) and clinicians (kappa=0.78); (2) 75 multi-turn conversations (780 turns) with paired Standard/Gen Alpha versions. Across evaluations of LLM architectures underlying therapy apps and general chatbots - Claude, GPT-4o, Llama-3.1 - models understand 76-82% of vocabulary but correctly calibrate only 64-72% of clinical risk, creating a 10-14 percentage point (pp) vocabulary-comprehension gap (p<.001, d>0.48) absent in human therapists (3pp, p=.22). The gap is architecturally consistent and widens with ambiguity (7pp -> 18pp). We identify six failure patterns: sarcasm masking (29pp), minimization acceptance (43pp), informal style bias (24pp), risk-stratified ambiguity (19pp), semantic drift (19pp), context-dependent violence (7pp). Patterns compound; three or more yield 94% miss rates. Lightweight mitigations fail; only heavy scaffolding achieves human performance (6.4x cost). With 34% baseline miss rate yielding 146,880 estimated annual missed crises, we recommend mandatory human-in-the-loop architectures, quarterly youth-specific validation, transparent performance disclosure, and regulatory frameworks for youth-facing mental health AI.

</details>


### [85] [Building and Evaluating a Synthetic Bengali Speech Resource for Telecom Customer Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20346)
*Kawshik Kumar Paul,Md. Nafiul Alam Fuji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向电信客服场景的孟加拉语合成语音数据集，包含10,000对音频-文本对（约26.82小时），在Hugging Face上以CC-BY-4.0许可公开。数据集通过OmniVoice语音克隆模式生成，并提供了归一化转写字段用于ASR/STT训练。自动评估显示平均WER为2.54%、CER为0.59%，表明文本-音频一致性良好，同时讨论了合成语音和STT评估的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向客户的语音系统通常需要领域特定的语言覆盖，而现有的孟加拉语语音资源在电信客服这一垂直领域严重缺乏，需要构建一个专用的数据资源来支持该领域的ASR/STT系统开发。

Method: 使用OmniVoice的语音克隆模式，基于真实女性参考录音和转写文本生成语音，采用bfloat16精度、16步扩散采样和1.0语速控制。数据集按9,000/500/500划分训练、验证和测试集，提供原始文本和归一化转写两个字段。评估通过领域适配的Whisper ASR模型（从bengaliAI/tugstugi_bengaliai-regional-asr_whisper-medium微调）对全部10,000个样本进行自动可懂度检查，并结合人工试听检查。

Result: 自动评估得到平均WER为2.54%、平均CER为0.59%，WER和CER的中位数均为0.00%，表明所选自动评估流程下文本-音频一致性较强。

Conclusion: 该数据集为电信客服场景的孟加拉语语音应用提供了可用的合成训练资源，但合成语音和基于STT的评估存在固有局限，论文对此进行了讨论。

Abstract: Speech systems used in customer-facing applications often require domain-specific language coverage. We present a synthetic Bengali speech dataset for telecom customer-care scenarios. The dataset contains 10,000 audio-text pairs, approximately 26.82 hours of 24 kHz speech, and predefined train, validation, and test splits of 9,000, 500, and 500 examples. It is publicly released on Hugging Face under the CC-BY-4.0 license. The speech was generated with OmniVoice in voice-cloning mode using a real female reference recording and transcript, with bfloat16 precision, 16 diffusion sampling steps, and a speaking-rate control value of 1.0. Along with the original Bengali text, the dataset provides a normalized transcript field designed for ASR/STT training and evaluation. We report an automatic intelligibility check over all 10,000 samples using a domain-adapted Whisper ASR model fine-tuned from bengaliAI/tugstugi_bengaliai-regional-asr_whisper-medium, along with a manual listening check on selected samples. The evaluation gives an average WER of 2.54%, an average CER of 0.59%, and median WER and CER values of 0.00%. These results suggest strong text-audio consistency under the selected automatic evaluation pipeline, while the paper also discusses the limitations of synthetic speech and STT-based evaluation.

</details>


### [86] [Who Do Language Models Think Is Competent? A Mechanistic Analysis of Occupational Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20347)
*Keren Fuentes,Aaron Mueller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个因果框架，将职业偏见分解为模型内部表征（用户能力表征）和可观察输出两个测量点，发现即使模型通过行为偏见评估，其内部表征中仍存在可检测的人口统计学偏见（性别、种族、社会经济地位），这些表征偏见可在干预下影响下游行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常能通过行为偏见评估，但无法确定模型是真正消除了偏见的底层关联，还是仅仅学会了不表达这些偏见。现有行为指标可能遗漏内部表征中仍然存在的偏见，需要一种方法深入检查模型内部表征层面的偏见。

Method: 引入因果框架将职业偏见分解为两个测量点：模型对用户能力的内部表征和可观察输出。为模型内部表征的用户专业知识推导转向向量（steering vectors），并在问答任务和招聘任务中验证这些向量因果中介模型行为。将该框架应用于多个开源权重模型。

Result: 发现人口统计学属性（性别、种族、社会经济地位）会影响模型对用户专业知识的内部表征，即使在行为指标未检测到人口统计群体间差异的情况下也是如此。在干预条件下，这些模型表征能影响下游行为，表明存在行为指标单独无法检测的失败模式。

Conclusion: 仅依赖行为偏见评估不足以全面衡量模型的偏见状况，内部表征层面的偏见即使在行为层面不可见时仍然存在并可能产生下游影响，研究框架为更稳健的偏见检测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) often pass behavioral bias evaluations, but it remains unclear whether they no longer represent the underlying associations that give rise to biases, or have merely learned not to express them. In this study, we show that representational biases are often detectable, even when behavioral biases are not visible. We introduce a causal framework that decomposes occupational bias into two measurement points: a model's internal representation of a user's competence, and its observable outputs. We derive steering vectors for representations of user expertise, and verify that they causally mediate model behavior in both a question-answering task and a hiring task. Applying this framework to several open-weight models, we find that demographic attributes, such as gender, race, and socioeconomic status, influence a model's representation of user expertise, even in cases where behavioral metrics detect no disparity between demographics. We show that these model representations can influence downstream behavior under intervention, suggesting failure modes that behavioral metrics alone may not detect.

</details>


### [87] [Inhibitory Attention for Clinical Long-Context Reasoning: Characterizing and Mitigating Lost-in-the-Middle Effects in EHR Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20348)
*Sanjay Basu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画了临床电子健康记录中的"lost-in-the-middle"效应（称为CLitM问题），并提出Query-Conditioned Clinical Suppression（QCCS）作为轻量级查询条件选择门控。在2,196个指令-响应对和六个语言模型上验证了CLitM效应，并证明QCCS在指令遵循准确率上显著优于BM25、密集检索和交叉编码器重排序等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）单患者动辄超过10万token，而大语言模型存在"lost-in-the-middle"效应，即长上下文中部信息检索可靠性低于边缘信息。在临床场景中，最关键的事实可能恰好位于笔记中部，这种偏差并非良性，可能导致临床决策出错。目前缺乏对这一问题在临床语境下的系统表征和解决方案。

Method: 作者：(1) 基于MedAlign数据集对2,196个指令-响应对和六个语言模型进行CLitM效应的系统表征；(2) 提出QCCS（Query-Conditioned Clinical Suppression），一种轻量级查询条件选择门控；(3) 在83个保留指令上，将QCCS与BM25、BM25+章节标题过滤、密集检索、交叉编码器重排序及完整上下文进行对比评估；(4) 使用LLM-as-judge评分标准评估指令遵循准确率。

Result: CLitM效应显著：峰值准确率59.5%（20-30%分位）与谷底准确率37.6%（70-80%分位）之间差距达21.9个百分点；67.8%的参考答案落在CLitM低谷区间。QCCS在所有比较方法中表现最优：对中部位置指令，QCCS达到16.7%准确率，而BM25仅3.3%、交叉编码器和密集检索均为0.0%、完整上下文仅6.7%。QCCS的总体准确率为25.3%，远超检索方法（最高3.6%）。值得注意的是，BM25在k=20时检索到黄金证据句的比例达98.8%（QCCS仅34.9%），但检索方法即使检索到证据句准确率也不超过2.6%，而QCCS即使未检索到也能达到25.0%的准确率。

Conclusion: 该概念验证研究表明，查询对齐的上下文选择对EHR指令遵循准确率的预测能力优于黄金句子检索召回率。这表明在临床长上下文中，单纯追求检索召回率并非优化目标，上下文选择策略的选择性（即"抑制"无关内容）可能比传统检索方法更关键。QCCS作为轻量级选择门控，为CLitM问题提供了一种有效且计算高效的解决思路。

Abstract: Electronic health records now routinely exceed 100,000 tokens per patient. Yet large language models exhibit the lost-in-the-middle (LitM) effect: information near the center of a long context is retrieved less reliably than information near the edges. In clinical use this is not benign: the single most consequential fact in a note can sit at its center. We term this the clinical lost-in-the-middle (CLitM) problem, give its first systematic characterization using MedAlign, and compare context-selection strategies as remedies. Across 2,196 instruction-response pairs and six language models, we observe a 21.9 percentage-point gap between peak accuracy (59.5%, 95% CI [46.3, 71.0], 20-30% decile) and trough accuracy (37.6% [23.2, 52.5] at 70-80%); 67.8% of reference answers fall between the 10th and 90th percentiles of the EHR timeline, inside the CLitM trough. We introduce Query-Conditioned Clinical Suppression (QCCS), a lightweight query-conditioned selection gate, and evaluate it against BM25, BM25 with section-header filtering, dense retrieval, and cross-encoder reranking (N=83 held-out instructions). With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (16k context), QCCS outperforms all five comparators under LLM-as-judge scoring: for middle-position instructions QCCS reaches 16.7% versus BM25 3.3%, cross-encoder 0.0%, dense 0.0%, and full context 6.7%; overall QCCS reaches 25.3% versus at most 3.6% for retrieval-only comparators. This advantage is not explained by retrieval recall: at k=20, BM25 retrieves the gold evidence sentence in 98.8% of instructions (QCCS 34.9%), yet retrieval arms stay at most 2.6% accurate even when they retrieve it, whereas QCCS reaches 25.0% even when it does not. In this proof-of-concept evaluation, query-aligned context selection predicts EHR instruction-following accuracy better than gold-sentence retrieval recall.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Prompt Engineering: A Systematic Analysis of Prompt Lexical Sensitivity and Its Impacts on Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20349)
*Qipeng Xie,Zi Liang,Jiafei Wu,Yufei Chen,Weizheng Wang,Wenao Ma,Zhong Ming,Haiqin Yang,Kaishun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL
