The account gives philosophers a new conceptual tool for understanding deep learning, and points to empirical directions for mechanistic interpretability research.

</details>


### [220] [Belief Without Behavior: Measuring the Translation of Theory of Mind into Coordinated Social Action in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20975)
*Tonglin Yan,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Rudrauf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC基准，用于评估智能体在需要多模态（语言、空间轨迹、注视方向、面部表情）协调的社会互动中的心智推理与实际行为表现。评估13个模型（含11个VLM）后发现VLM在生成与ToM约束一致的行为方面表现不佳，而带有显式ToM模块的PCM-LLM在所有条件下均成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ToM评估基准将心智推理与具身行为分开评测，未能衡量从社会推理到社会行动之间的差距。真实社交需要智能体同时将心理状态推断转化为跨多种通道（语言和非语言）的协调行为信号，因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一测量空白。

Method: 引入MOSAIC基准：两个具身智能体在合作与竞争场景中交互，需要整合言语陈述、空间轨迹、注视方向和面部表情，并在系统变化的ToM约束条件下进行评估。对13个模型（包括11个VLM）各进行了200次试验，并进行了信号级分析以定位瓶颈。

Result: VLM无法产生与预期结果一致的行为，且施加显式ToM阶约束不会带来与指定推理水平对齐的可靠行为变化。信号级分析揭示两个连续瓶颈：多数模型无法产生方向一致的非语言信号；即便存在信号，VLM智能体也无法解读他人行为并做出反应。PCM-LLM（含显式ToM模块的结构化架构）在所有条件下均成功。

Conclusion: 显式的信念-行动耦合（explicit belief-action coupling）是此类任务成功的充分条件，暗示当前VLM缺乏将推理转化为跨通道协调行为的内在机制，需要更结构化的架构设计。

Abstract: Effective social interaction requires agents to translate mental state inferences into coordinated behavioral signals across verbal and nonverbal channels simultaneously. Yet existing benchmarks evaluate theory of mind (ToM) reasoning and embodied behavior in isolation, leaving unmeasured the gap between social inference and social action. We introduce MOSAIC (Multimodal Orchestration of Social Action, Inference, and Communication), a controlled benchmark in which two embodied agents interact across cooperative and competitive scenarios requiring integration of verbal statements, spatial trajectories, gaze direction, and facial expression under systematically varied ToM constraints. Evaluating 13 models, including 11 VLMs, across 200 trials per model, we find that VLMs fail to produce behaviors consistent with the expected outcomes under ToM-order constraints, and that imposing explicit ToM-order constraints produces no reliable behavioral change aligned with the specified reasoning level. Signal-level analysis reveals two sequential bottlenecks: most models cannot produce directionally coherent nonverbal signals, and even when signals are present, VLM agents fail to interpret others behaviors and react to them. PCM-LLM, included as a structured architectural reference point with an explicit ToM module, succeeds across all conditions, suggesting that explicit belief-action coupling is a sufficient ingredient for this class of tasks.

</details>


### [221] [Don't Solve, Just Compare: Tiny Advisors for Runtime Intervention in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21027)
*Yanze Jiang,Mingxuan Li,Yuhao Wang,Shengfang Zhai,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COTA提出了一种仅基于比较的轻量级运行时干预框架，使用微型比较器判断备选方案是否优于actor的提议，通过重复比较决定何时需要干预，并在WebShop、ALFWorld和tau^3-Retail三个环境中九个实验设置中全部取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在实际任务中需要长时间的推理和决策，运行时干预可以在不重新训练底层模型的前提下提高可靠性。然而，仅检测失败是不够的，有效的干预还需要提供有用的恢复方向。现有方法要么依赖专家求解器（成本高），要么依赖能生成任务特定修正的批评者（对模型容量要求高），因此需要一种更轻量级的干预范式。

Method: COTA框架的核心是一个微型比较器，它判断采样得到的备选方案是否比actor的原始提议产生更好的延续。通过基于相同前缀反事实分支构造的成对监督信号来训练该比较器。当重复比较表明干预恰当时，将优选方案作为非约束性建议返回，由原始actor自行重新规划。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和tau^3-Retail三个环境、三种actor的共九个实验设置中，COTA均取得了改进，并优于对比基线。结果表明即使辅助模型的任务求解能力显著弱于actor，建设性的运行时干预仍然可以有效。

Conclusion: 该工作证明建设性的运行时干预不必依赖强大的专家求解器或任务级批评者，仅通过比较机制就能提供有效的恢复方向，为轻量级LLM代理干预提供了新的思路。

Abstract: LLM agents are emerging as an important paradigm for real-world tasks that require reasoning, tool use, and sequential decision-making. As these agents operate over longer horizons, runtime intervention offers a way to improve reliability without retraining the underlying actor. Failure detection alone is insufficient. Effective intervention must also provide a useful direction for recovery. Existing approaches often rely on an expert solver or a critic that generates task-specific corrections, incurring either the cost of another capable solver or the capacity demands of a task-capable critic. We introduce Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor (COTA), a comparison-only framework for constructive runtime intervention. In COTA, a tiny comparator judges whether sampled alternatives lead to better continuations than the actor's proposal, and repeated comparisons determine when intervention is warranted. We train the comparator using pairwise supervision constructed from same-prefix counterfactual branches. Preferred alternatives are returned as non-binding advice, leaving the original actor to replan. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and tau^3-Retail with three actors, COTA improves all nine evaluation settings and outperforms the compared baselines. These results show that constructive runtime intervention can remain effective even when the auxiliary model has substantially weaker task-solving capability than the actor.

</details>


### [222] [Evaluating Large Language Model Performance on International Maritime Dangerous Goods Code Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21036)
*Alexander Thomas,Hubert P. H. Shum,Darren Nellis,Manli Zhu,Phatpicha Yochum,William Bartle,Daniel Wrightson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DGEval，这是首个用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）对国际海运危险货物规则（IMDG第42-24修正案）知识掌握程度的基准测试，包含1,678道题，评估了6家提供商的13个模型。结果显示虽然最佳模型在多项选择题上超过人类从业者基线，但所有模型在积载、隔离和法规记忆等安全关键操作领域表现最弱，表明LLM可用于合规辅助（尤其在结构化DGL查询+网络搜索场景），但安全关键场景仍需人工监督和权威来源核验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海运危险货物运输是高后果活动，受复杂的IMDG规范约束。从业者越来越多地使用LLM作为决策支持工具，但缺乏系统性评估来验证LLM能否可靠地解释IMDG要求以用于安全关键场景，因此需要构建专门的评估基准。

Method: 构建了DGEval基准，数据来源于NCB Hazcheck电子学习平台的专家编写问题和危险货物清单（DGL）的结构化查询，共1,678道题，涵盖多项选择、开放问答、DGL查询和法规识别四类任务。评估了来自6家提供商的13个模型，在多种思考配置下测试，包括一个海事领域微调模型，并测试了网络搜索的影响。

Result: 最佳模型在多项选择题上超过人类从业者基线，但所有模型在积载、隔离和法规记忆等操作性安全关键领域表现最弱。带网络搜索的结构化DGL查询表现较好。

Conclusion: LLM可以辅助合规任务（尤其是结构化DGL查询配合网络搜索），但在操作关键领域和法规文本记忆方面的不可靠性意味着在安全关键场景部署前必须保留人工监督和权威来源核验。DGEval应作为持续应用的安全保证工具，而非对当前能力的静态定性。

Abstract: The transport of dangerous goods by sea is a high-consequence activity governed by the International Maritime Dangerous Goods (IMDG) Code, a complex regulatory framework where errors in classification, packaging, stowage, or segregation can result in fire, explosion, toxic release, or loss of life or vessel. Correct compliance requires accurately interpreting hundreds of pages of interacting provisions, updated on a two-year amendment cycle. Practitioners increasingly use Large Language Models (LLMs) as decision-support tools, yet no systematic evaluation exists of whether they can reliably interpret IMDG requirements for safety-critical use.
  This paper introduces DGEval, the first benchmark for evaluating LLM knowledge of IMDG Amendment 42-24. Built from expert-written questions on the NCB Hazcheck e-learning platform and structured lookups from the Dangerous Goods List (DGL), it comprises 1,678 questions across multiple-choice, open-ended, DGL lookup, and regulatory identification tasks. We evaluate 13 models from six providers across multiple thinking configurations, including one maritime domain-specific fine-tuned model, and test the effect of web search.
  Although the best-performing model exceeds the human practitioner baseline on multiple-choice questions, all models are weakest in the operationally safety-critical areas of stowage, segregation, and regulatory recall. These results indicate that LLMs may support compliance tasks, particularly structured DGL lookups with web search, but unreliability in operational areas and regulatory-text recall means human oversight and authoritative source verification remain necessary before deployment in any safety-critical context. DGEval is designed as a safety assurance instrument to be applied continuously as models evolve, not as a settled characterisation of current capability.

</details>


### [223] [Socialized Division and Collaboration: Rethinking Class-Incremental Learning under Optimization Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21044)
*Xinjie Yao,Zhihe Fan,Yunqi Zhu,Jiaqi Zhou,Dengyu Zhao,Zhoupeng Guo,Yan Fan,Guosong Jiang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出社交化分工与协作（SDC）框架，将类增量学习从单一模型范式重构为多专用模型协作范式，通过基于亥姆霍兹自由能的能量兼容性准则实现会话-模型的自适应分配，从而缓解连续会话间优化方向冲突导致的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统类增量学习采用单模型范式，在连续会话优化方向不兼容时会产生破坏性干扰和灾难性遗忘。作者认为这种遗忘源于在单一参数空间中强制异构学习动态的结构性局限，因此需要一种新的理论框架来化解优化冲突。

Method: SDC框架受社会团结理论启发，将会话学习分解到针对优化冲突而专业化的多个模型中，同时支持协调协作。提出基于亥姆霍兹自由能的能量-会话模型兼容性准则，为会话分配提供原理性机制，将会话分配、模型演化和协作推理整合为统一流程。

Result: 该框架作为传统的单模型持续学习范式的替代方案，有效应对持续优化冲突下的学习场景，证明了多模型分工协作范式能克服单模型结构性限制。

Conclusion: SDC提供了一种超越单一模型持续学习范式的新设计原则，通过专业化分工与协作应对持久优化冲突，为持续学习提供更普适的架构性启发。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning is commonly instantiated as a single-model paradigm, where a unified model sequentially adapts to an unbounded stream of sessions. While effective under mild distributional shifts, this formulation becomes strained when successive sessions induce incompatible optimization directions, leading to destructive interference and catastrophic forgetting. We argue that such forgetting reflects a structural limitation of enforcing heterogeneous learning dynamics within a single parameter space. Motivated by social solidarity theory, we propose Socialized Division and Collaboration (SDC) as a reformulation of continual learning that decomposes session learning across specialized models in response to optimization conflicts, while enabling coordinated collaboration. To support this formulation with a principled allocation mechanism, we introduce an energy-based session-model compatibility criterion grounded in Helmholtz free energy, which guides adaptive session allocation and model evolution under conflicting objectives. This framework integrates session assignment, model evolution, and collaborative inference into a unified pipeline, offering an alternative to monolithic continual learning formulations and highlighting a broader design principle for learning under persistent optimization conflicts.

</details>


### [224] [The Cost of a Physics Prior Is Bounded by the Ablation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21059)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明形状约束（shape-constrained）和物理信息学习中的"先验成本"（enforcing a prior的精度代价）主要由自由特征和验证集划分方式决定，而非先验本身。通过一个嵌套包含关系0 ≤ P ≤ D（约束模型的额外风险P不超过剔除先验特征的消融模型的风险D），作者指出该界限可作为符号检验，并在野火严重度排序任务上验证了坐标特征作为"盾牌"可大幅降低先验成本，同时提出筛选管线以剔除不可识别的实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将施加先验（如单调性、物理约束）所付出的精度成本视为先验本身的属性，但作者质疑这一观点，认为成本主要来自自由特征（free features）和验证集划分（validation split）的选择。若不能正确归因，研究者可能错误地放弃有价值的先验，或在不可识别的协议下得出误导性结论。

Method: 理论上，作者建立了一个嵌套包含关系：对特征S施加形状约束的假设类包含忽略S的消融类（因为关于x_j常数的函数同时是非递减也是非递增的），因此对任意风险泛函都有0 ≤ P ≤ D，无需凸性、光滑性或可实现性假设。经验上这构成一个符号检验：约束模型绝不能输给其自身的消融版本。作者在N=26,681、K=3的野火严重度排序任务上，施加四个气象驱动因子的硬单调约束，坐标留作自由特征，并通过从i.i.d.重采样到2度空间分块的验证阶梯验证界限。同时提出一个两拟合筛查（two-fit screen）用于在训练约束模型前拒绝不可识别的实验。

Result: 坐标作为"盾牌"：单独使用坐标即可在空间分块下恢复全模型92.9%的宏F1，将D从0.1288降至0.0427；相同物理先验在被坐标保护时成本为0.0473，无保护时高达0.3470，比值为7.3。D是协议依赖的：将分块从1度粗化到10度使D从0.0942降至0.0050，导致两种配置先验不可识别。318次比较中获得自校准下限0.0220宏F1，低于此值的报告价格不可解释，包括作者自身头部网格中的四个单元。无约束模型以0.48-0.49的比率违反先验，而执行先验成本仅0.0473，说明成本与合规性相互独立。

Conclusion: 先验成本不是先验的固有属性，而是自由特征与验证协议共同塑造的结果。经过认证的嵌套界限提供了诊断工具和自校准精度下限，帮助研究者识别"不可识别"实验并合理解读精度报告，避免对先验成本做出错误归因。

Abstract: Shape-constrained and physics-informed learning reports an accuracy cost of enforcing a prior and treats it as a property of the prior. We show it is mostly a property of the free features and the validation split. Let P be the excess risk of restricting a hypothesis class to functions with a shape constraint on features S, and D the excess risk of the ablated model that ignores S. Because a function constant in x_j is both non-decreasing and non-increasing in x_j, the ablated class is contained in the constrained class, so 0 <= P <= D for every risk functional, with no convexity, smoothness, or realizability assumption. Empirically the bound is a sign test: a constrained model must never be beaten by its own ablation. We instantiate it on an ordinal wildfire-severity task (N = 26,681, K = 3) with hard monotone constraints on four meteorological drivers, coordinates left free, and a validation ladder from i.i.d. resampling to 2-degree spatial blocking. Coordinates act as a shield: alone they recover 92.9% of the full model's macro-F1 under spatial blocking, collapsing D from 0.1288 to 0.0427; the same prior costs 0.0473 shielded and 0.3470 unshielded, a ratio of 7.3 with identical physics. Because D is protocol-dependent it does not transfer: coarsening blocks from 1 to 10 degrees drives D from 0.0942 to 0.0050, leaving two configurations unidentifiable a priori. Inversions of the certified nesting bound the pipeline's additive resolution: over 318 comparisons they give a self-calibrating floor of 0.0220 macro-F1, below which no reported price is interpretable, including four cells in our own headline grid. Cost and compliance are independent: the unconstrained model violates the prior at rate 0.48-0.49 while enforcing it costs 0.0473. We give a two-fit screen that rejects unidentifiable experiments before a constrained model is trained.

</details>


### [225] [CellPath-Bench: A Multidimensional Benchmark for Whole-Slide Cellular Representations in Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21060)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Hanqing Chao,Yawei Ma,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CellPath-Bench，一个细胞分辨率的病理学基础模型基准测试框架，用于系统评估冻结PFM的细胞类型信息可解码性和跨组织、跨数据集、跨器官的可迁移性。基于25个空间对齐的H&E-Xenium组织切片（11个器官、7,079,283个细胞），通过标准化的多类线性探针对30个基础模型进行30万余次评估，揭示了模型间显著的细胞解码能力和跨域泛化差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学基础模型（PFMs）基准无法系统诊断其全切片细胞表征能力，特别是细胞类型信息的可解码性以及这些信息在组织切片、数据集和解剖器官间的可迁移性。需要一个标准化框架来审计冻结PFM表征中的细胞信息。

Method: 从52个候选Xenium数据集中经质量控制选取25个空间对齐的H&E-Xenium组织切片（覆盖11个器官、7,079,283个细胞），构建细粒度和粗粒度两种分类体系。在注册的细胞核坐标处采样冻结的WSI特征图，使用标准化多类线性探针评估。提出两个指标：Cell Representation Advantage (CRA)衡量核锚定表征相对patch级均值池化的组内优势；Cell Representation Transferability (CRT)刻画细胞类型可解码性跨切片、数据集和器官的泛化能力。对30个病理专用及通用基础模型进行304,920次评估。

Result: 基准测试揭示模型间存在显著的细胞类型可解码性和跨域泛化差异，形成了不同的多维能力画像，表明不同PFM在细胞级表征能力上具有明显分化。

Conclusion: CellPath-Bench为审计冻结PFM表征中的细胞信息提供了标准化框架，能够系统评估和比较病理学基础模型在细胞分辨率上的表征能力及其跨域可迁移性。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose backbones, yet existing benchmarks cannot systematically diagnose their whole-slide cellular representation capabilities, including the decodability of cell-type information and the transferability of such information across tissue sections, datasets, and anatomical organs. We introduce CellPath-Bench, a cellular-resolution benchmark that evaluates frozen PFMs themselves. Following quality control of 52 candidate Xenium datasets, we construct a panel of 25 spatially aligned H\&E--Xenium tissue sections spanning 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized into fine- and coarse-grained taxonomies. CellPath-Bench samples frozen WSI feature maps at registered nuclear coordinates and evaluates them using standardized multiclass linear probes. Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) measures the within-section advantage of nucleus-anchored representations over patch-level mean pooling, while Cell Representation Transferability (CRT) characterizes the generalization of cell-type decodability across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. We benchmark 30 pathology-specific and general-purpose foundation models through 304,920 runs across spatial readouts, magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and evaluation protocols. The results reveal substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and its cross-domain generalization, yielding distinct multidimensional capability profiles. CellPath-Bench provides a standardized framework for auditing cellular information in frozen PFM representations.

</details>


### [226] [Can Legal AI Know When It Is Wrong? And Do Students Know When It Is?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21089)
*Angel Mary John,Vipin Kumar Singh,Jerrin Thomas Panachakel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对将大语言模型（LLMs）融入印度司法系统进行了社会技术审计，识别出"信心惰性"现象——LLM以接近最大置信度输出错误法律判决（类比邓宁-克鲁格效应），并提出"先例过拟合"假设解释成因。第一阶段测试ChatGPT、Meta AI和Perplexity AI在印度《1872年合同法》60个案例上的表现，引入高置信度错误率（HCER）量化危险性的错误判决，发现Meta AI最易出错（31.7% HCER）。第二阶段对380名印度法律学生进行调查，发现验证行为常是对机器幻觉的反应性适应，提出转向对抗性法律研究教学法和源基验证架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM引入印度司法系统虽有望促进司法可及性，但存在严重风险。作者旨在系统性地识别和量化LLM在法律判决中的过度自信问题及其对人类用户的影响，以防止系统性专业疏忽。

Method: 采用两阶段社会技术审计：第一阶段对三类主流LLM（ChatGPT/GPT-5.2、Meta AI、Perplexity AI）在60个印度合同法案例上进行测试，引入新的量化指标高置信度错误率（HCER）测量置信度>=9但仍然错误的判决比例；第二阶段通过调查380名印度法律学生，分析人类对AI过度自信的脆弱性，包括幻觉引用遭遇与验证行为的关系以及AI伦理培训的缺失情况。

Result: 所有模型在法规更新方面均表现不佳。Meta AI最脆弱（HCER为31.7%，平均置信度9.1/10，经常误用修订前规则），其次是Perplexity AI（15.0%）和ChatGPT（6.7%）。法律学生中，遭遇捏造引文的学生的验证得分（4.2/5）显著高于未遭遇者（2.8/5）；81.6%知道提交幻觉案例可构成藐视法庭，但71.1%未接受正式AI伦理培训。

Conclusion: LLM在法律领域存在系统性过度自信问题，且人类用户缺乏足够的防范意识和培训。作者建议从被动验证转向对抗性法律研究教学法，并实施基于来源的验证架构以防止系统性专业疏忽。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Indian judiciary promises access to justice but introduces severe risks. We identify the 'inertia of confidence'--an overconfidence phenomenon analogous to the Dunning-Kruger effect where LLMs provide incorrect legal verdicts with near-maximum confidence, driven by a hypothesized 'precedent overfitting' bias. Phase I of our socio-technical audit tested ChatGPT (GPT-5.2), Meta AI, and Perplexity AI on a 60-case battery regarding the Indian Contract Act, 1872, and the shift toward statutory enforcement of specific performance. We introduce the High-Confidence Error Rate (HCER) to quantify incorrect verdicts delivered with dangerous certainty (>= 9 on a 1-10 scale). All models struggled with statutory updates. Meta AI proved most vulnerable (31.7% HCER), frequently misapplying pre-amendment rules with a 9.1/10 mean confidence, followed by Perplexity (15.0%) and ChatGPT (6.7%).
  Phase II investigated human vulnerability to this overconfidence via a survey of Indian law students (N=380). Verification often functions as a reactive adaptation to machine hallucinations: students encountering fabricated citations reported higher verification scores (4.2/5) than those with no such encounters (2.8/5). Furthermore, while 81.6% knew submitting hallucinated cases can lead to contempt-of-court, 71.1% received no formal training on ethical AI use. We propose shifting toward adversarial legal research pedagogy and implementing source-grounded verification architectures to prevent systemic professional negligence.

</details>


### [227] [When Trust Meets Truth: Trust-Truth Separability in LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21097)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Yuchen Guo,Jiahuan Pei,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑LLM-as-Judge系统中信任评分与真值判断的独立性假设，通过正确性受控的QA实验和源线索压力测试证明，LLM评判器的信任分数与真值判定高度耦合，不应将其作为独立的证据来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-Judge系统常输出多维评估（如可信度、可靠性、事实性），这些输出常被视为相互独立的证据。但这种独立性假设从未被严格检验，值得系统验证。

Method: 1) 在正确性受控的QA任务上比较LLM评判器的信任评分与真值判定之间的一致性，并与人类行为基准对照；2) 设计压力测试：仅改变相同QA中的来源线索（Source cues），观察信任评分、真值判定以及logit推导的正确侧概率（correct-side probabilities）的变化。

Result: LLM评判器的信任评分与真值判定之间的关联比人类行为参考更紧密；来源变化不仅影响信任分数，还显著改变了真值判定和logit推导的概率。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM-as-Judge协议不应将信任分数视为真值判断的独立证据，二者之间存在系统性耦合。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems can produce multi-dimensional evaluations, such as trustworthiness, reliability, and factuality, and these outputs are often interpreted as independent evidence. We test this assumption for a common pair of judgments: trust scoring and binary truth classification. On correctness-controlled QA, LLM judges align trust scores with truth verdicts more tightly than human behavioral reference, suggesting weaker separations between trust and truth judgment. We then apply stress tests by changing only source cues of identical QA between Human and AI. Source attribution shifts not only trust scores but also truth verdicts and logit-derived correct-side probabilities. Results show that current LLM-as-Judge protocols should not treat trust scores as independent evidence for truth judgments.

</details>


### [228] [ReFrame: Evidence-Guided Test-Time Safety Alignment in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21100)
*Wenzheng Jiang,Xuankun Rong,Yuanzhao Zhai,Dawei Feng,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReFrame，一个无需训练的测试时多模态安全对齐框架，通过两个代理（证据生成代理和重写路由代理）共享轻量级本地MLLM，在不修改或访问下游闭源MLLM内部信息的情况下，通过重构输入提示和图像路由来实现越狱防御、安全感知和过敏感拒绝的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态安全对齐方法依赖重训练或内部状态检查，无法应用于部署的闭源MLLM，因此需要一种测试时的安全对齐方案。作者识别出两个关键障碍——效用优势（utility dominance）和推理惯性（reasoning inertia），导致模型忽略潜在风险或跟随恶意推理轨迹。

Method: ReFrame框架包含两个代理，均共享一个轻量级本地部署的MLLM：(1) 证据生成代理负责构建互补的风险证据和效用证据；(2) 重写与路由代理将这些证据转换为安全的代理提示和图像路由决策，然后调用下游MLLM，全程不修改下游模型也不访问其内部信息。

Result: 在多个MLLM和基准上的实验表明，ReFrame在越狱防御、安全感知和过敏感拒绝减少方面均有提升，同时保持了多模态效用。

Conclusion: ReFrame验证了通过训练无关的输入重构方式实现多模态测试时安全对齐的可行性，为闭源MLLM的安全部署提供了一条实用的新路径。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend model capabilities beyond text, they also make safety alignment increasingly challenging. Multimodal safety alignment methods must address cross-modal jailbreaks, safety-awareness failures, and over-sensitive refusals. However, existing methods often rely on retraining or internal-state inspection, limiting their applicability to deployed closed-source MLLMs and motivating test-time safety alignment. We analyze this setting and identify two key obstacles, utility dominance and reasoning inertia, which cause models to overlook latent risks or follow malicious reasoning trajectories. Guided by these insights, we propose ReFrame, a training-free multimodal input reframing framework where two agents share a lightweight locally deployed MLLM: the evidence-generation agent constructs complementary risk and utility evidence, and the rewrite-and-routing agent converts it into a safe proxy prompt and image-routing decision before calling the downstream MLLM, without modifying it or accessing its internal information. Experiments across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks show that ReFrame improves jailbreak defense, safety awareness, and oversensitivity reduction while preserving multimodal utility.

</details>


### [229] [Large Language Models at the Intersection of Software Engineering and Software Security:An Evidence-Centered Structured Survey and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21107)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对截至2026年5月31日的仓库级LLM智能体文献进行了基于证据的结构化综述，覆盖软件工程任务、软件安全任务、适应机制、工件粒度与评估设计五个维度，提出了将功能正确性、安全性、运行可靠性、证据溯源与智能体权限分离的保障框架，并给出了跨研究比较的最低报告协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从代码补全走向仓库级智能体，参与上下文检索、文件编辑、工具执行等安全敏感工作流。然而现有证据被割裂为以功能任务完成为导向的软件工程评估和以漏洞检测、安全生成或利用验证为导向的安全评估，缺乏统一的证据框架来综合判断这类系统的真实能力与风险。

Method: 本文采用证据中心的结构化综述方法，对截至2026年5月31日的代表性工作进行系统梳理，从软件工程任务、软件安全任务、适应机制、工件粒度和评估设计五个维度构建任务分类体系，并在此基础上提出一个将功能正确性、安全性、运行可靠性、证据溯源与智能体权限分离的保障框架，同时识别反复出现的效度威胁并推导出最小报告协议。

Result: 综述发现：执行反馈与仓库访问能显著提升工程任务完成度，但不足以确立安全性；反之，静态分析标签或漏洞分类分数也极少能确证可部署的正确性。研究还识别出弱测试预言、数据重复与时间泄漏、智能体测试框架变动、仅代理安全检查、预算与人工干预报告不足等反复出现的效度威胁，并提出了一套最小报告协议。

Conclusion: 模型能力应被视为一个由任务适配证据支撑的保障案例来评判，而非依赖单一基准分数。未来研究应优先发展安全与功能联合的基准、仓库级威胁模型、校准的人类监督、纵向可维护性证据和可复现的智能体评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are moving from code completion toward repository-scale agents that retrieve context, edit files, execute tools, and participate in security-sensitive workflows. The evidence for these systems, however, remains divided between software engineering evaluations centered on functional task completion and software security evaluations centered on vulnerability detection, secure generation, or exploit-oriented validation. This evidence-centered structured survey synthesizes representative work available through May 31, 2026 across software engineering tasks, software security tasks, adaptation mechanisms, artifact granularity, and evaluation design. In addition to a task taxonomy, we introduce an assurance framework that separates functional correctness, security, operational reliability, evidence provenance, and agent authority. The review shows that execution feedback and repository access can substantially improve engineering task completion, but do not by themselves establish security; conversely, static-analysis labels or vulnerability-classification scores rarely establish deployable correctness. We identify recurring validity threats--weak test oracles, duplicated and temporally leaked data, changing agent harnesses, proxy-only security checks, and under-reported budgets and human intervention--and derive a minimum reporting protocol for cross-study comparison. The resulting research agenda prioritizes jointly secure-and-functional benchmarks, repository-scale threat models, calibrated human oversight, longitudinal maintainability evidence, and reproducible agent evaluation. The central conclusion is that model capability should be judged as an assurance case supported by task-appropriate evidence, rather than by a single benchmark score.

</details>


### [230] [Root cause analysis via difference graph discovery from linear time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21117)
*Anouk Ruer,Timothée Loranchet,Daria Bystrova,Charles K. Assaad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性时间序列中的根因分析问题，将其形式化为差异图发现任务，聚焦于因果系数在正常与异常状态间发生变化的"效应违抗"根因，并通过模拟数据和真实数据（IT监控、重症监护）验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂动态系统中的根因分析旨在识别导致异常的机制，但现有的根因分析方法在处理时间序列中因果结构变化（尤其是因果系数在正常与异常状态间的转变）方面存在不足，需要形式化的框架和有效的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 将根因分析问题形式化为基于线性离散时间动态结构因果模型的差异图发现任务；将原本用于发现两个群体间差异图的方法改编到时间序列场景中，用正常状态和异常状态替代原来的两个群体。

Result: 在模拟数据上评估了所提方法，并在IT监控和重症监护监控的真实数据集上展示了其实用性，结果表明差异图发现能够有效定位导致异常行为的因果机制。

Conclusion: 差异图发现方法能够成功应用于时间序列根因分析，帮助定位异常行为的因果机制，验证了该方法在现实场景（如IT监控和医疗监控）中的实用价值。

Abstract: Root cause analysis aims to identify the mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems. In this paper, we study root cause analysis in linear time-series through the lens of difference graph discovery. We focus on effect-defying root causes, corresponding to variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime. We formalize this problem using linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models and adapt several methods originally introduced for discovering difference graphs between two populations to the time-series setting, where the two populations are replaced by a normal and an anomalous regime. We first evaluate the proposed approaches on simulated data, and then demonstrate their practical relevance on real-world datasets from IT monitoring and intensive care monitoring. Our results show how difference graph discovery can help localize causal mechanisms responsible for anomalous behavior.

</details>


### [231] [From Attention Masks to Inert Zero-Vector Tokens: OAttention and O-Closure for Token Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21174)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OAttention，一种基于"活跃存在系数"p_i的支持耦合注意力机制，通过将注意力掩码从关系级控制扩展到token级"非参与状态"，实现零向量token作为零元素，并构建完整OTransformer栈及O-Closure律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力掩码仅提供关系级控制（指定哪些query-source对可交互），但缺乏一种由表示携带（representation-carried）的token级状态，使token在注意力边界上处于"非参与"状态。作者旨在为每个token引入一个存在系数，让token既可以控制自身信息的发射，又决定其参与共享计算的权重，从而实现更精确的注意力语义，特别是对缺失值（null token）的表示。

Method: 为每个token的隐藏载体h_i定义活动存在系数p_i=||h_i||^2/(τ+||h_i||^2)，该系数双重作用：(1)门控token i发出的信息；(2)决定token i进入与其他token共享计算的质量。OAttention在标准注意力（保留score、可见性关系、指数竞争、值聚合）基础上，用p_i门控接收端输出，用p_j加权源端（分子和分区）。由此构建局部O组件（OFFN、ONorm、OInject）、存在加权OStandardize，并通过残差与组合闭包得到O-Closure律M(H⊕0)=M(H)⊕0和完整的OTransformer。

Result: 通过契约测试和GPU评估验证。在零微调改造的克隆预训练TabPFN v3回归器上，校准的隐藏载体OAttention与Full-O变体在18个匹配数据集-种子案例上分别改变平均RMSE +0.088%和+0.177%。双块消融显示单独OAttention无法通过普通宿主组件保留NULL状态，而OTransformer路径可以。这些是有范围的精确性、活动路径兼容性和组合必要性的测试。

Conclusion: OAttention实现了零向量token作为注意力中的零元素，具备精确的null-receiver、null-source插入、自注意力插入和空支持性质，并通过组合闭包构建了完整的OTransformer。但作者明确限定结论范围：这并不确立通用无损性、任意宿主闭包、学习到的原点吸引或缺失值的一般语义。

Abstract: Attention masks are relation-level controls: they specify which query--source pairs may interact. They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \(h_i\) an active-presence coefficient \(p_i=\lVert h_i\rVert^2/(τ+\lVert h_i\rVert^2)\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \(i\), and it determines the mass with which token \(i\) enters computations shared with other tokens.
  OAttention is the support-coupled attention realization of this rule. It gates the receiver output by \(p_i\) and weights source \(j\) by \(p_j\) in both the attention numerator and partition, while retaining the standard score, visibility relation, exponential competition, and value aggregation. This makes the zero-vector token a zero element and yields exact null-receiver, null-source insertion, self-attention insertion, and empty-support properties. The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [232] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SENTRY，一个用确定性机器学习流水线（XGBoost + 混合RAG）替代问卷式评分系统的风险评估平台，在金融机构变更管理中实现ROC AUC 0.87、准确率85%，高风险变更检出率约为现有流程的3.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融机构的技术变更管理依赖风险评估，但现有自报式问卷主观性强、容易被操纵，且难以区分日常变更与后续引发重大事故的变更，需要更准确、一致且可审计的评估方法。

Method: SENTRY采用梯度提升决策树（XGBoost）和混合检索增强生成（RAG）构建确定性机器学习流水线。系统融合结构化运营元数据、应用依赖图和历史事故记录，并通过混合语义与词法检索从历史变更请求中提取非结构化文本的风险信号，将该信号压缩为单一标量特征后再输入模型推理，以保持模型确定性并以SHAP值实现逐预测可解释性。

Result: 在企业级规模变更数据上，SENTRY实现ROC AUC达0.87、总体准确率85%，高风险变更检出率约为现有流程的3.25倍。

Conclusion: 作者最后审视了该设计背后的架构权衡，并讨论了这些权衡对受监管环境中使用机器学习的启示。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [233] [Personalized Privacy Control in LLMs via Attention Head Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21209)
*Junseok Kim,Nakyeong Yang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化隐私（personalized privacy）概念，将用户特定的披露偏好纳入隐私控制；构建了P3Bench基准来扩展上下文隐私策略；实验表明基于提示的策略无法可靠执行个性化隐私策略（Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma3-4B的平均策略忽略率分别达51.25%和74.28%）；并提出Repair方法——一种推理时的注意力头干预方法，通过调整披露行为使其符合策略，显著改善模型对用户特定隐私偏好的遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文隐私研究仅关注LLM是否根据上下文相关规范来调节信息披露，但忽略了即使在相同的上下文中，不同用户的可接受披露边界可能不同，即缺少个性化的隐私保护机制。

Method: 提出了两个主要贡献：(1) P3Bench基准，将上下文隐私策略扩展为包含个性化披露策略的基准；(2) Repair方法，一种推理时的注意力头干预方法，通过调整注意力头激活来引导模型的披露行为朝向符合策略的方向。

Result: 实验发现纯基于提示（prompt-based）的策略无法可靠执行个性化隐私策略，Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma3-4B的平均策略忽略率分别高达51.25%和74.28%；Repair方法显著降低了模型未能遵循给定策略的情况，改善了模型对用户特定隐私偏好的遵守程度。

Conclusion: 个性化隐私是超越上下文隐私的重要研究方向，P3Bench为该方向提供了系统化的评估基准，Repair方法展示了通过推理时干预有效增强LLM隐私遵循能力的可行性。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI enables LLMs to access diverse user data, raising critical privacy concerns. Prior work on contextual privacy studies whether LLMs regulate information disclosure according to context-dependent norms. However, acceptable disclosure boundaries may vary across users even within the same context. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{personalized privacy}, which incorporates user-specific disclosure preferences into privacy control. We further present P3Bench~(\textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rivacy \textbf{P}reservation \textbf{Bench}mark), a novel benchmark extending contextual privacy policies with personalized disclosure policies. Experiments show that prompt-based policies fail to reliably enforce personalized privacy policies, with Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma3-4B showing average policy ignorance ratios of 51.25\% and 74.28\%, respectively. Finally, to address this problem, we propose \textsc{Repair}, a robust inference-time attention head intervention method that adjusts disclosure behavior toward policy-consistent responses. Our method significantly improves adherence to user-specific privacy preferences by reducing cases where the model fails to follow the given policy.

</details>


### [234] [Enhancing LLMs in Predictive Political QA with Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21218)
*Yinan Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zichun Jin,Xinyu Wang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PSL框架，通过双视图（语义视角提取立场信号、向量视角学习结构感知表征）将半结构化政治记录转化为推理导向证据，用于提升预测性政治问答中LLM的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强方法（如基于行动者画像的模拟和知识图谱证据注入）将外部政治资源仅视为基于知识的证据，未能充分建模与预测相关的信号（如行动者立场和高阶结构依赖关系），限制了预测性政治问答的效果。

Method: 提出PSL（duaL-view framework）双视图框架：(1) 语义视图——从与问题相关的行动者记录中提取立场信号；(2) 向量视图——从行动者交互图中学习结构感知的行动者表征。将两类互补信号转化为面向推理的证据供LLM使用。

Result: 在三个真实数据集和多个LLM上，PSL一致优于基线方法，消融实验证实了立场信号与结构信号的互补增益。

Conclusion: 立场信号与高阶结构信号是预测性政治QA中互补且有效的两类证据，双视图框架能更充分地利用外部政治资源来增强LLM的预测性推理能力。

Abstract: Predictive political question answering (QA), such as predicting how a political actor will vote, goes beyond factual lookup. External political resources offer rich historical evidence, but rarely contain the answer itself. Existing LLM augmentation methods, including actor-profile-based simulation and knowledge graph evidence injection, improve political reasoning but largely treat external resources as knowledge-based evidence, leaving prediction-relevant signals under-modeled. We identify two complementary signals for predictive political QA: actor stances that capture issue-specific preferences, and high-order structure signals that capture indirect dependencies among political actors. We propose PSL, a dual-view framework that converts semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence for LLMs. PSL extracts stance signals from question-relevant actor records in a semantic view, and learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph in a vector view. Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baselines, with ablations confirming the complementary gains of stance and structure signals.

</details>


### [235] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于AI模型交换的平台，通过引入本体论来促进AI模型和数据集的有效交换、管理与分析，消除工业场景中的语义鸿沟，并通过实时关键系统的用例验证其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI方法研究的快速发展，不同组织产生和使用大量需要追踪、管理和发现的资产（如模型、数据集），但缺乏有效的交换和管理机制，存在语义鸿沟问题。本文旨在回答在工业背景下如何有效交换和管理AI模型及相关资产而不产生语义差异的问题。

Method: 提出一个AI模型交换平台，支持AI模型和数据集的使用、交换与分析。平台集成本体论，以促进对任务要求的共同理解，从而解决资产追踪、追溯、管理和发现等关键问题。

Result: 通过在实时关键系统背景下阐明一个用例，展示了平台的实用性和价值。

Conclusion: 该平台结合本体论方法，能够有效支撑工业场景中AI资产的无语义鸿沟交换与管理，为AI进度追踪、透明比较和深入理解提供基础设施支持。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [236] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大规模TSP问题的广义划分交叉(GPX)算子中partition阶段的细粒度GPU实现，通过将GPX划分重新表述为图并行问题，利用CUDA并行化父环并集、四度顶点分裂、公共边删除和重组分量识别等步骤，在1万到200万个城市的实例上实现了48x到625x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于遗传算法(GA)的TSP求解器（如EAX）虽然达到了最先进的性能，但在大规模并行架构上的可扩展性受限，因为交叉操作涉及不规则内存访问、图遍历和顺序依赖。现有的GPU-based TSP求解器主要利用种群级并行，仅适用于较小规模的问题。因此需要一种细粒度的、算子级别的并行方法来解决大规模TSP实例。

Method: 将GPX算子的partition阶段重构为图并行问题，具体包括：(1) 采用coalesced内存布局优化数据访问；(2) 使用ghost-node变换处理度数为四的顶点；(3) 利用连通分量分析识别重组分量。在CUDA中并行化四个关键子任务：父环的并集构造、四度顶点的分裂、公共边的删除以及重组分量的识别。

Result: 在包含10,000到2,000,000个城市的实例上，与朴素的顺序CPU实现相比，GPU划分实现获得了48x到625x的加速比，同时显著降低了内存开销。

Conclusion: 算子级并行（operator-level parallelism）能够显著提升GA-based TSP求解器在现代众核架构上的可扩展性，为大规模TSP问题的GPU高效求解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [237] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLEAR（Continuous Latent Adapter Routing）是一个条件安全适配框架，通过轻量级隐状态门控连续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度，在提升LLM安全性的同时减少对通用能力的损害，在HarmBench上大幅降低攻击成功率（从32.3%降至0.5%），同时比全局SFT或LoRA在GSM8K上高出最多7.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全对齐方法（如SFT或标准LoRA）通常采用全局应用的安全调优，这会不分输入地改变模型行为，在抑制有害内容的同时也损害了良性输入上的模型效用。作者旨在解决安全性与实用性之间的权衡问题，希望设计一种条件自适应机制，只在需要时激活安全适配器。

Method: CLEAR采用持续隐态适配器路由机制：使用一个轻量级隐状态门（hidden-state gate）来连续控制安全低秩适配器（safety low-rank adapter）的激活强度。该门控机制根据输入内容动态调节安全适配器的介入程度，确保在有害输入上充分激活以抑制危险生成，同时在良性输入上最小化干扰，保持冻结主干模型的原始性能。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上，CLEAR将HarmBench的攻击成功率（ASR）从32.3%降至0.5%，同时保留了基础模型的大部分效用，在GSM8K准确率上比全局SFT或LoRA高最多7.1个百分点。在广泛使用的安全性和效用基准上的实验验证了该方法的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: CLEAR提供了一种有前景的机制来改善LLM对齐中的安全-效用权衡，通过条件化、连续可调的适配器路由实现精确的安全干预，避免了全局安全调优带来的非必要性能损失。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [238] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AUSO提出了一种统一的技能优化框架，通过渐进式、动作感知的优化过程，让技能从外部监督源逐步转化为内化的决策知识，其利用与否取决于动作层面的益处，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA上持续提升智能体性能和分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未建模技能在智能体策略演变中的生命周期（学习知识→能力形成→选择性调用），要么将技能完全置于模型外部、要么完全内化，或通过噪声较大的任务级成功率在内部化与利用目标间切换。此外，这些设计给同一轨迹中的动作赋予统一重要性，忽视了技能引导可能对部分决策有益而对其他决策产生干扰的问题。

Method: AUSO采用三阶段渐进式优化：(1)训练初期，联合学习教师引导与环境结果，使策略获取基础技能而不丢失任务导向反馈；(2)随后侧重基于结果的策略优化以巩固自主问题解决能力；(3)策略成熟后，在每个采样动作上分别评估技能条件与技能无关两种情境，将动作级信息信号与轨迹结果优势耦合，使受益于技能的动作获得更强更新，有害动作被抑制。强化学习贯穿所有阶段作为共享骨干。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA三个基准上，AUSO相较竞争基线持续提升了智能体的任务表现和分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 技能应随策略演化从外部监督源逐步转变为决策知识，其利用应根据动作层面的实际益处自适应调整；统一的渐进式优化框架优于传统的任务级内化/利用切换策略。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [239] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在生成欧盟合规文档（如数字产品护照DPP和数据保护影响评估DPIA）时，数据提取指令和法规模糊性如何影响生成产出的质量和一致性。实验以人工构建的ground-truth模式为基准对多个模型进行评测，发现法规指引较宽松（如DPIA）时需要更高上下文的提示来维持一致性和完整性，而指引较严格（如数字电池护照DBP）时输出一致性稳定但更容易产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟在可持续性和隐私法规方面处于领先地位，许多法规要求生成合规文档（如ESPR引入的DPP、GDPR要求DPIA）。然而，工业数据格式异构且分散在不同系统中，难以提取为合规格式；DPIA文档则需要跨学科专业知识且无标准化格式。虽然已有研究提出用LLM辅助生成这些合规文档，但数据提取指令和法规模糊性对LLM输出质量的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者通过设置对照实验，比较不同指令指导力度（宽松vs严格）和不同上下文提示条件下，多个LLM模型生成的合规文档质量。以人工构建的ground-truth模式（schema）为基准，对不同模型生成的DPP/DBP及DPIA合规文档进行基准评测，衡量其一致性与完整性。

Result: 实验结果表明：对于指导较宽松的法规（如DPIA的格式要求），需要更高上下文的提示（更丰富的上下文信息）才能维持输出的一致性和完整性；而对于指导较严格的法规（如DBP的格式规范），无论提示上下文如何变化，输出都保持一致，但代价是更高的幻觉率（生成不实内容）。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了LLM在合规文档生成领域的研究空白，揭示了法规指导力度的松紧程度对LLM生成质量的影响呈现差异化模式：宽松指引依赖上下文支撑，严格指引虽一致但风险在于幻觉。这为设计针对不同法规类型的LLM合规生成系统提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [240] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VIALS是一个面向生命科学工作流的视觉问答基准，包含161项科学图像解释任务。研究发现前沿视觉语言模型虽能流畅描述自然图像，但无法准确解释这些科学图像，而具备领域知识的科学家则能轻松完成这些任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专业生命科学工作流程中，科学家经常需要解释各种视觉假象（如凝胶印迹、显微镜图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞术图谱、分子结构等）来指导研究决策。现有基准多基于出版物和教科书中的精美图像，而职场中实际遇到的是未修饰的原始图像。作者旨在评估前沿视觉语言模型在这类真实工作场景中的表现，因为如果AI无法解释这类图像，其在生命科学工作流程中的实用价值将极为有限。

Method: 作者构建了VIALS基准，包含161项视觉问答任务，覆盖生物技术行业实验工作流程中检查的各种类型视觉假象。通过对比前沿视觉语言模型与具备领域专业知识的科学家在这些任务上的表现来评估模型能力。

Result: 研究发现，前沿视觉语言模型虽然能够流畅描述自然图像，但无法准确解释这些科学图像，暴露出领域知识和领域特定视觉推理能力的不足。相比之下，拥有相关领域专业知识的科学家认为这些视觉解释任务非常简单。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在专业生命科学工作流程中的实用性受限，因为这类图像是科学家推理、沟通和决策的核心。要提升AI在这类场景的效用，需要克服领域知识与领域特定视觉推理方面的局限。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [NeuroStrata: An Electroencephalographic Connectivity-Aware Deep Representation Learning Framework for Dynamic Brain Network Analysis of Mental Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20354)
*Sayantan Acharya,Hamzeh Asgharnezhad,Abbas Khosravi,Douglas Creighton,Roohallah Alizadehsani,U Rajendra Acharya*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: NeuroStrata是一个基于时间变化偏定向相干（TV-PDC）的连通性感知深度学习框架，用于EEG精神压力分析，通过预训练CNN/ViT提取深度连通性嵌入，在SAM 40数据集上实现了97.3%的峰值准确率（beta频段，LAION-CLIP-ViT-L14+SVM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG精神压力分类方法依赖静态特征，未能捕捉压力相关神经活动中的时间演化动态连通性模式，缺乏对跨脑区频段特异性定向连接的建模能力。

Method: 使用32通道SAM 40数据集的心算任务EEG信号生成TV-PDC连通性图，经预训练CNN和ViT骨干网络提取深度连通性嵌入，再使用轻量级机器学习模型（如SVM）进行分类；同时评估不同频段（beta/alpha）和不同时间窗口的判别能力。

Result: beta频段连通性判别能力最强，使用LAION-CLIP-ViT-L14骨干加SVM分类器达到97.3%峰值准确率；alpha频段在各类模型配置下表现稳定；连通性分析揭示额叶主导的alpha影响和中央区域整合的beta连通模式；时间评估显示分类性能在中期至后期时间窗口趋于稳定。

Conclusion: NeuroStrata将时变有效连通性建模与深度表示学习相结合，提供了一种可解释且自动化的EEG精神压力分析方法，为理解压力相关的神经动力学提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This study introduces NeuroStrata, a connectivity-aware deep representation learning framework for EEG-based mental stress analysis using Time-Varying Partial Directed Coherence (TV-PDC). Unlike conventional EEG classification approaches based on static features, NeuroStrata models the temporal evolution of frequency-specific directed connectivity across distributed brain regions. EEG signals from the 32-channel SAM 40 dataset recorded during mental arithmetic tasks were used to generate TV-PDC connectivity maps. These maps were processed using pretrained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) to extract deep connectivity embeddings, which were subsequently classified using lightweight machine learning models. Experimental results demonstrate that beta-band connectivity provides the highest discriminative capability, achieving a peak accuracy of 97.3% using the LAION-CLIP-ViT-L14 backbone with a Support Vector Machine classifier, while alpha-band connectivity exhibits consistently stable performance across model configurations. Connectivity analysis revealed prominent frontal-driven alpha influences and centrally integrated beta connectivity patterns associated with stress-related neural dynamics. Temporal evaluation further indicated that classification performance stabilizes in mid-to-late temporal windows, suggesting progressive consolidation of stress-related connectivity signatures. The proposed framework integrates time-varying effective connectivity modelling with deep representation learning to provide an interpretable and automated approach for EEG-based mental stress analysis.

</details>


### [242] [Interpretable Information-Decomposed Brain Graph Learning for fMRI-based Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20380)
*Dengyi Zhao,Zhiheng Zhou,Zihan Wang,Guiying Yan,Xingqin Qi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出IID-GCN框架，利用偏熵分解将静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）的脑区间交互分解为冗余、独特性和协同三个信息图，并通过多通道图卷积网络进行整合诊断。在三个数据集上验证了该方法能捕获传统功能连接之外的互补诊断信息，并揭示了脑疾病重塑功能信息组织而非仅改变连接强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rs-fMRI诊断方法将脑区间关系简化为基于相关性的边权，仅表征共波动强度，无法揭示信息如何在脑区间共享。脑疾病可能不仅破坏连接强度，还会扰乱冗余性、独特性和协同性的组织，因此传统功能连接可能遗漏与疾病相关的信息结构。

Method: 利用偏熵分解（partial entropy decomposition）将rs-fMRI相互作用分解为冗余、独特性和协同三个信息图；随后通过多通道图卷积网络（GCN）整合分解后的图，采用边重标定（edge recalibration）、跨信息交互（cross-information interaction）、ROI注意力读出（ROI-attention readout）和通道注意力融合（channel-attentive fusion）机制。

Result: 在三个数据集上，IID-GCN始终捕获了超越传统功能连接的互补诊断信息，学习到的信息图谱揭示了疾病特异性的冗余、独特性和协同改变模式，表明脑疾病重塑了功能信息组织而非仅改变连接强度。

Conclusion: 信息分解的脑图可作为rs-fMRI诊断的一种可解释表示，为理解脑疾病对信息组织的影响提供了新视角，代码已开源。

Abstract: Resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) has enabled non-invasive mapping of functional brain interactions for computer-aided diagnosis, yet most existing approaches reduce inter-regional relationships to correlation-based edge weights. Such representations capture co-fluctuation strength but obscure how information is shared across brain regions. Because brain disorders may disrupt not only connectivity strength but also the organization of redundancy, uniqueness and synergy, traditional functional connectivity may miss disease-relevant information structures. Here we introduce IID-GCN, an interpretable graph learning framework that decomposes rs-fMRI interactions into redundancy, uniqueness and synergy graphs using partial entropy decomposition. These information-specific graphs separately characterize shared, region-specific and jointly emergent components of brain activity. A multi-channel graph convolutional network then integrates the decomposed graphs through edge recalibration, cross-information interaction, ROI-attention readout and channel-attentive fusion. Across three datasets, IID-GCN consistently captures complementary diagnostic information beyond traditional functional connectivity. The learned information profiles reveal disorder-specific patterns of altered redundancy, uniqueness and synergy, suggesting that brain diseases reshape functional information organization rather than merely changing connection strength. These results establish information-decomposed brain graphs as an interpretable representation for rs-fMRI-based diagnosis. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zdy12/IID-GCN.

</details>


### [243] [Conscious Access as Continuous-to-Discrete Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20723)
*Tianming Yang*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出C/D框架，将意识访问（conscious access）重新概念化为连续与离散两种神经处理体制之间的结构保持翻译计算，从而绕开意识研究的形而上学难题，聚焦于可测试的计算机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意识科学长期受困于"困难问题"的本体论争论，缺乏实证可追踪的研究路径。作者主张转向实用主义视角:不再追问意识在形而上学上是什么，而是问如何将意识访问建模为特定的计算转换，以解决神经科学和人工智能中的现有瓶颈。

Method: 提出C/D框架:大脑存在两个处理体制——System C(分布式感觉-运动网络，运行于连续高维流形)与System D(集中式引擎，围绕离散尺度不变符号组织)。意识访问被定义为两者之间的结构保持翻译:将局域连续状态映射为离散符号标记(前向投影)，再通过逆投影将这些符号落回感觉-运动动力学。由此推导出以表征几何为核心的可测试预测，即可测的从分级相似结构到低维类别等价类的坍塌。

Result: 该框架产出一组统一的、可测试的预测，基于表征几何中可测量的"相似性坍塌"现象，并明确将其与全局神经工作空间理论、整合信息论、预测加工和高阶理论区分开来。此外，该框架为神经符号人工智能提供了原理性的架构参考。

Conclusion: 将意识访问视为翻译性计算，提供了一条实用主义、经验上可操作的路径，能够阐明意识状态的功能作用，而无需解决现象学的困难问题。这与现有主流意识理论形成明确区分，并兼具神经科学与人工智能两个领域的适用性。

Abstract: The scientific study of consciousness frequently stalls on ontological debates regarding the "Hard Problem." This paper proposes a pragmatic pivot. Rather than asking what consciousness is metaphysically, we ask how modeling conscious access as a specific computational transformation may address existing bottlenecks in neuroscience and artificial intelligence. We introduce the Continuous/Discrete (C/D) framework, which holds that the brain implements two distinct processing regimes: System C, a distributed sensory-motor network operating over continuous, high-dimensional manifolds, and System D, a centralized engine structured around discrete, scale-invariant symbols. We argue that conscious access requires a structure-preserving translation between these regimes, which maps localized continuous states onto discrete symbolic tokens, coupled with an inverse projection that grounds those tokens back into sensorimotor dynamics. By formalizing conscious access as this continuous-to-discrete conversion, we derive a unified set of testable predictions centered on representational geometry, specifically, on a measurable collapse from graded similarity structures to low-dimensional categorical equivalence classes. These predictions explicitly differentiate our account from Global Neuronal Workspace Theory, Integrated Information Theory, Predictive Processing, and Higher-Order Theories, shifting the focus from ontological status to computational mechanism. Beyond neuroscience, the framework provides a principled architecture for neuro-symbolic artificial intelligence. We argue that treating conscious access as translational computation offers a pragmatic, empirically tractable pathway forward, clarifying what conscious states functionally accomplish without requiring resolution of the hard problem of phenomenology.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Scalpel3: A High-Performance Data Carving Architecture for Recovery of Fragmented Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20363)
*Karley Waguespack,Samuel Goodwin,George Hendrick,Samuel Hildebrand,Thomas Landaiche,Mingyang Li,Joshua McCain,Tyler Saizan,Jacob Tucker,James M. Ghawaly,Golden G. Richard*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍Scalpel3，一个通用、开源、大规模并行的文件雕刻框架，同时支持连续和碎片化恢复，提供可扩展的高性能架构，使研究人员无需实现多线程后端或I/O基础设施即可开发、测试和部署新的雕刻策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文件雕刻工具（如Foremost、Scalpel v1/2、PhotoRec）能有效处理连续文件，部分工具在简化假设下支持特定格式的碎片恢复，但缺乏一个通用、高性能的公开框架，能够支持跨多种文件类型的大规模碎片恢复策略开发与部署。

Method: Scalpel3的核心贡献是可扩展的高性能架构：新增文件类型通常只需开发单线程的文件验证逻辑，以及可选的块验证或自定义重组逻辑，而并行化、同步和快速I/O由后端自动管理。架构集成了ONNX Runtime，允许学习型分类器参与块验证和重组。此外还引入了持久化检查点、人机交互控制和块级去重等实际功能。

Result: Scalpel3是首个通用、开源、大规模并行的文件雕刻框架，支持连续和碎片化恢复，同时提供持久化检查点、人机交互控制、块级去重以及ONNX Runtime集成等实用特性。

Conclusion: 通过公开此框架，旨在降低实验门槛，帮助将文件雕刻研究成果转化为可供从业者使用的工具。

Abstract: File carving recovers files from raw storage media without relying on filesystem metadata, a key  capability in digital forensics, data recovery, and digital exploration. Traditional tools such as Foremost, Scalpel v1/2, and PhotoRec handle contiguous files effectively, and some support  fragmented recovery for specific formats under simplifying assumptions, but no publicly available tool provides a general-purpose, high-performance framework for developing and deploying  fragmented recovery strategies across many file types at scale. This paper presents Scalpel3, the first general-purpose, open source, massively parallel file carving framework designed to support  both contiguous and fragmented recovery at scale. Its primary contribution is an extensible, high-performance architecture that enables researchers to develop, test, and deploy new carving  strategies without implementing their own multithreaded backends or I/O infrastructure. Adding support for new file types typically requires only development of single-threaded file validation logic and, where useful, block validation or custom reassembly logic -- the Scalpel3 backend manages parallelization, synchronization, and fast I/O automatically. The architecture also integrates the ONNX Runtime, allowing learned classifiers to participate in block validation and reassembly when useful. Beyond raw performance, Scalpel3 introduces practical features for real-world investigations: persistent checkpointing for long-running jobs, human-in-the-loop control allowing operators to monitor progress and redirect effort interactively, and block-level deduplication to shrink the search space. By making this framework publicly available, we aim to  lower barriers to experimentation and help translate file carving research into practitioner-ready tools.

</details>


### [245] [Benchmarking LLM Serving Systems for Agentic AI Workloads with XPerf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20370)
*Michael Wang,Yikang Yue,Shaobo Li,Yirui Eric Zhou,Chen Wang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: XPerf是一个用于对LLM服务系统进行代理式AI工作负载基准测试的框架，通过细粒度trace重放方法最小化工作负载变化，支持多种代理应用的负载重放、性能剖析和系统调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI应用依赖非确定性的LLM输出来指导控制流，导致工作负载模式在不同运行间不可预测地变化，这使得对LLM服务系统进行基准测试变得困难，需要一种能够实现可重复基准测试的方法。

Method: XPerf采用细粒度trace重放方法：从真实代理应用中收集trace，支持合成新工作负载，并在不同LLM服务系统上可重复地重放，同时提供服务系统和硬件的详细性能剖析。框架默认包含8个覆盖不同用例（如编码、深度研究、问答）的代理应用。

Result: 实证研究显示XPerf能够准确重放代理工作负载，提供详细的性能分解，可扩展到更大的服务系统，并有助于服务系统的调试。

Conclusion: XPerf为代理式AI工作负载下的LLM服务系统基准测试提供了有效的解决方案，并计划在GitHub上开源。

Abstract: We present XPerf, a benchmarking framework that load-tests LLM serving systems with diverse agentic AI workloads. It provides detailed profiling of the serving system and hardware, enabling users to identify performance bottlenecks introduced by agentic workloads. Benchmarking LLM serving systems under agentic workloads is challenging - agentic applications rely on nondeterministic LLM outputs to guide their control flow; therefore, workload patterns vary unpredictably from run to run. XPerf minimizes this workload variation with a fine-grained trace replay approach: it enables users to easily collect traces from real agentic applications, synthesize new workloads with various patterns if needed, and reproducibly replay them on different LLM serving systems. XPerf includes eight agentic applications across diverse use cases (e.g., coding, deep research, and Q&A) by default. Our empirical study using these workloads shows that XPerf accurately replays agentic workloads, provides detailed performance breakdowns, scales to larger serving systems, and assists in serving system debugging. We will open-source XPerf on GitHub.

</details>


### [246] [Enabling Memory-efficient Im2win Convolution with Multi-precision Support on GPU CUDA and Tensor Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20725)
*Xiang Fu,Jixiang Ma,Xinpeng Zhang,Peng Zhao,Shuai Lu,Xu Tony Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了im2win卷积范式，使其在CUDA核心上全精度运行、在张量核心上半精度运行，通过引入之字形内存访问和异步数据传输等优化，实现了比CUDA核心实现最高2.8倍、比cuDNN最高1.4倍、比基于GEMM的cuBLAS卷积最高6.4倍的TFLOPS提升，同时内存占用仅为后两者的53%和35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积是深度神经网络中的主要计算瓶颈，其效率高度依赖算法与GPU硬件的紧密集成。现有GPU卷积方法存在内存开销大、缓存利用率差、对不同卷积核大小的适应性有限或数值不稳定等问题，亟需一种通用且高效的卷积实现方案。

Method: 本文在im2win范式基础上进行扩展，设计了新的kernel和优化策略，包括之字形（zig-zag）内存访问模式以提高缓存局部性，以及异步数据移动实现计算与数据加载的重叠，从而充分利用硬件加速的半精度矩阵乘累加（MMA）操作。

Result: 在十二个CNN基准测试中，im2win相比其CUDA核心实现提升最高2.8倍TFLOPS，相比cuDNN提升1.4倍，相比基于GEMM的cuBLAS卷积提升6.4倍；内存使用量分别仅为cuDNN的53%和cuBLAS的35%。

Conclusion: im2win被确立为一个统一、高性能的卷积框架，能够适配现代GPU架构（同时支持CUDA核心和张量核心），在性能与内存效率上均优于现有主流方案。

Abstract: Convolution is a principal computational bottleneck in deep neural networks, and its efficiency depends on tight integration between algorithms and GPU hardware. Existing GPU convolution methods suffer from large memory overhead, poor cache utilization, limited effectiveness across kernel sizes, or numerical instability. This work extends the im2win paradigm -- a universal, memory-efficient convolution method with contiguous memory access for all kernel sizes -- to run efficiently in full precision on CUDA cores and half precision on tensor cores. By introducing new kernel designs and optimizations such as zig-zag memory access and asynchronous data movement, im2win efficiently exploits hardware-accelerated half-precision matrix multiply-accumulate operations. Across twelve CNN benchmarks, im2win achieves up to 2.8x higher TFLOPS than its CUDA core implementation, 1.4x higher than cuDNN, and 6.4x higher than GEMM-based convolution with cuBLAS, while using as little as 53% and 35% of their memory, respectively. These results establish im2win as a unified, high-performance convolution framework for modern GPU architectures.

</details>


### [247] [MEMPOWER: Efficient Power Management with Fine-grained Memory Analysis and Modeling for HPC Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20734)
*Nanda Velugoti,Joseph Manzano,Andres Marquez,Nathan Tallent,Kyle Hale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MEMPOWER是一种基于模型的方法，通过精细刻画并行工作负载的内存访问行为来暴露计算与数据移动之间的不平衡，并自动化地在应用程序二进制中静态插入电压/频率转换指令，从而在HPC基准测试中实现6%至42%的EDP（能耗延迟积）降低，且对执行时间影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高性能计算和云环境中，并行应用的能耗与功率效率管理至关重要。新兴应用不断逼近现代机器的内存墙，计算与数据移动之间的不平衡日益加剧，为智能调节CPU功耗提供了新机会。然而，现有的频率/电压缩放技术无法充分捕捉内存访问行为的细粒度变化，导致计算/数据访问不平衡对系统相关组件不可见，留下了潜在的节能空间未被利用。

Method: MEMPOWER采用基于模型的方法：首先对并行工作负载的细粒度内存行为进行特征刻画，揭示计算与数据移动之间的不平衡；然后利用该特征信息驱动自动化软件框架，通过静态插桩在应用程序二进制中注入由模型确定的电压/频率转换点，以平衡内存访问行为的细粒度变化与硬件转换的开销。

Result: 在多个HPC基准测试上，与标准OS/硬件管理的功耗控制机制相比，MEMPOWER实现了EDP 6%至42%的降低，同时对执行时间的影响极小。

Conclusion: MEMPOWER验证了通过建模和静态插桩技术来暴露并利用计算/数据移动不平衡的有效性，能够在不显著牺牲性能的前提下显著提升并行应用的能效。

Abstract: Managing the energy consumption and power efficiency of parallel applications is a significant issue in both HPC environments and in the cloud. As emerging applications continue to push against the memory wall of modern machines, the growing imbalance between compute and data movement creates new opportunities to intelligently tune CPU power consumption. Unfortunately, existing frequency and voltage scaling techniques do not adequately capture fine-grained changes in memory access behavior, rendering the compute/data access imbalance invisible to the components of the system that could capitalize on it, thus leaving potential power savings on the table. In this paper, we propose MEMPOWER, a flexible, model-based approach to exposing compute/data movement imbalance that characterizes the fine-grained memory behavior of parallel workloads. This characterization then informs our automated software framework which can statically instrument the application binary with model-determined voltage/frequency transitions that balance fine-grained changes in memory access behavior with the costs of hardware transitions. Using MEMPOWER, we demonstrate a reduction in EDP of 6% to 42% on a range of HPC benchmarks with minimal impact on execution time when compared to the standard OS/hardware-managed power control mechanism.

</details>


### [248] [PRICE: Pricing-based Resource Incentives for Quality-of-Result-aware Computing at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20819)
*Uwe Gropengießer,Sebastian Frenz,Max Mühlhäuser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PRICE机制，一种将利用率驱动的价格信号与逐请求质量选择相结合的激励机制，通过在过载时将请求向轻量级执行倾斜，在保持接近容量运行的同时显著提升边缘节点的吞吐量和CPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘节点在容量上天然受限，但许多边缘负载可在运行时通过牺牲结果质量来换取资源效率。现有边缘定价机制将请求视为固定配置提交，很少在过载条件下利用逐请求的质量灵活性，导致无法在过载时最大化节点服务能力。

Method: PRICE是一种激励机制，将利用率相关的价格信号与逐请求质量选择耦合。当利用率上升时，接受价格随之升高，使得资源密集型变体的被选择概率降低，从而将已接受的请求转向更轻量的执行方式，使节点能够在接近容量处服务更多请求。

Result: 在真实硬件上的持续过载评估中，PRICE在已接受吞吐量和CPU利用率方面均优于固定分配和动态定价基线。结果在不同定价函数族、任务持续时间分布和客户端群体下均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 结果质量灵活性是一种强大但未被充分利用的过载管理控制维度，而定价是挖掘该维度的有效机制。

Abstract: Edge nodes are capacity-constrained by design, yet many edge workloads can trade result quality for resource efficiency at runtime. Existing edge pricing mechanisms largely treat requests as fixed-configuration submissions and rarely exploit per-request quality flexibility under overload. We present PRICE, an incentive mechanism that couples a utilization-dependent price signal to per-request quality selection. As utilization increases, rising acceptance prices make resource-intensive variants less likely to be selected, shifting accepted requests toward lighter execution and allowing the node to serve significantly more requests while operating near capacity. Evaluation on real hardware under sustained overload shows that PRICE outperforms both fixed-allocation and dynamic-pricing baselines in accepted throughput and CPU utilization. The results are robust across pricing function families, task-duration distributions, and client populations. Result-quality flexibility is a powerful but underused control dimension for overload management at the edge, and pricing is an effective mechanism to exploit it.

</details>


### [249] [AI Infrastructure in Space: How Far Can We Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21034)
*Qing Li,Qiyang Zhang,Daliang Xu,Tianze Huang,Dingge Zhang,Yihao Zhao,Xiaolong Huang,Jinfeng Wen,Xiameng Hu,Tao Qi,Mengwei Xu,Shangguang Wang,Xuanzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了"太空AI基础设施"的系统愿景，将卫星、轨道网络、地面站和云后端统一视为一个可管理的AI计算层，并通过三颗卫星（BUPT-1/BUPT-2）的在轨案例验证了热、能量等物理约束对AI服务部署与恢复的系统性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星正成为可编程计算平台并承担日益增长的AI工作负载，但在轨期间计算能力、连接性、能量和热余量会随轨道时间动态变化，导致AI服务在发射后难以保持可部署性、可管理性和可恢复性，这是一个亟待解决的系统问题。

Method: 作者首先定义太空AI基础设施的系统层概念，将轨道和物理状态纳入资源模型；然后综合了地面AI基础设施、卫星网络和卫星边缘计算的相关基础，分析了塑造系统设计的物理约束；最后通过三个在轨案例研究（节点级BUPT-1热/能量约束、平台级SateLight应用更新优化、服务级有状态VLM推理恢复）进行实证验证。

Result: BUPT-1遥测显示可用计算容量受热与能量包络限制；BUPT-2上的SateLight将应用更新传输时延平均降低56.54%、最高达91.18%，且更新正确率100%；有状态VLM服务案例表明热中断使执行状态恢复成为一等系统问题。

Conclusion: 这些观测推动了一个面向太空原生资源管理、生命周期支持和跨天地持续AI服务的研究议程，表明需要将物理约束作为系统设计的一等公民来构建太空AI基础设施。

Abstract: Satellites are becoming programmable computing platforms capable of running increasingly demanding AI workloads. This shift raises a systems problem: how can AI services remain deployable, manageable, and recoverable after launch when compute capacity, connectivity, energy, and thermal headroom vary over orbital time? This paper develops a systems vision for AI infrastructure in space. We define it as the systems layer that manages AI capabilities across spacecraft, orbital networks, ground stations, and cloud backends, while treating orbital and physical state as part of the resource model. We synthesize relevant foundations from terrestrial AI infrastructure, satellite networking, and satellite edge computing, and examine the physical constraints that directly shape system design. We further ground this vision in three in-orbit case studies spanning the node, platform, and service levels. Telemetry from BUPT-1 satellite shows that usable compute capacity is bounded by thermal and energy envelopes. SateLight on BUPT-2 satellite reduces application-update transmission latency by 56.54% on average and up to 91.18%, with 100% update correctness. A stateful VLM serving case further shows that thermal interruptions make execution-state recovery a first-class systems problem. These observations motivate a research agenda for space-native resource management, lifecycle support, and sustained AI service across space and ground.

</details>


### [250] [Integrating a Python Dynamical core into ICON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21150)
*Mauro Bianco,Till Ehrengruber,Enrique González Paredes,Andreas Jocksch,Christos Kotsalos,Ioannis Magkanaris,Philip Müller,Edoardo Paone,Mikael Simberg,Hannes Vogt,Jacopo Canton,Yilu Chen,Anurag Dipankar,Nicoletta Farabullini,Michael Jähn,Matthieu Leclair,Ong Chia Rui,Nathan Beech,Nicolas Gruber,Christoph Müller,Daniel Hupp,Xavier Lapillonne*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文展示了将基于Python的ICON动力核心集成到原有Fortran模拟代码中的方法，利用GT4Py DSL和DaCe优化框架，证明Python编排不会引入HPC开销，反而在全局模拟中实现了20-30%的性能提升（相比高度优化的Fortran+OpenACC实现），为气候建模提供了可持续、高效且硬件无关的未来路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统模型向百亿亿次（exascale）计算迁移面临两大障碍：僵化、单一化的Fortran代码库以及维护成本高昂的编译器指令。虽然高层DSL（领域特定语言）提供了解决方案，但往往因集成复杂而失败。作者旨在证明Python可以在不损失性能的前提下无缝集成到传统HPC基础设施中，颠覆"Python编排会引入过高HPC开销"的固有假设。

Method: 作者将Python实现的ICON动力核心集成到原始Fortran模拟代码中，采用GT4Py DSL编写高层Python代码，并结合Data-Centric（DaCe）优化框架进行数据流优化和自动化的核融合（kernel fusion）。该方法从单一的、可移植的Python源代码生成优化的设备代码，替代了硬件耦合的编译器指令（如OpenACC），从而实现了可移植性与高性能兼得。

Result: 在生产级全球模拟中，Python动力核心相比高度优化的Fortran+OpenACC实现实现了20-30%的性能提升；在耦合设置中，总运行时间改善了10%。该结果表明Python编排并未引入预期的HPC性能开销，反而通过先进的数据流优化和自动核融合获得了性能优势。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明Python可以成为全球气候建模的可持续、高效且硬件无关的未来方向。通过DSL加数据驱动优化的组合策略，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下实现高层语言对遗留科学代码的现代化改造，为地球系统模型走向exascale时代提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The transition of Earth-system models to exascale is often hindered by rigid, monolithic Fortran codebases and maintenance-heavy compiler directives. While high-level DSLs offer a solution, they frequently fail due to cumbersome integration. We present the integration of a Python-based ICON dynamical core into the original Fortran simulation code. Leveraging the GT4Py DSL and the Data-Centric (DaCe) optimization framework, we demonstrate that high-level Python can be seamlessly integrated into legacy infrastructure without performance loss. Our results challenge the assumption that Python orchestration introduces prohibitive HPC overhead. In production-grade global simulations, our Python dynamical core achieves a 20--30\% performance improvement over the highly-optimized Fortran+OpenACC implementation, with a 10\% improvement on the total time for a coupled setup. Driven by advanced data-flow optimizations and automated kernel fusion, this approach replaces hardware-entangled directives by generating optimized device code from a single, portable Python source. This work proves that Python can provide a sustainable, efficient, and hardware-agnostic future for global climate modeling.

</details>


### [251] [HIERA: Workload-Aware Planning Across Implementation Spaces for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21157)
*Jinghao Wang,Qiqi Gu,Chenpeng Wu,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HIERA是一个面向GPU内核优化的层次化搜索空间规划框架，通过构造契约增强的任务规格、跨PyTorch算子/CUDA库/自定义CUDA内核选择实现空间，并利用profiling反馈和专家知识指导结构化迭代优化，在KernelBench上相较现有免训练方法在实现有效性、样本效率和优化性能上更强，并与需训练的CUDA-L1相当，在科学计算stencil算子案例中较cuDNN取得1.53x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能GPU内核是现代深度学习和科学计算的基础。随着工作负载日益多样化和GPU硬件快速演进，自动化GPU内核生成与优化变得愈发重要。现有基于LLM的方法通常在固定的实现空间内优化，要么限制优化灵活性，要么牺牲搜索效率。这促使作者提出一个能够动态选择并规划搜索空间的方法。

Method: HIERA采用层次化搜索空间规划框架：(1)构造契约增强的任务规格（contract-augmented task specifications），明确描述任务约束；(2)在PyTorch算子、CUDA库和自定义CUDA内核等多个实现空间中选择合适的实施空间；(3)利用profiling反馈和专家知识指导结构化的迭代细化过程，实现高效且灵活的搜索。

Result: 在KernelBench上的多个工作负载层级和多种基础LLM上，HIERA在整体实现有效性、样本效率和优化性能方面均优于现有免训练方法；与需要额外模型训练的CUDA-L1相比保持竞争力。在科学计算专用stencil算子案例中，实现了较cuDNN 1.53倍的加速，展示了框架在标准机器学习工作负载之外的普适潜力。

Conclusion: HIERA通过层次化搜索空间规划有效平衡了优化灵活性与搜索效率之间的矛盾，证明了在无需额外模型训练的情况下，基于LLM的GPU内核优化可以达到与训练方法相当的竞争力，并且框架具有超越标准机器学习负载的通用性，可推广到科学计算等领域。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels underpin modern deep learning and scientific computing. As workloads become increasingly diverse and GPU hardware evolves rapidly, developing efficient methods for automated GPU kernel generation and optimization has become increasingly important. Existing LLM-based methods typically optimize within a fixed implementation space, limiting either optimization flexibility or search efficiency. We propose \textsc{HIERA}, a hierarchical search-space planning framework for GPU kernel optimization. \textsc{HIERA} constructs contract-augmented task specifications, selects an appropriate implementation space across PyTorch operators, CUDA libraries, and custom CUDA kernels, and uses profiling feedback and expert knowledge to guide structured iterative refinement. Experiments on KernelBench across multiple various workload levels and base LLMs show that \textsc{HIERA} delivers stronger overall implementation validity, sample efficiency, and optimization performance than existing training-free methods, while remaining competitive with the training-based CUDA-L1 without additional model training. A case study on a specialized stencil operator from scientific computing further achieves a \(1.53\times\) speedup over cuDNN, demonstrating the potentiality of the general framework beyond standard machine-learning workloads.

</details>


### [252] [GrAND: GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21163)
*Karthik Venkatasubba,Shivendra Deshpande,Shivram S,Jyothi Vedurada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GrAND是一个面向GPU的原生动态图索引算法集合，用于近似最近邻搜索（ANNS），支持Vamana和CAGRA两种主流图索引的高效动态更新（插入和删除）。通过批处理整合图修复、无锁find-and-replace并行邻接表更新、以及按需构建反向图实现可靠的就地删除，在7个真实数据集和5种流式工作负载上，相比SVFusion和FreshDiskANN-GPU分别提升2.2x-8.7x和6.5x-25.4x的整体吞吐量，同时保持高搜索吞吐量和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ANNS应用面向持续演化的向量集合，需要支持高吞吐搜索的同时处理插入和删除并保持高召回率。然而大多数GPU图索引是静态的或更新支持有限，因为更新涉及邻居发现、反向边创建、剪枝和删除引发的图修复，这些并发操作带来冗余距离计算和共享邻接表的冲突访问；基于后台重建的删除策略会带来大量额外计算、内存消耗并干扰前台查询。

Method: GrAND提出了三个核心技术：1) 批处理整合图修复（batch consolidation），消除冗余的剪枝计算；2) 无锁find-and-replace策略，用于并行邻接表更新；3) 按需构建反向图（on-demand reverse graph），在GPU上精确识别入边，实现可靠的就地删除而不永久复制索引。GrAND将这些算法应用于Vamana和CAGRA两种主流图索引。

Result: 在7个真实世界数据集上、5种流式工作负载下，GrAND相比SVFusion提升整体工作负载吞吐量2.2x-8.7x，相比FreshDiskANN-GPU（其GPU适配版本）提升6.5x-25.4x，同时在高吞吐搜索和召回率方面保持稳定。

Conclusion: GrAND证明了GPU原生动态图索引可以实现高效、可靠的持续更新，通过批处理修复合并、无锁更新和按需反向图等创新设计，在显著提升吞吐量的同时保持了搜索质量，为GPU上ANNS动态更新提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search (ANNS) applications operate over continuously evolving vector collections and require graph indexes that sustain high-throughput searches while incorporating insertions and deletions with high recall. However, most GPU graph indexes are static or provide limited update support. Updates require neighbour discovery, reverse-edge creation, pruning, and deletion-induced graph repair; executing these operations concurrently introduces redundant distance computations and conflicting accesses to shared adjacency lists. Background-rebuild-based deletion further incurs substantial computation, additional memory consumption, and interference with foreground queries.
  We present GrAND (GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search), a GPU-native collection of dynamic-update algorithms for two popular graph indexes, Vamana and CAGRA. GrAND consolidates graph repair across a batch, eliminating redundant pruning computations, and employs a lock-free find-and-replace strategy for parallel adjacency-list updates. For reliable in-place deletion, GrAND constructs an on-demand reverse graph on the GPU, accurately identifying incoming edges without permanently duplicating the index. We evaluate GrAND on seven real-world datasets across five streaming workloads, comparing it against SVFusion and FreshDiskANN-GPU (our GPU adaptation of FreshDiskANN). GrAND improves overall workload throughput by 2.2x-8.7x and 6.5x-25.4x, respectively, while maintaining high search throughput and recall over sustained updates.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.GN'></div>

# q-bio.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [PEN-STACK: A non-fabricating tool layer for language-model agents in genome writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20412)
*Anees Ahmed Mahaboob Ali,Radhakrishnan Delhibabu,Everette Jacob Remington Nelson*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本文介绍PEN-STACK，一个为语言模型智能体提供基因组写入（genome writing）设计任务的开源工具层，通过对每个数量强制溯源验证来消除幻觉（fabrication）。结果显示，在没有工具辅助时，三个模型家族在240项必需数量中捏造了90.8%-98.8%；接入工具后，四项目标审计中模型零捏造。八项预注册声明中有十项未通过，返回机器可读降级标志。结论认为：消除幻觉靠的是grounding（接地/锚定）而非提示词或模型规模，且grounding需要可导入代码作为基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在生物学中被广泛使用，但报告数量时缺乏可验证的来源，且存在未管理的生物安全风险。基因组写入使问题加剧：写入计划必须指定位置、写入酶、货物和递送载体，这些参数都定量且相互依赖，若无集成工具层，智能体只能自行凭空提供这些数值，导致捏造和生物安全隐患。
