Motivation: 无限维输入（如函数型数据）的浅层神经网络的逼近和学习理论尚缺乏系统的构造性保证。已有的无限维通用逼近结果大多是非构造性的，且无法清晰刻画有限观测坐标带来的截断误差、网络宽度以及样本量之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出参数归一化神经字典及关联的加权变差类；将逼近误差分解为坐标截断项与贪心有限宽度项；采用全校正贪心程序进行经验回归，建立与保留分辨率一致的统计复杂度；将框架推广到希尔伯特值响应；以准Polish构造作为动机示例并用合成实验验证。

Result: 获得维度无关的（不依赖输入维度或输出维度）统计保证；逼近误差在加权变差类中有分离结构；经验回归的贪心程序具有均匀（于输入分辨率）的统计复杂度；合成实验验证了理论预测的分辨率、宽度与样本量区间。

Conclusion: 维度无关的统计保证是可行的，但计算可扩展性仍是开放挑战（非凸参数搜索）。该框架为无限维输入的浅层神经网络提供了统一的构造性逼近与学习理论，并明确了统计复杂度与计算复杂度之间的界限。

Abstract: We develop constructive approximation and learning guarantees for shallow neural models with infinite-dimensional inputs observed through finitely many coordinates. The analysis is based on a parameter-normalized neural dictionary and its associated weighted variation class. Within this class, the approximation error separates into a distribution-dependent coordinate-truncation term and a greedy finite-width term. For empirical regression, a fully-corrective greedy procedure yields population guarantees whose statistical complexity is uniform in the retained input resolution. The same framework extends to Hilbert-valued responses without an explicit dependence on the output dimension. The dimension-free statements are statistical, not computational: selecting a new neuron still requires solving a nonconvex parameter-search problem. The quasi-Polish construction underlying recent infinite-dimensional universal approximation results provides a motivating example, and synthetic experiments illustrate the predicted resolution, width, and sample-size regimes.

</details>


### [286] [Scaling Muon for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20818)
*Chenghao Li,Xiao Han,Xinxin Huang,Wei Liu,Boyang Li,Bing Xiao,Heran Zhang,Juanma Perez Rua,Ke Xu,Kangning Liu,Linjun Kuang,Na Li,Tan Wang,Tian Xie,Wei Peng,Yang Pei,Yifan Xu,Yuanhao Zhai,Yuwei Lin,Zhe Wang,Zihao He,Daniel Li,Junbiao Tang,Ziyang Jiang,Dake Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Periodic Row-wise Muon优化器，通过每K步执行一次完整的NS5谱分解更新、其余步骤采用低开销的行向约束更新，并结合分布式协同设计（分片动量直接操作、bucketed all-gather和计算通信重叠），在1.3B-15B规模的Diffusion Transformers上，相比vanilla Muon将优化器时间减少46.9-54.3%、端到端步时间减少15.7-24.3%、逻辑通信量减少66.7%，同时保持或提升生成质量（相比AdamW提升12.9-19.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在平衡奇异方向更新上展现优势，但其在大规模DiT上的扩展行为和端到端效率尚不明确。关键问题在于每步执行5步Newton-Schulz迭代（NS5）以及全动量物化引入了大量计算和通信开销，可能抵消Muon的步效率优势。

Method: 提出Periodic Row-wise Muon：(1) 每K步执行一次完整的NS5谱更新；(2) 其余步骤基于当前动量执行低计算和通信成本的行向约束更新；(3) 分布式协同设计——非刷新步直接操作分片动量，刷新步通过bucketed all-gather和计算通信重叠加速谱刷新。在1.3B-15B DiT规模上系统评估其扩展行为。

Result: 在所有规模下，Muon相较AdamW最佳生成质量提升12.9-19.1%。Periodic Row-wise Muon在1.3B-4B模型上与vanilla Muon质量差距在0.5%以内，在9B规模上反而提升4.5%；优化器时间减少46.9-54.3%，端到端步时间减少15.7-24.3%，逻辑通信量减少66.7%，以33.7-64.8%更少的活跃训练时间达到各自最佳生成质量。

Conclusion: Periodic Row-wise Muon在保持Muon生成质量优势的同时，将其转化为大规模DiT训练的端到端效率提升，证实了其在优化质量和计算效率之间的有效权衡。

Abstract: The matrix-aware optimizer Muon improves large model training by balancing updates across singular directions, yet its scaling behavior and end-to-end efficiency on large Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) remain unclear. We first establish Muon's scaling behavior on DiTs from 1.3B to 15B parameters, showing that its optimization and generative quality advantages over AdamW persist across model scales. However, at scale, the 5-step Newton--Schulz iteration (NS5) performed at every optimization step, together with full-momentum materialization, introduces substantial computation and communication overhead that can offset Muon's step-efficiency advantage. We introduce \emph{Periodic Row-wise Muon}, which performs a full NS5 spectral update once every \(K\) steps and applies a low compute and communication cost row-wise constrained update based on the current momentum at the remaining steps. We further co-design a distributed implementation that operates directly on sharded momentum during non-refresh steps and accelerates spectral refreshes through bucketed all-gather and communication--computation overlap. Across all scales, Muon improves the best observed generative quality over AdamW by 12.9--19.1\%. Compared with vanilla Muon, Periodic Row-wise Muon remains within 0.5\% in best generative quality on the 1.3B--4B models and improves it by 4.5\% at 9B. It reduces optimizer time by 46.9--54.3\%, end-to-end step time by 15.7--24.3\%, and logical communication volume by 66.7\%, while reaching its respective best generative quality with 33.7--64.8\% less active training time. These results show that Periodic Row-wise Muon preserves Muon's generative quality advantage while translating it into end-to-end training efficiency for large DiTs.

</details>


### [287] [Nothing Changed but the Model: CellFill -- Bounded In-Cell Learning for Bit-Identical, Revocable Updates to Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20873)
*Zifeng Liu,Zhiyong Du,Yaxin Lu,Yiming Mao,Zhenhe Wang,Wenqi Shi,Zhengkun Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在模型反量化间隙中学习新知识的方法：冻结4-bit量化发布的整数码和缩放因子，仅将新知识写入每个权重的残差中，该残差严格位于量化决策单元内部。重量化后返回与原发布物逐位相同的工件（机器可验证的保证），更新可通过丢弃残差精确撤销，且漂移有界。作者给出六条命题和三条训练路径，包括CellFill——一种有界的重参数化方法，使不变性从强制的变为结构性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模型更新方式（全量微调、适配器合并、模型编辑）都会替换发布的检查点，从而使得所有基于原始精确比特的评估和缓存失效。作者希望找到一种方式，在不改变已发布工件的前提下注入新知识，保证发布物的位级不变性、可撤销性和漂移有界性。

Method: 方法核心是将学习限定在反量化间隙中：冻结4-bit发布的整数码和缩放因子，只把新知识写入每个权重在量化决策单元内部的残差。提供六条命题和三条训练路径，其中CellFill是有界重参数化方法。经过对比实验验证，在约束密集路径与无约束参考路径的配对种子比较中，该方法取得了几乎相同的表现。

Result: 约束稠密路径在三个配对种子上达到了与无约束参考相当的成绩（58.9 vs 59.3事实召回，配对差-0.5个点，95% CI [-5.0,+4.0]），且在保留集跨域困惑度上更优。对比未合并适配器，投影入单元在每次收敛的运行中减少了跨域遗忘；发散控制实验表明投影是信任区域而非修复。方法迁移到27B混合线性注意力模型（2.4e10约束权重，已验证位级一致），匹配召回时跨域困惑度成本约为1.7B模型的一半。

Conclusion: 精确不变性几乎免费：在保持位级不变的约束下，可以获得与无约束方法相当甚至更好的性能。但知识本身有代价——域内困惑度表面上的"免费午餐"是重放与指标共享语料的伪影。不同方法在匹配重放时，每点跨域困惑度的知识购买量相差三倍，且该排序与召回排序不同。

Abstract: Every way of teaching a deployed language model something new -- full fine-tuning, adapter merging, model editing -- replaces the released checkpoint, and with it every evaluation and cache that referred to those exact bits. We instead learn inside the dequantization gap: with the integer codes and scales of a 4-bit release frozen, new knowledge is written only into the per-weight residual that lives strictly inside each quantization decision cell. Re-quantization then returns the released artifact bit-for-bit, a machine-checkable guarantee; updates are exactly revocable by dropping the residual; and drift is bounded. We give six propositions and three training paths, including CellFill, a bounded reparameterization that makes invariance structural rather than enforced.
  Exact invariance turns out to be nearly free: across three paired seeds the constrained dense path matches an unconstrained reference whose weights provably escape the artifact (58.9 vs 59.3 percent fact recall; paired difference -0.5 points, 95% CI [-5.0,+4.0]), and is better on held-out cross-domain perplexity. Against the natural null hypothesis -- serving the same update as an unmerged adapter -- projecting into the cells reduces cross-domain forgetting in every run that converged, and a diverged control shows the boundary: projection is a trust region, not a repair. What no method escapes is the cost of knowledge itself, and the apparent free lunch of in-domain perplexity improving past the anchor is an artifact of rehearsal sharing a corpus with the metric. Methods differ threefold at matched rehearsal in knowledge bought per point of cross-domain perplexity, a ranking that is not the recall ranking. The method transfers to a 27B hybrid linear-attention model (2.4e10 constrained weights, verified bit-identical), where matched recall costs about half as much cross-domain perplexity as at 1.7B.

</details>


### [288] [Decoupling Policy Extraction for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20909)
*Xuyao Lin,Yixiang Shan,Jinru Duan,Tao Yang,Xinyu Zhao,Runyu Lei,Yiming Zhao,Jiaxin Fan,Zongbao Feng,Peng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的离线强化学习范式——解耦策略提取范式，将策略改进从actor训练中解耦出来：actor仅用于建模行为分布，推理时通过独立学习的critic对多个actor生成的候选动作进行重排序来实现策略改进。实验表明该范式优于行为克隆和传统的联合训练的离线RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线RL方法将actor和critic联合训练，但离线数据固定，actor的策略改进无法产生新数据来验证或纠正critic，导致两个问题：(1) actor更新可能漂移到高价值但分布外（OOD）的动作，放大critic过估计；(2) 保守值估计或行为克隆正则化需要在抑制OOD动作和选择数据支持区域内高价值动作之间进行困难的权衡。

Method: 提出解耦策略提取范式：将策略改进与actor训练解耦。具体地，(1) 训练阶段：actor仅用于建模行为分布（如行为克隆），critic独立学习价值函数；(2) 推理阶段：actor生成多个行为支持的候选动作，由独立学习的critic在这些候选动作中进行基于价值的选择（重排序），从而实现策略改进。

Result: 大量实验表明，解耦策略提取范式在性能上同时优于行为克隆和联合训练的离线RL方法，并且即使使用简单的Q-learning critic也能保持有效性。

Conclusion: 解耦策略提取范式为离线RL提供了一种新的思路：让actor专注于提供行为支撑的动作候选集，而critic负责在候选集中进行价值选择，避免了两者在联合训练中相互干扰的问题，同时有效规避了OOD动作的过估计风险。

Abstract: Offline RL methods commonly jointly train the actor and critic, where the critic is used to guide the actor toward higher-value actions. This coupled learning process is well motivated in online RL, where an improved actor collects new data that can further update the actor and the critic. However, training data remains fixed in offline RL, making actor-side policy improvement unable to generate new data to validate or correct the critic. Moreover, retaining this coupled paradigm leads to two related challenges. Firstly, actor updates can drift toward high-valued but potentially out-of-distribution (OOD) actions and amplify critic overestimation. Secondly, conservative value estimation or behavior-cloning regularization creates a difficult trade-off between suppressing OOD actions and selecting high-value actions within the data-supported region. Motivated by this observation, we revisit the conventional offline RL paradigm and propose decoupling policy improvement from actor training. Specifically, we train the actor solely to model the behavior distribution and perform policy improvement at inference time by reranking multiple actor-generated proposals with a separately learned critic. We refer to this paradigm as the decoupled policy extraction paradigm. Under such paradigm, the actor provides behavior-supported action candidates, while the critic performs value-based selection within this candidate set. Extensive experiments show that the decoupled policy extraction paradigm outperforms both behavior cloning and jointly learned offline RL methods, while remaining effective even with a naive Q-learning critic.

</details>


### [289] [Training, learning and inference: unified dynamics of neural systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20965)
*Mian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了原子生成事实f=(u,tau,omega,z;rho)并编译为生成事实图(GFG)，构建基于GFG的递归科学过程。利用nanoGPT建立统一训练-学习动力学，将训练视为带状态的参数-优化器系统的演化，学习视为分布式功能支持的持续重组。提出三个主坐标（目标边界状态、目标特定更新几何、参数-Adam接收状态）构成二阶预测器，在测试集上取得91.43%准确率和91.49%宏平均召回率。推理被建模为训练-学习动力学的冻结投影，并在ResNet/CIFAR-100和扩散模型/CIFAR-10上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学事实表示缺乏对生成历史的记录与可编译性，难以支持AI原生的递归科学研究。训练与学习过程缺乏统一的数学描述框架，无法从机理上解释能力形成、维持、衰退与恢复等可观察现象。

Method: 提出原子生成事实与生成事实图(GFG)作为AI原生可编译科学事实基板；建立基于GFG的递归科学流程（分析、干预、回放、验证）。将训练建模为带状态与记忆的参数-优化器系统演化（接收状态+有限振幅非线性功能响应），将学习建模为分布式功能支持的持久重组。定义目标边界状态、目标特定更新几何、参数-Adam接收状态三个主坐标构造二阶预测器，并将推理形式化为训练-学习动力学的冻结投影。

Result: nanoGPT上二阶预测器在四类转换的保留运行中达到91.43%准确率和91.49%宏平均召回率；组件门控与回滚实验揭示了注意力实现的因果募集与查询条件支持的非加性组合；ResNet/CIFAR-100与扩散模型/CIFAR-10实验验证了接收状态条件响应、持久支持重组及冻结推理投影的普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的AI原生科学事实表示与训练-学习-推理一体化动力学框架，揭示了训练中有条件功能响应、学习中的持久重组及推理的冻结投影机制，并通过多模型验证表明其超越单一架构的普适性。受控反馈下的双刃强化效应提示需进一步关注训练动力学的潜在不稳定性。

Abstract: We define an atomic generation fact f=(u,tau,omega,z;rho), recording the origin, realized transformation, concrete occurrence, generated result and relation role. Compiled into a Generation-Fact Graph (GFG), these facts provide an AI-native, compilable scientific fact substrate preserving generation histories. We establish a GFG-based recursive scientific process in which analysis, intervention, replay and validation form facts for later cycles. Using nanoGPT, we establish unified training-learning dynamics. Training is the evolution of a parameter-optimizer system with state and memory: each actual training action enters the receiving state and produces a finite-amplitude nonlinear functional response conditioned by that state and target-specific update geometry. Learning is the persistent reorganization of distributed functional support by these responses; capability formation, maintenance, decline or recovery becomes observable when target-specific states are evaluated against their readout boundaries. Three primary coordinates - target-boundary state, target-specific update geometry and parameter-Adam receiving state - yield a second-order predictor operating before post-update outputs are read. On held-out runs, it achieved 91.43% accuracy and 91.49% macro-averaged recall across four transitions. We further establish inference as a frozen projection of training-learning dynamics. Component gating and rollback show causal recruitment and non-additive combination of query-conditioned support formed during training, deriving organizational conditions realized by Attention. Controlled feedback indicates possible double-edged reinforcement effects. ResNet/CIFAR-100 and diffusion/CIFAR-10 experiments confirm receiving-state-conditioned responses, persistent support reorganization and frozen inference projection beyond nanoGPT.

</details>


### [290] [A Critical Audit of Spatiotemporal Forecasting Benchmark Datasets and Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20980)
*Kenneth Martin,Simon Heilig,Asja Fischer,Michel F. C. Haddad,Adam M. Sykulski,Moshe Eliasof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对GNN在多变量时间序列短期预测中常用基准数据集（Chickenpox、PedalMe、WikiMaths、METR-LA、PEMS-BAY）进行统计分析，发现空间无关的线性模型在这些数据集上的表现优于此前报道，质疑了这些数据集的判别可靠性，并提供了统计工具集来识别时空相关性及一阶差分引入的结构性偏差，最终提出减少对这类数据集的依赖、倡导更严谨的统计评估，并通过简单混合模型展示了新方法论。 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN常被用于带空间图结构的多变量时间序列短期预测，但方法创新评估过度依赖有限的基准数据集，且这些数据集上线性基线模型表现出竞争力，引发了对其判别可靠性的质疑，因此需要系统性分析这些数据集以揭示根本原因。

Method: 对基准数据集运用经典时间序列方法进行统计分析，识别显著的时空相关性，揭示一阶差分数据集引入的结构性偏差；基于分析结果提出统计工具集，并构建一个简单混合模型来验证方法论的有效性。

Result: 发现空间无关的线性模型在常用基准数据集上比此前报道的更具竞争力；识别出数据集中的显著时空相关模式；发现一阶差分处理引入的结构性偏差；通过混合模型展示了新方法论可衍生出GNN模型开发的新途径。

Conclusion: 建议减少对这些基准数据集的过度依赖，倡导更严格的统计评估方法；分析结果可用于指导GNN模型的创新开发方式。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are routinely employed for short-range forecasting on multivariate time series with a spatial graph structure. Despite the availability of many alternative datasets, method innovations within this domain are predominantly assessed against a rather limited set of benchmark datasets, most notably Chickenpox, PedalMe, WikiMaths, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY. The evaluation protocols contain baselines spanning from historical averages to classical machine learning approaches. These baselines often show competitive performance compared to GNNs. In the present work, we take a step back and analyse the benchmark datasets via classical time series methods to uncover why spatially-unaware linear models pose a stronger competitor than previously reported, casting further doubt on the discriminative reliability of the aforementioned widely adopted datasets. Our statistical analysis provides a toolset for identifying significant spatial and temporal correlations, while revealing a structural bias introduced by first-order differenced datasets. We therefore recommend reducing the over-reliance on such datasets for method comparison, and instead advocate for more rigorous statistical evaluation. By applying the results of our analysis to a simple hybrid model, we show how our methodology can lead to novel ways of developing GNN models

</details>


### [291] [Jacobian-guided Noise Injection for Quantization Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20988)
*Deepanshu Pandey,Arnav Chavan,Nahush Lele,Sankalp Dayal,Deepak Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Jacobian范数的噪声注入训练策略，用于提升大语言模型在低比特量化下的鲁棒性，在视觉模型和语言模型上分别实现了最高+37%的Top-1准确率提升和40%的相对困惑度改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型量化常受限于自注意力机制对离散化误差的敏感性。现有方法要么依赖启发式策略，要么直接惩罚Jacobian，缺乏理论指导来确定最优的噪声方差，导致量化后的性能下降难以有效控制。

Method: 本文首先从理论上证明softmax算子是量化不稳定性的瓶颈，并建立抑制softmax Jacobian范数可有效约束量化诱导性能退化。基于此提出Jacobian引导的噪声注入训练策略：在注意力logits计算前注入零均值高斯噪声，噪声方差直接由Jacobian Frobenius范数推导而来，从而根据局部注意力敏感性自适应地确定最优噪声水平。

Result: 在SigLIP视觉模型上，ImageNet-1K Top-1准确率相对提升最高达37%；在语言模型的WikiText数据集上，低比特量化设置下相对困惑度改善最高达40%。方法在多种SOTA LLM架构上均优于主流PTQ方法。

Conclusion: Jacobian引导的噪声注入方法为低比特量化下的LLM鲁棒性提供了一种有理论支撑且有效的训练策略，相比启发式方法能更精准地定位最优噪声强度，从而显著提升模型在量化后各任务上的性能。

Abstract: Quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often hindered by the sensitivity of the self-attention mechanism to discretization errors. We identify the softmax operator as a bottleneck for quantization stability due to its sensitivity to outliers and state-dependent Jacobian. We theoretically establish that suppressing the norm of this Jacobian helps in bounding quantization-induced performance degradation. Based on this, we propose Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection, a training strategy that injects zero-mean Gaussian noise into pre-attention logits, with variance derived directly from the Jacobian Frobenius norm. Unlike prior approaches that rely on heuristic or penalise jacobian directly, our method provides a way to identify the optimal noise variance based on the local attention sensitivity. We evaluate the method on SOTA LLM architectures, where it demonstrates improved robustness over popular PTQ methods. Empirical analysis reveals that the proposed method gives up to +37% relative gains on Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K for SigLIP and improves relative perplexity by upto 40% on WikiText for language models in low bit quantisation settings, proving the efficacy of the approach.

</details>


### [292] [Trojaning the Alignment: Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20991)
*Minhua Lin,Zhicheng Gao,Yilong Wang,Hanqing Lu,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAG，一种针对文本属性图上的图基础模型的隐蔽木马攻击框架，通过协调图侧触发器生成与文本侧软提示，使触发图表示和触发文本表示在共享语义空间中向同一目标类别文本区域靠拢，并设计可读触发器节点和结构正则化以保证隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型在文本属性图上通过图-语言对齐实现可迁移图学习，但其后门脆弱性尚不清晰。已有后门攻击主要单独针对图侧或文本侧，无法有效适配图-语言对齐场景：图侧触发器受干净文本语义约束，文本侧触发器无法直接位移被对齐和打分的图表示。同时，TAG中触发器同时暴露为节点文本和局部图结构，不连贯的触发器属性或异常子图容易被检测和过滤。

Method: STAG框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 图触发器生成器与文本侧软提示协同工作，使附有触发器的图表示和触发后的文本表示共同向目标类别文本区域移动；(2) 通过候选检索将触发器节点实现为可读文本，并对触发器子图的局部结构进行正则化，使其保持与原始子图接近，解决TAG特有的隐蔽性挑战。

Result: 在多个TAG数据集和代表性GFM上进行了大量实验，验证了STAG的攻击有效性和隐蔽性。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: STAG证明了在GFM的图-语言对齐接口处成功实施隐蔽木马攻击的可行性，填补了该方向的研究空白，为后续防御研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) align graph representations with language semantics to support transferable graph learning. Despite these advantages, the backdoor vulnerability of GFMs on TAGs remains insufficiently understood, especially under graph-language alignment, where graph and text representations are trained to constrain each other in a shared semantic space. Existing backdoor attacks mainly target either the graph side or the text side, treating the two modalities independently. This makes direct adaptation ineffective: graph-only triggers can be constrained by clean text semantics, while text-only triggers alter the language view but do not directly shift the graph representation being aligned and scored. TAGs also impose a stealth challenge because triggers are exposed as both node text and local graph structure, making incoherent trigger attributes or anomalous subgraphs easy to inspect or filter. In this paper, we propose STAG, a stealthy trojan attack framework designed for the graph-language alignment interface of GFMs on TAGs. STAG coordinates a graph-trigger generator with a text-side soft prompt so that trigger-attached graph representations and triggered text representations move toward the same target-class text region. To address TAG-specific stealthiness, STAG realizes trigger nodes as readable text through candidate retrieval and regularizes the trigger-attached subgraph so that its local structure remains close to the original subgraph. Extensive experiments on multiple TAG datasets and representative GFMs demonstrate the effectiveness and stealthiness of STAG. Our code is available at https://github.com/ventr1c/STAG.

</details>


### [293] [Free-Probability Kernels for Zero-Rollout Hyperparameter Selection in Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20998)
*Sara Malacarne,Andrea Ceni,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自由概率的确定性选择器，用于泄漏线性储层计算（RC）的超参数选择。无需实例化或滚动运行储层，仅通过在短标签引导序列上执行核岭回归即可对候选运行状态进行排序，大幅减少验证回放的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层计算（RC）将固定的循环动力学系统与轻量级训练读取相结合，效率较高，但在超参数选择（如循环增益、输入尺度和泄漏率）时通常需要大量回放（rollout）来调整，导致效率优势被削弱。现有方法依赖海量仿真回放来搜索操作状态，成本高昂。

Method: 利用自由概率理论推导出跨滞后传播系数，该系数概括储层如何混合过去输入。在大宽度极限下，这些系数定义了一个确定性时间核，近似有限储层的特征几何。通过在短标签引导序列上进行核岭回归，无需实例化或滚动储层即可对候选操作状态进行排序，且所选配置可跨宽度迁移。

Result: 在十个合成时间基准上，零回放选择获得平均部署分数0.772，接近穷举仿真搜索的0.774，同时避免156,600次选择回放。在有限回放预算下，所提排序方法在所有测试预算下均表现最强，仅用4.8%的回放成本即达到穷举参考。在四个ETT数据集上，保留五个候选可在三个数据集上恢复穷举操作点；在多变量蜂窝流量预测中，每单元15次回放即达到462次回放穷举参考，并在低预算下优于随机搜索和贝叶斯优化。

Conclusion: 自由概率核可作为确定性代理，用于在验证回放稀缺时选择储层运行状态，兼具高准确度与极低的计算成本。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) couples a fixed recurrent dynamical system with a trained lightweight readout, but this efficiency is partly lost during hyperparameter selection: the recurrent gain, input scale, and leakage rate determine the reservoir's stability and temporal processing regime and are usually tuned through many rollouts. We introduce a deterministic, pilot-informed selector for leaky linear reservoirs followed by coordinate-wise nonlinear features. Free probability yields cross-lag propagation coefficients that summarize how the reservoir mixes past inputs. In the large-width limit, these coefficients define a deterministic temporal kernel that approximates the finite-reservoir feature geometry. Kernel ridge regression on a short labelled pilot sequence therefore ranks candidate operating regimes without instantiating or rolling out a reservoir, and the selected configuration transfers across widths. Across ten synthetic temporal benchmarks, zero-rollout selection obtains a mean deployment score of $0.772$, compared with $0.774$ for exhaustive simulation-based search, while avoiding $156\,600$ selection rollouts. With a small rollout budget, the proposed ranking provides the strongest mean performance at every tested budget and reaches the exhaustive reference using $4.8\%$ of its rollout cost. On four public electricity-transformer-temperature (ETT) forecasting datasets, five retained candidates recover the exhaustive operating point on three datasets. On multivariate cellular-traffic forecasting, 15 rollouts per cell reach the 462-rollout exhaustive reference and outperform random search and Bayesian optimization at low budgets. These results position free-probability kernels as deterministic surrogates for selecting reservoir operating regimes when validation rollouts are scarce.

</details>


### [294] [RODE: A Radial-Orthogonal Decoupled Engine for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21024)
*Guoxiang Xu,Bince Qu,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RODE优化器，将矩阵感知优化的步长解耦为径向（Frobenius范数）和方向（切空间）两个独立分量，各自拥有独立的更新规则和步长。在GPT-2干预实验、两个语言建模任务、两个图像分类任务以及Qwen3.5-9B全参数微调中，RODE均优于Muon及其变体，显著降低损失和模型范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵感知优化器（如Muon）将条件化的矩阵步直接加到权重上，同时改变其范数和方向。但范数决定角向运动、方向学习又驱动范数增长，二者紧密耦合且相互干扰，导致缺乏对更新过程的精细化控制。

Method: RODE将参数更新拆分为两个独立通道：径向通道通过标量规则显式更新矩阵Frobenius范数；方向通道在切空间中执行Newton-Schulz条件化更新。两个通道拥有各自独立的更新规则和步长，从而实现径向与方向动力学的完全解耦。

Result: 在1.5B规模下，直接迁移Qwen2风格LM扫描的学习率时，RODE将损失从Muon RMS的4.145降至3.346，最终全局范数从11964降至2183，固定半径版本进一步提升。在Qwen3.5-9B全参数微调中，RODE在全部四个评估任务上均优于两个Muon变体，并在GSM8K和MATH-500上取得最高平均值。

Conclusion: 将径向与方向动力学解耦是矩阵优化中更有效且更可控的方法，RODE在训练损失、模型范数控制及下游任务性能上全面超越Muon。

Abstract: Modern neural network training increasingly uses matrix-aware optimizers, yet their conditioned matrix step is typically added directly to the weight, jointly changing its norm and direction. This interaction matters because the current norm determines angular motion, while directional learning can drive norm growth and thereby alter later steps. We introduce RODE, which gives the radial and directional components separate update rules and step sizes. RODE explicitly updates the matrix Frobenius norm through a scalar radial rule, while its directional channel performs Newton--Schulz-conditioned updates in the tangent space. Controlled GPT-2 interventions show gains from both direct norm control and RODE's directional update. Across two language-modeling and two image-classification tasks, RODE outperforms both Muon variants in every direct comparison and ends with lower full-model norms. At 1.5B scale, using the learning rate transferred directly from the Qwen2-style LM sweep, RODE lowers loss from 4.145 to 3.346 and final global norm from 11964 to 2183 relative to Muon RMS, with fixed-radius RODE improving further. For Qwen3.5-9B full-parameter fine-tuning, all six optimizers use the same tuning budget and the same formal-training and evaluation settings; RODE outperforms both Muon variants on all four evaluation tasks and attains the highest mean on GSM8K and MATH-500. Thus, decoupling radial and directional dynamics offers a more effective and controllable approach to matrix optimization.

</details>


### [295] [Designing a Robust LLM-Based Evaluation System for Agentic AI in Drug Discovery Through Human Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21057)
*Emma Granqvist,Rocío Mercado,Samuel Genheden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向ChatInvent（阿斯利康部署的智能体药物发现助手）的LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架，定义了四个输出质量评估维度（完整性、相关性、结构清晰度和范围遵循），并通过五名专家标注员的人类对齐研究验证了不同LLM法官的表现，用少样本示例优化后对齐度从0.80提升至0.86，为科学领域智能体系统提供了可复用的人类对齐评估模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM系统在化学和药物发现中正重塑科学工作流，但评估其开放式、工具增强的输出仍是根本瓶颈。基于参考的指标（BLEU、ROUGE）无法捕获语义正确性，专家人工评估又无法跟上系统迭代速度。LLM-as-a-Judge范式虽是可扩展的替代方案，但现有药物发现基准在部署LLM法官时未验证其与人类专家的对齐性。

Method: 首先定义了四个输出质量评估维度（Completeness、Relevancy、Structural Clarity、Scope Adherence）及确定性Tool Call Correctness检查；其次通过五名专家标注员的人类对齐研究，比较Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5和Llama 3.1 70B作为候选法官的表现；再次使用人类标注示例的少样本演示优化最佳法官；最后将优化后的法官应用于70个保留问题以揭示系统局限性。

Result: 少样本优化将法官与人类多数投票的一致性从0.80提升到0.86；对70个保留问题的分析表明，非正式措辞不会系统性降低输出质量，反而由LLM在查询智能体前重写原始问题是有帮助的。

Conclusion: 该框架为科学领域智能体系统提供了可复用的人类对齐评估模板，推动了LLM-as-a-Judge在药物发现等开放科学任务中的可信应用。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems are reshaping scientific workflows in chemistry and drug discovery, but evaluating their open-ended, tool-augmented outputs remains a fundamental bottleneck. Reference-based metrics such as BLEU and ROUGE fail to capture semantic correctness, while expert human evaluation does not scale to the iteration speed these systems demand. The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative, but existing drug discovery benchmarks deploy LLM judges without validating their alignment with human experts. In this work, we present an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation framework for ChatInvent, an agentic drug discovery assistant deployed at AstraZeneca, with four contributions. First, we define four output-quality evaluation dimensions---Completeness, Relevancy, Structural Clarity, and Scope Adherence---alongside deterministic Tool Call Correctness checks. Second, we validate the judge through a human alignment study with five expert annotators, comparing Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, and Llama 3.1 70B as candidate judges. Third, we optimize the best-performing judge using few-shot demonstrations of human-annotated examples, improving alignment with the human majority vote from 0.80 to 0.86. Fourth, applying the optimized judge to 70 held-out questions, we surface concrete limitations and find that informal phrasings do not systematically degrade output quality; if anything, it is helpful to have the LLM rewrite the original question before querying the agent. Our framework provides a reusable template for human-aligned evaluation of agentic systems in scientific domains.

</details>


### [296] [TracingFlow: A Simulation-Free Trajectory Inference Framework Based on Second-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21070)
*Yuhao Sun,Zekun Wu,Zixun Huang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TracingFlow是一种基于Flow Matching的仿真自由框架，将最优传输推广到二阶动力学（Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport, DOAT），通过神经网络回归加速度场来推断连续系统演化，在合成数据和单细胞RNA-seq数据上均优于一阶方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最优传输框架多局限于一阶动力学，假设无记忆的速度场，无法捕捉细胞分化等过程固有的调节动量和时间延迟响应，导致轨迹过度平滑、表达能力受限。

Method: TracingFlow采用仿真自由的Flow Matching框架，推广到二阶动力学。通过神经网络回归加速度场，精确高效地求解DOAT问题，学习底层力场而非仅速度场，并可整合谱系追踪先验信息。

Result: 在复杂的合成数据集和大规模scRNA-seq数据集上，TracingFlow在分布重建精度和轨迹忠实度方面均优于现有方法；整合谱系追踪先验后，能恢复数学最优且生物学合理的动力学结构。

Conclusion: 二阶动力学建模能捕捉高曲率转变和非线性演化，比一阶方法更具表达力，为稀疏时间快照下的连续系统推断提供了更准确和生物学可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring continuous system evolution from sparse temporal snapshots is a key challenge in generative modeling and single-cell omics. While Optimal Transport (OT) is popular, existing frameworks are largely restricted to first-order dynamics, assuming memoryless velocity fields. This limits expressiveness, as first-order systems fail to account for regulatory momentum and time-delayed responses inherent in processes like cell differentiation. Here, we introduce TracingFlow, a simulation-free Flow Matching framework generalizing to second-order dynamics. By using neural networks to regress the acceleration field, TracingFlow provides an exact, efficient solution to the Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT) problem. Unlike first-order methods yielding over-smoothed trajectories, our second-order formulation captures high-curvature transitions and nonlinear evolutions by learning the underlying force fields. Evaluated on complex synthetic and large-scale scRNA-seq datasets, TracingFlow achieves superior accuracy in distributional reconstruction and trajectory faithfulness. Moreover, by integrating lineage tracing priors, it recovers dynamical structures that are both mathematically optimal and biologically plausible.

</details>


### [297] [Causal Modeling of Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes via Adaptive LLM Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21079)
*Kavimayil P. Komarasamy,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,David M. Haas,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号框架，用于生成因果假设，迭代地将LLM的先验知识经验数据评分结合，生成更可靠的因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 妊娠不良结局（APO）如早产和妊娠期糖尿病对母婴有长期影响，但其成因尚不明确。由于数据稀缺和领域知识不完整，纯数据驱动方法失败，LLM输出也不一致。

Method: 将LLM作为自适应提议分布，生成因果假设；对假设在经验数据上进行评分，将高分图反馈到LLM上下文中，引导后续生成朝向更有希望的假设空间。

Result: 在真实临床数据集上评估，方法恢复了所有专家验证的边，并识别出专家未列出的额外合理因果关系。

Conclusion: 该神经符号方法优于纯数据驱动和纯LLM方法，有望为目标干预措施提供新见解。

Abstract: Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes (APOs) such as preterm birth and gestational diabetes can have long-term consequences for both the mother and child, yet an understanding of their causes remains elusive. Causal discovery in this domain is especially challenging due to a paucity of data and incomplete domain knowledge. As a result, pure data-driven methods fail, and Large Language Model (LLM) outputs remain inconsistent or contradictory. We introduce a neurosymbolic framework for generating plausible causal hypotheses that iteratively combines the broad prior knowledge of LLMs with empirical scoring on data. Our method treats the LLM as an adaptive proposal distribution, generating hypotheses that are scored against empirical data; the resulting high-scoring graphs are then used to update the LLM's context, steering subsequent generations toward more promising regions of the hypothesis space. We evaluate our approach on a real-world clinical dataset for modeling APOs and their risk factors, comparing our results against an expert-constructed causal graph. Our method recovers all expert-validated edges and identifies additional plausible causal relations not previously listed by experts, potentially providing new insights for targeted interventions.

</details>


### [298] [FlatLand: Personalized Graph Federated Learning via Tailored Lorentz Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21096)
*Jiahong Liu,Ram Samarth B B,Xinyu Fu,Menglin Yang,Weixi Zhang,Rex Ying,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlatLand是一种新颖的个性化联邦图学习方法，通过在定制洛伦兹空间中嵌入不同客户端数据，利用双曲几何的负曲率特性和类时维度编码客户端异质性，并通过参数解耦策略实现无需客户端相似度估计的直接聚合，在低维设置下表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习（PFL）方法忽略了异构图结构中固有的几何特性。真实世界图普遍具有内在负曲率，而双曲几何天然适配这种结构，但此前的工作未将其系统性地应用于图联邦学习的个性化中。

Method: FlatLand将不同客户端的图数据嵌入到量身定制的洛伦兹空间（双曲几何的一种模型）中。核心策略包括：(1) 利用双曲几何的负曲率特性适配真实图的拓扑结构；(2) 利用洛伦兹空间的类时维度编码客户端特异性异质性；(3) 采用参数解耦策略，将异质性信息（类时参数）与共性知识（类空参数）分离，实现无需客户端相似度估计和额外计算模块的直接聚合。

Result: 在多样化的联邦图学习任务上，FlatLand取得了优越的性能，尤其在低维嵌入设置下表现突出，验证了其在高度异构客户端数据场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: FlatLand证明了将双曲几何与个性化联邦学习相结合的有效性，其参数解耦设计为图联邦学习中的异质性处理提供了一种简洁且高效的解决方案，避免了传统方法中复杂的客户端相似度估计负担。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaborative training, but highly heterogeneous client data remain challenging, especially in graph federated learning where clients possess structurally diverse graphs. Existing personalized federated learning (PFL) methods ignore the intrinsic geometric properties of diverse graph structures. We propose FlatLand, a novel personalized federated learning method that embeds different clients' data in tailored Lorentz space of hyperbolic geometry. Our key insight is that hyperbolic geometry naturally accommodates the intrinsic negative curvature prevalent in real-world graphs, while the time-like dimension in Lorentz space provides a principled way to encode client-specific heterogeneity. We develop a parameter decoupling strategy that separates heterogeneous information (captured in time-like parameters) from common knowledge (preserved in space-like parameters), enabling direct aggregation without requiring client similarity estimation and extra calculation modules. Empirical results on diverse federated graph learning tasks demonstrate that FlatLand achieves superior performance, particularly in low-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [299] [COEC: Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation for Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21142)
*Peiqi Yu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COEC（Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation），一种无需训练的剪枝后补偿框架，通过对保留权重施加交替的左右正交旋转和奇异值重缩放来恢复结构化剪枝LLM的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练免费的补偿方法仅使用加性偏置或输出侧单一正交旋转来修正剪枝误差，这些方法保留了权重的输入奇异框架不变，限制了保留权重在列移除后的适应能力，导致精度损失无法充分恢复。

Method: COEC框架在保留权重上交替施加左右正交旋转：右旋转在约简Stiefel流形上优化，左旋转保持权重几何结构；使用广义交叉验证为每层选择正则化强度以重缩放奇异值；通过缩减校准Gram矩阵来降低高能量激活方向的支配性；引入对齐惩罚以保持相邻注意力投影之间的几何关系。所有组件仅使用小校准集的二阶统计量，无需反向传播或重训练。

Result: 在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5模型族的多项结构化稀疏水平上，COEC在每个模型上均改善了困惑度，在大多数设置中提升了零样本准确率，且在高稀疏度下收益更大，优于现有补偿方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，剪枝后补偿能够部分恢复列移除导致的性能损失，COEC作为与剪枝准则无关的训练免费补偿框架，可广泛应用于多种结构化剪枝方法。

Abstract: Structured pruning reduces the size and inference cost of large language models (LLMs) by removing weight columns, but the resulting output error can degrade accuracy. Existing training-free compensation methods use an additive bias or a single orthogonal rotation on the output side of the retained weight. These corrections leave its input singular frame unchanged and therefore limit how the retained weight can adapt after column removal. We propose COEC (Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation), a training-free compensation framework that applies alternating left and right orthogonal rotations to the retained weight. The right rotation is optimized on a reduced Stiefel manifold, while singular values are rescaled using generalized cross-validation to select the regularization strength for each layer. COEC further tempers the calibration Gram matrix to reduce the dominance of high-energy activation directions and introduces an alignment penalty that preserves the geometric relation between adjacent attention projections.All components use second-order statistics from a small calibration set and require neither backpropagation through the LLM nor retraining of the model parameters. COEC is independent of the column pruning criterion and can be applied to multiple structured pruning methods. Experiments on the Llama-3, Llama-3.1, and Qwen2.5 model families across multiple structured sparsity levels show that COEC improves perplexity on every model and zero-shot accuracy in most settings over existing compensation methods, with larger gains at higher sparsity. These results show that post-pruning compensation can recover part of the performance lost to column removal.

</details>


### [300] [Capturing Cardiac Cyclicity through Phase-Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21147)
*Blaise Delaney,Dominic Dootson,Juan Jose Juan Castella,Salil Patel,Andrew Pfaff,Yuji Xing,Jonny Hancox,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于心搏周期对称性的自监督表示学习方法Winder，通过相位等变目标将表征组织为相位不变坐标和相位旋转谐波子空间，其传输算子是封闭解析形式而非学习得到的。在PTB-XL数据集上，Winder以约1M参数规模达到了与最先进自监督方法相当的诊断准确率，同时具有清晰的相位等变潜在几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理过程的循环结构为自监督表示学习提供了天然先验，但现有方法未充分利用心搏周期这一明确定义的周期性。作者希望显式编码心搏相位对称性，以保留诊断信息的同时获得参数高效且可解释的潜在表征。

Method: 推导了相位等变自监督目标，并引入Winder联合嵌入架构。该架构将表征组织为相位不变坐标和相位旋转谐波子空间，其传输算子固定且具有封闭形式，直接从周期几何结构推导而来而非学习得到，不增加任何参数。

Result: 在PTB-XL数据集上采用冻结线性探针协议评估，Winder以约1M参数规模达到最先进自监督方法报告的诊断准确率范围，并展现出相位等变的潜在几何结构。

Conclusion: 显式编码心搏相位对称性能够保留诊断上有用的信息，同时产生可读、参数高效且直接联系可测量生理量的潜在几何结构。

Abstract: The cyclic structure of physiological processes offers a natural prior for self-supervised representation learning, and the cardiac cycle provides a particularly well-defined setting in which to exploit it. We derive a phase-equivariant self-supervised objective and introduce Winder, a joint-embedding architecture that organises representations into phase-invariant coordinates and phase-rotating harmonic subspaces. Its transport operator is fixed and closed-form, derived from the cycle's geometry rather than learned, and adds no parameters. Evaluated on PTB-XL under a frozen linear-probe protocol, Winder attains diagnostic accuracy within the range reported by state-of-the-art self-supervised methods at a ~1 M parameter footprint, while exhibiting phase-equivariant latent geometry. These findings demonstrate that explicitly encoding cardiac-phase symmetry can preserve diagnostically useful information while yielding a latent geometry that is legible, parameter-efficient, and directly tied to a measurable physiological quantity.

</details>


### [301] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Constructing Planning Domain Models from Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21186)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Michael Skinner,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NSPIN是一个神经符号框架，用于从非结构化临床叙述中归纳概率规划领域模型。它利用预训练LLM从原始文本中提取并补全结构化事件序列，然后归纳PPDDL模型，并通过LLM提出的修订建议结合经验验证来优化前提条件。在9位外科医生的2,660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术记录上评估，模型能泛化到未见过的记录，且专家临床审查表明其归纳的知识与外科实践高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜阑尾切除术等外科手术是复杂且高风险的流程，但其工作流的正式化用于决策支持仍具挑战。由于缺乏结构化事件数据以及临床叙述中普遍存在隐式动作，传统的经验符号方法和LLM都无法单独充分解决该问题。

Method: NSPIN采用神经符号混合方法：(1) 使用预训练LLM从非结构化临床文本中提取并补全结构化事件序列；(2) 基于提取的事件序列归纳概率规划领域模型（PPDDL）；(3) 由LLM提出前提条件的修订建议，并通过经验验证指导优化。

Result: 在9位外科医生撰写的2,660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术记录上评估，NSPIN生成的模型能够泛化到未见过的临床记录；专家临床审查表明其归纳的知识与外科实践大体一致。

Conclusion: NSPIN成功证明了将LLM的语义理解能力与传统符号归纳方法相结合的神经符号框架，能够有效地从非结构化临床叙述中诱导出可泛化且符合临床实践的规划领域模型，为手术流程的决策支持提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

</details>


### [302] [Tydra: An Efficient Hybrid Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21199)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tydra是一种用于表格数据上下文学习的混合Transformer-SSM架构，通过交错叠加注意力层和SSM层，在保持接近TabPFN预测性能的同时将推理时间减少30%，并优于约10倍规模的Hydra模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的表格基础模型（如TabPFN）预测性能强但计算复杂度随上下文长度呈二次增长；而基于SSM的次二次复杂度替代方案（如Hydra）虽然效率高但牺牲了精度。现有方法难以在精度和效率之间取得良好平衡，因此需要探索能兼顾两者的架构。

Method: 提出Tydra，一种混合Transformer-SSM架构，将注意力层与SSM层交错组合（interleaving）应用于表格数据的上下文学习场景，以结合注意力机制的强建模能力与SSM的次二次计算效率。

Result: 在30个OpenML数据集上，Tydra相比TabPFN将推理时间降低30%且保持了大部分预测性能；同时优于参数规模约大10倍的Hydra模型，且推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明混合架构（Transformer + SSM）是构建表格基础模型的一个有前景的研究方向，能够在不显著牺牲精度的情况下大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [303] [Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine: Learned Physiological Time-Series Imputation under Realistic Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21207)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Haochen Zhang,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAIR框架，一种两阶段的生理时间序列缺失值插补方法，通过"先插值后细化"策略结合双向GRU插补器和Transformer细化器，在MCAR/MAR/NMAR三种缺失机制下的连续血糖监测和ICU动脉血压数据上均取得最优精度，且能同时保持插补准确性和临床负担指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理时间序列插补对临床数据至关重要，但现有学习方法在真实临床信号上表现不佳，甚至被简单线性插值超越。原因是生理缺失具有两个独特特性：缺失可能发生在临床极端时刻而非典型时刻，且缺失长度可跨越多个数量级，而这些特性被通用插补器忽略。

Method: CAIR采用两阶段框架：(1) 首先使用双向GRU插补器学习粗糙的基线曲线；(2) 然后通过Transformer细化器对自身估计进行三次连续纠正，逐步逼近生理真实性。二者在信号无关的随机缺口课程（curriculum）下联合训练。

Result: 在AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III ICU动脉血压数据上，按缺口长度和缺失机制分层评估，CAIR在所有机制下均为最准确方法。相对最强基线，优势随难度增加而扩大：MCAR下提升9%，值依赖缺失（NMAR）下提升19%。此外证明低重建误差不能保证临床负担指标的恢复，CAIR是唯一在两个维度上都表现优异的方法。

Conclusion: 生理时间序列的插补需要专门设计而非套用通用方法。CAIR通过"先插值后细化"的迭代纠正策略有效应对了缺失的极端值偏差和缺口长度跨度大两大挑战，并且同时兼顾统计精度与临床可用性，验证了临床导向评估的必要性。

Abstract: Imputing physiological time series (arterial blood pressure, blood glucose, etc.) is essential for addressing the missingness that pervades clinical data. Yet modern imputation methods perform poorly in this domain: a recent benchmark found that simple linear interpolation outperformed every learned imputer on real-world clinical signals with realistic gaps. We show that this reflects two properties of physiological missingness that generic imputers ignore: gaps may occur when the signal is clinically extreme rather than typical, and gap lengths can easily span orders of magnitude. To this end, we introduce Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine (CAIR), a two-stage framework for physiological time-series imputation. Our key motivation is to learn a coarse base curve and then repeatedly correct it toward physiological realism, rather than predict a gap in a single pass. Consequently, CAIR couples a bidirectional-GRU interpolator with a Transformer refiner that corrects its own estimate over three successive passes, trained jointly under a broad, signal-agnostic random-gap curriculum. We evaluate imputers stratified by gap length and missingness mechanism (MCAR, MAR, NMAR) rather than by a single average, and CAIR is the most accurate under every mechanism on continuous glucose monitoring (AI-READI) and arterial pressure in intensive care (MIMIC-III). Its margin over the strongest baseline grows with difficulty, from 9% under MCAR to 19% under value-dependent dropout, where generic learned imputers are weakest. We further show low reconstruction error alone does not recover the burden metrics clinicians act on: interpolants matching CAIR's error fail to preserve those metrics, imputers that recover them are far less accurate, and CAIR alone ranks among the best on both axes.

</details>


### [304] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-C 是一种可审计的、严格先验秩校准的多流遥测异常检测器，用于检测每个单独流都正常但整体联合异常的情况。它通过三个窗口通道（局部归一化残差和、高斯copula形式依赖对比、AR(1)创新项）的Fisher聚合来生成窗口级排名。在英国电网六条时间序列上的评估显示其对Storm Atiyah的检测主要来自局部通道而非copula通道，并存在记录规则饱和等解释性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作遥测数据可能整体联合异常，而每个单独流仍在其常规范围内。传统逐流检测无法捕获跨流联合异常模式，需要一种对齐多流遥测的联合异常检测方法。此外，现有方法缺乏可审计性和严格的先验校准，导致检测结果难以追溯和验证。

Method: TRACE-C 使用同区间滚动中位数/MAD残差作为基础，构建三个窗口通道：(1) 最大归一化局部和通道，捕捉局部异常；(2) 基于稳健z残差的高斯copula形式依赖对比通道，捕捉跨流相关性变化；(3) 最差标准化AR(1)创新项通道，捕捉时序动态异常。三个通道的秩通过Fisher方法聚合，并与早期聚合值进行排名比较，实现联合异常检测。

Result: 在英格兰电网六个数据流上，使用2019年1-4月拟合、7-12月开发验证、2020年作为冻结保留集的评估中，TRACE-C将Storm Atiyah排在2019年测试窗口首位，但通道消融显示该排名主要来自局部通道（copula通道单独仅排第59位）。9月8日的短时频率事件融合检测器排名143位，远低于时序通道单独排名（40位），而重建基线将其排第一。2020年无窗口被选中，归因于记录规则饱和而非无事件年份。

Conclusion: TRACE-C提供了一种可审计的严格先验秩校准多流联合异常检测框架，但存在三个关键解释限制：所得p值是选择量而非事件概率；copula形式通道并非真正的copula密度（未应用概率积分或正态得分变换）；经验秩计数是诊断量而非覆盖或FDR证明。所有图表均基于已提交的机器可读报告生成，确保可复现性。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [305] [ConceptTS: LLM-Guided Concept Bottlenecks for Interpretable Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21277)
*Yichen Jiang,Yueqiao Chen,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptTS是一个可解释的多变量时间序列预测框架，通过大语言模型自动生成命名概念和可执行的标注规则，将概念组织为三个互补瓶颈（历史上下文、局部预测区间、完整预测范围），并通过共享解码器组合概念激活来生成预测，在保持与黑盒模型相当精度的同时提供概念级可解释性和干预能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多变量时间序列预测模型虽然能建模复杂的时间与跨变量依赖关系，但其不透明的表示方式使预测结果难以理解，限制了在需要理解并评估预测依据的实际场景中的应用。研究者希望在不牺牲预测精度的前提下，提升模型的可解释性。

Method: ConceptTS使用大语言模型提出任务相关的命名概念并生成可执行的标注规则，无需昂贵的人工概念标注即实现直接监督。概念被组织为三个互补的瓶颈层：历史上下文、局部预测区间和完整预测范围，分别描述不同时间维度的信息。共享解码器将三个瓶颈层预测的概念激活表示进行组合以构建最终预测，使决策过程显式化，并支持直接的概念级干预。

Result: 在北京多站点空气质量数据集上的实验表明，ConceptTS在预测精度上与强黑盒基线模型相当，同时生成语义上有意义的概念激活。

Conclusion: ConceptTS证明了大语言模型驱动的概念化方法可以有效弥合可解释性与预测精度之间的差距，为多变量时间序列预测提供了既准确又可理解的解决方案，并支持概念层面的模型干预。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multivariate time-series forecasters can model complex temporal and cross-variable dependencies, yet their opaque representations provide limited insight into why a particular forecast is produced. This lack of transparency restricts their use in settings where practitioners must understand and assess the factors underlying a prediction. We introduce ConceptTS, an interpretable forecasting framework that organizes its predictions around named, human-readable concepts. ConceptTS uses a large language model to propose task-relevant concepts and generate executable labeling rules, translating the language model's domain knowledge into direct supervision without costly manual concept annotation. The proposed concepts are organized into three complementary bottlenecks that describe the historical context, local forecast intervals, and the full forecast horizon. A shared decoder combines representations derived from their predicted activations to construct the forecast, making the model's decision process explicit and supporting direct concept-level interventions. Experiments on the Beijing Multi-Site Air Quality dataset show that ConceptTS achieves accuracy competitive with strong black-box baselines while producing semantically meaningful concept activations.

</details>


### [306] [SPARCL: Spectral Partitioned Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21307)
*James Hartley,Zeropy Surio,Daniel Whitmore,Hannah Clarke,Thomas Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARCL，一种谱分区解析持续学习方法，通过将运行自相关分解为高能核心和残差互补部分，冻结旧类在核心子空间中的分类器分量，仅用递归最小二乘更新残差块，从而从根本上解决解析持续学习中因频谱干扰导致的旧类漂移问题。在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A上使用冻结的ViT-B/16协议，SPARCL显著缩小了经典解析学习器与强大表示匹配方法之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析持续学习虽以闭式岭回归取代迭代优化，避免了随机梯度覆盖问题，但旧类上仍存在漂移。传统"梯度覆盖"叙事无法解释这一现象，因此需要揭示其根本原因，并提供针对性解决方案。

Method: 将运行自相关算子分解为高能核心子空间和残差互补子空间；冻结旧类分类器在核心子空间的分量；仅通过递归最小二乘更新残差块，并可选地进行残差随机投影扩展；保证核心子空间中旧类对数贡献的稳定不变性。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A基准上，采用冻结的ViT-B/16协议，SPARCL几乎完全弥合了经典解析学习器与强表示匹配方法之间的性能差距，同时与Fly-CL等稀疏特征去相关方法保持互补关系。

Conclusion: 频谱干扰（而非梯度覆盖）是解析持续学习中旧类漂移的根本原因；SPARCL通过谱分区与核心子空间冻结机制实现了有效的闭式更新，在保持简洁性的同时达到了与强表示匹配方法相当的性能，为解析持续学习提供了新的理论视角和实用方案。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning has emerged as a strong exemplar-free alternative to gradient-based class-incremental learning because it replaces iterative optimization with closed-form ridge updates. Yet the usual forgetting narrative, centered on stochastic gradient overwriting, does not explain why analytic methods still drift on old classes despite exact recursive solvers. We identify the culprit as spectral interference: the joint ridge classifier for all tasks shares the inverse autocorrelation operator $(R+λI)^{-1}$, so incoming task samples that load onto old dominant eigendirections dilute the spectrum and perturb old-class logits even when old labels are never revisited. Based on this view, we propose SPARCL, a spectral partitioned analytic continual learner that decomposes the running autocorrelation into a high-energy core and a residual complement, freezes old-class classifier components in the core subspace, and updates only the residual block through recursive least squares with an optional residual random-projection expansion. This yields a simple closed-form update with a provable invariance guarantee for the core contribution of old logits. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, ImageNet-R, and ImageNet-A under a frozen ViT-B/16 protocol, SPARCL closes most of the gap from classical analytic learners to strong representation matchers, while remaining complementary to sparse feature-decorrelation approaches such as Fly-CL.

</details>


### [307] [Rethinking Expressivity and Efficiency in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21308)
*Zeyun Zhong,Joya Chen,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Juergen Gall,Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出E²-TTT方法，通过推导封闭形式的区间状态转移，在保持逐token更新动力学表达性的同时实现完全并行的chunk级训练，在语言建模、上下文检索和长度外推上超越现有TTT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTT方法在逐token更新的表达性与chunk级近似的硬件效率之间存在权衡，需要一种方法同时兼顾两者。

Method: 在标准的chunk起始权重梯度近似下，推导出精确复现chunk结束快速权重和动量状态的封闭形式状态转移，从而实现完全并行的chunk级训练，同时保留逐token递归更新规则的时间结构。

Result: 训练了高达1.3B参数的模型，在语言建模上与现有TTT和混合注意力基线持平，在上下文检索上超越它们；在长度外推上，在8倍训练上下文长度的passkey测试中保持90%以上准确率；训练吞吐量与高效chunk方法相当。

Conclusion: E²-TTT成功调和了表达性与效率之间的矛盾，展示了TTT在处理长上下文任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) enables long-context processing via continuous weight updates during inference, but current methods struggle to balance the expressivity of per-token update dynamics with the hardware efficiency of chunk-wise approximations. We propose E$^2$-TTT (Expressive and Efficient TTT) to bridge this gap. Under the standard approximation of taking gradients at the chunk-start weights, we derive a closed-form state transition that exactly reproduces the chunk-end fast-weight and momentum states of the per-token recurrence. This enables fully parallelized chunk-level training while preserving the temporal structure of the update rule that prior chunk-wise methods discard. We validate E$^2$-TTT by training models up to 1.3B parameters from scratch. It performs on par with previous TTT and hybrid attention baselines in language modeling while outperforming them on in-context retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced in length extrapolation: on the standard ``Needle in a Haystack'' passkey test, it retains over 90% accuracy at $8\times$ the training context length. Meanwhile, E$^2$-TTT can match the training throughput of efficient chunk-wise methods, demonstrating that it effectively reconciles expressivity with efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/zeyun-zhong/E2-TTT.

</details>


### [308] [Time-Aware Tranformer-Based Prediction Model for AECOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21324)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Jiacun Wang,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间感知Transformer的AECOPD（慢性阻塞性肺疾病急性加重）预测模型，仅使用家用呼吸机的呼吸数据，通过捕获症状及其时间进展来生成患者表示，相比传统方法在多个分类任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD症状变化迅速，需要具有时间敏感性的预测模型。然而现有机器学习模型多依赖临床和实验室数据，不可避免地存在延迟。为了确保AECOPD的及时发现并最小化延迟，需要利用家庭监测场景中日常使用呼吸机产生的呼吸数据进行建模。

Method: 提出Time-Aware Transformer模型，使用时间感知的Transformer架构处理家用呼吸机的呼吸数据，通过捕获症状及其时间进展模式来生成有意义的患者表示，用于AECOPD预测任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，该Time-Aware Transformer方法在多个分类任务中优于传统方法，展现了提升AECOPD预测准确率的潜力。

Conclusion: 基于时间感知Transformer的AECOPD预测模型能够有效利用家庭呼吸机数据实现及时预测，优于传统方法，有望增强AECOPD预测的准确性和时效性。

Abstract: The rapid symptom change of Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) makes it critical to have time-sensitive prediction models. However, most current machine learning models studying AECOPD use clinical and laboratory data, which will inevitably cause latency. To ensure timely detection of AECOPD and minimize latency, this paper focuses on home monitoring scenarios where only respiratory data from daily-use ventilators is available. We introduce a Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD prediction model, which generates meaningful patient representations using the Time-Aware transformer to capture the symptoms and their temporal progression in ventilator data. Our experimental results demonstrate that our Time-Aware transformer-based approach outperforms traditional methods in multiple classification tasks, highlighting its potential to enhance AECOPD prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [309] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究短观测价格面板数据中"观测多但价格变动少"带来的推断后果。通过校准至稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程，区分了给定价格轨迹条件下的不确定性（条件不确定性）与不同价格轨迹间的估计误差变异（跨设计变异）。基线模拟中，跨设计变异占梯度提升规范估计误差方差的97.6%。实证发现跨设计离散度可由σ̂ ≈ 0.182V^(-0.271)描述（V=变动次数×幅度平方）；共享价格路径的区域不产生独立轨迹，而独立定价单元的平均化以标准平方根速率降低离散度；Paule-Mandel方差分量估计将经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。总体含义是应转向设计能产生独立识别变异的DGP，而非仅依赖固定被动面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短观测面板可能包含大量观测值但只有少数不同的价格变动，这种"观测丰富但变动稀疏"的区分在推断上具有重要意义。传统的面板内重抽样方法仅利用单条实现轨迹的信息，无法识别跨设计（不同轨迹间）的方差成分，导致推断可能严重低估不确定性。

Method: 采用校准至稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程，将估计误差的不确定性分解为两部分：（1）给定已实现价格轨迹的条件不确定性；（2）同一定价过程生成的不同备选轨迹之间的估计误差变异。通过基线模拟、经验关系拟合（σ̂ ≈ 0.182V^(-0.271)）、区域/单元聚合分析以及Paule-Mandel方差分量估计来系统研究跨设计变异的影响。

Result: （1）基线模拟中跨设计变异占梯度提升规范估计误差方差的97.6%；（2）跨设计离散度遵循幂律关系σ̂ ≈ 0.182V^(-0.271)；（3）共享价格路径的区域不能创造独立价格轨迹，而独立定价单元的平均化按标准平方根速率降低离散度；（4）使用Paule-Mandel方差分量估计可将经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。

Conclusion: 面板内重抽样程序存在根本性局限：它们无法识别跨设计变异这一占主导地位的不确定性来源。更广泛的含义是，实证研究应转向设计能产生独立识别变异的DGP，而非仅依赖固定被动面板。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [310] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了自我精炼（self-refinement）流水线中各阶段（生成、批评、修订）对模型规模的依赖关系，基于5个基准、Qwen3的6种规模和Gemma 3的4种规模得出：更大的生成器和修订器能提升性能，但过小的修订器反而有害；批评器规模对性能影响极小，但包含小型批评器仍优于完全省略批评环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我精炼范式（生成-批评-修订）广泛用于改进LLM生成，但现有研究大都把模型规模当作实现细节而非研究对象，未系统考察模型大小对各阶段的影响，可能导致计算资源浪费。作者旨在回答：有效的自我精炼是否需要三个环节具备同等能力的模型？

Method: 采用逐阶段（stage-wise）的模型规模对照实验设计，在5个不同领域的基准上，使用Qwen3的6种规模变体和Gemma 3的4种规模变体，分别操纵生成器、批评器和修订器的模型大小，系统测量各阶段规模变化对自我精炼整体性能的影响。

Result: （1）更大的生成器和修订器通常能提升流水线整体性能，但修订器规模不足时甚至会损毁性能；（2）批评器规模对性能高度不敏感，然而即使采用小型批评器，其效果也一致优于完全省略批评环节。

Conclusion: 模型容量不应在自我精炼流水线中各阶段均匀分配，不同阶段展现出截然不同的规模缩放特性。这一发现为设计计算效率更高的多阶段语言模型系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [AEGIS: Preventing Cross-Domain Resource Abuse in MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20481)
*Shriti Priya,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS，一个基于策略执行的组件，利用大语言模型的推理能力分析、分类并归一化异构MCP（模型上下文协议）工具调用，为不同模态（文本、图像、视频、位置）和跨域工具定义细粒度的资源滥用防护策略，并与Open Policy Agent和ContextForge AI Gateway集成，检测和缓解恶意或过度的工具调用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议标准化了LLM与外部系统的交互方式，但攻击者或恶意代理可利用各类MCP工具的机制来降低基于代理的应用服务质量，例如请求过大的搜索半径或过长的视频导致后端过载甚至拒绝服务（DoS）。多模态和跨域工具的不同请求模式和参数使得制定既通用又精确的资源约束策略非常困难。

Method: AEGIS通过以下方式实现：1）利用大语言模型的推理能力分析、分类和归一化多样的工具调用，转换为统一的策略友好表示；2）与管理层提供可理解的细粒度安全防护定义；3）与Open Policy Agent（策略引擎）和ContextForge AI Gateway（网关）集成，在运行时检测并缓解滥用行为。

Result: AEGIS能够在异构MCP工具和多种模态下定义细粒度的资源滥用防护，检测并缓解滥用行为，同时保持MCP代理生态系统的灵活性。

Conclusion: AEGIS提供了一种统一的策略执行方案，解决了MCP生态中多模态、跨域工具带来的资源滥用防护难题，具备较强的可扩展性和普适性。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open source JSON-RPC protocol that standardizes how large language models (LLMs) interact with external systems through programmatic functions known as tools. Attackers or malicious agents can exploit certain modalities of these MCP tools to degrade the overall quality of service of agent-based applications. For example, an agent may request an excessively large search radius or very long videos, overloading backend systems and potentially causing slowdowns or denial-of-service. Each modality including text, images, video, and location introduces distinct vectors for resource abuse, complicating the development of consistent mitigation strategies. Moreover, multimodal and crossdomain tools expose diverse request schemas and parameters, making it difficult to define policies that are both generalizable and precise enough to enforce meaningful resource constraints. In this paper, we present AEGIS, a policy enforcement component that enables administrators to define fine-grained safeguards against resource abuse across heterogeneous MCP tools and modalities. AEGIS leverages the reasoning capabilities of large language models to analyze, categorize, and normalize diverse tool invocations into a unified, policy-friendly representation accessible to security practitioners. Integrated with the Open Policy Agent and the ContextForge AI Gateway, AEGIS detects and mitigates abusive behaviors while preserving the flexibility of MCP-based agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [312] [aiXamine: Unified Black-Box Evaluation of Cross-Dimensional Trade-offs in LLM Safety, Security, and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20554)
*Fatih Deniz,Yazan Boshmaf,Dorde Popovic,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出aiXamine，一个统一的黑盒评测平台，在45+测试和9个服务上联合评估LLM在安全、安防、隐私三个维度上的可信度，覆盖120+模型和5000+次测试，揭示了安全税、隐私正交性和蒸馏导致的鲁棒性崩溃三种跨维度现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测框架孤立地评估安全、安防和隐私，无法检测模型在这些维度之间相互牵制的跨维度失败模式，例如模型在安全对齐上得分99.3却拒绝三分之一正常查询，或能力指标全面提升但隐私下降21分。

Method: aiXamine通过自动化红队流水线在九个服务上编排46项测试，生成从提示级诊断到跨服务权衡分析的层次化风险画像，在相同条件下对专有和开源模型进行可复现的比较评测。

Result: 对120余个LLM进行5000余次测试，发现了三类单轴评测无法观察到的跨维度现象：(1)安全税收——更强的对齐系统性增加过度拒绝率;(2)隐私与其他可信度维度近似正交;(3)蒸馏诱导的鲁棒性崩溃——离策略蒸馏无在线校正导致熵崩溃，同一基础架构上鲁棒性从56.9骤降至2.6。此外还发现规模收益递减和类别依赖的安全行为。

Conclusion: 可信度本质上是多维的：在一个维度上的进步不能保证其他维度的进步，甚至可能主动削弱其他维度，但当前的alignment方法仍将其当作单一目标来对待，需要向多目标联合优化转变。

Abstract: The critical failure modes in deployed large language models (LLMs) are cross-dimensional: a model can score 99.3 in safety alignment while refusing one in three benign queries, or improve across every capability metric while losing 21 points in privacy. Existing evaluation frameworks that assess safety, security, and privacy independently cannot detect these patterns. We introduce aiXamine, a unified black-box platform that evaluates LLM trustworthiness across safety, security, and privacy as interdependent properties. aiXamine orchestrates 46 tests across nine services through an automated red-teaming pipeline, producing hierarchical risk profiles, from prompt-level diagnostics to cross-service trade-off analytics, that enable reproducible comparison of proprietary and open-weight systems under identical conditions. Applying aiXamine to over 120 LLMs through more than 5,000 test runs, we conduct the largest joint safety, security, and privacy study to date and uncover three cross-dimensional phenomena invisible to single-axis evaluation. First, safety enforcement incurs a quantifiable safety tax: stronger alignment systematically increases over-refusal, forcing providers to choose between protection and utility. Second, privacy is near-orthogonal to other trustworthiness dimensions and not captured by standard alignment. Third, we identify and formally characterize distillation-induced robustness collapse: off-policy distillation without on-policy correction causes entropy collapse, catastrophically destroying robustness (56.9$\to$2.6) on the same base architecture. These findings, compounded by diminishing returns from scale and category-dependent safety behaviors, demonstrate that trustworthiness is inherently multi-dimensional: progress along one axis does not guarantee, and can actively undermine, progress along others, yet current alignment methods treat it as a single objective.

</details>


### [313] [Beyond End-to-End Success: Diagnosing Failures in Long-Horizon Security LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20563)
*Wei Shao,Chongzhou Fang,Zuxiong Tan,Zequan Liang,Setareh Rafatirad,Avesta Sasan,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种诊断方法，通过为长时程安全LLM智能体任务设置检查点，将任务失败区分为"能力暴露前"和"能力暴露后"两类，并使用受控干预测试上游瓶颈。在四个任务族上的实验显示，模型代际间的主要失败来源可能发生显著变化，仅依赖聚合任务成功率不足以指导改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程安全LLM智能体需要在大量相互依赖的交互中传递信息和决策，后续动作往往依赖早期发现的服务、状态或访问权限。这使得最终任务成功率难以解释：智能体可能在到达目标能力被实际运用的节点之前就已失败，导致聚合成功率无法揭示真正的失败原因和瓶颈所在。

Method: 提出一种诊断方法论，包括三个关键组件：(1) 在安全任务中埋设检查点(checkpoints)以定位失败发生阶段；(2) 将失败区分为能力暴露前的失败和能力暴露后的失败；(3) 使用受控干预(controlled interventions)来测试怀疑的上游瓶颈。该方法在四个任务族上进行评估：延迟复用已发现信息、复用已观察状态、从失败策略中恢复、以及在不确定结果后进行决策。

Result: 在观察状态复用任务上，检查点分析显示Gemini 2.5 Flash的大量失败发生在模型观察到它后续需要复用的状态之前。在预先指定的92种子研究中，目标协议消歧指导将状态观察率从匹配非指导对照消息下的65.5%提升到95.4%。然而在Gemini 3.7 Flash上重复相同设计时产生了相反的效果，且状态观察不再可靠预测任务完成。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，长时程安全智能体的主要失败来源可能随模型代际迁移而变化，这凸显了采用诊断式评估方法的重要性——即诊断智能体在何处以及为何失败，而非仅依赖聚合任务成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon security LLM agents must carry information and decisions across many dependent interactions, where later actions often depend on services, state, or access discovered much earlier. This makes final task success difficult to interpret: an agent may fail before it ever reaches the point where the capability of interest can be exercised. We present a diagnostic methodology that instruments security tasks with checkpoints, separates failures before and after capability exposure, and uses controlled interventions to test suspected upstream bottlenecks. We evaluate the methodology across four task families involving delayed reuse of discovered information, reuse of observed state, recovery from failed strategies, and decision making after uncertain outcomes. On observed state reuse, checkpoint analysis shows that many Gemini 2.5 Flash failures occur before the model observes the state it is later expected to reuse. In a pre-specified 92-seed study, targeted protocol-disambiguation guidance increases state observation from 65.5\% under a matched non-guidance control message to 95.4\%. Repeating the same design with Gemini 3.7 Flash produces the opposite effect, while state observation no longer reliably predicts task completion. These results show that the dominant source of failure can shift across model generations, motivating evaluation that diagnoses where and why long-horizon security agents fail rather than relying only on aggregate task success.

</details>


### [314] [Temporal Risk on Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20575)
*Shiqi Liu,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种将时间作为显式维度的卫星时态风险评估框架，通过五维时间能力模型和时间窗口离散化来生成时间索引的风险矩阵，帮助操作员规避高风险时段并动态调整防御资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星的脆弱性会随轨道漂移、功率裕度变化和空间环境恶化而随时间变化，但现有网络安全风险框架大多将威胁视为静态。同一漏洞在关键机动期间可能比日常操作造成更大损害，因此需要将时间维度纳入卫星安全分析。

Method: 提出一个时态风险评估框架：1）扩展现有对手行为分类法，加入五维时间能力模型；2）在不同任务时间窗口内估计利用难度；3）将任务离散化为操作上有意义的时间窗口和环境带；4）输出一系列时间索引的似然-影响矩阵而非单一风险评分。

Result: 框架能够显示系统在何时最易受到攻击，帮助操作员避免在高风险期安排敏感操作，并使防御资源配置与随时间变化的威胁态势保持一致。

Conclusion: 该框架使卫星安全分析从静态评估转向动态时态评估，为卫星运营的安全调度和资源分配提供了新的决策支持视角。

Abstract: Satellite vulnerabilities change over time as orbits shift, power margins tighten, and the space environment deteriorates. However, most cybersecurity risk frameworks still treat threats as static. In practice, the same exploit can be far more damaging during a critical maneuver than during routine operations. We propose a temporal risk assessment framework that makes time an explicit axis in satellite security analysis. It extends existing adversary behavior taxonomies with a five-dimensional temporal capability model and estimates exploitation difficulty across distinct temporal windows of a mission. Rather than producing a single risk score, the framework outputs a series of time-indexed likelihood-impact matrices. It discretizes missions into operationally meaningful time windows and environmental bands to show when systems are most exposed. This view helps operators avoid scheduling sensitive operations in high-risk periods and align defensive resources with a threat landscape that shifts over time.

</details>


### [315] [Keyed Provenance Watermarking with Complementary Lattice-Based Secure Aggregation for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20580)
*Xinyun Liu,Zhi Lu,Yu Chen,Ronghua Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端可信联邦学习框架，将基于物理锚定元数据的密钥水印（PAM+FMGAN）与基于格的零知识安全聚合（LZKSA）耦合，同时解决数据泄露、未授权复用和恶意梯度操纵等多层攻击问题，并支持后量子安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临多层次攻击（数据泄露、未授权复用、恶意梯度操纵），但现有方法通常单独处理这些威胁，缺乏一个统一的、端到端的保护框架。此外，传统方案在后量子时代的安全性也存疑。

Method: 框架分为两层：(1) 数据层：提出Kerckhoffs合规的PAM方案，利用可信基础设施数据（时间、位置、服务器ID）经密钥化HMAC-SHA-256变换生成水印载荷；设计FMGAN（基于GAN的图像水印框架），采用特征融合模块和Mamba引导的线性注意力机制嵌入水印。(2) 计算层：采用基于RLWE的格上零知识安全聚合协议（LZKSA），在提交的梯度上验证密钥正确性、L2范数边界和余弦相似度约束，且不泄露私有更新。

Result: 大量实验验证了数据层与计算层在复合攻击场景下的互补保护效果。该框架是首个在混合、端到端可信FL框架中联合评估两层的方案。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个同时覆盖数据来源认证和梯度安全聚合的双层防护架构，解决了FL中多层攻击被孤立处理的问题，并具备后量子安全特性，为可信联邦学习提供了新的综合方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to multi-level attacks. However, existing methods address them separately, leaving FL exposed to data leakage, unauthorized reuse, and malicious gradient manipulation. In this work, we propose an FL framework that couples keyed context-provenance watermarking with verifiable lattice-based secure aggregation of Real-World Anchored Watermarking and Lattice-Based Zero-Knowledge Secure Aggregation. At the data layer, we propose a Kerckhoffs-compliant scheme that utilizes Physical Anchor Metadata (PAM) to ensure data provenance. PAM is defined as a context-provenance token derived from trusted infrastructure data (time, location, and server ID) and then subjected to a keyed HMAC-SHA-256 transformation to produce a watermark payload that cannot be generated without the client's secret key. We further design FMGAN, a GAN-based robust image watermarking framework that embeds this transformed payload using a feature fusion module and a Mamba-guided linear attention mechanism. At the computation layer, we adopt a lattice-based zero-knowledge secure aggregation (LZKSA) protocol that verifies key correctness, L2 norm bounds, and cosine similarity constraints over committed gradients without revealing private updates. The RLWE-based design guarantees post-quantum security. Extensive experiments validate the complementary protection of the two layers under composite attack scenarios. To our knowledge, no prior verification workflow has jointly evaluated both layers in a hybrid, end-to-end trustworthy FL framework.

</details>


### [316] [MATEE: Efficiently Bridging the Semantic Gap in TrustZone via Arm Pointer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20583)
*Shiqi Liu,Xiang Li,Jie Wang,Yongpeng Gao,Jiajin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MATEE系统，利用Arm指针认证（PA）技术弥合Arm TrustZone安全世界与普通世界之间的语义鸿沟，通过将请求绑定到客户端应用（CA）的身份来防御语义间隙漏洞（SGV），防止恶意请求伪造，运行时开销仅为2.19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arm TrustZone将系统划分为安全世界和普通世界，但安全世界对普通世界的运行信息可见性有限，形成语义鸿沟。安全世界在接收普通世界的数据请求时缺乏有效的用户身份认证，导致恶意的CA可以通过精心构造的请求参数欺骗安全世界中的TA，危害其他CA存储的敏感数据。

Method: MATEE系统首先系统地分类了语义间隙漏洞（SGV），然后利用Arm指针认证（PA）技术将每个请求绑定到对应CA的身份标识上，在CA访问敏感数据时验证该身份，从而防止恶意请求伪造。MATEE的核心优势在于无需修改现有的CA和TA即可隔离不同CA的敏感数据。

Result: 实验评估表明，MATEE能够成功防御语义间隙漏洞，且运行时开销极小，仅为2.19%。

Conclusion: MATEE通过利用Arm PA技术有效解决了TrustZone中安全世界与普通世界之间的语义鸿沟问题，在不修改现有CA和TA的前提下实现了对SGV的防御，且开销可接受，为TEE安全性提供了一种实用的加固方案。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) employ hardware-based isolation mechanisms to safeguard the confidentiality and integrity of sensitive code and data. One such prevalent implementation is Arm TrustZone, which partitions the system into the secure and normal (non-secure) worlds. However, this partitioning results in the secure world having very limited visibility into the operating information of the normal world, creating a semantic gap between these two worlds. Specifically, the secure world lacks an effective user identity authentication when receiving data requests from the normal world. Consequently, malicious Client Applications (CAs) in the normal world can deceive Trusted Applications (TAs) in the secure world by utilizing elaborate request parameters, compromising the sensitive data stored by other CAs. We systematically classify these Semantic Gap Vulnerabilities (SGVs) and propose a mate system for the TEE called MATEE to defend against SGVs. MATEE utilizes Arm Pointer Authentication (PA) to bind each request to the corresponding CA's identity and then verifies the identity when the CA accesses sensitive data, thereby preventing malicious request forgery. In particular, MATEE isolates sensitive data of different CAs without modifying existing CAs and TAs. Our evaluation demonstrates that MATEE successfully defends against SGVs with a minimal runtime overhead (2.19%).

</details>


### [317] [More Granular, Less Trust: Enforcing Intra-Process Isolation with Arm CCA in an Untrusted Management Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20584)
*Shiqi Liu,Zhouqi Jiang,Jie Wang,Wei Zhou,Kun Sun,Zhaohui Chen,Yulai Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CCAegis，一个基于Arm CCA扩展的进程内隔离系统，通过静态分析追踪敏感数据流并在函数调用点插入权限切换指令，利用GPT配置权限，仅信任安全监控器，实现对敏感数据和操作的进程内隔离，性能开销为1.01x到1.43x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算环境中，即使使用SHELTER等进程级隔离方案，仍存在进程内攻击以及OS与进程内对手合谋的威胁。现有进程内隔离技术过度依赖OS管理隔离域，导致TCB过大，需要更细粒度、更少信任依赖的隔离方案。

Method: CCAegis扩展Arm CCA架构，采用静态分析追踪敏感数据流并识别处理函数，在函数调用和返回点插入权限切换指令，通过Granule Protection Table (GPT)调整权限，确保只有指定函数能访问隔离数据。系统仅信任负责配置GPT和管理域切换的Secure Monitor，最小化TCB。

Result: 在官方模拟器和真实开发板上实现并评估，CCAegis能有效隔离敏感数据和操作，在实际密码学工作负载下性能开销为原始版本的1.01x到1.43x。

Conclusion: CCAegis通过将信任基础缩减到仅Secure Monitor，并利用GPT实现细粒度的进程内隔离，有效防御了OS特权攻击和进程内攻击，为机密计算提供了更安全、更高效的内存隔离方案。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of confidential computing, security-sensitive applications are often deployed in confidential virtual machines (CVMs), which reduce reliance on third-party cloud providers. However, privilege attacks originating from the OS remain a significant threat in these environments. Existing finer-grained isolation schemes, such as SHELTER, provide process-level protection but are still vulnerable to intraprocess attacks and potential collusion between the OS and intra-process adversaries. Many current intra-process isolation techniques continue to depend on the OS to manage and enforce isolation domains, leading to a large Trusted Computing Base (TCB). This gap highlights the need for more granular, less trust-dependent confidential computing solutions. In this paper, we present CCAegis, a system that extends the Arm Confidential Compute Architecture (CCA) to enforce intra-process isolation of sensitive data and operations, safeguarding them from both intraprocess adversaries and the OS. We employ static analysis to track the flow of sensitive data and identify functions that handle such data. Permission-switching instructions are inserted at the function call and return points, adjusting permissions via the Granule Protection Table (GPT) to ensure that only designated functions can access the isolated data. Notably, CCAegis places trust solely in the Secure Monitor, which configures the GPTs and manages domain switching, thereby minimizing the TCB. We implemented CCAegis on both an official emulator and a real development board to assess its performance. Our experimental results show that CCAegis effectively isolates sensitive data and operations, with performance overheads ranging from 1.01x to 1.43x compared to the original version across real-world cryptographic workloads.

</details>


### [318] [ARQ: Agentic CodeQL Query Refinement for C/C++ Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20637)
*Chunyi Wang,Yunfei Ke,Junfeng Yang,Yun-Yun Tsai,Penghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ARQ是一个基于智能体（agentic）框架，利用合成C/C++程序执行结果作为ground truth，自动精炼CodeQL查询以修复误报和漏报，无需要标签数据集、提交历史或模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于查询的静态分析器（如CodeQL）在漏洞检测中仍存在大量误报（FP）和漏报（FN），而传统的查询精炼方法依赖标注数据集、提交历史或特定漏洞模板，成本高且通用性差。作者希望提出一种无需这些外部依赖的自动查询精炼方法。

Method: ARQ的核心机制是：通过合成C/C++程序来暴露查询的弱点——当程序实际执行结果与查询判定不一致时，即为查询存在FP或FN弱点。具体地，如果程序确实存在漏洞但查询未报告则为FN弱点；程序安全但查询报告则为FP弱点。随后利用LLM驱动的精炼循环，以这些执行不一致性作为ground truth来修复查询。框架使用三个商业LLM（GPT-5.4、Claude-Sonnet-4.6、Gemini-3.5-flash）进行实验。

Result: 在Juliet v1.3和FormAI v2数据集上，ARQ精炼后的CodeQL查询比原始查询多检测出最多119.8%的真实正例（True Positives），且全程精确率（Precision）不低于98.0%。ARQ成功修复了官方CodeQL查询仓库中3个悬而未决的GitHub issues（最长已开放27个月），并在真实世界库libpng和zlib中暴露了2个此前未被发现的漏洞。

Conclusion: ARQ通过将"程序执行"这一真实信号引入查询精炼过程，实现了高效、自动化且无需外部标注的CodeQL查询优化，显著提升了漏洞检测能力，并在真实场景中展现了实用价值。

Abstract: Static analyzers have been widely adopted for vulnerability detection in C/C++ programs. Query-based static analyzers (e.g., CodeQL) encode vulnerable code patterns in detection queries and match them against source code. However, existing queries still suffer from false positives (FPs, incorrectly flagging benign code as vulnerable) and false negatives (FNs, missing real vulnerabilities). We present ARQ, an agentic framework that automatically refines C/C++ CodeQL queries using execution-grounded evidence from synthesized C/C++ programs. Our key insight is that a synthesized program exposes a query's weakness whenever its execution disagrees with the query's verdict. If the program is genuinely vulnerable but the query stays silent, the query has an FN weakness; if the program is safe but the query fires anyway, it has an FP weakness. ARQ then runs an LLM-based refinement loop that repairs the query using these disagreements as ground truth. Unlike previous query refining methods, ARQ requires no labeled datasets, no commit history, and no vulnerability-specific templates. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ARQ by refining 12 official CodeQL queries using three commercial LLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude-Sonnet-4.6, and Gemini-3.5-flash).
