We compare both ARQ-refined and original CodeQL queries on the Juliet v1.3 and FormAI v2 datasets and show that ARQ-refined queries detect substantially more true positives, by up to 119.8\%, with a Precision of at least 98.0\% throughout. ARQ successfully fixed three unresolved GitHub issues raised in the official CodeQL query repository that had remained open for as long as \textit{27 months}. The refined queries also exposed two previously undiscovered bugs in the real-world libraries libpng and zlib.

</details>


### [319] [The Claws in Plain Sight: Unauthorized Context Disclosure through LLM Agent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20658)
*Ben Dong,Zhonghao Guo,Tianyi Lu,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Claw in Plain Sight"攻击方法，通过任务相关上下文将受保护属性框架为操作或程序所需，诱使LLM在生成工具调用参数时泄露敏感信息。在6个压力级别×4个隐私策略级别×5个模型配置的受控基准中，会话级泄露率介于20.8%至75.0%，表明提示级隐私策略无法提供可移植的强制边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在构造工具调用参数时，虽然合法访问上下文信息，但并不意味着被授权将这些信息传输给每一用途或目的地。现有工作未能系统性地研究"权威压力"（authority pressure）如何导致模型在工具调用参数中泄露受保护属性，这是上下文到参数边界上的一个安全漏洞。

Method: 构建受控合成基准，交叉6个压力级别与4个隐私策略级别，在5个DeepSeek和Claude模型配置上进行评估，共产生120次调用。攻击方法通过将受保护属性框架为操作性或程序性必需内容，使模型在看似有效的工具调用参数中包含这些属性。实验使用合成档案并在本地捕获提议参数。

Result: 在完整的压力-策略矩阵中，会话级泄露率在测试模型中介于20.8%至75.0%。更强的隐私指令能减少聚合泄露但无法在所有模型中一致消除，说明提示级策略不能构成可移植的强制执行边界。

Conclusion: 研究结果呼吁在工具参数执行前引入目的感知和目标地感知的检查机制，以防范上下文到参数边界的策略违规生成。实验限于合成档案和本地捕获，测量的是策略违规生成而非完整网络外渗或真实用户泄露。

Abstract: LLM agents routinely construct tool-call arguments from user profiles, conversation history, retrieved documents, and prior tool results. However, legitimate access to contextual information does not imply authorization to transmit that information for every purpose or destination. We present Claw in Plain Sight, an authority- pressure attack in which task-adjacent content frames protected attributes as operationally or procedurally required, causing a model to include them in otherwise valid generated arguments. We evaluate Claw in Plain Sight using a controlled synthetic benchmark that crosses six pressure levels with four privacy-policy levels across five DeepSeek and Claude model configurations, producing 120 calls. Across the complete pressure-policy matrix, session-level disclosure rates range from 20.8% to 75.0% among the tested models. Stronger privacy instructions reduce aggregate disclosure but do not eliminate it consistently across models, showing that prompt-level policies do not provide a portable enforcement boundary. Our experiments use only synthetic profiles and capture proposed arguments locally; they measure policy-violating generation at the context-to-argument boundary, not completed network exfiltration or leakage from deployed users. These findings motivate purpose- and destination-aware inspection of generated tool arguments before execution.

</details>


### [320] [Bootstrapping Mutual Attestation with Kleene's Second Recursion Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20671)
*Takuma Imamura*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解决互证明中参考值引导（reference-value bootstrapping）问题的新方法。通过将引导问题表述为互定点方程系统，并利用Kleene第二递归定理求解，节点可以仅凭内置数据重构出每个对等方的源代码，从而获得其参考测量值。该方法无需可信第三方或外部提供的参考值，并通过PyReflect（Python转译器）和NixReflect（Nix转译器）分别实现了TPM和AWS Nitro Enclaves两种场景的概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无中心可信操作者的互证明场景中，每个节点需要持有对等方的参考值（预期代码测量值）。朴素方法（在节点代码中互相嵌入参考值）会导致无限回归（infinite regress）。现有解决方案要么依赖可信第三方（TTP），要么依赖外部提供的参考值，要么依赖架构特定的测量机制，都缺乏通用性和自举能力。

Method: 本文将引导问题形式化为互定点方程系统，并利用Kleene第二递归定理求解。构造出的节点能够互相引用对方的代码，并仅凭内置数据重构出每个对等方的确切源代码。对于直接测量的源文件（如Python脚本），节点通过将测量函数直接应用于重构源码来获得参考值；对于内建镜像（如AWS Nitro Enclaves），节点通过可重现地从重构源码重建对等方镜像并推导参考测量值。作者开发了PyReflect（Python转译器）和NixReflect（Nix转译器）分别支持两种场景。

Result: 通过PyReflect实现了TPM互证明的概念验证，通过NixReflect实现了两个Nitro Enclaves从内置数据重现彼此参考PCR的概念验证。该方案架构无关，不需要TTP或外部参考值，且与现有证明栈无需修改即可兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作为解决互证明引导问题提供了首个不依赖TTP或外部参考值的通用解决方案，利用递归定理从理论上保证了节点能够自我引用并重构对等方代码，适用于多种测量场景和现有证明基础设施。

Abstract: Mutual attestation among nodes with no central trusted operator requires each node to hold reference values (expected code measurements) for its peers. The naïve approach of mutually embedding these reference values in the nodes' code leads to an infinite regress. We call the problem of resolving this infinite regress the reference-value bootstrapping problem for mutual attestation. Existing solutions avoid this regress by relying on a trusted third party (TTP), externally supplied reference values, or architecture-specific measurement mechanisms. We instead express the bootstrapping problem as a system of mutual fixed-point equations and solve it by Kleene's second recursion theorem. The construction produces nodes that mutually reference one another's code and reconstruct every peer's exact source from built-in data alone. When a deployed source file is measured directly, as with a Python script, a node obtains the peer's reference value by applying the measurement function directly to the reconstructed source. When a built image is measured, as with AWS Nitro Enclaves, a node instead reproducibly rebuilds the peer's image from the reconstructed source and derives its reference measurement. For the first case, we develop PyReflect, a Python transpiler, and use it to implement a TPM mutual-attestation PoC. For the second, we develop NixReflect, a Nix transpiler, and use it in a PoC in which two Nitro Enclaves reproduce each other's reference PCRs from built-in data alone. Our solution is architecture-independent, requires neither a TTP nor externally supplied reference values, and works with existing attestation stacks unchanged.

</details>


### [321] [The Rising Cost of Trust: Practitioners' Trust Signals, Controls, and Responses in the Software Supply Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20675)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Siri Paidipalli,Laurie Williams,Christian Kästner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对38位从业者的半结构化访谈，从信任视角系统研究了软件供应链中信任的侵蚀现象及其成本，发现从业者正通过自动化验证、信任守护者和退出供应链等方式应对日益上升的信任成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链日益复杂，AI重塑其威胁格局（如AI生成依赖的质量问题），信任的侵蚀风险高，但目前缺乏关于从业者信任的实证基线。

Method: 对38位来自工业界和开源社区的从业者进行半结构化访谈，聚焦其揭示偏好（实际采纳的控制措施）而非陈述态度，并利用社会科学中成熟的信任概念进行主题分析。

Result: 发现信任正在侵蚀且代价高昂：有意识的从业者在不断积累控制措施。为应对信任成本上升，从业者采取自动化验证、将信任决策委托给守护者，甚至考虑完全退出软件供应链。

Conclusion: 通过信任透镜理解软件供应链动态，提供术语和概念（如信任守护者、系统信任、信号），有助于设计未来干预措施，构建信任水平适度的良好运作的供应链。

Abstract: The software supply chain is becoming more complex, and AI is reshaping its threat landscape, e.g., raising concerns about the quality of AI-generated dependencies. Seen through the lens of trust, the stakes of eroding trust in the software supply chain are high, yet we lack an empirical baseline on practitioners' trust. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in taking informed actions as trust in the software supply chain evolves, through an interview study with 38 practitioners. We conducted semi-structured interviews with industry and open-source practitioners, focusing on their revealed preferences (the controls they adopted) rather than their stated attitudes, and analyzed the data using thematic analysis grounded in established trust concepts from the social sciences. We find that trust is eroding, which is becoming costly: aware practitioners are accumulating controls. To cope with the rising cost of trust, practitioners automate verification, delegate trust decisions to guardians, or consider exiting the software supply chain entirely. Understanding software supply chain dynamics through the lens of trust provides the vocabulary and concepts (e.g., guardians of trust, system trust, signals) to shape future interventions for a well-functioning supply chain with appropriate levels of trust.

</details>


### [322] [The Software Supply Chain as a Market for Lemons: A Multivocal Review of Trust Signal Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20678)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Christian Kästner,Laurie Williams*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过多声部综述（252个Google Search来源和870个Reddit帖子）研究开源依赖采纳信任信号的可靠性，发现廉价信任信号在对抗性操纵、与合法行为难以区分的作弊手段以及非对抗性AI驱动的通胀三重力量下崩溃，且生态系统应对主要是建议而非实际行动，最终提出应强制采用加密证明等更昂贵的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者在评估开源依赖时依赖下载量、星标、贡献者活动等廉价信任信号来替代直接代码审查，此前研究仅记录了单个信号被操纵的现象，但所有依赖采纳信号同时崩溃的全局图景及生态系统的应对方式尚未被探索。

Method: 采用多声部综述（multivocal review）方法，对252个Google Search来源和870个Reddit帖子进行系统性收集与编码分析，识别信任信号崩溃的模式以及社区提出的应对策略。

Result: 发现廉价信任信号在三种并行力量下崩溃：（1）对抗性操纵；（2）与合法行为难以区分的作弊手段；（3）非对抗性的AI驱动通胀。生态系统的回应以建议为主而非实际行动——54.6%的Google Search来源仅提供建议而未采取实际行动。提出的替代方案（用另一廉价信号替代或聚合多信号）本身也可被操纵。

Conclusion: 当伪造信号的成本低于获取真实信号的成本时，好的和坏的依赖将变得不可区分，形成"柠檬市场"。依赖个体实践者验证廉价信号不可持续，应强制采用加密证明等更昂贵的信号，使其成为默认而非少数人的自愿选择。

Abstract: Practitioners evaluating open-source dependencies rely on cheap trust signals, e.g., stars, download counts, and contributor activity, as substitutes for direct code inspection, assuming those signals reflect genuine trustworthiness. Prior work has documented individual signal gaming, but the landscape of collapses across all dependency-adoption signals, as well as the ecosystem's response, remains unexplored. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in understanding the reliability of dependency adoption trust signals, such as download counts and contributor activity, by conducting a multivocal review of 252 Google Search sources and 870 Reddit threads. After coding the corpora, we find that cheap trust signals collapse under three simultaneous forces: adversarial manipulation, gaming techniques indistinguishable from legitimate behavior, and non-adversarial AI-driven inflation. The documented responses are more advice than actual action: 54.6% of Google Search sources contain advice on what practitioners should do, with no actual action taken. Responses proposed substituting one cheap signal for another or aggregating multiple signals, which are now also gameable. Non-adversarial inflation, i.e., degradation caused by the emergence of legitimate AI tooling, lacks documented actual behavior change in either corpus. The gap between known remedy and actual practice points toward a market for lemons: when faking signals costs less than earning them, good and bad dependencies become indistinguishable. Relying on individual practitioners to verify the cheap signals is not sustainable. Costlier signals, such as cryptographic attestation, should be made mandatory so that they become the default for all, not a voluntary choice for the few.

</details>


### [323] [Enabling Threshold Custody for the Lightning Network with Nested Threshold Multi-Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20705)
*Paul Gerhart,Nadav Kohen,Jesse Posner,Matias Furszyfer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了嵌套阈值多重签名这一新原语，并基于此构建了Iceberg——首个嵌套阈值MuSig2签名方案，使闪电网络通道可以单方面部署t-of-n阈值托管，且无需修改Bitcoin、闪电协议或对端节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bitcoin闪电网络管理着数亿美元的资金，但通道端点依赖脆弱的单一在线密钥。虽然阈值签名已常规用于保护链上Bitcoin，但闪电通道目前无法实际部署阈值方案，因为阈值化闪电参与方需要在不改变现有两方MuSig2协议的nonce交换和消息流的前提下，将阈值签名方案嵌套在内。

Method: 形式化定义了嵌套阈值多重签名（nested threshold multi-signatures）这一新的密码学原语；作为该原语的实例，提出了Iceberg——首个嵌套阈值MuSig2签名构造，使闪电通道一方可以以t-of-n阈值组的形式运作，同时在对端看来仍是一个标准MuSig2参与者；并提供了安全证明、生产级闪电节点原型集成及性能基准测试。

Result: Iceberg的原型已集成到生产级闪电节点中。性能测试表明，阈值化闪电通道的开销很小：可容忍一个腐败成员的阈值组仍能维持未修改端点的93%以上的支付吞吐量。

Conclusion: Iceberg首次实现了闪电通道的实用化阈值托管部署，证明阈值化可以在当前闪电网络上单方面完成部署，无需对Bitcoin、闪电协议或通道对端做任何修改，且性能开销可接受。

Abstract: The Bitcoin Lightning Network secures hundreds of millions of dollars, yet channel endpoints rely on vulnerable single online keys. Although threshold signatures are routinely used to protect on-chain Bitcoin, no practical deployment has been possible for Lightning channels. This is because thresholdizing a Lightning party requires nesting a threshold signature scheme inside of an established two-party MuSig2 protocol without altering its nonce exchange or message flow.
  In this work, we resolve this limitation by formalizing nested threshold multi-signatures, a new cryptographic primitive for thresholdizing one participant inside a multi-signature protocol. As an instance of this primitive, we present Iceberg, the first construction for nested threshold MuSig2 signatures. Iceberg enables one side of a Lightning channel to operate as a $t$-of-$n$ threshold group while appearing to the counterparty as a standard MuSig2 participant. As a result, threshold custody can be deployed unilaterally on today's Lightning Network without requiring any modifications to Bitcoin, the Lightning protocol, or channel counterparties.
  We prove the security of Iceberg, integrate a prototype into a production Lightning node, and benchmark its performance. Our measurements show that thresholdizing a Lightning channel incurs only modest overhead, since a threshold group tolerating one corrupted member sustains over $93\%$ of the payment throughput of an unmodified endpoint.

</details>


### [324] [Privacy-Preserving Object Detection for Vision Transformer-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20712)
*Homare Sueyoshi,Kiyoshi Nishikawa,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于ViT和感知加密的目标检测方法，首次在目标检测任务中实现测试图像的敏感视觉信息保护，通过利用ViT的嵌入结构和带密钥的域自适应技术，在保证视觉保护的同时获得与无保护模型几乎相同的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究中的视觉信息保护方法仅针对图像分类任务，尚未有工作将视觉信息保护应用于目标检测任务。本文旨在填补这一空白，在目标检测场景中同时实现敏感视觉信息的保护和高精度的检测性能。

Method: 提出一种基于感知加密的目标检测方法，核心思路包括：(1) 利用Vision Transformer（ViT）的嵌入结构保留图像特征信息；(2) 引入带密钥的域自适应技术，使模型能够适应加密后的输入分布，从而实现加密图像上的有效目标检测。

Result: 在基于ViT的目标检测模型ViTdet上进行了实验验证，结果表明所提方法在检测精度上与无保护模型几乎持平，同时在视觉信息保护方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: 首次将感知加密引入目标检测任务，验证了在ViT框架下利用嵌入结构和密钥域自适应技术可以兼顾视觉信息保护与检测精度的可行性，为目标检测中的隐私保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose a novel object detection method that enables us to protect sensitive visual information of test images. Previous studies considering visual information protection focus on image classification tasks. This paper proposes an object detection method using perceptual encryption for the first time. The proposed method can achieve almost the same accuracy as that of models without any protection by utilizing the embedding structure of the Vision Transformer (ViT) and a domain adaptation technique with keys. In experiments, the effectiveness of the proposed method is verified in terms of accuracy and visual protection under the use of ViTdet, which is a ViT-based object detection model.

</details>


### [325] [Uncovering and Understanding Hidden Dependencies in the LLM API Reseller Ecosystem via Prefix-Cache Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20732)
*Zimo Ji,Xin Wei,Congying Xu,Wenyuan Jiang,Xin Yang,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CacheTracer是首个通过API-only方式测量LLM API转售商之间隐藏依赖关系的系统，利用前缀缓存复用作为侧信道，在39个端点、110万次请求的实测中揭示了七层深的缓存可达结构和高度集中的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM API转售商已成为现代LLM服务的重要访问层，但多级转售形成了不透明的供应链——用户请求可能经过未披露的上游转售商，每一层都能检查或修改提示和响应，带来生态系统级别的机密性和完整性风险。现有研究只审计单个转售商，无法揭示跨转售商的隐藏依赖关系。

Method: CacheTracer的核心洞察是利用前缀缓存复用作为侧信道，通过缓存可达关系（cache-reach relations）测量依赖关系。方法包含两个原语：Flood（通过一个端点填充新鲜缓存状态）和Prove（探测另一个端点是否能复用该缓存，同时排除探测自身创建的命中）。据此构建两个端点间的依赖图。

Result: 在39个转售商端点、636个端点对、110万次API请求的真实测量中，37.1%的端点对表现出共享缓存可达性，包含关系跨越七层，一个缓存可达节点至少被包含在31个其他节点中。恢复的结构具有模型特异性。通过真实一致性检查和受控实验验证了CacheTracer的高可靠性和准确性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了看似独立的API转售商之间存在大量隐藏依赖关系。深层且集中的依赖可能产生巨大的潜在爆炸半径——公共上游路径上的机密性或完整性故障可能影响多个下游转售商的用户。

Abstract: LLM API resellers have become an important access layer to modern LLM services. However, multi-level resale creates an opaque supply chain: a user's request may traverse undisclosed upstream resellers, each of which can inspect or modify prompts and responses, inducing ecosystem-level confidentiality and integrity risks. Existing studies audit individual resellers, but provide little visibility into hidden dependencies across resellers. We present CacheTracer, the first API-only measurement of such hidden dependencies. Our key insight is to exploit prefix-cache reuse as a side channel to measure dependency via cache-reach relations. CacheTracer operationalizes this insight with two primitives: Flood populates fresh cache state through one endpoint, and Prove probes whether another can reuse it while excluding probe-created hits.
  We then conduct a real-world measurement study with CacheTracer on 39 reseller endpoints, sending 1.1 million API requests across 636 endpoint pairs. Our measurements reveal a deep, concentrated cache-reach structure: 37.1% of measured pairs exhibit shared cache reach, the containment order spans seven layers, and one cache reach is contained within at least 31 of other nodes. We further find that the recovered structure is model-specific. We also evaluate the validity of CacheTracer through both real-world consistency checks and controlled experiments. The results show its high reliability and accuracy. These findings reveal substantial hidden dependencies among seemingly independent API resellers. Such deep and concentrated dependencies can create a large potential blast radius, where a confidentiality or integrity failure along a common upstream path may affect users across multiple downstream resellers.

</details>


### [326] [Beyond Explicit Generators: Distribution-Free Linear-Decomposition Attacks on Public-Key Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20798)
*Ziyan Chen,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明线性分解攻击可在不恢复秘密代数作用的情况下打破公钥方案。作者建立了采样轨迹维度框架，证明分布无关的一次性恢复定理、未来密文覆盖的高概率证书以及最优采样跨度复杂度 Θ((r+log(1/δ))/ε)，并推导出通用不可能性定理：可公开采样的线性密钥传输若具有多项式采样轨迹维度，则与IND-CPA安全不兼容。该框架应用于2024年扭曲-斜群环概率PKE，展示采样器专用线性攻击可实现明文恢复和恒定IND-CPA优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性分解攻击是一种强大且被低估的攻击范式：攻击者无需恢复底层秘密代数作用，仅需利用公钥状态位于已知线性子空间这一事实，通过分解系数在未知作用下的传递来揭示共享值。现有研究假设攻击者能访问完整代数跨度或恢复整个作用，但现实中攻击者通常只能使用公开的采样-评估预言机。本文旨在系统化这一更受限但更实际的攻击场景，并建立其理论上限与安全不可能性。

Method: 作者首先对固定秘密线性传输下的公开配对样本进行建模，定义采样轨迹维度作为加密分布的有效维度度量。在此基础上证明三个核心结果：(1) 分布无关的一次性恢复定理；(2) 未来密文被采样跨度覆盖的高概率证书；(3) 最优采样跨度复杂度 m*_span(r,ε,δ)=Θ((r+log(1/δ))/ε)。这些结果被用于推导通用不可能性定理，随后应用于2024年提出的扭曲-斜群环概率PKE方案，利用其底层CTSP问题的结构实施采样器专用线性攻击。

Result: 证明了对2024年扭曲-斜群环概率PKE方案的有效攻击：仅使用独立生成的公开协议样本即可实现线性攻击，获得明文恢复和恒定IND-CPA优势。实验验证了线性传输假设和端到端的恢复能力，并进一步揭示未来密文的高覆盖率可以在完整代数跨度恢复之前实现，表明线性分解攻击的威胁范围比先前认识的要大。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了线性分解攻击在采样器专用设置下的理论能力边界，给出了一个通用不可能性定理：任何具有多项式采样轨迹维度的可公开采样线性密钥传输方案都不可能达到IND-CPA安全（当传输值决定解密负载时）。这为未来公钥密码方案设计提供了重要的安全性警示——必须避免低维线性结构在公开样本中可被有效利用的情况。

Abstract: Linear-decomposition attacks can break public-key schemes without recovering the secret algebraic action: when a target public state lies in a known linear span, its decomposition coefficients transfer through the unknown action to reveal the shared value. We study a setting in which the adversary uses only the public sampling-and-evaluation oracle available to honest participants, the induced distribution is arbitrary, and the goal is to attack future ciphertexts rather than recover the full algebraic span.
  We model public paired samples under a fixed secret linear transport and define the sampled-orbit dimension as the effective dimension of the encryption distribution. We prove distribution-free one-shot recovery, a high-probability certificate for the future-ciphertext coverage of a sampled span, and the optimal sampled-span complexity $m^\star_{\mathrm{span}}(r,\varepsilon,δ) =Θ((r+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon)$. These results yield a generic impossibility theorem: publicly samplable linear key transport with polynomial sampled-orbit dimension is incompatible with IND--CPA security when the transported value determines the decryption payload.
  We apply the framework to the 2024 probabilistic PKE from twisted--skew group rings. Its underlying Computational Twisted--Skew Problem admits a sampler-only linear attack using independently generated public protocol samples, yielding plaintext recovery and constant IND--CPA advantage. Experiments verify the linear transport and end-to-end recovery, and show that high future-ciphertext coverage may precede recovery of the full algebraic span.

</details>


### [327] [GhostTac: Manipulating Tactile Sensors without Physical Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20817)
*Kun Wang,Xuancun Lu,Ruochen Zhou,Kai Wang,Tongjun Ye,Yihao Shao,Chen Yan,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对机器人触觉传感器的无接触电磁干扰攻击GhostTac，通过非线性整流和有限带宽放大将精心设计的EMI信号转化为持续直流偏移，绕过板载滤波并诱导稳定测量偏差，实现对传感器输出的精细操控，进而引发机器人危险行为（如过度施力）。在10个传感器模块和两只灵巧手上（覆盖15种不同类型的触觉传感器）验证了一致有效性，并通过触觉抓取、滑移检测和材料分类三个案例展示了实际影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉传感器在现代系统中至关重要，但其物理层安全长期被忽视。现有研究缺乏针对触觉感知的攻击手段，而触觉传感的物理层安全漏洞可能被利用来操纵机器人行为，造成物体损坏或人身伤害。

Method: GhostTac利用非线性整流效应和有限带宽放大特性，将精心构造的电磁干扰信号转换为持续的直流偏移，从而绕过传感器板载滤波机制，在目标位置产生稳定且可控的测量偏差。通过调控干扰信号的空间分布和幅度，实现对传感器输出的细粒度操纵。

Result: 在10个传感器模块和两只灵巧手上对15种不同类型触觉传感器进行了系统评估，所有被测设备均表现出一致的有效攻击效果。三个案例研究（触觉抓取、滑移检测和材料分类）进一步证明了该攻击对真实机器人任务具有实际破坏性影响。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种针对机器人触觉感知的全新物理攻击向量，表明触觉传感器存在严重且此前未被认识的物理层安全漏洞，亟需行业关注和防护研究。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are integral to modern robotic systems, enabling robots to perceive and interact with the physical environment through tactile feedback. However, the physical-layer security of tactile sensors has received little attention. We present GhostTac, the first contactless attack, to the best of our knowledge, that manipulates tactile sensing through electromagnetic interference (EMI). GhostTac exploits nonlinear rectification and limited-bandwidth amplification, converting carefully crafted EMI signals into persistent DC offsets that bypass onboard filtering and induce stable measurement deviations. It enables fine-grained, controllable manipulation of sensor outputs by shaping the spatial distribution and magnitude of interference at targeted locations. Such manipulation can induce harmful robot behaviors, including excessive force that may damage objects or injure people. We evaluate GhostTac on 10 sensor modules and two dexterous hands, covering 15 tactile sensors of different types, and demonstrate consistent effectiveness across all tested devices. Three case studies involving tactile grasping, slip detection, and material classification further illustrate its practical impact on real robotic tasks. These findings reveal a new physical attack vector against tactile sensing in robotic systems.

</details>


### [328] [On the Additive FFT Techniques over Binary Extension Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20855)
*Susanta Samanta,Mohammadtaghi Badakhshan,Guang Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文受Bailey四步FFT算法启发，提出了一种针对二元扩域仿射子空间上多项式求值的加法FFT（AFFT）技术。关键是利用子空间湮没多项式的Taylor展开来分解AFFT为独立的列和行子AFFT。文中提出通用基AFFT，并特化到Cantor特殊基上得到两个算法：一个支持任意分解且Taylor展开阶段无需有限域乘法，另一个保持二项式形式，恰好需要1/2 n log₂ n次乘法。实现结果显示该算法在两个平台42种配置中37种快于LCH AFFT。此外还形式化了部分Cantor特殊基概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bailey的四步FFT算法为经典FFT提供了高效的矩阵分解框架，但类似的"四步"结构此前尚未被系统性地引入到二元扩域上的加法FFT中。作者希望将这一思想迁移到AFFT，建立统一的数学框架，以提高多项式求值效率并减少乘法/加法的复杂度。

Method: 利用子空间湮没多项式的Taylor展开，将AFFT分解为与矩阵行列对应的独立子AFFT（类似Bailey四步法）。先提出适用于任意有序基和任意维度拆分的通用基AFFT，然后特化到Cantor特殊基：第一个算法支持任意分解且Taylor展开无需有限域乘法；第二个算法采用保持子空间多项式二项式形式的分解，实现全递归结构。最后形式化部分Cantor特殊基，分析参数区间。

Result: 第二个算法恰好需要1/2 n log₂ n次乘法，加法定数为m二进制表示所确定的闭式表达式。在两个硬件平台42种配置中，37种配置下该算法快于LCH AFFT。在部分Cantor特殊基的参数区间分析中，von zur Gathen-Gerhard算法和通用基AFFT在加法和乘法上都优于第一个Gao-Mateer算法。

Conclusion: 通过Taylor展开框架成功将Bailey四步FFT的结构思想带入加法FFT领域，证明了Cantor特殊基上全递归AFFT由于内存局部性和避免基转换/求值分离而具有显著的实践优势。该工作为AFFT提供了统一的理论基线和更优的实践算法。

Abstract: Motivated by Bailey's four-step FFT algorithm (1989), we develop additive FFT techniques for polynomial evaluation over affine subspaces of binary extension fields. Our key insight is that the Taylor expansion with respect to vanishing polynomials of subspaces provides a structural counterpart to Bailey's matrix formulation. It decomposes an additive FFT (AFFT) into independent sub-AFFTs associated with the columns and rows of a matrix. We first present a general-basis AFFT that applies to any ordered basis and any split of the dimension, providing a unified baseline for measuring the gains from specialization. We then specialize the framework to the Cantor special basis and obtain two AFFT algorithms. The first supports an arbitrary decomposition of the AFFT dimension and exploits the Cantor special basis structure to perform the Taylor expansion stage without finite field multiplications. The second uses a decomposition that preserves the binomial form of the relevant subspace polynomials. It requires exactly $\frac{1}{2}n\log_2 n$ multiplications, together with a closed-form addition count determined by the binary representation of $m$. Our implementation results show that this algorithm is faster than the LCH AFFT over a Cantor special basis in 37 of the 42 configurations tested across two hardware platforms. This performance advantage stems from its fully recursive structure, which provides memory locality by design and avoids separate basis-conversion and evaluation stages. Finally, in a separate analysis, we formalize the notion of partial Cantor special bases and identify parameter regimes in which both the von zur Gathen-Gerhard algorithm and our general-basis AFFT require fewer additions and multiplications than the first Gao-Mateer algorithm.

</details>


### [329] [Vibe Coding and Web Application Security: A Twin-Prompt Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20963)
*Darko Andročec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在同一Agentic编码助手和相同模型版本下，通过仅追加安全需求章节（安全感知变体B）与基线变体A生成12个Web应用，并采用静态、依赖、动态和手动四种技术分析，发现安全感知变体在所有应用中的已确认安全问题更少（24 vs 51），且无Critical或High级别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型日益广泛地用于从自然语言提示生成完整Web应用，但明确请求安全最佳实践是否能改善生成结果的安全性尚不明确，需要实证研究来回答这一关键问题。

Method: 研究选取六个功能各异的Web应用，每个应用生成两个仅在末尾附加安全需求章节上不同的提示变体（基线A与安全感知B），在单一非迭代生成轮次下由同一Agentic编码助手和同版本模型生成12个程序，随后通过静态分析、依赖分析、动态分析和手动测试四类技术进行安全审计，对85个候选发现进行确认得到75个已确认发现。

Result: 安全感知变体B在每个应用中的已确认发现数量都少于基线变体A（24 vs 51），且B变体不存在Critical或High级别问题；最严重的发现仅通过手动测试检测到。由于语料库规模小且每个变体仅生成一次，结果以描述性观察而非统计性结论呈现。

Conclusion: 该工作定位为初步研究，表明在提示中显式请求安全最佳实践可能有助于降低LLM生成Web应用的安全漏洞数量和严重程度，但需将流水线扩展到多个模型和重复运行以验证效果的可推广性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete web applications from natural-language prompts, raising the question of whether explicitly requesting security best practice improves the result. We study six functionally distinct web applications, each generated in two prompt variants that are identical except for an appended security-requirements section: a baseline (A) and a security-aware (B) variant. All twelve programs were produced by the same agentic coding assistant and the same model version in a single, non-iterative generation round, and were then analyzed with static, dependency, dynamic and manual techniques, yielding 75 confirmed findings out of 85 candidates. The security-aware variant produced fewer confirmed findings in every application (24 versus 51) and contained no Critical or High issues; the most severe finding was detected only by manual testing. Because the corpus is small and each variant was generated once, we report descriptive observations rather than statistically established effects, and position the work as a preliminary study whose pipeline is being scaled to multiple models and repeated runs.

</details>


### [330] [Structured but Fragile: On the Limits of LLMs in Cybersecurity Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20966)
*Pasquale Malacaria,Yunxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在网络安全防御选择任务中的结构化推理能力。通过在真实攻击图（包括勒索软件、供应链攻击、云滥用、Kubernetes攻击等场景）上进行防御选择实验，发现LLM在显式攻击图结构下能产生接近博弈论最优解的防御策略，但其能力非常脆弱：对提示措辞高度敏感、随图复杂度增加而退化、评估排序与形式化风险存在非单调关系，且生成的求解器规模扩展性差。LLM能近似但无法稳健地应用结构化安全推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地被用于网络安全工作流，但尚不清楚它们是否具备真正的结构化安全推理能力，还是仅依赖表面线索和先验知识。作者希望在防御选择这一具体任务上系统评估LLM的推理质量，以指导AI辅助安全决策系统的设计与评估。

Method: 在由真实威胁场景（勒索软件、供应链攻击、云滥用、Kubernetes攻击、POS恶意软件、ICS/OT入侵）构建的攻击图上，给定预算约束，要求LLM选择安全控制以最小化攻击者成功概率。将LLM的策略与博弈论优化基线进行比较，该基线作为结构化推理的规范参照。通过改变提示措辞（如重标记非最优策略为"最优"）、增加图复杂度、让LLM生成求解器等方式，系统探测LLM推理的稳健性。

Result: 1) 当提供显式攻击图结构时，LLM通常能产生接近优化基线的连贯策略；2) 能力随图复杂度增加而脆弱退化；3) 对框架/提示措辞高度敏感，小改动即可大幅改变排名；4) 将差策略重标为"最优"可显著提升其评估得分；5) 形式化风险与LLM判断之间呈非单调关系（最接近最优的策略不一定是LLM评分最高的）；6) LLM生成的求解器能恢复正确的高层公式，但扩展性远不如专用求解器。

Conclusion: LLM在受控表示下可以近似结构化网络安全推理，但不会稳健地应用这种推理。其能力是条件性的、脆弱的，对提示框架和图复杂性高度敏感。这对AI辅助安全决策支持系统的设计和评估具有重要启示，暗示需要在系统设计中考虑校准机制、鲁棒性验证以及对人机协作界面的审慎设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in cybersecurity workflows, yet it remains unclear whether they can perform structured security reasoning or merely rely on superficial cues and prior knowledge. We study this question in the context of defence selection over attack graphs derived from real-world threat scenarios, including ransomware, supply-chain compromise, cloud abuse, Kubernetes attacks, POS malware, and ICS/OT intrusion. Given a budget constraint, LLMs must select security controls to minimise attacker success. We compare their strategies against each other and against a game-theoretic optimization baseline used as a normative reference for structured reasoning. Our results show that LLMs exhibit conditional competence. When explicit attack-graph structure is provided, they often produce coherent strategies close to the optimization baseline. However, their capabilities are fragile. LLM behaviour becomes increasingly fragile with graph complexity and is highly sensitive to framing. Small prompt changes can substantially alter rankings, and merely relabeling a poor strategy as ``optimal'' dramatically improves its evaluation. We further observe a non-monotonic relationship between formal risk and LLM judgement: strategies closest to the optimum are not necessarily ranked highest by LLM evaluators. To further probe reasoning ability, we ask LLMs to generate solvers for the same optimization problem. While the generated implementations recover the correct high-level formulation, they scale poorly compared to a purpose-built solver. Overall, our findings show that LLMs can approximate structured cybersecurity reasoning under controlled representations, but do not apply it robustly. This has important implications for the design and evaluation of AI-assisted security decision-support systems.

</details>


### [331] [$Z^2$-ACT: End-to-End Verifiable Agentic Intent Control for Open 6G RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21049)
*Sunder Ali Khowaja,Kapal Dev,George C. Alexandropoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Z²-ACT架构，将零知识审计控制与零信任可验证智能体意图集成到6G开放RAN中，整合四类原始原语（智能体协调、形式化意图约束、零信任提示验证、密码学问责），以实现跨非实时和近实时RIC的安全AI辅助控制环路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放和解聚的6G RAN需要支持多供应商，但AI辅助控制环路在并发运营商意图和不可信模型输入下必须保持安全、可验证和可审计。现有研究孤立地处理智能体协调、形式化意图约束、零信任提示验证和密码学问责，单独使用时无法完整实现预实现安全性、持续语义验证和跨域审计。

Method: 提出Z²-ACT架构，将四类原始原语集成到非实时和近实时RIC中：(1) 将类型化Intent Contracts编码为运营商目标；(2) 仅在通过实用对抗性意图检查后才接受LLM输入；(3) 技能序列仅在满足自管理门控条件下发布；(4) 每次成功提交记录为带零知识证明的绑定承诺。实验采用公开ColO-RAN数据，与消融实验和传统强化学习基线对比。

Result: 在非实时路径使用真实LLM将运营商意图翻译为Intent Contracts，报告了翻译准确率、无效或幻觉合同率、非实时延迟及对抗/误导意图下的行为。近实时控制基于公开KPM序列进行trace驱动。结果表明在近实时时延和信令成本范围内改善了执行过滤和攻击鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Z²-ACT在保持近实时性能包络（适度时延和信令开销）的同时，显著提升了执行过滤能力和对抗攻击韧性，为多供应商开放6G RAN中的安全AI控制提供了系统化认证框架。

Abstract: With the progression in open and disaggregated 6G radio access networks, it is expected that the system will be able to host multi-vendors. In order to host multi-vendors, it is essential that AI-assisted control loops remain safe, verifiable, and auditable under concurrent operator intents and untrusted model inputs. The existing studies address the agentic coordination, formal intent constraints, zero-trust prompt verification and cryptographic accountability in isolation, which leaves pre-realization safety, continuous semantic verification and cross-domain audit incomplete when used individually. In this regard, we propose zero-knowledge auditable control and zero-trust verifiable agentic intent architecture ($Z^2$-ACT), which integrates the aforementioned four primitives across the non-real-time and near-real-time RICs. We encode the typed Intent Contracts as operator goals while the large language model inputs are only admitted after a practical adversarial intent check. The skill sequences in the proposed study are released only when a self-management gate is satisfied while every successful commit is recorded as a binding commitment with a zero-knowledge proof. Our experimental evaluation on public ColO-RAN measurements compares the full architecture against targeted ablations and a conventional reinforcement-learning baseline. A live large language model is used in the non-real-time path to translate operator intents into Intent Contracts; we report translation accuracy, the rate of invalid or hallucinated contracts, non-real-time latency, and behavior under adversarial or misleading intents. Near-real-time control remains trace-driven on the public KPM sequences. Results indicate improved actuation filtering and attack resilience at modest latency and signaling cost inside the near-real-time envelope.

</details>


### [332] [ClawSentry: A Progressive Multi-Tier Security Monitor for Safeguarding Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21101)
*Kai Wang,Zeming Wei,BiaoJie Zeng,Chang Jin,An Wang,Xiaokun Luan,Zhixiao Lin,Jingjing Qu,Xia Hu,Xingcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ClawSentry是一个框架无关的开源安全监督网关，针对LLM智能体在技能采纳、调用意图、执行效果和事后后果四个控制环节点上的渐进式风险，提供确定性审核与渐进式推理引擎结合的防护，将Contextual ASR从39.55%降至2.61%且几乎不牺牲TSR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从对话转向执行代码、读取文件、编排外部工具，被恶意第三方技能劫持会导致数据泄露、权限提升或级联破坏。现有防护通常局限于单一生命周期边界或单次调用，无法应对危险目标在不同表面形式、工具或轮次中的重现。

Method: ClawSentry提出四倍风险模型（技能采纳、调用时意图、执行时效果、事后后果），包含三个核心组件：(1) 首次使用技能包审查（FSPR）--确定性证据底限审计，未决案件升级为只读智能体审查；(2) 三层渐进式决策引擎--确定性L1层、规则锚定L2语义审查器、只读L3证据寻求智能体，仅对残余歧义消费上下文审查，并配备会话级反绕过机制识别工具切换和改写重试；(3) 代理编排协议（AHP）抽象—跨Codex、Claude Code、Kimi CLI和Gemini CLI统一策略，无需修改智能体内部。另有事后路径将高严重度证据非追溯性喂入后续审查。

Result: 在SkillInject基准（Codex/GPT-5.4）上，Contextual ASR从39.55%降至2.61%，Contextual TSR仅从83.78%微降至83.05%。在SkillsSafety基准五个Work Agent上，ASR从无防护的33.5-49.7%降至9.09-15.03%，干净技能的聚合TSR保持98.7%。

Conclusion: ClawSentry通过在四个控制环节点上提供渐进式审查，并在保持高可用性（TSR几乎不降）的同时大幅降低攻击成功率（ASR），证明了框架无关的安全监督网关能有效缓解LLM智能体的渐进式攻击风险。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents move from conversation to executing code, reading local files, and orchestrating external tools, a single agent hijacked by a malicious third-party skill can cause data exfiltration, privilege escalation, or cascading compromise. We argue that agentic risk is progressive: it can enter at four loci of the agent control loop--skill admission, invocation-time intent, execution-time effect, and post-action consequence--while a denied dangerous objective can reappear across surface forms, tools, or turns; existing safeguards are typically local to one lifecycle boundary or one call. Guided by this threat model, we present ClawSentry, an open-source, framework-agnostic security supervision gateway for agent runtimes. Before a skill package is ever executed, First-use Skill Package Review (FSPR) audits it under a deterministic evidence floor, escalating unresolved cases to bounded read-only agentic review (locus A). At runtime, a three-tier progressive decision engine--a deterministic L1 layer, a rule-anchored L2 semantic reviewer, and a read-only L3 evidence-seeking agent--spends contextual review only on the residual ambiguity, while a session-level anti-bypass mechanism recognizes tool-switching and rephrased retries (loci B--C); a post-action path feeds high-severity evidence non-retroactively into later review (locus D). An Agent Harness Protocol (AHP) abstraction applies one policy across Codex, Claude Code, Kimi CLI, and Gemini CLI without modifying agent internals. On SkillInject with Codex/GPT-5.4, contextual ASR falls from 39.55% to 2.61% while contextual TSR moves only from 83.78% to 83.05%. Across five Work Agents on the full SkillsSafety benchmark, ClawSentry confines ASR to 9.09--15.03% from 33.5--49.7% unprotected, and aggregate TSR on clean skills remains 98.7%.

</details>


### [333] [TraceGrant: A Contract-Governed Security Framework for the Task-Effect Lifecycle of Networked LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21126)
*Bohao Liao,Jingchao Wang,Qipeng Song,Jin Cao,Jieling Wang,Boyu Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TraceGrant是一个安全框架，通过显式的契约（Contract）来治理网络化LLM代理的任务-效果生命周期，在任务执行前建立边界、执行中仅允许契约既定的权限被实例化、执行后验证任务完成情况。在949个AgentDojo和400个Agent Security Bench攻击案例中实现零攻击成功，同时保持77.32%和83.00%的效用保持率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化LLM代理需要从多个外部服务检索信息以完成具有持久外部影响的多步任务，但合法执行所需的内容中可能包含间接提示注入，从而重定向工具使用、篡改敏感参数或中断任务完成。现有防御主要约束不可信内容或单次工具调用，未将用户意图、运行时证据、实际效果和任务完成情况充分关联起来。

Method: TraceGrant通过三个阶段治理任务-效果生命周期：（1）执行前，从可信的用户请求中建立任务-效果边界（Contract）；（2）执行中，只有被Contract已经确立的权限才能被所接受的证据实例化；（3）执行后，基于实际工具结果验证任务完成情况。

Result: 在949个AgentDojo和400个Agent Security Bench攻击案例的固定基准设置下，TraceGrant未记录任何攻击成功案例，同时在攻击率下的效用保持分别为77.32%和83.00%。此外，通过白盒防御感知攻击、契约质量分析、阶段消融、针对性压力测试和运行时开销测量进一步验证。

Conclusion: TraceGrant提供了一个统一的治理层，将可信用户意图、运行时证据、具体工具执行和已验证的任务完成情况连接起来，为网络化LLM代理提供了有效的安全防护方案。

Abstract: Networked large language model (LLM) agents retrieve information from email, cloud storage, calendars, transaction platforms, and Web services to complete multistep tasks that produce persistent external effects. The same content needed for legitimate execution may also contain indirect prompt injections that redirect tool use, alter sensitive arguments, or disrupt task completion. Existing defenses mainly constrain untrusted content or individual tool calls, leaving user intent, runtime evidence, realized effects, and task completion insufficiently connected. We present TraceGrant, a security framework that governs the task-effect lifecycle of networked LLM agents through an explicit Contract. Before execution, TraceGrant establishes a task-effect boundary from the trusted user request. During execution, admitted evidence can instantiate only authority already established by the Contract. After execution, task completion is verified against actual tool results. Across 949 AgentDojo and 400 Agent Security Bench attack cases under fixed benchmark settings, TraceGrant recorded no attack successes while retaining utility under attack rates of 77.32% and 83.00%, respectively. We further evaluate TraceGrant through white-box defense-aware attacks, Contract quality analysis, stage ablations, targeted stress tests, and runtime overhead measurements. The results show that TraceGrant provides a unified governance layer that connects trusted user intent, runtime evidence, concrete tool execution, and verified task completion.

</details>


### [334] [Billion-Scale Nearest-Neighbor Search under Fully Homomorphic Encryption on a Single GPU, Balancing Leakage and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21131)
*Isamu Isozaki,Madison Bratina,Edward Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于全同态加密(FHE)的私有相似性搜索系统，服务器可在不看到明文查询的情况下完成对数十亿向量的最相似检索。通过秩缩减和层级路由在GPU上加速，在DataComp-1B(13.9亿向量)上达到recall@10=0.90、约6秒/查询的延迟，在Deep1B上达到recall@10=0.90、2.3秒/查询。同时揭示了层级路由访问模式会泄露数据库几何信息的隐私风险，并提出固定分组填充可将泄露从72%降至约2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决私有相似性检索的核心矛盾：服务器需要计算查询与数据库向量的相似度，但又不应看到查询内容。FHE为加密计算提供了理论方案，但在十亿级向量规模下对全库逐行加密打分过于缓慢，亟需可扩展的加速策略。

Method: 结合两大技术：(1)秩缩减——降低每个向量的维度以加速加密下的打分运算；(2)层级路由——通过粗粒度分桶将检索范围从全库缩小到少量候选集，避免全量扫描。整套流程在FHE密文下于单一GPU上执行，并设计了完整的客户端-服务器协议以保证可复现性。

Result: 在DataComp-1B(13.9亿向量,512维CLIP)上达到recall@10=0.90(含近重复图像时为0.95)，热延迟约6秒/查询；轻量配置在约1.8秒达到0.78/0.83。在Deep1B(10亿向量,96维)上全层级FHE下recall@10=0.90，热延迟2.3秒，与明文路由的0.906高度一致(96→128零填充相关性1.0)。隐私方面，层级访问模式可恢复72%的粗粒度邻居图；固定分组填充可将其降至约2%，而朴素填充易被重复查询攻击破解。

Conclusion: 研究表明FHE私密相似性检索可以在十亿级规模上以实用延迟(秒级)运行，但隐私并非免费——层级加速带来的访问模式泄露需要通过针对性填充策略来缓解，在效率与隐私之间需要权衡。

Abstract: We build a system that answers "which database vectors are most similar to my query?" without the server ever seeing the query. The query is encrypted with fully homomorphic en- cryption (FHE); the server does all its scoring on ciphertexts and returns encrypted results that only the client can read. The challenge is speed: at a billion vectors, scoring every row under encryption is far too slow, so we combine two ideas - rank reduction (shrink each vector's dimen- sion) and a hierarchy (route to a small candidate set instead of scanning everything) - executed under encryption on a single GPU. We evaluate on three corpora at very different scales: a face corpus of 222 049 centroids clustered from ~10 M face images (512-dim), DataComp-1B (1.39 x 10^9 vectors, 512-dim CLIP), and Deep1B (10^9 vectors, 96-dim). On DataComp-1B we reach a recall@10 of 0.90 against the single labeled answer, or 0.95 when a near-duplicate im- age in the top-10 also counts as correct (the data is web-scraped and full of duplicates), at ~6 s per encrypted query on a GPU; a lighter configuration reaches 0.78/0.83 at ~1.8 s. These are warm (deployable) server-side latencies - client decryption and network transfer are excluded. On Deep1B we reach recall@10 0.90 under all-levels FHE (0.9045 measured over 2000 FHE queries, matching the 0.906 plaintext routing - the 96 -> 128 zero-pad is exact, correlation 1.0) at 2.3 s warm per query. We describe the full client-server protocol in enough detail to repro- duce it, and report accuracy and latency for every configuration. We also measure what this speed costs: the hierarchy's access pattern leaks the database geometry (an observer recovers 72% of the coarse-cell neighbor graph from access patterns alone), and we show that seeded (fixed-group) padding cuts this leak by ~35x (to ~2%), where naive padding is defeated by a repeated-query attack.

</details>


### [335] [AID-Guard: Stateful Authorization for Delegated Agent Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21159)
*Yingzhe Tong,Leyu Dai,Songhui Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AID-Guard,一个状态化的授权到效果闭包协议,通过提交时重验证请求和提供者状态、在模糊情况下保留单一预留、仅允许在终态结果或带交付栅栏的无效果证明后释放或继任,从而在重试和恢复过程中保证一个预留最多产生一个效果。实现了Python/SQLite原型,在多种提供者合约和故障场景下验证了无重复效果和授权到效果绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用工具的AI代理将委派任务转化为提供者副作用,但授权通常在准入阶段就结束,而提供者状态、交付、重试和恢复过程仍持续演化。请求可能在提交前改变,或响应丢失导致替换请求从一个批准中产生第二个效果。需要一种协议来跨越整个生命周期保持授权的安全边界。

Method: 提出AID-Guard协议,在提交时重新验证已批准的请求和提供者状态;在歧义情况下保留单一预留;只有在终态结果或带交付栅栏的认证无效果后才允许释放或一个继任者。设计了协议原型,并针对MCP回环域、Stripe/Resend提供者合约以及组合攻击场景进行了系统评估。

Result: 在声明式回环MCP域中,13个实时突变无未授权提供者效果,3个并发历史可线性化。210个Stripe合约试验与预声明结果匹配;40个终结-继任调度、30个重叠竞争、10个崩溃恢复调度均无重复效果。在完全提议者妥协下,阻断44/44攻击并接受44/44合法提议。严格精确清单配置使良性效用降低35.4-43.8个百分点,类型化边界恢复9-10个完成且无观察到不安全效果。组合研究阻断20/20准入后生命周期攻击并保留8/8合法或精确重试执行。

Conclusion: AID-Guard的评估结果支持在测试的效果路径清单、提供者合约和故障调度下,授权到效果绑定的可行性,是首个在单一生命周期中统一这些控制的可评估代理授权协议。

Abstract: Tool-using AI agents turn delegated tasks into provider effects, yet authorization often ends at admission while provider state, delivery, retry, and recovery evolve. A request may change before commit, or response loss may cause a replacement to create a second effect from one approval. We present AID-Guard, a stateful authorization-to-effect closure protocol. It revalidates the approved request and provider state at commit, retains one reservation under ambiguity, and permits release or one successor only after a terminal result or certified no effect with a delivery fence. For supported provider contracts, one reservation yields at most one effect across retry and recovery. To our knowledge, it is the first evaluated agent-authorization protocol to unify these controls in one lifecycle.
  We implement a Python/SQLite prototype. In a declared loopback MCP domain, 13 live mutations caused no unauthorized provider effects, three concurrent histories were linearizable, and evidence bundles supported public verification and replay. All 210 Stripe provider-contract trials matched predeclared outcomes. Across Stripe and Resend, 40 terminalize-successor schedules, 30 overlapping races, and 10 crash-recovery schedules completed without duplicate effects. Under complete proposer compromise, AID-Guard blocked 44/44 attacks and admitted 44/44 matched legitimate proposals. Its strict exact-manifest profile reduced benign utility by 35.4 to 43.8 percentage points; a typed frontier recovered 9-10 completions without observed unsafe effects. A composition study blocked 20/20 post-admission lifecycle attacks and preserved 8/8 valid or exact-retry executions. The results support authorization-to-effect binding under the evaluated effect-path inventory, provider contracts, and failure schedules.

</details>


### [336] [Workplace Surveillance and Insider Threat Risk Management: Legal Limits and Privacy Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21205)
*Haywood Gelman,John D. Hastings,Suvineetha Herath,Quentin Covert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨工作场所监控与内部威胁检测之间的平衡，指出过度监控可能违反法律和隐私原则，并提出改进监控透明度、开展内部威胁培训、优化检测工具等建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织使用工作场所监控来保护资产和监测员工生产力，但过度监控可能引发法律合规和隐私问题。同时，内部威胁是监控的主要安全目标，如何在不侵犯隐私的前提下有效检测内部威胁成为一个关键研究问题。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，通过调查内部威胁角色画像以理解行为和心理检测标准，回顾员工监控工具与技术以刻画监控格局，并审视工作场所隐私法律、过度监控案例及其隐私损害。

Result: 识别出与研究差距相关的过度监控问题，包括产生过量告警可能掩盖有意义的内部威胁指标；同时提出了提高监控透明度、实施内部威胁培训计划、调整内部威胁工具以检测相关心理和行为指标的改进建议。

Conclusion: 工作场所监控需在合法目的与隐私保护之间取得平衡；通过透明度提升、培训强化和工具优化，可以改善组织检测内部威胁的能力，同时降低过度监控带来的隐私风险。

Abstract: Workplace surveillance is used by organizations to protect corporate assets and monitor employee productivity. This research presents two central arguments on workplace surveillance: although surveillance serves legitimate organizational purposes, over-surveillance can violate legal requirements and data privacy principles; and a primary security objective of workplace surveillance is the detection of insider threats (InT). InT are comprised of individuals with authorized resource access whose intentional or unintentional actions may damage or compromise corporate assets. This paper investigates InT personas to understand behavioral and psychological detection criteria. Employee surveillance tools and techniques are reviewed to characterize the employee surveillance landscape. Workplace privacy laws, examples of over-surveillance, and the resulting privacy harms are addressed. The review identifies research gaps related to over-surveillance, including the generation of excessive alerts that may obscure meaningful InT indicators. The paper concludes with recommendations to improve workplace surveillance transparency, implement InT training programs to improve organizational detection capabilities, and tune InT tools to detect relevant psychological and behavioral indicators.

</details>


### [337] [Utility Under Attack: Agent Memory Poisoning and the Limits of Content Screening and Provenance Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21230)
*Arulnidhi Karunanidhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现大规模语言模型的持久性记忆使得虚假信息一旦写入即难以清除：仅污染1.2%的LongMemEval语料即可将准确率从0.850降至0.300；内容级筛选管线对虚假断言完全无效；基于来源加权的检索防御在参数上不存在可用设置，作者主张采用有界占用约束而非加性来源惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性记忆（persistent memory）使得虚假信息一旦被存储，就可能在未来的会话中被检索出来，构成一种独特且持久的失败模式。现有防御主要依赖内容筛选或来源加权检索，但它们能否有效抵御这种"单次写入、长期污染"的攻击尚未被系统评估。

Method: 1) 在LongMemEval语料中注入1.2%的普通措辞虚假断言进行污染实验；2) 评估四阶段写入时筛选管线对间接提示注入（0.832召回率）的表现及其对中毒记忆的拒绝率；3) 对来源加权检索进行统计分析，比较默认权重（p=0.80，与无防御无显著差异）与更强权重在混合来源语料中的效果。

Result: 污染1.2%语料使准确率从0.850降至0.300；筛选管线拒绝了360条中毒记忆中的0条；默认来源权重与无防御统计上不可区分，更强权重只在排除不可信内容时恢复效用；当答案相关证据本身来自不可信来源时，证据召回降至零，准确率降至0.0417。

Conclusion: 内容级筛选存在根本性边界——区分真假断言需要文本之外的外部依据；加性来源惩罚在测量的相似性机制下无可用参数设置。作者主张在检索时采用有界占用约束而非加性来源惩罚，并发布相关工具、语料和报告。

Abstract: Persistent memory makes false information durable: once a false statement is stored, it can be retrieved into future sessions that match it. We measure the cost of this failure mode using plainly worded false assertions generated in a single pass, with no instruction, trigger, or retriever optimization. Poisoning 1.2% of a LongMemEval corpus reduces accuracy from 0.850 to 0.300. A four-stage write-time screening pipeline that reaches 0.832 recall on indirect prompt injection while flagging 1.5% of trigger-word-laden benign text rejects 0 of 360 poisoned memories. We argue this exposes a boundary of content-only screening: distinguishing a false assertion from a true one generally requires external grounding beyond the text itself.
  We then evaluate provenance-weighted retrieval. The shipped weight is statistically indistinguishable from no defense (p=0.80), while a stronger weight recovers utility only by excluding untrusted content. In a mixed-provenance corpus where untrusted content is mostly benign, accuracy rises from 0.3167 to 0.7000; when the answer-bearing evidence itself arrives untrusted, evidence recall falls to zero and accuracy to 0.0417. Under the measured similarity regime, the additive provenance term has no usable setting: a weight strong enough to resist query-shaped poison is also strong enough to suppress legitimate untrusted evidence. We therefore argue for bounded occupancy constraints at retrieval rather than additive provenance penalties, and release the harnesses, corpora, and aggregate run reports.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [338] [Event-triggered Implicit Perturbation for Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21223)
*Tengteng Lei,Prabodh Katti,Rashi Dutt,Houssem Sifaou,Tan Peng,Osvaldo Simeone,Kai Xu,Bipin Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向脉冲神经网络（SNN）在存内计算（IMC）加速器上微调的隐式扰动零阶优化（IPZO）架构，通过事件触发的扰动生成单元（PGU）和地址驱动的XOR重组方案（PGU-XOR）消除显式权重扰动带来的重复读写（RMW）操作和随机数生成器（RNG）的硬件开销，在精度、能效和面积之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶（ZO）优化适合微调非可微的脉冲神经网络（SNN），但在存内计算（IMC）加速器上部署面临两大挑战：(1) 显式权重扰动导致的重复读-修改-写（RMW）操作破坏IMC的权重固定（weight-stationary）执行模式；(2) 生成统计独立逐权重扰动所需的随机数生成器（RNG）硬件开销过大。

Method: 提出隐式扰动ZO架构（IPZO）：(1) 事件触发扰动生成单元（PGU）计算扰动和，并与IMC阵列产生的加权和结合，消除扰动引起的RMW操作，保持权重固定执行；(2) 利用脉冲稀疏性，PGU仅对脉冲激活的权重行生成和累加扰动贡献，减小RNG阵列所需行数；(3) 引入地址驱动的XOR重组方案（PGU-XOR）缓解直接RNG重用（PGU-Reuse）导致的空间相关性。

Result: (1) 在Spikingformer/CIFAR-10上PGU-XOR达到76.41%准确率（软件RNG为76.53%），在SpikeGPT/WikiText-2上PPL为54.20（软件RNG为53.23），而PGU-Reuse准确率下降9.56个百分点、PPL增加11.8；(2) 在TSMC 16nm工艺下，PGU-XOR较PGU-Reuse每次矩阵向量乘增加40.3%-46.0%面积和15.2%-48.9%能量开销，但因收敛更快，总扰动能量仅为PGU-Reuse的0.51倍（等精度下）；(3) 在批量B=64、T=4时间步条件下，IPZO相比传统显式权重扰动将扰动能量降至0.46x-0.83x，且优势随BT减小而增大。

Conclusion: IPZO架构成功解决了ZO优化在IMC加速器上部署的核心瓶颈，通过隐式扰动和地址驱动XOR重组的硬件方案，在几乎不损失精度的前提下显著降低了扰动能量和硬件开销，且对不同的批量和时间步设置具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization estimates gradients using only forward-pass evaluations, making it suitable for fine-tuning non-differentiable, event-driven spiking neural networks (SNNs). However, its deployment on in-memory computing (IMC) accelerators is constrained by the repeated read-modify-write (RMW) operations arising from explicit weight perturbation and the prohibitive hardware footprint of random number generators (RNGs) for statistically independent per-weight perturbations. To address these challenges, we propose an implicit-perturbation ZO (IPZO) architecture in which perturbation sums computed by an event-triggered perturbation generation unit (PGU) are combined with the weighted sums produced by the IMC array, eliminating perturbation-induced RMW operations while preserving weight-stationary execution of IMC. By exploiting spike sparsity, the PGU generates and accumulates perturbation contributions only for spike-activated weight rows, reducing the required row dimension of the RNG array. An address-driven XOR recombination scheme (PGU-XOR) is further introduced to mitigate the spatial correlations caused by direct RNG reuse (PGU-Reuse). The results show that (1) PGU-XOR matches software RNGs in accuracy on Spikingformer/CIFAR-10 (76.41% vs. 76.53%) and perplexity (PPL) on SpikeGPT/WikiText-2 (54.20 vs. 53.23), whereas PGU-Reuse degrades accuracy by 9.56 percentage points and increases PPL by 11.8; (2) implemented in a TSMC 16-nm CMOS technology, PGU-XOR incurs 40.3%-46.0% area and 15.2%-48.9% energy overhead per matrix-vector multiplication relative to PGU-Reuse, yet its faster convergence reduces the total perturbation energy to 0.51x that of PGU-Reuse at iso-accuracy; (3) IPZO reduces the perturbation energy to 0.46x-0.83x that of conventional explicit weight perturbation for a batch size of B=64 and T=4 time steps, with the advantage growing as BT decreases.

</details>


### [339] [SPICE: Speculative Prefetching with Low-Rank Expert Surrogates and Heterogeneous Orchestration for MoE Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21240)
*Yongxiang Lyu,Ning Li,Bonian Jia*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPICE是一个用于MoE模型卸载的推测性预取框架，结合轻量级专家预测与置信度感知的CPU-GPU编排，在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Qwen2-57B-A14B上实现最高3.12倍TPOT加速且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然通过稀疏激活解耦了模型容量与计算成本，但大量专家参数的驻留内存需求常超过GPU显存容量，导致推理延迟主要由专家加载的主机到设备PCIe传输所主导。

Method: SPICE包含两个核心组件：(1) 构建与目标MoE架构对齐的轻量级草稿模型，采用置信度感知的自适应前瞻算法预取高置信度专家；(2) 当预测未命中时，采用成本感知的CPU-GPU异构编排：低置信度未命中由驻留的共享专家和低秩专家（LoRE）代理近似，而精确的残差工作被卸载到CPU异步执行，与GPU计算并行。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Qwen2-57B-A14B及多种GPU平台上评估，SPICE在Time Per Output Token (TPOT)上实现最高3.12倍加速，同时保持最小质量损失。

Conclusion: 有效的MoE卸载不仅需要预测未来专家，还需要决策哪些未命中值得近似、哪些需要精确恢复、以及精确残差工作应在何处执行。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are increasingly used in LLMs because sparse activation decouples model capacity from compute cost. However, the large expert parameter footprint often exceeds GPU memory capacity, making inference latency dominated by the host-to-device PCIe transfers for expert loading. To address these challenges, this paper presents SPICE, a speculative prefetching framework for MoE offloading that combines lightweight expert prediction with confidence-aware CPU-GPU orchestration. On one hand, SPICE builds a lightweight draft model aligned with the target MoE architecture, using a confidence-aware adaptive lookahead algorithm to prefetch high-confidence experts. On the other hand, when speculative predictions miss, SPICE switches to a cost-aware CPU-GPU heterogeneous orchestration: low-confidence misses are approximated by the resident shared expert with low rank expert (LoRE) surrogates, while exact residual work is offloaded to the CPU and executed asynchronously in parallel with ongoing GPU computation. Evaluated on DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Qwen2-57B-A14B across diverse GPU platforms, SPICE achieves up to 3.12 speedup in Time Per Output Token (TPOT) with minimal quality loss, showing that effective MoE offloading requires not only predicting future experts, but also deciding which misses deserve approximation, which require exact recovery, and where exact residual work should execute.

</details>


### [340] [Assessing Triple Modular Redundancy for Wide-Link, Low-Latency NoC Routers: Reliability and Physical Design Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21288)
*Chen Wu,Michael Rogenmoser,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对物理AI芯片中NoC路由器在恶劣环境下的单粒子效应（SEE）防护问题，评估了粗粒度、仅状态和全量三种粒度的三模冗余（TMR）方案。基于TSMC 7nm工艺从RTL到GDSII完成物理设计，并在RT级和网表级进行SEU/SET故障注入。结果表明仅全量TMR能消除所有故障，虽在独立路由器层面带来7.04倍面积开销，但在完整AI加速tile中仅增加16.8%面积和15.2%功耗，且不影响关键路径，证明先进工艺节点使全量TMR成为实际可行的NoC防护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可靠NoC研究多聚焦于窄链路（如32位）、深流水线路由器和单粒子翻转（SEU），但当前NoC已演进为超宽链路（512位）、低延迟、工作频率超1GHz的先进工艺路由器，其面临的SEE风险（包括SEU和SET）尚未得到充分评估。需要系统性研究在不同TMR粒度下超宽链路NoC路由器的可靠性与成本权衡。

Method: 在TSMC 7nm工艺下，对2周期延迟、512位宽链路的NoC路由器实施三种粒度的TMR（粗粒度、仅状态、全量），完整走完RTL到GDSII的物理设计流程，并分别在RTL级和网表级开展SEU与SET故障注入实验，评估可靠性、成本和物理设计策略。评估范围从独立路由器扩展到完整AI加速tile（含处理器和L1存储器），在GEMM基准下衡量系统级面积、功耗和关键路径开销。

Result: 故障注入超过一百万次/实验表明：粗粒度和仅状态TMR不足以抵御SEE，而全量TMR消除了所有观测到的故障。独立全量TMR路由器面积开销为7.04倍，但在集成到完整AI加速tile后，面积仅增加16.8%、功耗增加15.2%，关键路径完全不受影响，证明先进工艺节点的布线与资源充裕度足以容纳全量TMR的开销。

Conclusion: 在超宽链路、高频率的先进工艺NoC路由器中，更细粒度的TMR并非总是更安全——仅状态和粗粒度TMR均存在保护盲区，只有全量TMR能提供完备的SEE防护。更重要的是，虽然独立层面的TMR开销看似高昂，但在系统级集成后开销大幅摊销，使全量TMR成为物理AI系统在恶劣环境中保护NoC的切实可部署方案。

Abstract: Protecting the Network-on-Chip (NoC) of physical-AI tile-based accelerators deployed in harsh environments against single-event effects (SEEs) is paramount for preventing NoC failures that can lead to deadlocks and silent data corruption (SDC). Prior work on reliable NoCs has largely focused on narrow links (e.g., 32-bit), deeply pipelined routers, and single-event upsets (SEUs). However, the state of the art has evolved toward low-latency NoC routers with ultra-wide links, implemented on advanced technology nodes and operating at frequencies above 1 GHz. We evaluate the cost and reliability trade-offs of implementing Triple Modular Redundancy (TMR) at three granularities (coarse, state-only, and full) for a 2-cycle-latency NoC router with 512-bit wide links. We carry out RTL-to-GDSII physical design in TSMC 7nm technology, as well as both RTL- and netlist-level SEU and SET fault injection campaigns. We evaluate the three TMR approaches in terms of reliability, cost, and physical design strategies, further extending the assessment from a standalone router to a full AI acceleration tile. Our results show that state-only and coarse-grained TMR do not provide sufficient protection against SEEs, whereas full TMR eliminates all observed failures across more than one million injected faults per experiment. Although the standalone full-TMR router incurs a 7.04x area overhead, this cost is drastically amortized once integrated into a complete AI accelerator tile with processors and local L1 memories: the same design adds only 16.8% area and 15.2% power consumption under a GEMM benchmark at the system level, with the critical path of the tile entirely unaffected. These results demonstrate that advanced technology nodes provide sufficient routing capacity to make full TMR a practical and deployable solution for protecting NoCs in Physical AI systems operating in harsh environments.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [341] [Rigorous Evaluation of Large Language Models for Malaria Drug Discovery: Trade-offs in Performance, Scale, and Resource Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20418)
*Marvellous O. Ajala,Zainab Ashimiyu-Abdusalam,Comfort Adesina*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文介绍Malaria-Instruct数据集（基于ChEMBL Legacy Malaria语料构建的指令遵循数据集），系统评估了五个开源LLM（Gemma-2 2B/9B、TxGemma-2B/9B和LlaSMol-Mistral-7B）在抗疟疾虚拟筛选中的应用，并与经典ML模型及专有前沿模型（Gemini 2.5、OpenAI o3）在严格分布外分割下进行对比。微调后的LLM显著优于所有基线，TxGemma-9B取得最高ROC-AUC（0.731），LlaSMol-Mistral-7B取得最佳富集因子（EF@1%≈4.99），且领域特化微调被证明是不可或缺的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗疟疾虚拟筛选（VS）是药物发现中的关键任务，但现有方法在结构挑战性（分布外）条件下往往表现不佳。作者旨在探索开源LLM是否能在该任务中超越经典机器学习和专有推理模型，同时验证领域微调和预训练策略（生物医学预训练 vs 化学感知预训练）的相对价值，为资源受限场景下的抗疟疾筛选提供高效方案。

Method: 作者构建了Malaria-Instruct指令微调数据集，来源于ChEMBL Legacy Malaria语料库。他们系统评估了五个开源LLM（Gemma-2 2B/9B、TxGemma-2B/9B、LlaSMol-Mistral-7B），在严格的分布外数据划分下进行few-shot评测，并与Random Forest、XGBoost等经典ML模型以及Gemini 2.5、OpenAI o3等专有推理模型对比，采用ROC-AUC和EF@1%等指标评估。

Result: 微调后的开源LLM显著超越所有基线：TxGemma-9B达到最高ROC-AUC（0.731±0.005），LlaSMol-Mistral-7B获得最佳富集因子（EF@1%≈4.99）。领域特化微调至关重要——TxGemma-9B未经微调时ROC-AUC从0.731骤降至0.499；专有模型Gemini 2.5（~0.53）和o3（~0.59）在无微调情况下无法达到可靠判别。生物医学预训练在同规模下提供可测优势，化学感知预训练则带来更好的前瞻性富集效果。

Conclusion: 微调后的开源LLM代表了抗疟疾虚拟筛选的一种具有说服力且资源高效的范式，在结构挑战性条件下同时超越经典ML流程和专有推理模型。该工作为疟疾药物发现领域提供了开源、可复现的高效筛选方案。

Abstract: We introduce Malaria-Instruct, a curated instruction-following dataset derived from the ChEMBL Legacy Malaria corpus for Malaria virtual screening, and conduct a systematic evaluation of five open-source LLMs; Gemma-2 2B/9B, TxGemma-2B/9B, and LlaSMol-Mistral-7B, on a rigorous out-of-distribution data split. Performance was benchmarked against classical ML models (Random Forest, XGBoost) and frontier proprietary models (Gemini 2.5, OpenAI o3) under few-shot conditions. Fine-tuned LLMs substantially outperformed all baselines: TxGemma-9B achieved the highest ROC-AUC ($0.731 \pm 0.005$) and LlaSMol-Mistral-7B the best enrichment factor (EF@1\% $\approx$ 4.99). Domain-specific fine-tuning proved categorically indispensable with TxGemma-9B collapsing from ROC-AUC 0.731 to 0.499, under its best few-shot condition, and neither Gemini 2.5 (ROC-AUC $\approx$ 0.53) nor o3 (ROC-AUC $\approx$ 0.59) achieved reliable discrimination without fine-tuning. Biomedical pretraining conferred a measurable advantage at equivalent scale, while chemistry-aware pretraining yielded superior prospective enrichment. Fine-tuned open-source LLMs represent a compelling, resource-efficient paradigm for antimalarial VS, outperforming both classical pipelines and proprietary reasoning models under structurally challenging conditions.

</details>


### [342] [PerturbRx: Learning Treatment-Conditioned Latent Transitions for Patient Drug Response Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21349)
*Yoshitaka Inoue,Minoh Jeong,Alfred Hero,Rui Kuang,Augustin Luna*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 提出PerturbRx框架，通过学习药物干预引发的潜在状态转移来改进患者级癌症治疗反应预测，在不需治疗后测量数据的情况下取得最优聚合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏数据和肿瘤异质性限制了患者级癌症治疗反应预测。现有方法仅从治疗前的分子谱和药物表征预测反应，未显式建模治疗预期引起的分子变化，缺乏对干预效应的利用。

Method: PerturbRx是一种治疗条件化的表示学习框架。它先从上下文匹配但细胞未配对的对照与处理单细胞群体中训练药物和剂量条件化的转移预测器，学习干预诱导的潜在状态转移；随后冻结该预测器并将其迁移到治疗前患者档案上，无需治疗后测量数据；最后将转移结合患者和药物表示用于反应预测。

Result: 在TCGA和患者来源异种移植（PDX）基准上，PerturbRx在所有评估方法中取得最强的聚合预测性能。

Conclusion: 扰动预训练的潜在状态转移可作为一种有效的表示用于患者级药物反应预测，表明干预诱导的分子变化建模对治疗反应预测具有重要价值。

Abstract: Scarce data and tumor heterogeneity limit patient-level cancer treatment-response prediction. Existing approaches predict response from pretreatment molecular profiles and drug representations, without explicitly modeling the molecular changes expected under treatment. We propose PerturbRx, a treatment-conditioned representation learning framework that learns intervention-induced latent transitions and uses them as patient-drug response features. PerturbRx trains a drug- and dose-conditioned transition predictor from context-matched but cell-unpaired control and treated single-cell populations, then freezes and transfers the predictor to pretreatment patient profiles without requiring post-treatment measurements. The transition is combined with patient and drug representations to predict response. Across TCGA and patient-derived xenograft benchmarks, PerturbRx achieves the strongest aggregate predictive performance among the evaluated methods. These results support perturbation-pretrained latent transitions as useful representations for patient-level drug-response prediction.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [343] [Harmonic Torsional Diffusion for Protein-Ligand Flexible Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20366)
*Maksim Zhdanov,Pavel Strashnov,Vladislav Kurenkov*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: Harmony是一种谐波扭角扩散框架，用于柔性蛋白-配体对接。它将配体和侧链扭角的分数场参数化为圆上学习的谐波势的导数，通过方差爆炸扩散在环面上的热半群解析提供噪声级依赖，从而显式处理周期性几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的对接模型大多使用通用的欧几里得空间头部来预测扭角更新，忽略了角变量的周期性几何。这种不匹配在柔性对接中尤为突出，因为配体构象和口袋侧链需要协同适应形成结合复合物，频率感知的结构先验是必要的。

Method: Harmony将配体和侧链扭角的分数场参数化为圆上学习的谐波势的导数，其噪声级依赖由方差爆炸扩散在环面上的热半群解析提供。这一构造显式纳入周期性，并赋予模型关于旋转异构体运动的频率感知归纳偏置。

Result: 在PDBBind基准上，Harmony在配体姿态精度和口袋全原子重建方面优于近期柔性对接方法；在PoseBusters上改善了生成复合物的物理有效性。EBNA1（极性结合位点）和KRAS G12D（浅结合位点）的案例研究进一步验证了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 将分数参数化与扩散过程的几何对齐是一种简单而有效的改进柔性对接的策略，表明周期性感知的模型设计能显著提升柔性对接性能。

Abstract: Molecular docking requires reasoning jointly about ligand pose and protein flexibility. Most diffusion-based docking models predict torsional updates with generic Euclidean heads that ignore the periodic geometry of angular variables. This mismatch is especially limiting in flexible docking, where ligand conformations and pocket side chains co-adapt to form the bound complex. Here, we introduce Harmony, a harmonic torsional diffusion framework for flexible protein-ligand docking. Harmony parameterizes ligand and side-chain torsional score fields as derivatives of learned harmonic potentials on the circle, whose noise-level dependence is supplied analytically by the heat semigroup of variance-exploding diffusion on the torus. This construction makes periodicity explicit and gives the model a frequency-aware inductive bias over rotameric motion. On the PDBBind benchmark, Harmony improves ligand pose accuracy and pocket all-atom reconstruction over recent flexible docking methods. On PoseBusters, it improves the physical validity of generated complexes. Case studies on EBNA1 and KRAS G12D illustrate the method's behavior on a polar and a shallow binding site, respectively. Together, these results indicate that aligning the score parameterization with the geometry of the diffusion process is a simple and effective lever for improving flexible docking.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [344] [Humanoid Musical Robots as Experimental Interfaces for Music-Evoked Emotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20433)
*Vincent K. M. Cheung,Jia-Yeu Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将音乐类人形机器人作为研究音乐诱发情绪的受控实验界面的新视角，并通过WAS-5人形机器人进行案例分析证明其技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音乐诱发情绪研究受限于依赖预录听觉刺激的实验范式，无法捕捉真实音乐体验中多模态、具身性和交互性的本质，需要新的方法论平台。

Method: 基于音乐认知与情绪理论识别需要同时操控声学与非声学变量的机制，论证人形机器人具备参数控制、跨试验可重复性以及听觉/视觉/交互组件解耦与重组能力；以WAS-5萨克斯机器人作为案例验证技术可行性。

Result: 案例分析证明WAS-5能够实现对声学与交互变量的可重复控制，满足未来音乐情绪实验的先决条件，验证了该视角的技术可行性。

Conclusion: 音乐类人形机器人可作为方法论平台，支撑未来对音乐诱发情绪的受控实验研究。

Abstract: Advances in technology have led to increasingly sophisticated musical humanoid robots. However, their use has largely been limited to performance and related research in human-robot interaction. In this position paper, we propose a novel perspective: musical humanoid robots as experimental interfaces for investigating music-evoked emotions. We argue that current research is constrained by paradigms relying on pre-recorded auditory stimuli, which fail to capture the multimodal, embodied, and interactive nature of real-world musical experience. Building on existing theories of music cognition and emotion, we identify mechanisms that require controlled manipulation of both acoustic and non-acoustic variables. We show that humanoid robots are well-suited as they enable parametric control of performance variables, reproducibility across trials, and the decoupling and recombination of auditory, visual, and interactive components. We illustrate the technical feasibility of this perspective through a case study of the WAseda Saxophonist Robot 5 (WAS-5), demonstrating reproducible control of acoustic and interaction variables that are prerequisites for future music-emotion experiments. Our work positions musical humanoid robots as a methodological platform that enables future controlled investigations of music-evoked emotions.

</details>


### [345] [EndoLIFT: Language-Disambiguated Latent-Conditioned Rectified Flow for Bidirectional Endoscopic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20478)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Lui Siu Hing,Jinsong Lin,Tianchun Wu,Ho Yin Chim,Zhiqing Tang,Tao Yang,Huxin Gao,Trevor Yeung,Raymond Shing-Yan Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoLIFT，一种面向双向内窥镜控制的视觉-语言-动作策略，通过语言指令条件化与潜变量条件化的修正流动作专家相结合，解决内窥镜操作中视觉场景不变但轴向动作意图相反的问意图混叠（intent aliasing）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胃肠道内窥镜检查本质上是双向的：器械需要前进到达目标解剖位置，随后撤回或反转进行检查，而外部指令可能在视觉场景改变之前就要求方向反转。这导致几乎相同的视觉观测需要相反的轴向动作，形成"意图混叠"歧义。现有方法缺乏对这种歧义的显式建模，需要一种能够区分语言意图并稳健执行正确轴向动作的策略。

Method: EndoLIFT由显式语言意图条件化与潜变量条件化的修正流（rectified-flow）动作专家组成。策略输入为RGB图像、语言指令和上一动作状态；一个32维变分轨迹潜变量（VTL）随机条件化连续动作块生成。通过受控的同观测指令交换实验来验证语言对轴向模式的选择能力，并对比无潜变量条件的匹配模型和1位模式标志（mode-flag）参考架构。

Result: 相比无潜变量条件的匹配模型，EndoLIFT导航方向准确率提升11.1个百分点，错误方向前进减少83%。在44个保留语言变体上保持82.8%的意图跟随准确率。闭环评估中，EndoLIFT在已见结肠模型和未见的肺、胃模型上相比无VTL版本整体成功率提升30个百分点，并在离体猪气管试验中完成10/10。

Conclusion: 语言基的意图选择与轨迹潜变量的贡献是可分离的：语言负责轴向模式的选择，而轨迹潜变量负责方向正确性和稳健的撤回动作。这种分离设计使EndoLIFT在双向内窥镜控制中实现了更高的意图跟随能力和更泛化的闭环操作性能。

Abstract: Routine gastrointestinal endoscopy is intrinsically bidirectional: the instrument is advanced to reach target anatomy and later withdrawn or retroflexed for inspection, while an external cue may require earlier reversal. When the requested phase changes before the visual scene does, nearly identical observations can require opposite axial actions. We identify and formalize this ambiguity in bidirectional endoscopic control as intent aliasing. We propose EndoLIFT (Endoscopic Language-Instruction Flow with Trajectory Latents), a vision-language-action policy that combines explicit language-based intent conditioning with a latent-conditioned rectified-flow action expert. The policy receives RGB, a language instruction, and the previous-action state; a 32-D variational trajectory latent stochastically conditions continuous action-chunk generation. Controlled same-observation instruction swaps establish that language selects the axial mode, independently of whether the trajectory latent is present. Relative to the matched model without latent conditioning, EndoLIFT improves navigation-direction accuracy by 11.1 percentage points and reduces wrong-direction advance by 83\%. An architecture-controlled 1-bit mode-flag reference exhibits weaker canonical-anchor switching, while EndoLIFT retains 82.8\% intent-following accuracy across 44 held-out linguistic variants. In closed-loop evaluation, EndoLIFT improves overall success by 30 percentage points over EndoLIFT w/o VTL on both the seen colon phantom and the unseen lung and stomach phantoms, and completes 10/10 ex-vivo porcine-trachea trials. These results separate language-based intent selection from the trajectory latent's contribution to directional correctness and robust retraction.

</details>


### [346] [Koala Gripper: Co-designing Robotic Grippers and Data-Capture Devices for Scaling Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20546)
*Amar Hajj-Ahmad,Zubin Kremer Guha,Tim Fofonoff,Zhi Ern Teoh,Ciarán T. O'Neill,Ben Thacher,Igor Fala,Vidullan Surendran,Murphy Wonsick,Peter Whitney,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种共设计框架，用于同时开发手持数据采集设备和对应的机器人执行夹爪，并据此设计了Koala Gripper系统——一种结合了人手操控灵活性与机器人夹爪可靠性的数据采集与执行平台，通过新颖的力优化扳机连杆机构、单片双拇指结构以及人性化设计，实现了比平行夹爪更高的灵巧性和抓取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模操作数据集需求的增长，手持机器人夹爪数据采集设备及其夹爪设计变得愈发重要。然而现有的数据采集设备设计趋向于匹配已有机器人夹爪的形态，牺牲了人体工程学和操作性能。作者希望打破这一局限，通过将数据采集平台和机器人执行平台的约束同时纳入设计过程，实现两者的协同优化。

Method: 提出一个共设计框架（co-design framework），将数据采集设备与机器人执行设备的约束同时纳入设计流程。基于此框架设计了Koala Gripper系统，其关键创新包括：(1) 一种力优化的手指/扳机连杆机构，具有方向性反射质量特性（directional reflected mass）；(2) 独特的单片双拇指（monolithic dual-thumb）结构；(3) 以用户为中心的人体工学设计。该设计的机器人手指可反向驱动（backdrivable），有效质量在数十克量级。

Result: 实验表明Koala Gripper能够在广泛物体范围内实现稳固抓取、强力工具使用和精确分拣。此外，通过端到端的数据采集与策略执行流水线（学习从示范中学习）验证了该平台的可行性和实际部署能力。

Conclusion: Koala Gripper证明了通过共设计框架可以同时兼顾数据采集的易用性和机器人执行的性能，相比平行夹爪在灵巧性和抓取能力上有所提升，同时保持可扩展性和易用性，为大规模操作数据采集与机器人学习提供了一种新的硬件平台方案。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [347] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Logic-VLA是一个形式化需求感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过基于语法图的信号时序逻辑（STL）编码器，结合两阶段策略适应（STL条件监督微调+轨迹级偏好优化），在不牺牲自然语言任务性能的前提下显著提升STL满足率（24.8-40.7个百分点），并在未见过的STL公式上展现泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖自然语言指令，但自然语言难以精确表达安全关键或时空约束需求（如时序、空间位置条件）。这导致模型在需要满足严格形式化约束的场景下无法保证行为正确性。

Method: Logic-VLA包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于语法图的STL编码器，预训练以捕获时序逻辑语义；(2) 第一阶段STL条件监督微调（在满足演示上训练）；(3) 第二阶段轨迹级偏好优化，使用流匹配代理实现身份偏好优化（Identity Preference Optimization），在满足-违反配对的轨迹之间进行优化。

Result: 在闭环四旋翼导航仿真中，Logic-VLA相比STL盲基础策略将STL满足率提高了24.8至40.7个百分点，而自然语言任务成功率最多仅降低1.8个百分点。同时能够泛化到训练中未见过的STL公式。

Conclusion: 单个VLA模型能够在推理时适应不同的形式化需求（STL规范），无需为每个规范单独训练策略，在满足形式化约束的同时保持自然语言任务表现。这为VLA模型在安全关键场景中的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>


### [348] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Trajectory Tracking of Differentially Flat Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20655)
*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）相结合，用于固定翼无人机的规划与控制。该框架引入风感知采样策略，在NMPC内部生成动态可行的参考轨迹，主动考虑风扰动并严格满足气动和控制输入约束。通过仿真和真实飞行实验验证，在强风条件下提升了轨迹跟踪精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机的规划与控制面临非线性动力学、气动极限和环境扰动等挑战。微分平坦性虽能高效生成可行轨迹，但多局限于无模型控制器，缺乏预测能力且需要大量参数调优。因此需要一种既能高效规划又具备预测和约束感知能力的统一控制框架。

Method: 提出统一框架：（1）基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成模块，实现计算高效的路径规划；（2）NMPC控制器，提供预测性和约束感知的控制能力；（3）在NMPC框架内嵌入风感知采样策略，生成考虑风扰动的动态可行参考轨迹，并严格强制执行气动和输入约束。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实飞行实验验证，该框架在复杂轨迹下表现出改进的跟踪精度和鲁棒性；在强风条件下，采用所提出的风感知采样策略时性能提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 提出的集成框架有效融合了微分平坦性规划的高效性与NMPC的预测控制优势，并借助风感知采样显著增强了强风环境下的鲁棒性，为固定翼无人机的复杂轨迹跟踪提供了一种实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [349] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向机器人模仿学习的"重新训练校准式"遗忘审计框架，从行为（行为与重新训练策略的差异）和证据（成员攻击能否检测出训练痕迹）两个轴评估演示遗忘效果，并引入符合性检验将两轴联合为单一假设来检验与重新训练的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习依赖人类演示数据，但用户可能要求删除部分演示。重新训练是自然参照，但随策略和数据集规模增长代价过高，因此需要更廉价的编辑算子。然而现有的机器学习遗忘指标（如遗忘损失、单一成员攻击）无法评估闭环运行的策略编辑实际删除了什么，缺乏具有参照锚定的评估框架。

Method: 提出一个"重新训练校准式"审计框架，从两个轴评估演示遗忘：一是行为轴，在匹配状态下度量编辑策略与重新训练策略的动作散度，并用独立重新训练之间的差异作为校准地板；二是证据轴，对每条演示执行成员攻击并对照重新训练零分布，同时报告排名和绝对成员损失水平。最后用符合性检验将两轴结合为联合的重新训练一致性假设，并与多个独立重新训练组成的舰队进行显著性检验。

Result: 在三个真实机器人策略类别和两个模拟套件上的五个预先注册条件下，两个轴可在同一检查点上双向分离：编辑可能修复任务行为但证据不变，或降低证据但行为偏离重新训练。在ACT分支上，一种重定向编辑将盲评机器人成功率恢复到20次试验中的18次。

Conclusion: 仅靠单一轴或传统遗忘指标无法充分刻画闭环策略的演示遗忘效果，行为与证据两轴常发生分离，需要联合审计才能可靠评估编辑是否符合重新训练参照。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [350] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需示范或参考轨迹、仅凭强化学习从零合成自然拟人坐立（STS）动作的方法。通过单一PPO策略，结合耦合力/椅子高度课程、逆运动学生成的多样化初始与目标姿态采样、以及基于生物力学和最优控制的奖励设计，策略在确定性评估器上实现了跨八种椅子高度的97%以上平衡站立成功率，并具备优于现有技术的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人从坐姿起身（STS）存在无穷多种保持平衡的可行路径，是一个非常具有挑战性的控制问题。现有方法通常依赖示教数据或参考运动轨迹，限制了可扩展性和泛化能力。本文旨在实现无需任何外部示范、完全从零开始的自然拟人STS动作合成。

Method: 方法由三个互补组件构成：(i) 耦合的力/椅子高度课程：垂直骨盆辅助力在训练早期辅助轨迹探索并随训练衰减，椅子高度逐步解锁，避免过早分布偏移导致的泛化崩溃；(ii) 运动鲁棒性：从大量逆运动学生成的初始与目标姿态中随机采样，覆盖八种以上椅子高度；(iii) 奖励设计：基于生物力学与最优控制研究，通过角动量塑造seat-off动作，并利用质心压力吸引函数实现支撑区域过渡，确保平滑低损耗驱动。

Result: 在确定性无外力评估器上，策略在八种椅子高度下实现了超过97%的平衡站立成功率。策略能够在不同椅子高度间泛化出平滑运动，并能使机器人从相比现有技术更深的坐姿姿态中起身。

Conclusion: 本文证明了纯强化学习结合精心的课程设计、姿态多样性和生物力学启发式奖励，可以在无需示范的情况下合成高质量、可泛化的自然人形STS运动，为类人机器人运动控制提供了无需参考数据的可行范式。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories.
